# Woldeok Moneyverse — Integrated Planning Master
- **GitHub:** Draft PR #2119 OPEN/HOLD, parent #2113, engineering issue #2112 updated; no merge or deployment.
## v2026.10.31.1546 — F675-192 Expiry Reminder: Post-Lock Clock, Safe Inbox CTA and Atomic Evidence (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1544 → Current Feature Improvement v1546 → Next Feature Addition v1548 (provisional). **Authority:** latest origin/main `761a55a094fb9dd383d213e817305e68c79fa23c` verified at start/mid; AGENTS.md, README.md, canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean counterpart; parent Draft #2113, issue #2112. This supersedes the v1544 timing/CTA details only; all other v1544 requirements persist. No runtime or financial changes.

### Evidence, alternatives and actual user impact
1. **Verified code:** `backend/src/notification/notification.service.ts` builds search results from `public.in_app_notifications` and returns `link: null` unless it matches a regex beginning with /account, /wallet, /shop, /work, /quests, /stocks, /banking, /businesses, /board, /help, /casino, /marketplace or /notifications. `/inventory/expiring` is absent. The existing `NotificationQueryDto` and `NotificationSearchDto` allow `PRODUCT_ACTIVITY`; no new category is needed absent schema evidence. This is a source-level navigation mismatch, not proof that an unimplemented reminder was delivered.
2. **Time race in v1544 proposal:** PostgreSQL `statement_timestamp()` is fixed at the beginning of the current statement. A worker waiting on a locked preference row can cross the 7d/6d/1d/expiry boundary without changing that value. Reading opt-in or holding state from a pre-lock snapshot can similarly misclassify an opt-out or updated expiration. Options: (A) pre-lock timestamp (reject); (B) app-host clock (reject); (C) **choose** bounded DB transaction with ordered locks, fresh post-lock reads and one post-lock DB decision timestamp. A verified source failure is not asserted; this is a design race closed before implementation.
3. **Unverified prerequisite:** Numbered migrations do not establish `in_app_notifications` creation in the targeted scan; actual clean-schema table, constraints, category CHECK, `dedupe_key`, index, ownership and `moneyverse_app` privileges must be measured before a migration or implementation. Never treat the test-only CREATE TABLE as Production evidence.

### Owner flow and state contract
- Keep v1544's authenticated, consent-current owner-only `/inventory/expiring/reminders`, explicit master OFF, independently selected 7-day/1-day thresholds, Save/Cancel/Refresh, no email/push/auto-equip/payment/WLD. The predecessor `/inventory/expiring` is Draft; if it is not deployed, feature flag remains OFF and direct deep links fail closed.
- A committed inbox item may offer “View expiring items” to a verified `/inventory/expiring` route, but **never** promise that the item is still owned or unexpired when clicked. On open, reauthenticate and requery authoritative inventory; show changed/expired/removed states, not fabricated current holdings. When route is disabled or source unknown, show plain text without link.
- Status vocabulary: DISABLED, SAVED, UNSAVED, SCANNING (not delivery), COMMITTED_INBOX (receipt+inbox committed), SUPPRESSED_OPT_OUT, SUPPRESSED_WINDOW, DUPLICATE_SUPPRESSED, SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE, AUTH_REQUIRED, CONSENT_REQUIRED, CONFLICT, RATE_LIMITED. No “sent” claim from job enqueue, retry or dedupe conflict. On account/session switch, abort and discard previous member's data and pending responses. UI 320px, 44px targets, WCAG 2.2 AA, ko/en, UTC evidence/KST display.

### Transaction, precision and authorization
- **Lock order:** one short READ COMMITTED transaction; acquire owner preference row lock first (or use a stable owner-level advisory lock for an absent row, coordinated with preference creation), then eligible `user_items` row lock (`FOR SHARE` or stronger if needed for write conflict); skip/defer contended rows rather than waiting past a window. All preference PUTs and workers must follow the same order. Ensure no deadlocks with existing shop updates in isolated concurrent DB tests; if order cannot be made compatible, block rollout.
- **Fresh decision:** after acquiring locks, re-read preference enabled/threshold/revision and holding quantity, owner, catalog ID, exact `expires_at` in a fresh SQL statement; sample a single `clock_timestamp()` **after locks** as `decisionAtUtc`. If the implementation uses a SECURITY DEFINER PL/pgSQL worker, it must be VOLATILE and must not reuse a pre-lock STABLE snapshot. Evaluate `quantity>0`, `expires_at IS NOT NULL`, `decisionAtUtc < expires_at` and the half-open 7d [expiry−7d,expiry−6d) / 1d [expiry−1d,expiry) windows. Do not backfill after window exit. Never call a pre-lock `statement_timestamp()` the authoritative decision time.
- **Decision-vs-commit truth:** v1544's phrase “enabled at commit” is replaced with “enabled in a lock-serialized, post-lock decision and committed atomically before lock release”. A commit can occur after a wall-clock boundary; do not promise commit-time expiry eligibility. Enforce bounded lock/statement/transaction timeouts, and skip if the boundary passed **before** inbox insertion. Record `decisionAtUtc`, `committedAtUtc` and measured lag separately; the latter is an observation, not the eligibility clock. An opt-out committed before the worker acquires the preference lock suppresses it; opt-out committed afterward cannot recall an earlier committed inbox item.
- **Exactly one committed inbox record:** retain a unique receipt key (owner UUID, catalog UUID, PostgreSQL full-precision `timestamptz` expiry, threshold 1/7), no JS millisecond roundtrip or string truncation. In one transaction insert receipt `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING RETURNING`; only a returned row may insert inbox notification and update receipt with actual inbox ID. Require FK/unique constraint after schema review; rollback receipt if inbox insert/update fails. A conflict must create zero inbox rows. A changed expiry creates a distinct event only if it qualifies in a later window; historical receipts remain historical. Avoid reusing generic `dedupe_key` without confirming its actual unique/index policy.
- **Security:** owner identity is server-session-derived, preference writes CSRF+expectedRevision, no user ID in client body; dedicated least-privilege worker EXECUTE only, no direct app-role inventory/inbox table grants. Do not call any financial mutation function. Notification body uses escaped current catalogue text and an allowlisted internal route only; no sensitive item ID or user UUID in link, logs, analytics or external referrer.

### Inbox navigation and API compatibility
- The existing search projection's link filter must be extended narrowly to an anchored, normalized same-origin `/inventory/expiring` and reviewed descendants, or the notification must have a null link. Reject `//evil`, `/inventoryevil`, `/inventory/../admin`, encoded slash/backslash, percent-encoded control bytes, URL credentials, protocols, fragments or open-redirect query keys. Test browser inbox and keyword search projections; both must preserve the same safe navigation semantics. Keep category `PRODUCT_ACTIVITY` only if actual DB CHECK and UI filter support it. If not, gate implementation on a reviewed forward-only migration and parity tests.
- Keep Nest GET/PUT `/api/v1/shop/expiry-reminders/preferences`, private Next BFF, Android app-api parity, exact actor+consent guards, strict DTO and optimistic revision. Response 400/401/403/409/429/503 and 2xx/4xx/5xx/redirect private/no-store/noindex/no-referrer; no CDN caching or sitemap. Worker has no public API endpoint. No reminder click triggers purchase, equip, renewal or financial side effect.

### Administration, abuse, metrics and safe rollout
- Aggregate eligible, locked, deferred, window-skipped, committed, deduped, opt-out-suppressed, source-unavailable, p95 decision→commit and scan lag; distinct-owner cohorts >=20 only, no per-member settings or catalog identifiers. Targets (NOT measured): duplicate committed rows 0, cross-owner leaks 0, p95 eligible-to-inbox <=60 min, no notification created after a pre-insert window check fails. Alert on >1h backlog, lock starvation and missing schema/ACL.
- Feature flag `inventory_expiry_reminders_enabled` OFF by default; first schema/ACL provenance, clean PostgreSQL 17 replay and 10,001-row EXPLAIN, then independent exact-SHA Test and owner/Android/edge QA, limited opt-in cohort, separate Production promotion only after P0 gates. Disable flag stops future inserts without deleting historical receipts; forward-only rollback is flag OFF and no data loss. No new migrations or runtime changes in this planning cycle.

**Fresh runtime read-only probe (2026-10-31 09:10 KST):** Test public FX portal HTTP 500, Production public FX portal HTTP 200, Test /inventory HTTP 307 without redirect following. No exact-SHA or authenticated flow attestation; #2111 remains an independently reproduced release hold.

### Engineering backlog — P0 4 / P1 6
1. **P0 OPS1546-01 (Ops):** prove actual running frontend/backend SHA+digests, rollback artifact, independent encrypted restore, RPO/RTO and ledger/inventory/notification reconciliation (#1984). BLOCKED.
2. **P0 SEC1546-02 (B/DB):** reconcile DEV broad app-role DML and migration checksums, clean 311/311, notification schema/category/link/dedupe/ACL provenance, least-privilege worker roles (#993). BLOCKED.
3. **P0 FIN1546-03 (B/QA):** certify double-entry Treasury/MINT/SWF and prove worker has no WLD/ledger write capability (#1976). BLOCKED.
4. **P0 REL1546-04 (Ops/QA):** prevent Draft/HOLD bypass (#2031), resolve dependency audit, Test FX ACL (#2111), private-space owner reads (#2109), exact-SHA route ledger (#2103), independent restore and operator-reviewed promotion. BLOCKED.
5. **P1 REMIND1546-05 (B/DB):** forward-only owner preference/receipt schema and SQL functions, absent-preference lock strategy, post-lock fresh read/DB time, lock order and microsecond unique key; negative ACL and 10,001-row EXPLAIN. PROPOSED.
6. **P1 REMIND1546-06 (B/worker):** two-worker conflict-free inbox+receipt transaction, preference opt-out race, holding expiry mutation, 7d/6d/1d boundaries during lock wait, failure rollback and metrics. PROPOSED.
7. **P1 REMIND1546-07 (B/API + A/Next):** authenticated revisioned preference GET/PUT, strict CSRF, fail-closed UI/status, no false sent state, account switch purge. PROPOSED.
8. **P1 REMIND1546-08 (C/Android):** private app-api parity, opt-out/session fencing, offline cache disabled, secure link route support. PROPOSED.
9. **P1 REMIND1546-09 (A/B/Ops):** safe notification search/list CTA whitelist with malicious URL corpus, existing PRODUCT_ACTIVITY compatibility, edge no-store/noindex and admin cohort>=20 KPIs. PROPOSED.
10. **P1 REMIND1546-10 (QA):** isolated PostgreSQL 17 with 10,001 holdings, two owners/workers, 100 lock-wait-crossing-boundary cases, 100 opt-out/expiry mutation races, 100 rollback/retry/dedupe cases, 50 unsafe link/category/Android/edge cases, explicit no financial writes; Test exact SHA and rollback flag OFF. PROPOSED, NOT RUN.

**Verification boundary:** historical QA v1405 backend 2,778 PASS/501 SKIP, frontend 2,770 PASS, lint/typecheck/build PASS, dependency audit FAIL; later #2103 still lacks actual authenticated exact-SHA route ledger; #2111 Test FX 500 and #2031 Draft/HOLD bypass unresolved. No fresh runtime QA or synthetic acceptance for v1546. **DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD.** External independent references new approved 0, cumulative deduplicated/classified UNKNOWN / 10,000 = BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS.

## v2026.10.31.1544 — F675-192 Owner Inventory Expiry Reminder Opt-In (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1542 → Current Feature Addition v1544 → Next Feature Improvement v1546 (provisional). **Authority:** origin/main `761a55a094fb9dd383d213e817305e68c79fa23c`, AGENTS.md, README.md, English PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean counterpart rechecked at start/mid. Draft #2113 stacked after Draft #2105; engineering issue #2112. This is a proposal, not an implemented or release-approved capability.

### 1. Evidence, intent, boundaries and alternatives
- **Problem:** The current owner inventory page lists holdings but has no explicit owner opt-in for advance in-app holding-expiry notices. Current `frontend/src/app/inventory/page.tsx` calls `apiOrNull('/api/v1/shop/holdings')` and defaults a failed read to []; it cannot prove a verified empty inventory. `frontend/src/app/inventory/inventory-view.tsx` renders equipment from that payload. Migration `123` `shop_my_items_v2` filters quantity>0 and held `expires_at`, samples `clock_timestamp()` independently, and exposes separate effect/upkeep fields. NotificationController already has authenticated/consented owner inbox routes; NotificationService queries `public.in_app_notifications` directly. No numbered migration creating this table was found by the targeted source search: schema provenance, production availability and app-role privileges must be measured, **not assumed**. No evidence of actual exploit, delivery or user demand was measured.
- **Options:** A) client-local timers only (no offline reminders, unreliable); B) email/push (external permissions, privacy and deliverability burden); C) **chosen: opt-in in-app inbox reminder, scheduler plus transactionally deduplicated delivery**, no external provider. The current member calendar owned by C (#2096) is not extended or modified. The existing notification inbox remains the presentation channel; its schema must be independently verified first.
- **Target/KPI:** authenticated, consent-current member who owns expiring items. Intended outcome: timely, accurate notice without false financial or ownership claims. Hypotheses (not measured): opted-in activation rate, eligible-window delivery success, duplicate rate 0, p95 delivery lag <=60 minutes, 0 cross-owner exposures. Measure separately; do not call hypotheses actual results.

### 2. Entry, UX, CTA and complete states
- Entry `/inventory/expiring` (F675-188, still Draft) → “Expiry reminder settings” → `/inventory/expiring/reminders`. Direct deep link must require member+current consent. Show separate 7-day and 1-day checkboxes, master Enable/Disable, Save, Cancel, Refresh status; default disabled and no preselected consent. Explain reminders are informational, based on holding expiry, **not** effect expiry, unpaid maintenance, automatic unequip or permanent ownership. Use 7×24h/1×24h windows, UTC DB truth and KST-localized text; expose as-of timestamp and next scheduled scan only if verified.
- States: loading skeleton, disabled/no preferences, saved, unsaved changes, saving, verified empty eligible set, no expiration data (UNKNOWN), source unavailable 503, auth 401/consent 403, revision conflict 409, rate 429, offline/timeout/retry, account switch/logout clears local state and cancels stale responses. Never label 503 as “no expiring items”, never promise delivery before a committed inbox receipt. Disabled UI must not leak previous member's reminders.
- Accessibility: 320px responsive, labelled toggles with explicit status text, keyboard/focus/ARIA live confirmation without noisy announcements, contrast and 44px targets, WCAG 2.2 AA; i18n Korean/English with no misleading timezone or financial terminology.

### 3. Authentication, API and app-api
- Nest `GET /api/v1/shop/expiry-reminders/preferences` → `{enabled, remind7d, remind1d, revision, updatedAtUtc, asOfUtc}`. `PUT` same path accepts exact boolean allowlist and `expectedRevision` integer; CSRF, SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard, ConsentGuard, actor derived only from session, optimistic concurrency 409; never accept userId in body. Idempotent retry with same revision must not double-toggle; return conflict with fresh GET on stale revision. Reject unknown fields, malformed types, oversize body, duplicate JSON keys where parser supports detection. 400/401/403/409/429/503; 200 on verified update, 503 on unverified DB state.
- Next same-origin private BFF `/api/inventory/expiry-reminders/preferences` GET/PUT with CSRF protection and internal token server-only. No owner UUID in URL, localStorage, analytics, or logs. Android `/app-api/v1/shop/expiry-reminders/preferences` parity with session+consent and CSRF equivalent, strict response allowlist, no disk/offline cache; test gateway supports PUT and fail-close if not. No unauthenticated edge exception.
- Response policy on 2xx/4xx/5xx/redirect: `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`, no CDN shared cache, robots metadata on private page. BOLA cross-owner tests must assert indistinguishable 404/403 where applicable.

### 4. Database, constraints, index and transaction contract
- **Gate first:** inspect actual clean-schema creation and privileges for `in_app_notifications` and the currently deployed schema. Do not assume fixture-only schema is a production table. Confirm column names, allowed category, dedupe key, FKs and index before implementing. Any absent schema must be explicitly introduced in a reviewed forward-only migration; never grant app role direct SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE on member inventory or notification tables.
- Proposed `inventory_expiry_reminder_preferences`: `user_id uuid PRIMARY KEY` referencing actual identity lifecycle, `enabled boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false`, `remind_7d boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false`, `remind_1d boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false`, `revision bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK(revision>0)`, `updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL`. Explicit account-deletion policy and no orphan preferences. All writes through actor-scoped SECURITY DEFINER functions with fixed `search_path=pg_catalog,public` and EXECUTE only for `moneyverse_app`.
- Proposed `inventory_expiry_reminder_deliveries`: `user_id`, `catalog_id`, `holding_expires_at timestamptz`, `threshold_days smallint CHECK(IN (1,7))`, `notification_id`, `delivered_at`; unique (`user_id,catalog_id,holding_expires_at,threshold_days`), optional unique notification_id after verifying inbox schema. Historical receipt remains factual even if later item expiration changes; never rewrite it to imply currently expiring. Retention/account erasure must be approved against existing policy, not invented. Evaluate `user_items(expires_at,user_id,catalog_id)` partial index where quantity>0 and expires_at nonnull using EXPLAIN on 10,001 synthetic rows; create online where supported, no blocking production DDL without gate.
- Scheduled worker has a dedicated least-privilege login + NOLOGIN executor role; **not** `moneyverse_app` with table INSERT. Dedicated reviewed SECURITY DEFINER batch function processes max 200 eligible items per transaction, lock preference row, recheck opt-in and quantity/holding expiry under one DB UTC `statement_timestamp`, insert dedupe receipt `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING RETURNING` and inbox notification in **same transaction**; commit or roll back both. Two concurrent workers must yield at most one committed notification. Bound work via timeout, SKIP LOCKED where applicable, rate/batch budget and checkpoint. Do not send email/push or create WLD/ledger/outbox financial events.
- Eligibility: `quantity>0`, `expires_at IS NOT NULL`, `expires_at>asOfUtc` and exact owner. For 7-day: `expires_at-interval '7 days' <= asOfUtc < expires_at-interval '6 days'`. For 1-day: `expires_at-interval '1 day' <= asOfUtc < expires_at`. No catch-up outside window, no reminder on null/malformed expiry, expired holdings or disabled threshold. A late opt-in inside window may be sent once; opt-in after window is not backfilled. Opt-out and scheduler race is serialized on preference lock; already committed inbox items are historical and cannot be recalled. A changed expiry may produce a distinct later reminder only for the new expiry and its eligible window; show historical as-of context.

### 5. Admin, abuse, SEO, analytics and operations
- No member-level admin inventory of reminder settings or text. Aggregate daily eligible, attempted, committed, duplicate-suppressed, opt-out-suppressed, stale/source-error and p95 lag by threshold; suppress cohorts <20 and avoid reidentification by narrow filters. Rate-limit preference writes, CSRF, session replay, permission revocation, malformed payload and bot bursts; redact owner/catalog identifiers and message content from metrics and logs. Untrusted catalog names must be escaped and links allowlisted to same-origin inventory.
- Scheduler flag `inventory_expiry_reminders_enabled` OFF; staged read-only schema verification → isolated DB tests → exact-SHA Test → one small cohort opt-in → measured delivery and rollback → Production only after independent gate. Disable flag stops future deliveries without deleting historical inbox rows or altering balances. Migration rollback must be additive/forward-safe and preserve receipts. Worker outage => lag metric/alert, not “sent”. Respect noindex/private edge and Android parity.

### 6. Concrete P0/P1 development backlog and acceptance
- **P0 OPS1544-01:** prove Production component executable source SHA, artifact digests, schema/config and rollback identity; isolated newest-backup decrypt/restore, reconcile identity/inventory/notification/ledger and measure RPO/RTO (#1984). **BLOCKED**.
- **P0 SEC1544-02:** verify clean 311-migration schema, DEV checksum drift, notification table provenance, `moneyverse_app` direct privileges and no broad grants (#993). **BLOCKED**.
- **P0 FIN1544-03:** prove Treasury/MINT/SWF and double-entry invariants remain untouched; alert worker cannot call economic mutation functions (#1976). **BLOCKED**.
- **P0 REL1544-04:** preserve Draft/HOLD merge gate, dependency audit and exact-SHA official Test; address QA route ledger (#2103), Test FX 500/ACL (#2111) and private space BOLA (#2109), private SEO and independent restore. **BLOCKED**.
- **P1 REMIND1544-05:** owner-scoped SQL preference functions, eligibility and migration/index/ACL tests; inspect actual inbox schema before choosing FK/category. **PROPOSED**.
- **P1 REMIND1544-06:** dedicated scheduler/transactional inbox+receipt idempotency, opt-out lock and metrics; no financial writes. **PROPOSED**.
- **P1 REMIND1544-07:** Nest DTO/guards/CSRF/versioned PUT and Next page/BFF states, fail-closed response mapping. **PROPOSED**.
- **P1 REMIND1544-08:** Android gateway PUT/GET contract, session fencing, offline privacy, no stale cached preferences. **PROPOSED**.
- **P1 REMIND1544-09:** edge privacy/noindex, a11y, localization, admin aggregate cohort>=20, rate/abuse budgets. **PROPOSED**.
- **P1 REMIND1544-10:** isolated PostgreSQL 17 test with 10,001 holdings, 2 owners, 2 workers, exact 7d/6d/1d/expiry boundaries, null/changed expiry, opt-out race, retry after rollback, duplicate keys, 401/403/409/429/503, Android/edge/crawler, restore and rollback. **PROPOSED, NOT RUN**.
- Historical QA v1405: backend 2,778 PASS / 501 SKIP; frontend 2,770 PASS; lint/typecheck/build PASS; full dependency audit FAIL. Later exact-SHA route evidence is incomplete (#2103), and fresh #2111 reports Test FX HTTP 500 from ACL and stale Production reference quote. These are separate from this unimplemented feature. No claim of latest branch tests, Test acceptance or Production readiness. External independent references newly approved **0**, cumulative verified/deduped/classified **UNKNOWN / 10,000**, gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. **DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD**.

## v2026.10.31.1542 — F675-191 Preflight: Bind the Affected Target and Reject Stale Evidence (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1540 → Current Feature Improvement v1542 → Next Feature Addition v1544 (provisional). **Authority:** origin/main 761a55a094fb9dd383d213e817305e68c79fa23c rechecked at start and mid, AGENTS.md, README.md, canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean parity; Draft #2105 stacked after Draft #2101, handoff #2100. Docs only. No implementation, merge or release.

### Source-grounded defect and design alternatives
- v1540's preflight request includes only candidateId and slot. The parent F675-190 preview is expressly for an equipped item **expiring within 7 or 30 days**, but without expectedTargetCatalogId and withinDays the preflight cannot prove it is reviewing that affected item. An owner may submit a valid candidate for an unrelated slot, or the slot occupant may have changed between screens; a generic REVIEWABLE would be misleading. This is a **design-contract omission**, not a confirmed exploit.
- Migration 114 inventory_equip_item(p_actor,p_catalog_id,p_slot,p_equip) checks owner quantity and the eight allowed slots, then updates equipment without a category-to-slot/expiry check or owner-slot unique index. It does not take an explicit per-owner serialization lock. Its profile_get_cosmetics may show expired/zero-quantity equipped items that migration 123 shop_my_items_v2 excludes. The current inventory client hardcodes badge_1 on badge equip. These are observed source-level risks, not proof of live data corruption.
- Alternatives: (A) accept candidateId+slot and trust the prior UI (rejected: stale/unrelated target), (B) carry a client-side opaque proof as equip authority (rejected: replay/TOCTOU and new write capability), (C) **chosen**: read-only owner SQL re-derives the current target from raw rows and binds expected target UUID + horizon to the request. No auto-equip, purchase, upkeep, financial write or promise of equip success.

### Owner flow, authority and state machine
- Only authenticated, consent-current owner. Entry /inventory → expiring 7/30-day radar → equipped impact → replacement candidates → Review swap. Proposed private panel remains /inventory/expiring/equipped-impact/replacements/preflight; no public deep link or index. Keep prior screens and this feature default OFF until all dependencies pass.
- Request contract: candidateId UUID, expectedTargetCatalogId UUID, slot in frame/background/effect/nameplate/title/badge_1/badge_2/badge_3, withinDays exactly 7 or 30. All fields required, no actor UUID supplied by client; server derives owner and actual session ID from authenticated request. Reject duplicate/unknown keys, malformed UUIDs, unsupported slot/horizon with 400. A selected badge slot is not silently converted to badge_1.
- In a single consistent owner-source snapshot: (1) validate **all** owner equipment rows including invalid flags, duplicate slots, zero quantity, expired flagged rows, category mismatch and ghost slot values; (2) locate exactly one currently equipped occupant of requested slot; (3) require its catalog_id equals expectedTargetCatalogId; (4) require non-NULL target holding expiry and asOfUtc < expiry <= asOfUtc + withinDays*24h; (5) validate candidate belongs to same owner, quantity>0, not equipped, distinct catalog ID, same category for slot and unexpired if expiry is known. Do not infer NULL expiry means permanent or that arrears=0 means paid.
- Target absent/replaced or no longer impacted → 409 TARGET_CHANGED / TARGET_NOT_IMPACTED; already expired target → 409 TARGET_ALREADY_EXPIRED; duplicate/corrupt full equipment source → 409 EQUIPMENT_SOURCE_CONFLICT. Candidate absent **or foreign** → indistinguishable 404. Auth 401, consent 403, throttled 429, unavailable 503. Never convert 503 or malformed 200 into empty/REVIEWABLE. These 409 codes do not disclose another member's identifiers.
- Only a fully verified owner/target/candidate read may return 200 REVIEWABLE (manual review, **not** permission or likelihood of successful equip), 200 BLOCKED_EXPIRED / BLOCKED_SLOT_MISMATCH / BLOCKED_ALREADY_EQUIPPED, or 200 UNKNOWN_POLICY (insufficient expiry/effect/upkeep policy evidence). **Supersede** v1540's proposed 200 BLOCKED_NOT_OWNED: missing/foreign candidate must be 404. A structural target conflict always uses 409. No success-shaped verified-empty for a missing target.
- UI: show selected affected slot and verified current occupant, target and candidate current catalog names (not historical purchase labels), KST expiry with UTC source, explicit policy unknown and Refresh checks / Back to candidates. No Equip now CTA, no automatic writes. Preserve existing /inventory manual equip only under independent B/DB write-side hardening. 320px, 200% zoom, 44px targets, WCAG 2.2 AA, keyboard and screen-reader table, aria-live without focus theft, ko/en parity. Account/session/consent/slot/horizon changes clear panel, abort and fence in-flight responses, offline is not verified-empty.

### API, SQL, transaction, evidence lifetime and abuse protection
- Proposed internal Nest GET /api/v1/shop/holdings/equip-preflight with strictly allowlisted candidateId, expectedTargetCatalogId, slot, withinDays; SessionGuard + AuthenticatedGuard + ConsentGuard and per-actor/IP rate limit. Route is **read-only**; never accept an evidence token as permission on the existing equip POST. Nest binds actor via requireUserId(request), session via requireSession(request).id; no client actor. Validate strict response DTO, <=16 KiB and stable reason codes.
- Prefer **browser-facing Next POST-for-read BFF** with CSRF, origin checks, validated JSON body and Cache-Control private,no-store: this avoids UUIDs in browser address/history and edge query logs. The server-only BFF calls internal Nest GET without exposing internal credentials. For Android, first prove the existing app-api gateway supports a private POST-for-read with identical body and status semantics; otherwise keep a private GET only with verified URL/query-log redaction and record this as a blocker, not a silently assumed parity. No mutation or cache even though the external transport may be POST.
- Forward-only proposed public.inventory_equip_preflight_v1(p_actor uuid,p_candidate uuid,p_expected_target uuid,p_slot text,p_within_days integer) SECURITY DEFINER, fixed search_path pg_catalog,pg_temp, schema-qualified tables, explicit owner WHERE on all user_items. REVOKE PUBLIC EXECUTE, GRANT EXECUTE only to moneyverse_app; **no direct app table grants**. New migration number unreserved, coordinate B. Do not modify applied 114/123 or live DB. Check existing owner/catalog PK/FK and EXPLAIN 10,001 seeded rows before any extra index.
- One DB-authoritative statement_timestamp() in the SAME SQL statement; if more than one SQL statement, use a single PoolClient BEGIN READ ONLY ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ with explicit COMMIT/ROLLBACK. Calculate asOfUtc, target/candidate/source digest and validUntil from the same snapshot. Fingerprint all owner equipment rows (including invalid/expired/zero rows), actual target and candidate with category, name, active, quantity, equip flag, slot, expiry at microsecond precision; a catalogue rename or target swap invalidates evidence. No independent clock_timestamp calls that can split the decision.
- validUntilUtc = min(asOfUtc+30 seconds, target.expires_at, candidate.expires_at if known, earliest time-only 7/30-day horizon entry/exit affecting target). At DB now >= validUntilUtc, return 409 STALE_PREFLIGHT, including when there was no DB write; signed diagnostic evidence is NOT a capability. Expired evidence age 410, source fingerprint/session change 409 or require fresh authenticated request. The evidence cannot lock the future equip transaction; B's write function must revalidate category, quantity, expiry, target/slot and serialize owner equipment under its own transaction. No optimistic claim of atomicity across GET and POST.
- Private response headers at Next, app-api and real public Nginx for 200/400/401/403/404/409/410/429/503/redirect: Cache-Control private,no-store; X-Robots-Tag noindex,nofollow; Referrer-Policy no-referrer; no sitemap or analytics UUIDs. Redact internal query/body logs and tracing. Admin dashboard sees only cohort >=20 distinct owners and aggregate REVIEWABLE/UNKNOWN/409/410/429/503 rates, p95 read latency, stale refresh, zero identifiable fields. Targets: zero cross-owner disclosures, zero equipment/WLD/ledger writes, p95 <300ms on synthetic 10,001 rows; targets are NOT measured.

### Concrete developer handoff (4 P0 + 6 P1)
1. **P0 OPS1542-01 (Ops)** — #1984/#2097: prove actual Production running SHA/digest, immutable rollback artifact, independent off-host restore. Do not restart/prune from planning.

4. **P0 REL1542-04 (Ops/QA)** — #2031/#1088/#2012/#2103: Draft/HOLD merge protection, complete dependency audit, exact-SHA official Test authenticated E2E/Android/edge and full-route evidence, independent restore, immutable release promotion and operator approval.
5. **P1 PREFLIGHT1542-05 (B/DB)** — implement owner-source function with expected target + horizon, full-set invariants, one timestamp, source fingerprint, negative ACL, 10,001-row EXPLAIN. B write-path category/expiry/serialization fix is a separate branch.

## v2026.10.31.1540 — F675-191 Owner Equipment Swap Readiness Preflight (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **Review:** Draft PR #2101 / engineering issue #2100 / parent Draft #2093 / RELEASE HOLD. Not implemented or deployed.

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1538 → Current Feature Addition v1540 → Next Feature Improvement v1542 (provisional). **Authority:** latest origin/main `761a55a094fb9dd383d213e817305e68c79fa23c` verified at start/mid; AGENTS.md, README.md, canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean parity reviewed. Stacked on Draft #2093 (itself on #2089), no merge; independent engineering handoff for F675-191. This is a proposed, not implemented, owner-only read feature. No additional scattered planning specification.

### Problem, actual source, scope and alternatives
- Migration `114-economy-faucet-sink-and-shop-2.sql` `inventory_equip_item(p_actor,p_catalog_id,p_slot,p_equip)` checks quantity>0 and slot allowlist, then executes two UPDATEs. It does **not** validate item holding expiry, catalog category-to-slot compatibility or serialize all equipment changes per owner. The same migration declares eight slots (frame, background, effect, nameplate, title, badge_1/2/3), with no owner-slot uniqueness constraint. `profile_get_cosmetics` can display expired/zero-quantity equipped rows while migration 123 `shop_my_items_v2` filters them. These are source-level gaps, not evidence of exploitation.
- Existing `frontend/src/app/inventory/inventory-view.tsx` POSTs `/api/v1/shop/holdings/:catalogId/equip` with `slot=badge_1` for all badges, and no server-confirmed preflight. Nest `shop.controller.ts` POST is guarded by Session, Authenticated, Consent and CSRF; this existing write path must remain separately guarded. Current /inventory `apiOrNull || []` can falsely render outage as empty (#1977). F675-188/189/190 are unmerged Draft/OFF prerequisites.
- **Chosen V1:** a separate read-only readiness comparison for one selected already-owned candidate and one selected slot, reached from the F675-190 replacement candidate card via `Review swap / 교체 가능 여부 확인`. Alternative client-only validation is rejected because it trusts incomplete holdings; alternative automatic swap/write is rejected because the existing DB write invariants are unproven. Do not silently create a purchase, equip, upkeep payment, refund, mint, WLD transfer, notification or scheduled task. Never label a successful read as guaranteed successful equip.

### Member UX, permissions, states and contract
- Only authenticated, consent-current owner. Private route `/inventory/expiring/equipped-impact/replacements/preflight?candidateId=<uuid>&slot=<enum>` (server validated and never indexed); compare current slot occupant (if any), candidate's current catalog label, quantity, holding expiry, slot compatibility and an evidence timestamp. Current catalog name is not historical purchase name. Effect expiry and upkeep settlement are separate, unknown unless independently proven.
- CTA: `Review swap` opens a read-only confirmation panel; `Back to candidates`, `Refresh checks`. No `Equip now` action in V1; existing manual equip path remains separate and disabled from this panel until Backend B's write hardening, owner-slot uniqueness and exact-SHA acceptance are complete. For badges, respect selected badge_1/2/3; never default all replacements to badge_1.
- Status union `REVIEWABLE` (eligible for manual consideration, **not** a permission to equip), `BLOCKED_NOT_OWNED`, `BLOCKED_EXPIRED`, `BLOCKED_SLOT_MISMATCH`, `BLOCKED_DUPLICATE_EQUIPMENT`, `BLOCKED_SOURCE_INCONSISTENT`, `UNKNOWN_POLICY`. Fail closed: 400 invalid UUID/slot; 401 unauthenticated; 403 consent; 404 owner candidate absent (do not reveal foreign existence); 409 changed owner source or equipment conflict; 410 expired evidence; 429 rate; 503 upstream/DB failure. Distinguish verified empty occupant from unknown, unavailable or malformed response; never substitute an empty panel for 503. Null expiry = UNKNOWN, not permanent. No optimistic equip-success UI.
- Proposed Nest `GET /api/v1/shop/holdings/equip-preflight?candidateId=&slot=` with session/consent guards; Next server-only BFF `GET /api/v1/shop/holdings/equip-preflight` and Android `GET /app-api/v1/shop/holdings/equip-preflight` must have matching typed contract. No actor UUID from client. Response `{candidateId,slot,asOfUtc,validUntilUtc,status,reasonCodes,currentOccupant?,candidate:{name,category,quantity,expiresAtUtc},sourceStatus:'VERIFIED'}`; bounded data, no raw ledger or other owner's identifiers. `asOfUtc` and expiry in microsecond-accurate UTC; screen uses KST with explicit zone.

### DB, transaction, security, consistency, accessibility and telemetry
- New **proposed forward-only migration** (no number reserved; coordinate with B) defines `public.inventory_equip_preflight_v1(p_actor uuid,p_candidate uuid,p_slot text)` as `SECURITY DEFINER`, fixed `search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp`, schema-qualified relations, owner predicate for all `user_items`, `REVOKE EXECUTE FROM PUBLIC`, `GRANT EXECUTE TO moneyverse_app` only; reassert deny direct app-role SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE on private holdings and related tables. Never rewrite migration 114 or 123. Existing user_items owner/catalog PK, FK, quantity constraints remain; evaluate only justified owner+slot index with EXPLAIN (10,001 synthetic items) and no duplicate index. No new table, materialized state or financial DDL.
- Read-only, one statement timestamp and one consistent snapshot (one SQL statement, or READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ for multiple). Inspect **all** owner's equipped rows before filtering: duplicates per slot, NULL slot with equipped true, non-NULL slot with equipped false, quantity<=0, category/slot mismatch, expired equipped rows, candidate already equipped, candidate expiry, current slot occupant, source drift. Return evidence status, not a mutation authorization. A signed, server-generated evidence identifier may be displayed for diagnostics but MUST NOT be accepted as authorization by the existing equip POST. No cross-request transaction, lock, or promise of TOCTOU-free write. Fresh POST must independently revalidate after Backend B hardens the SQL write path.
- `validUntilUtc = min(asOfUtc+30 seconds, earliest relevant candidate/current holding expiry, relevant 7/30-day horizon boundary)`; a future horizon-entry boundary also invalidates cached evidence without any row write. If source changes or session generation changes, discard panel and require Refresh. No stale results on account switch, logout, consent revocation, offline, or browser back navigation. Bounded response <=16 KiB, per-actor rate budget and statement timeout, no sensitive params in logs. Avoid cache and referer leakage of candidate ID: prefer server-only POST-for-read BFF if URL logs cannot be suppressed, while Nest remains read-only GET with validated query; ensure CSRF semantics if using POST-for-read.
- Privacy and SEO: authenticated edge and app-api must emit `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` for success/errors/redirects (#2012); no sitemap, public previews or analytics with UUIDs. 320px layout, 200% zoom, 44px controls, focus restore, keyboard/screen-reader semantic comparison, aria-live non-focus-stealing error/status, ko/en parity, WCAG 2.2 AA. Android must bind session generation, cancel in-flight requests and never disk-cache evidence.
- Admin: only aggregate adoption, reviewable/block/unknown breakdown, 409/410/429/503, p95 query latency, refresh rate and account-switch stale-response drops; suppress cohorts <20 distinct owners, no candidate IDs, item names, user IDs or exact timestamps. KPIs **targets**, not measured: 0 cross-owner disclosure, 0 misleading "equip succeeded" claims, 0 financial/holding writes, p95 <300ms under 10,001 synthetic items.

### P0/P1 developer backlog and release gate (4+6)
1. **P0 OPS1540-01** — #1984/#2097 prove Production executable generation, actual running process SHA, rollback assets, and independent external backup restore; never restart or prune by planning.
2. **P0 SEC1540-02** — #993/#2094 fix DEV effective direct DML/TRUNCATE privileges and five applied checksum mismatches; verify fresh 311/311 migrations and deny table access for `moneyverse_app` before preflight SQL.
3. **P0 FIN1540-03** — #1507/#1854 and finance QA: preserve ledger-first invariants; no claim of upkeep settlement, purchase proof or WLD movement from equipment readiness.
4. **P0 REL1540-04** — #2031/#1088/#2012 require Draft/HOLD protection, edge noindex, authenticated official Test exact-SHA full frontend/backend/DB/app-api/Android E2E, independent restore, GitOps approval and operator signoff; no Production promotion until all gates PASS.
5. **P1 PREFLIGHT1540-05 (B/DB)** — forward-only owner-only SECURITY DEFINER function, slot/category/quantity/expiry/full-equipped validation, negative grants, index EXPLAIN and concurrency/TOCTOU oracle; coordinate B equip write hardening separately.
6. **P1 PREFLIGHT1540-06 (B/API)** — guarded typed Nest GET, strict UUID/slot validation, error mapping, no sensitive logging, bounded payload, session/consent and rate limits.
7. **P1 PREFLIGHT1540-07 (A/Next)** — private comparison panel, accessible CTA, verified-empty/error distinction, badge slot fidelity, account-switch fence; preserve existing equip path without enabling new mutation.
8. **P1 PREFLIGHT1540-08 (C/Android)** — app-api parity, login/consent/session switch/offline handling, no disk cache, exact-source response parser and privacy headers.
9. **P1 PREFLIGHT1540-09 (A/QA/ops)** — public edge noindex/no-store on 200/4xx/5xx/redirect, aggregate admin KPIs >=20, abuse budget, no PII/UUID telemetry.
10. **P1 PREFLIGHT1540-10 (QA/B)** — proposed **NOT RUN** isolated PG 10,001 synthetic holdings/2 owners/8 slots; 100 category/slot/expiry/null/duplicate cases, 100 account-switch/session cases, 100 time-only horizon crossings, 100 concurrent equip/preflight races, 50 Android/edge cases; verify zero writes and rollback flag OFF.

**Rollout:** `inventory_equip_swap_preflight` defaults OFF and requires F675-188/189/190 read flags. Separate branch/test Docker, exact-SHA official Test and owner approval; OFF → internal cohort → limited cohort → broader only with P0 gates and verified telemetry. Rollback OFF restores existing inventory and does not delete data/indexes. ## v2026.10.31.1538 — F675-190 Replacement Preview: Equip-Path and Public-Profile Truth Reconciliation (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1536 → Current Feature Improvement v1538 → Next Feature Addition v1540 (provisional). Stacked on Draft #2089; engineering #2088. Main 761a55a094fb9dd383d213e817305e68c79fa23c checked at start and midpoint; English canonical, Korean equivalent. Design only, NOT implemented or approved for release.

### Source-level contradiction and user impact
- Migration 071 defines owner+catalog primary key, nonnegative quantity and nullable holding expires_at. Migration 114's inventory_equip_item(p_actor,p_catalog_id,p_slot,p_equip) verifies quantity>0 and allowlisted slot, **but does NOT verify that p_slot matches shop_catalog.category, nor that expires_at is in the future**. Its two UPDATE statements lack a documented per-owner serialization lock/unique slot constraint. A caller can therefore request an incompatible slot or equip an already-expired holding if no later code guards it. This is a source-level gap, NOT proof of actual abuse.
- Migration 114's profile_get_cosmetics(p_target_user_id) is granted PUBLIC EXECUTE, selects is_equipped=true by slot with LIMIT 1 (badge aggregate), and does **not** check quantity or expires_at. A stale/expired or duplicate equipped row can therefore still appear on a public cosmetic profile. This is a public display consistency issue, NOT evidence of unauthorized private inventory access.
- Migration 123's shop_my_items_v2(p_actor) **does** require quantity>0 and (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at>clock_timestamp()); it omits expired holdings even if is_equipped=true. Thus owner inventory and public cosmetic profile can disagree. Neither read model proves that a proposed replacement can be equipped. The preview must NOT use the filtered holdings function alone to validate all flagged equipped rows or infer successful equip.
- The migration 114 check allows equipped_slot NULL on any row; no invariant requires is_equipped=true iff equipped_slot is non-NULL. Validate both directions, including is_equipped=false with a non-NULL equipped_slot; do not silently normalize or hide anomalies. Existing equip write path is owned by B/DB; this planning run does not change it.

### Revised product and source-truth contract
- Preserve v1536 owner-only, read-only manual replacement preview; existing /inventory remains the ONLY path to a user-initiated equip attempt. Feature inventory_equipped_replacements and predecessors F675-188/F675-189 stay OFF until implemented and accepted. No auto-equip, purchase, payment, WLD, outbox, or direct mutation.
- Read the **unfiltered owner user_items and catalog source** inside the proposed SECURITY DEFINER function, NOT just shop_my_items_v2 (which omits expired flagged rows). First validate all eight slots across ALL owner rows: duplicate flagged slot, flagged-without-slot, unflagged-with-slot, zero-quantity flagged, category-slot mismatch, invalid slot, and catalog mismatch => sourceStatus CONFLICT, HTTP 409 EQUIPMENT_SOURCE_CONFLICT; never return verified-empty. Do not expose another member's rows.
- Separate target states: (a) future-expiring flagged slot within 7/30-day horizon => candidate preview; (b) flagged but already expired => 409 TARGET_ALREADY_EXPIRED, with neutral warning and /inventory CTA, not a future-expiry preview; (c) target not equipped / outside horizon => 409 TARGET_NOT_IMPACTED; (d) NULL holding expiry => UNKNOWN, never permanent; (e) missing/invalid source => 503, never empty. Public profile disagreement is diagnostic-only and cannot be treated as equip evidence.
- For badge_1/badge_2/badge_3 require category badge; other five slots require identical category. Candidate filter still owner, quantity>0, currently unequipped, same category, different catalog id, and unexpired or NULL expiry. Inactive catalog rows remain visible with REVIEW_ONLY and no guarantee of purchase, equip, upkeep or effect eligibility. Do not equate current catalog name with purchase-time name.
- SQL read contract: single DB statement_timestamp asOf in one statement, or one PoolClient READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ transaction; forward-only SECURITY DEFINER with fixed search_path, schema-qualified tables, PUBLIC EXECUTE revoked, app-role EXECUTE only and NO new direct table grants. The function's p_actor is NOT an identity proof; Nest session/consent guard binds authenticated actor. No new index without real 10,001-row EXPLAIN evidence.
- Cursor: HMAC-SHA256 actor+session+slot+horizon+asOf+validUntil+full owner source digest+last (catalog_name COLLATE C,catalog_id), 15m maximum; exact PostgreSQL microseconds; constant-time MAC; reject replay/tamper. Fingerprint must include ALL owner equipment rows (including unflagged ghost-slot and expired flagged rows) and ALL relevant candidate rows, including catalog category/name/active, quantity, expiry and equip flags. Recompute each page; 409 source change, 410 expired cursor, 503 DB failure. validUntil=min(asOf+60s, earliest candidate expiry, target expiry, horizon entry) with equality stale; return VERIFIED_EMPTY only after complete invariant validation.
- Strict Nest/Next/Android GET DTO and errors; 200 candidate sourceStatus VERIFIED and REVIEW_ONLY only; no raw SQL anomalies or IDs of other members in response. UI distinguishes conflict, already expired, outside horizon, expired cursor, outage, and truly empty; clear stale rows and cancel/fence on account/session switch. Android rejects malformed/stale 200 and cross-session response.
- Security/SEO/admin: actual public Nginx, Nest, Next, Android enforce private/no-store, noindex/nofollow, no-referrer on 200 and error; no user inventory cache, IP/session rate limits, 320px/200% WCAG 2.2 AA, keyboard focus/aria-live. Aggregate admin cohorts >=20, no per-member source data, no raw cursor/catalog/user IDs in telemetry. Metrics: 409 conflict rate by class, profile/owner discrepancy aggregate, 503, p95<=500ms and manual CTA; avoid PII.
- Write-path hardening handoff to Development B/DB (SEPARATE branch and approval): add category-slot/expiry/quantity checks and per-owner equip serialization or uniqueness, plus public profile expired/quantity/duplicate display policy; prove legacy behavior before choosing forward-only migration. Do NOT silently alter profile visibility or write function in this documentation PR; no retrospective claim that legacy rows were repaired.

### Concrete acceptance and rollback
- PROPOSED, NOT EXECUTED: isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 full 311 source migrations, app-role/PUBLIC negative ACL; two owners and 10,001 synthetic holdings; expired flagged target (owner holdings omits, public profile includes), NULL expiry, zero-quantity flagged, is_equipped=false+equipped_slot!=NULL, duplicate slot, mismatched category, 8 slots including badges, inactive catalog, out-of-horizon and exact microsecond boundaries; malicious slot, two concurrent equip calls as **separate B test**, 409/410/503 vs verified-empty, HMAC replay, source mutation, time-only expiry, malformed 200, Android and public edge headers, WCAG, EXPLAIN; assert zero WLD/ledger/outbox writes.
- Deploy only via separate dev branches, all required full CI and authenticated official Test exact final SHA, real DB privilege negatives, independent backup restore, immutable rollback digest, signed promotion approval and zero-downtime smoke. Feature default OFF. Rollback by flag OFF and removing read-only route/page without touching balances, inventory or profile. Any write-path fix requires separate reviewed forward-only migration and explicit B/QA ownership.

### P0/P1 development backlog (4 P0 + 6 P1)
| Priority | ID | Owner and acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| P0 | OPS1538-01 | Ops: Production executable SHA/digest, immutable rollback and independent restore (#1984), no service restart by planning. |

## v2026.10.31.1536 — F675-190 Owner-Held Replacement Candidates for Expiring Equipped Slots (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1534 → Current Feature Addition v1536 → Next Feature Improvement v1538 (provisional). Latest main 761a55a094fb9dd383d213e817305e68c79fa23c rechecked at start and midpoint; parent Draft #2080; engineering issue #2088. English is canonical, Korean is equivalent. Documentation proposal ONLY, not an implemented feature.

**Mid-run main change:** at 2026-10-31 04:05 KST, merged admin PR #2045 advanced main from bf8d0d48 to 761a55a0; main canonical planning files were unchanged, but backend shop controller changed. Rechecked current route/source before finalization; prior QA v1402 is NOT proof for this new main. Release hold unchanged.

### Source truth and product decision
- Migration 071: user_items PK(user_id,catalog_id), quantity >=0, expires_at nullable; catalog has mutable name, category and active flag. Migration 114: eight equipped slots (frame, background, effect, nameplate, title, badge_1/2/3); no per-owner unique equipped-slot constraint and no category/slot coherence check. Existing inventory_equip_item accepts caller slot and performs two UPDATEs. Migration 123: shop_my_items_v2 exposes current holdings and upkeep/effect status; NULL expiry is not proof of perpetual ownership, arrears=0 is not proof of payment.
- Actual Next /inventory inventory-view displays active cosmetics and performs POST /api/v1/shop/holdings/:id/equip. Nest GET /api/v1/shop/holdings calls owner-scoped shop_my_items_v2; no member replacement candidate preview route exists on the inspected main. The predecessor F675-188 expiry radar and F675-189 equipped impact are unmerged DRAFT/OFF. This feature must not display a dead CTA before both dependencies are enabled.
- Chosen feature: for an affected equipped slot, let the owner inspect OTHER ALREADY-OWNED, same-category, currently unequipped holdings as manual review candidates. No auto-equip, loadout preset duplication (#1142), calendar/ICS duplication (#1267), purchase upsell, refund, WLD transaction, guaranteed equip eligibility, upkeep settlement, or future effect claim. The member may navigate to the existing inventory to manually equip only after source revalidation.
- Alternatives: (A) client filter over current /inventory (rejected: false-empty on outage, unsound expiry clock and cross-page staleness); (B) owner-scoped read-only SQL + strict API (selected); (C) automatic replacement and payment (rejected: unapproved money/equipment mutations).

### User, permissions, entry, UI and states
- Target: authenticated, consented member with an expiring currently equipped cosmetic. Entry /inventory → /inventory/expiring?withinDays=7|30 → /inventory/expiring/equipped-impact → per-impacted-slot CTA "보유 중 대체 후보 보기". Page /inventory/expiring/equipped-impact/replacements?slot=frame|background|effect|nameplate|title|badge_1|badge_2|badge_3&withinDays=7|30 (default 7). No other-member lookup or admin bypass.
- Show impacted slot, current mutable catalog name (NOT purchase-time snapshot), holding expiry KST, 20 candidate cards initially, next-page CTA (max 50/page), candidate quantity, inactive-catalog badge, NULL-expiry "만료 정보 확인 필요", and neutral REVIEW_ONLY label "장착 가능 여부는 적용 시 재확인". Primary CTA "보관함에서 직접 확인" routes to existing /inventory, NEVER invokes equip POST from this new feature.
- Distinguish loading, VERIFIED_EMPTY, verified candidates, cursor/page loading, source-changed 409, time-window-stale 409, expired cursor 410, invalid 400, unauthenticated 401, consent 403, throttled 429, unavailable/invalid upstream 503. Never render 503/malformed 200 as a verified empty list. Clear old rows on actor/session/slot/horizon change and cancel/fence in-flight requests. 320px/200% zoom, keyboard/focus, aria-live without countdown spam, WCAG 2.2 AA, KST display with UTC source truth.

### API, SQL, DB invariants, pagination, and transaction
- Nest GET /api/v1/shop/holdings/expiring/equipped-impact/replacements; Next BFF GET /api/shop/holdings/expiring/equipped-impact/replacements; Android GET /app-api/v1/shop/holdings/expiring/equipped-impact/replacements. Query slot, withinDays=7|30, optional pageSize 1..50 (default 20), optional opaque cursor. Reject duplicates, unknown fields and actor UUID. SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard, ConsentGuard; actor requireUserId(request), session requireSession(request).id; server-side flag inventory_equipped_replacements OFF.
- Proposed forward-only SECURITY DEFINER public.shop_my_equipped_replacement_candidates_v1(p_actor uuid,p_slot text,p_within_days integer), fixed search_path pg_catalog,pg_temp, schema-qualified relations, owner WHERE ui.user_id=p_actor inside function; revoke PUBLIC EXECUTE, grant moneyverse_app EXECUTE only; no direct SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE grants. App role must not read raw user_items/catalog by bypass. SECURITY DEFINER actor argument alone is not a cryptographic session identity proof: HTTP guard must bind actor to authenticated session, and threat-model a compromised SQL caller.
- One DB-authoritative statement_timestamp() inside ONE SQL statement, with a single PoolClient READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ transaction if multiple statements are unavoidable. Validate ALL flagged-equipped rows before candidate filtering: duplicate slot (even outside horizon), NULL/invalid slot, category mismatch (badge slots require badge), quantity <=0 => 409 EQUIPMENT_SOURCE_CONFLICT. Expired-but-equipped flag is a warning, NOT proof of auto-unequip. Target slot must actually have a holding expiring in (asOf,asOf+withinDays*24h]; else 409 TARGET_NOT_IMPACTED, not a generic inventory search.
- Candidates: same category as slot, ui.quantity>0, ui.is_equipped=false, ui.catalog_id != target equipped catalog_id, ui.expires_at IS NULL OR ui.expires_at>asOf. Include inactive catalog owned rows, label them; NULL expiry UNKNOWN, never "permanent". Do not treat suspended=false, arrears_due=0 or an effect receipt as verified equip entitlement. If actual equip eligibility requires additional business rules, display REVIEW_ONLY and do not assert success.
- Sort by (sc.name COLLATE "C" ASC, sc.id ASC); keyset cursor HMAC-SHA256 bound to actor, session, slot, withinDays, asOfUtc, validUntilUtc, sourceFingerprint, lastName and lastCatalogId; TTL <=15min. Strict cursor parsing and constant-time MAC compare, key rotation. Fingerprint covers FULL owner equipped set and FULL same-category candidate set (including outside current page): catalog_id, quantity, category, current name, expiry microseconds, is_equipped, catalog active. Recompute per request; any change => 409 SOURCE_CHANGED; do not pretend cross-request snapshot isolation.
- validUntilUtc=min(asOf+60s, earliest candidate known expiry, target impacted equipped expiry, earliest future equipped horizon-entry boundary). At DB now>=validUntilUtc, reject stale 409 including VERIFIED_EMPTY; cursor expired 410. No partial page if source invalid, no LIMIT 1 to conceal duplicates, no precision loss in UTC microseconds. Use existing PK (user_id,catalog_id), catalog PK(id), existing equipped index; propose an additional index only after EXPLAIN ANALYZE on 10,001 synthetic holdings shows a regression. No new table or mutation.
- **Cursor-page clock contract:** first page pins asOfUtc=the single statement_timestamp(); a subsequent page supplies the server-verified signed cursor asOfUtc to the read function, while that SAME statement captures fresh dbNow=statement_timestamp() to check dbNow<validUntilUtc and re-evaluate full-set source fingerprint against pinned asOf. The cursor may not reset asOfUtc, validUntilUtc, horizon or sort. A function returning all 10,001 rows to Nest for JS pagination is prohibited: SQL keyset applies p_after_name/p_after_catalog_id and p_limit<=51, while the fingerprint/invariant subquery still covers the full owner set. Source changes or time boundary return 409 before emitting any partial page; p95 must be measured against the real SQL plan.
- Strict 200 DTO: {asOfUtc,validUntilUtc,slot,withinDays,candidates:[{catalogId,currentName,quantity,holdingExpiresAtUtc|null,catalogActive,reviewStatus:'REVIEW_ONLY'}],nextCursor:null|string,sourceStatus:'VERIFIED'}. Errors 400/401/403/409/410/429/503 with stable error codes. No payment, idempotency key, money ledger transaction, outbox write or background job in this feature.

### Security, SEO, admin, analytics, test and rollout
- Feature flag inventory_equipped_replacements default OFF; require both predecessor flags. Nest, Next, Android and actual public Nginx edge must return Cache-Control private,no-store; X-Robots-Tag noindex,nofollow; Referrer-Policy no-referrer on success AND errors. Known Production private-edge index/follow blocker #2012 remains P0. Per-session/IP rate limiting, CSRF only if a future mutation is separately approved; this GET has no side effects.
- Admin: aggregate-only reporting for cohorts >=20 (opens, candidate-present rate, verified-empty rate, 409/503, p95). No per-user inventory bypass or identifiable logs. Analytics exclude actor/catalog IDs, names, exact expiry times, tokens, raw cursor; structured error counts only. Target p95 <=500ms on synthetic 10,001 rows; sample plan review and abandon-to-inventory CTA conversion without implying purchase.
- Proposed NOT EXECUTED: isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 all forward-only migrations, owner-vs-other and PUBLIC/app-role negative ACL, 10,001 synthetic holdings, 8 slots, duplicate equipped outside horizon, inactive catalog, NULL expiry, quantity=0, category mismatch, asOf upper-bound equality and +1 microsecond, candidate expires between pages without DB write, catalog rename, quantity change, HMAC tamper/foreign-session replay/age, malformed 200, 503 vs empty, account switch, Android contract, edge privacy, keyboard/320px/200%, concurrency, EXPLAIN, no WLD/ledger writes. Need full CI, exact-SHA official Test authenticated E2E, independent backup restore, immutable rollback image/digest and explicit Production promotion approval.
- Rollout: separate development branch and isolated Test exact-SHA only after blockers clear, flag OFF by default; no migration needed unless index evidence demands a separately reviewed forward-only migration. Rollback: disable feature flag and remove read-only route/UI while retaining existing inventory/equip; verify no writes or orphaned state. Do not merge Draft/HOLD PR or deploy.

## v2026.10.31.1534 — F675-189 Equipped Expiry Boundary and Slot Integrity (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1532 → Current Feature Improvement v1534 → Next Feature Addition v1536 (provisional). Main bf8d0d48f810932b43783d8605dce53954c60cff; parent Draft #2075, engineering #2074, independent QA v1402 FAILED/P0. English canonical, Korean equivalent; documentation only.

### Source-verified defects and design decision
- **Time-only horizon ENTRY:** v1532 validUntilUtc=min(asOf+60s, earliest impacted expiry) handles rows EXITING the 7/30-day window but misses a row initially outside the upper bound ENTERING as time advances without a DB write. A previously verified empty result can become false before its TTL. Eligibility remains asOf < expires_at <= asOf+withinDays*24h, UTC instants, inclusive upper bound.
- **Full-set slot integrity:** migration 114 adds eight allowed slot names and a NON-UNIQUE (user_id,is_equipped) index. It does NOT enforce is_equipped=>non-NULL slot, one equipped row per user/slot, or catalog category/slot compatibility. inventory_equip_item accepts an allowed caller slot and runs two UPDATEs without explicit per-owner serialization; profile_get_cosmetics uses LIMIT 1 on five slots and does not check quantity/expiry. These are source-level risks, not proof of an actual duplicate or exploit.
- Validate ALL owner rows flagged is_equipped BEFORE filtering for upcoming expiry. Duplicate slot (even when only one row falls in horizon), NULL/unknown slot, category mismatch (badge_1/2/3 require badge; other slots require matching category), or quantity<=0 must return 409 EQUIPMENT_SOURCE_CONFLICT, never an arbitrary LIMIT 1 or verified-empty. Expired rows whose equip flag persists are distinct EXPIRED_FLAG_STILL_SET warnings, not upcoming items or proof of auto-unequip. NULL expiry is UNKNOWN, not permanent; inactive catalog items remain visible if owned. No effect, upkeep or refund claim.
- Selected approach: one DB-authoritative owner snapshot with next entry AND exit boundary; reject client-only 60s TTL and scheduled automatic unequip. No new member table, background worker, notification, money write or equipment mutation.

### Precise DB/API and temporal contract
- Nest GET /api/v1/shop/holdings/expiring/equipped-impact, Next same-origin /api/shop/holdings/expiring/equipped-impact, Android /app-api/v1/shop/holdings/expiring/equipped-impact. Query withinDays=7|30 (default 7); actor from guarded authenticated session and consent, never from client. Strict response {asOfUtc,validUntilUtc,withinDays,items:[{catalogId,currentName,equippedSlot,quantity,holdingExpiresAtUtc}],warnings:[{equippedSlot,reason:EXPIRED_FLAG_STILL_SET}],sourceStatus:VERIFIED}. Warning list bounded by eight slots. Empty items with warnings is NOT “all equipment valid”; malformed 200 fails closed 503.
- New forward-only owner SECURITY DEFINER shop_my_equipped_expiry_impact_v1(actor,within_days), schema-qualified relations, fixed search_path, REVOKE PUBLIC EXECUTE, grant moneyverse_app EXECUTE only. Capture ONE statement_timestamp() INSIDE the same SQL statement and return that exact asOfUtc with the rows and bounds; querying timestamp in a separate statement can shift it even inside REPEATABLE READ. Use one PoolClient, READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ and COMMIT/ROLLBACK for multi-statement reads. No JS Date.now() authority, direct table grants, applied migration edits or financial writes.
- Let H=withinDays*24 hours. Across all valid owner-equipped future-expiring holdings, exitBoundary=min(expires_at where asOf<expires_at<=asOf+H); entryBoundary=min(expires_at-H where expires_at>asOf+H); validUntilUtc=min(asOf+60s, exitBoundary if present, entryBoundary if present). At dbNow>=validUntilUtc the result is stale, including verified-empty; refresh using a new DB clock. If boundary is invalid/nonfuture fail closed. No pagination/cursor: at most eight valid slots, but validate the entire flagged set before limiting. No cross-request snapshot claim.
- Separate Backend B/DB write-side handoff: preflight duplicates, evaluate forward-only partial UNIQUE (user_id,equipped_slot) WHERE is_equipped AND equipped_slot IS NOT NULL, enforce equip-flag/slot/category coherence and per-actor serialization, test two simultaneous same-slot equips. Do NOT apply DDL here; read endpoint fails closed before any write-side fix.

### UI, privacy, analytics, tests and release
- Existing flag-gated /inventory → /inventory/expiring?withinDays=7|30 → “장착 영향 확인” → /inventory/expiring/equipped-impact. No dead CTA while predecessor F675-188 is OFF. Show current mutable catalog name, slot, quantity, holding expiry KST and “보관함에서 확인”; distinguish verified-empty, expired-flag warning, conflict 409, entry/exit stale Refresh, auth/consent, 429, 503 Retry. Preserve existing showcase/counts. Cancel/fence in-flight requests on account/horizon switch; 320px, 200% zoom, keyboard/focus/aria-live, WCAG 2.2 AA.
- Default-OFF inventory_equipped_expiry_impact; Cache-Control private,no-store; X-Robots-Tag noindex,nofollow; Referrer-Policy no-referrer on 200/4xx/5xx at Nest/Next/Android AND public Nginx edge. No actor/catalog IDs, item names, raw expiry timestamps or tokens in analytics. Admin aggregates cohorts>=20 only; KPI opens, impact count, expired-flag warning rate, 409 rate, horizon-entry stale rate and p95. No member-detail bypass.
- **Proposed NOT EXECUTED:** isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 all migrations; 10,001 synthetic holdings; all eight slots; upper-bound equality/+1 microsecond; entry in 5s without writes; exit in 5s; verified-empty→nonempty; duplicate slot with one row outside horizon; NULL/mismatched slot, zero quantity, expired-flag warning, inactive catalog, NULL expiry, foreign actor, PUBLIC/app-role negative grants, concurrent equip race, Android/edge privacy, account switch, a11y. Assert no stale 200, arbitrary LIMIT 1 or financial mutation; p95<=500ms and bounded memory. Write-side tests remain BLOCKED until implemented separately.
- **QA v1402 (2026-10-31 02:18–02:32 KST):** backend 2,653 PASS/126 FAIL/501 SKIP; frontend 2,701 PASS/55 FAIL; lint 1 parser error; typecheck/build PASS; DEV 293/311 migrations applied, 5 checksum mismatches and excessive app-role DML; official Test exact-SHA and independent restore BLOCKED; Production private pages index/follow, runtime-check FAILED. RELEASE HOLD. Docs CI is not runtime proof.
- **External independent reference gate:** new verified 0; cumulative independently verified/deduplicated/classified UNKNOWN/10,000; BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. Internal GitHub and synthetic fixtures do not count.

## v2026.10.31.1532 — F675-189 Owner Equipped-Item Expiry Impact Preview (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1530 → Current Feature Addition v1532 → Next Feature Improvement v1534 (provisional). Remote main `bf8d0d48f810932b43783d8605dce53954c60cff` checked at start/mid; predecessor Draft #2066 → #2061; engineering issue #2074; P0 #2031. English is canonical, Korean is meaning-equivalent. Planning-only, NOT implemented or release-authorized.

### Product purpose, independent source proof and non-duplication
- **User/need:** A signed-in, consented member who has cosmetics equipped needs to see which CURRENTLY equipped slots are associated with holdings due to expire within 7 or 30 elapsed days, rather than scanning every holding. Existing `/inventory` displays equipped frame/background/effect/nameplate/title/badges but no expiry-impact summary; the separate F675-188 upcoming-expiry list (#2060) is NOT an equipment-specific impact summary and is still unmerged Draft. Existing calendar/ICS watchlist (#1267), loadout presets (#1142), and Development A inventory outage truth (#2056) own different scopes. Do not duplicate them.
- **Schema facts:** Migration 071 creates `public.user_items` with PK (user_id,catalog_id), quantity >=0 and nullable `expires_at`. Migration 114 adds `is_equipped`, `equipped_slot` with allowed frame/background/effect/nameplate/title/badge_1/badge_2/badge_3 and an index on (user_id,is_equipped) WHERE is_equipped. Migration 123 `shop_my_items_v2` exposes both equipment fields, filters holding expiry via `clock_timestamp()`, and separately derives effect receipt expiry. Existing Nest `GET /api/v1/shop/holdings` is guarded and owner-scoped; the Next inventory page's `apiOrNull` can mask an outage as empty (A #2056). Do not infer that a holding expiry automatically unequips a slot, that an effect is active, that upkeep is paid, or that a refund exists. Current catalog name is not a purchase-time snapshot.
- **Options:** (A) client-filter existing unbounded holdings (reject: mixed clock, outage-to-empty and no snapshot proof); (B) owner-scoped one-shot SQL read + guarded API (selected); (C) scheduled reminders/auto-replacement (reject: privacy and mutation scope). No new user table, push/email/ICS, money mutation, auto-equip or background worker.

### User flow, precise UI and CTA
1. `/inventory` → existing F675-188 `/inventory/expiring?withinDays=7|30` → **장착 영향 확인** CTA → private `/inventory/expiring/equipped-impact?withinDays=7|30` (7 default). If F675-188 is still OFF/unimplemented, keep this feature OFF and do not publish a dead link. An optional direct inventory CTA may be added only after integration verifies the route exists.
2. Each impacted row shows `equippedSlot` (Korean slot label), **현재 상품명**, quantity, exact **보유 만료 시각 (KST)**, and **보관함에서 확인** navigation to the existing inventory; it must NOT present an auto-unequip prediction or claim an actual effect will stop. Keep the full inventory/equipped counts separate; no replacement of existing holdings state.
3. Distinguish loading, verified-empty (only after verified DB success), impacted rows, stale at `validUntilUtc` with **새로고침**, 401/403 login/consent, 409 inconsistent equipment/source, 429 rate limit, 503 upstream failure with Retry. Never render a 503 as an empty shelf. Account/horizon change cancels in-flight reads and rejects stale response application. No color-only meaning; keyboard/focus/aria-live, 320px and 200% zoom, WCAG 2.2 AA.

### API and PostgreSQL contract
- Nest `GET /api/v1/shop/holdings/expiring/equipped-impact?withinDays=7|30`, Next same-origin `GET /api/shop/holdings/expiring/equipped-impact`, Android `GET /app-api/v1/shop/holdings/expiring/equipped-impact`. `SessionGuard,AuthenticatedGuard,ConsentGuard` with server-derived `requireUserId(request)` and actual `requireSession(request).id`. Reject client actor UUID and unknown query keys. Response `{asOfUtc:string,validUntilUtc:string,withinDays:7|30,items:[{catalogId:string,currentName:string,equippedSlot:string,quantity:number,holdingExpiresAtUtc:string}],sourceStatus:'VERIFIED'}`. No cursor/pagination: eight allowed slot names; fail closed rather than silently truncate anomalous >50 rows.
- New forward-only `public.shop_my_equipped_expiry_impact_v1(p_actor uuid,p_within_days integer)` SECURITY DEFINER function; fixed `SET search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp`, schema-qualified tables, owner predicate inside SQL, explicit `REVOKE EXECUTE FROM PUBLIC` and narrow `GRANT EXECUTE TO moneyverse_app` only. No direct table grants, no change to applied migrations 071/114/123. Coordinate any new migration number with Backend B/DB owner. Read-only `REPEATABLE READ` transaction on ONE PoolClient; one DB `statement_timestamp()` as asOf; `quantity>0 AND is_equipped=true AND equipped_slot IS NOT NULL AND asOf<expires_at<=asOf+withinDays*interval '24 hours'`, UTC instant comparisons, inactive catalog included, order (expires_at ASC,equipped_slot ASC,catalog_id ASC). NULL expiry is UNKNOWN, not permanent; effect expiry/arrears are distinct.
- Detect malformed equipment: equipped=true with NULL/unrecognized slot, duplicate equipped slot for the same actor, >50 impacted rows, missing catalog join, or inconsistent result => fail closed 409 `EQUIPMENT_SOURCE_CONFLICT` or 503 `EQUIPMENT_SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE` as appropriate; never silently return partial success. A successful empty result requires successful invariant checks. `validUntilUtc=min(asOf+60 seconds, earliest impacted expires_at)` (or asOf+60 seconds if none). At the boundary the UI marks STALE and refreshes; no cross-request snapshot guarantee. No HMAC cursor because there is no pagination. Strict errors 400/401/403/409/429/503, no exposure of foreign IDs.
- Existing migration 114 equipped partial index may suffice; any extra `(user_id,expires_at)` partial index needs isolated `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE,BUFFERS)` at 10,001 synthetic holdings and DB owner approval. No writes to user_items, WLD, ledger, outbox or Test/Production.

### Privacy, admin, KPI, tests and release
- Default-OFF `inventory_equipped_expiry_impact` flag. Private `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex,nofollow`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` on 200/4xx/5xx at Nest/Next/Android AND actual Nginx edge. Same-login account switch clears state. Session/IP rate limits. No owner IDs, catalog IDs, item names, raw request tokens or dates in telemetry; aggregate only page opens, verified impact counts, stale/error rates and p95. Admin cannot bypass member detail; cohorts >=20 only.
- **Proposed, NOT RUN:** isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 replay with owner/foreign actor and direct-table privilege negatives, 10,001 synthetic holdings, eight slot labels, inactive catalog, NULL expiry, malformed and duplicate slots, exact UTC/KST/one-microsecond boundaries, time-only expiry without DB writes, account switching, 429/503, Android/edge private headers, 320px/200% a11y; assert no partial success and zero financial mutations; p95 <=500ms, bounded memory. Full CI and official Test E2E at exact candidate SHA, immutable rollback digest, independent restore, explicit Production approval. Last verified QA v1400: backend 2,653 PASS/126 FAIL/501 SKIP, frontend 2,701 PASS/55 FAIL, lint parser error 1; overall FAILED. #2031 Draft/HOLD merge incident, #993 DB least privilege, #1984 runtime provenance remain P0. Release HOLD.
- **External independent reference gate:** newly verified 0, cumulative verified/deduplicated/classified UNKNOWN / 10,000 → BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. Synthetic/internal GitHub items do not count.
