## v2026.10.31.1530 — F675-188 Owner Inventory Expiry Radar Integrity (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1528 → Current Feature Improvement v1530 → Next Feature Addition v1532 (provisional). Canonical remote main bf8d0d48f810932b43783d8605dce53954c60cff, verified at start and mid; stacked on Draft #2061 (after #2055). Engineering issue #2060; independent QA v1400; release incident #2031. Documentation-only; no feature implementation or release approval.

### Source findings and corrected semantics
- Migration 071 declares user_items PRIMARY KEY (user_id,catalog_id), quantity >=0, nullable expires_at; shop_catalog contains mutable code/name and active. Migration 123 shop_my_items_v2 uses separately evaluated clock_timestamp() calls, includes owned rows even when catalogue.active=false, and independently computes effect expiry. Nest GET /api/v1/shop/holdings uses SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard, ConsentGuard and owner-scoped function; ShopCatalogRepository.holdings uses a single pool query, NOT a multi-statement snapshot transaction. The proposed new radar MUST NOT reuse that repository query as proof of cross-page consistency.
- **Critical time-only drift:** A row can pass the first-page asOf filter but expire before page two without any UPDATE; an unchanged source fingerprint alone would incorrectly keep it in “upcoming”. Every response includes asOfUtc and validUntilUtc = minimum of first-page issued-at + 15 minutes and the earliest expires_at in the full asOf-eligible result set. If DB current time reaches validUntilUtc before continuation, return 409 STALE_EXPIRY_WINDOW with Refresh CTA; if 15-minute cursor age is exceeded, return 410 CURSOR_EXPIRED. Recheck DB time on every continuation; the UI must mark previously rendered results stale at validUntilUtc, not silently display expired holdings as upcoming.
- **Catalog mutation drift:** The fingerprint covers the entire asOf-eligible owner set, not merely the current 20 rows: catalogId, quantity, expires_at at PostgreSQL microsecond precision, and joined current catalog code/name. Include membership additions/deletions, zero-quantity transitions, changed expiry, renames and code changes. Deterministic ordering (expires_at ASC,catalog_id ASC), unambiguous length-prefixed canonical encoding, SHA-256, algorithm version. An item with catalog.active=false still appears if owned; currentName is a mutable display label, NOT historical name. A mutation in the eligible result set between requests returns 409 SOURCE_CHANGED; do not merge pages or misstate a global count.
- **Snapshot boundary:** Each HTTP page request obtains one pg PoolClient and BEGIN TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY; obtain DB statement_timestamp() once as dbNow, derive first asOf from that clock or validate signed continuation asOf, compute the complete fingerprint, validUntil and the bounded keyset page in the SAME transaction snapshot, then COMMIT or ROLLBACK on failure. Separate pool.query calls or a JS Date.now() do not establish this guarantee. Cross-request snapshots cannot be held open; compare the canonical fingerprint on every continuation. For 10,001+ rows use bounded-memory hashing or a measured equivalent, not unbounded JSON aggregation; if p95 >500ms or memory grows without bound, leave the feature OFF and revise the design.
- **Cursor and identity:** Nest reads requireSession(request).id and requireUserId(request), never a client actor; bind HMAC cursor to actor UUID, actual session UUID (not an imaginary session-generation field), 7/30-day horizon, asOf microseconds, last (expires_at,catalog_id), fingerprint, validUntil, issuance time, version and key id. Validate signature in constant time and key rotation against an explicit allowlist. On same-login account switch, revoked session, forged cursor or cross-user replay, reject without exposing other users; 400 malformed token, 401/403 invalid session/consent, 409 data/time drift, 410 expired age, 429 abuse, 503 DB/contract fault. Prefer 401/403 for lost session rather than interpreting the cursor as evidence of membership.
- **Time bounds:** Eligible iff quantity>0 AND expires_at IS NOT NULL AND asOfUtc < expires_at <= asOfUtc + withinDays*24 hours, comparing UTC instants, NOT KST calendar dates. Exactly asOf is excluded; exactly upper bound included. PostgreSQL microseconds must survive cursor round trips, while UI renders KST with timezone and exact timestamp; no rounded “days left” claim. If a new first-page asOf has already crossed an expiry, that row is excluded. NULL expiry is UNKNOWN, not permanent. Holding expiry, effect expiry, upkeep arrears, paid settlement and item ownership are separate facts.
- **DB privilege:** Forward-only owner-filtered SECURITY DEFINER functions with SET search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp and qualified tables; revoke PUBLIC EXECUTE, grant moneyverse_app EXECUTE only. Never grant direct SELECT/DELETE/UPDATE on user_items, catalog, or financial tables. Optional partial (user_id,expires_at,catalog_id) index only after EXPLAIN (ANALYZE,BUFFERS) with 10,001 synthetic rows and DB-owner coordination; no edits to applied 071/123. This GET is read-only: no financial ledger, item or outbox mutations.

### UI/API, privacy, analytics, and release acceptance
- Existing member CTA “만료 예정 보기” at /inventory leads to /inventory/expiring?withinDays=7|30 (default 7). Nest GET /api/v1/shop/holdings/expiring; Next /api/shop/holdings/expiring; Android /app-api/v1/shop/holdings/expiring. Strict success DTO {asOfUtc,validUntilUtc,withinDays,items:[{catalogId,code,currentName,quantity,holdingExpiresAtUtc}],nextCursor?,sourceStatus:'VERIFIED'}. Default page 20, hard max 50. No owner UUID query parameter. The 200 empty state requires successful authoritative read; loading, verified-empty, rows, stale refresh, auth failure, and 503 retry are distinct. Preserve existing full inventory/equipped/count, coordinate A Draft #2056 instead of overwriting its outage/condition changes.
- At validUntilUtc, UI marks the page STALE and offers Refresh even if there was no write. On 409/410 clear all accumulated pages and restart from first page; never append pages across snapshots. Cancel in-flight fetch and fence stale responses on account switch or horizon change. Keep Next/Nest/Android/public Nginx edge headers private/no-store, noindex/nofollow and no-referrer for 200/4xx/5xx; feature flag inventory_expiring_radar defaults OFF. Admin sees only aggregated cohorts >=20, no per-member bypass; analytics store counts/latency/errors only, no actor/catalog IDs or cursor contents. Mobile 320px, 200% zoom, keyboard focus, aria-live, WCAG 2.2 AA; SEO private.
- Proposed **NOT EXECUTED** isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 acceptance: replay all numbered migrations, owner/foreign actor and direct-table privilege negatives; 10,001 synthetic holdings with duplicate expiry timestamps and mixed catalog.active flags; 100 exact asOf/upper-bound/KST and 1-microsecond cases; 100 cursor tamper/session switch/key-rotation cases; 100 insert/update/delete/catalog rename drift cases; 100 expiration-without-write between pages; 100 concurrent reads; 50 Android/edge/a11y cases; verify no duplicate/missing keys or stale 200. Assert 409 at time-only expiry, 410 after age TTL, 503 on DB faults, verified empty only on successful SQL. Measure p95 <=500ms and memory, EXPLAIN index cost, zero financial writes.
- QA v1400 collected 2026-10-31 00:20–00:27 KST on main: backend 2,653 PASS/126 FAIL/501 SKIP; frontend 2,701 PASS/55 FAIL; full lint 1 parser error/1,043 warnings; typecheck/build PASS but overall QA FAIL. Official Test exact SHA, independent restore, Production provenance and GitOps gates remain BLOCKED. P0 #2031 Draft/HOLD merge, #993 least privilege, #1984 executable provenance are independent release blockers. No main merge, runtime build/deploy, DB ACL/migration application, Test/Production changes in planning.
- **External independent reference gate:** new verified/deduplicated/classified 0; cumulative UNKNOWN/10,000; BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. Do not count synthetic data, repository files, or unverifiable references.

## v2026.10.31.1528 — F675-188 Owner Inventory Upcoming Expiry Radar (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1526 → Current Feature Addition v1528 → Next Feature Improvement v1530 (provisional). Canonical origin/main `bf8d0d48f810932b43783d8605dce53954c60cff` rechecked start/mid; stacked after Draft #2055 (after #2048 → #2042 → #2036); engineering handoff #2060; release P0 #2031. This is a proposal, not implementation or Production authorization.

### Product problem, source truth and alternatives
- **Who/why:** authenticated and consented inventory owners need to identify holdings that will expire soon without manually opening every item. The existing `frontend/src/app/inventory/page.tsx` loads all holdings via `apiOrNull`; `inventory-view.tsx` offers category tabs and equip toggles, but no chronological expiry radar. A separate Development A Draft #2056 already addresses false-empty and lifecycle detail; coordinate rather than overwrite it.
- **Database oracle:** migration 071 creates `user_items` PK `(user_id,catalog_id)`, `quantity>0` for current holdings and nullable `expires_at timestamptz`. Migration 123 `shop_my_items_v2(p_actor)` filters `expires_at > clock_timestamp()`, uses `acquired_at DESC`, and independently calculates `effect_expires_at` from effect receipts. This does NOT mean holding expiry equals effect expiry or weekly upkeep due; neither is proof of payment or permanent ownership.
- **Options:** (A) client filter the existing unbounded holdings response (quick but no server-side pagination or consistent asOf); (B) new owner-filtered bounded read function and paginated API (selected); (C) background reminder worker (rejected for V1: delivery/privacy and scheduled job scope). No push/email, renewal, auto-charge, or mutation CTA in V1.

### User journeys, navigation and screen states
1. Authenticated member opens `/inventory` → **“만료 예정 보기”** CTA → private `/inventory/expiring?withinDays=7`; selector **7일 / 30일**, default 7. Preserve original inventory grid, equipped showcase and full count; do not replace the full holdings array with the 20-row result.
2. Each card displays **현재 상품명**, quantity, exact **보유 만료 시각** in Asia/Seoul with timezone label, and a return-to-inventory link. Only `quantity>0` and `expires_at IS NOT NULL` and `asOfUtc < expires_at <= asOfUtc + N*24h` are included. N is 7 or 30; the interval is absolute elapsed days, not KST calendar-day bucketing. At exactly asOf, an item is already expired and excluded; exactly at upper bound, included.
3. Distinguish skeleton/loading, verified-empty **“선택 기간에 만료 예정인 보유 아이템이 확인되지 않았습니다”**, result rows, stale/expired during viewing with Refresh CTA, auth/consent denial, and upstream unavailable Retry. A failed read is NEVER an empty shelf. Null `expires_at` means **미지정/UNKNOWN**, not perpetual; not included in upcoming results. Do not infer current active effect, upkeep settlement or refund.
4. Pagination has accessible next-page CTA; no global item count unless independently verified. A changed source or cursor yields a refresh notice without claiming that items were deleted. Keyboard focus, ARIA live announcements, no color-only states, 320px/200% zoom and WCAG 2.2 AA. Date localization is presentation only; backend comparisons use UTC instants.

### API contract and privacy
- Nest `GET /api/v1/shop/holdings/expiring?withinDays=7|30&cursor=<opaque>`, guarded by `SessionGuard,AuthenticatedGuard,ConsentGuard`, derives actor from server session. Next same-origin BFF `GET /api/shop/holdings/expiring`; Android `GET /app-api/v1/shop/holdings/expiring` must preserve identical authorization/error semantics.
- Strict success: `{asOfUtc:string,withinDays:7|30,items:[{catalogId:string,code:string,currentName:string,quantity:number,holdingExpiresAtUtc:string}],nextCursor?:string,sourceStatus:'VERIFIED'}`. Page size 20 default, hard maximum 50; no client-provided actor ID, no raw table rows or maintenance fields. Exact timestamp precision retained internally. No WLD values or money transfers in this read.
- Errors: 400 invalid horizon/cursor syntax, 401 unauthenticated, 403 consent, 409 source-set fingerprint drift, 410 cursor age>15m, 429 quota, 503 DB/query/contract failure; 200 verified-empty only after authoritative query. HMAC cursor binds actor/session generation, horizon, `asOfUtc`, last `(expires_at,catalog_id)` key, schema version and canonical result-set fingerprint. Reject tampering and cross-account reuse; rotate keys safely. Stable sort `expires_at ASC,catalog_id ASC`; preserve PostgreSQL microseconds in the opaque cursor.
- Privacy at Nest, Next, Android and real public Nginx edge: `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` for success and error responses; route metadata noindex; account-switch clears state and aborts in-flight responses; avoid PII, item IDs and query contents in logs/analytics. Rate limit per actor and IP. Admin access is NOT a bypass to member detail.

### PostgreSQL, transactions, index and least privilege
- Implement a forward-only owner-scoped `SECURITY DEFINER` read function (proposed `public.shop_my_expiring_holdings_v1`) with fixed `SET search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp`, qualified table names, bounded `withinDays IN (7,30)` and `limit<=50`. Use a **single** DB `statement_timestamp()` for the first page; continuation cursor carries that asOf, with bounded age and explicit no-future guard. Query a consistent READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ snapshot when validating result fingerprint and page, not separate uncoordinated clocks. Server must bind actor from authenticated session; SQL always filters `user_id=p_actor`.
- Revoke EXECUTE from PUBLIC; grant only narrow function EXECUTE to `moneyverse_app`. **Never grant direct SELECT/DELETE/UPDATE on user_items** to make the feature work. Proposed optional partial index `user_items(user_id,expires_at,catalog_id) WHERE quantity>0 AND expires_at IS NOT NULL`, only if isolated `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE,BUFFERS)` on 10,001 synthetic rows shows benefit; coordinate with DB migration owners and 5 historical checksum mismatches; do not edit applied migration 071/123.
- This is a read-only operation; no WLD postings, financial transaction, outbox, audit mutation, item expiration worker, scheduled notification or migration deployed by planning. Read snapshot consistency is not a promise that an item remains owned after response. Optional fingerprint validation may be expensive: benchmark p95 <500ms and enforce timeout/fail-closed, otherwise revise design before release.

### Operations, security, analytics and release
- Feature switch `inventory_expiring_radar` **OFF by default**. No SEO indexing of owner pages; link to public catalogue only through separate explicit navigation, never share owner query state. Android screen parity, accessibility at 320px and 200%, KST text and UTC comparisons. Admin can view only privacy-safe aggregate `expiry_radar_opened`, `horizon_selected`, `refresh_required`, `source_unavailable` metrics at cohort >=20, never per-user holdings. KPI: feature use per eligible owner, verified query success %, 409 rate, 503 rate, latency p95, accessible task completion; baseline UNKNOWN until measured.
- Release sequence: independent feature branch → review → forward migration on isolated PG17 → fresh clean-schema chain and app-role negative tests → full backend/frontend/security/a11y/Android checks → official Test at **exact candidate SHA** with authenticated owner/foreign and Nginx edge assertions → independent backup restore → approved GitOps canary and rollback. Never interpret docs-only CI or candidate image build as promotion approval. Rollback via feature OFF and prior immutable image, leave forward migration intact; no Production restart by planning. Existing Draft/HOLD merge incident #2031 and DB ACL #993 are P0 blockers.
- Proposed acceptance (NOT RUN): 10,001 synthetic holdings across two owners, 100 exact expiry/UTC/KST/DST-like boundaries, 100 foreign owner/session switch, 100 forged/expired/drift cursors, 100 concurrent updates/requests, 50 Android/private-header/320px tests; verify no direct table grants, no false empty, no duplicate/missing rows at equal timestamps, no false financial status. Actual QA v1399 evidence: backend 126 FAIL / 2,653 PASS / 501 SKIP, frontend 55 FAIL / 2,701 PASS, lint 1 parse error / 1,043 warnings; build/typecheck passed separately but full gates failed. Exact-SHA Test/restore BLOCKED.

| P0 | SEC1528-02 | Repair DEV app-role direct privileges and 5 migration checksum discrepancies via forward-only clean-schema tests (#993/#2000); negative app/PUBLIC access PASS. |
| P0 | FIN1528-03 | Reconcile Treasury/MINT/SWF/bond WLD ledger and prove no expiry label implies upkeep payment; no false green proof. |

## v2026.10.30.1526 — F675-187 Owner Shop Purchase Ledger Proof Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1524 → Current Feature Improvement v1526 → Next Feature Addition v1528 (provisional). Main bf8d0d48f810932b43783d8605dce53954c60cff checked at start and mid; stacked on Draft #2048 (following #2042 and #2036). Engineering issue #2046; release incident #2031. This is documentation only; prior v1524 V1 scope and all unspecified requirements below remain binding.

### Source-grounded corrections — migrations 001, 003 and 009
1. shop_purchase computes request_hash as digest(jsonb_build_object('actorUserId', actor::text, 'itemId', item::text)::text,'sha256'). Both purchases.idempotency_key and ledger_transactions.idempotency_key originate from the SAME request key. Verification MUST compare the keys and recompute the exact 32-byte hash in PostgreSQL, not hash a JavaScript JSON object, nor merely check that 32 bytes exist. The original purchase price is NOT part of this hash. Mismatched actor/item/hash/key fails closed.
2. economy_post_transaction writes outbox_events.aggregate_id = ledger transaction UUID (NOT purchase UUID), type = shop.purchase.completed, payload keys userId, itemId, amount; there is NO purchaseId payload field. A proof query joining aggregate_id to purchase ID or requiring payload.purchaseId will reject genuine purchases. Require exactly ONE matching event for the transaction/type/actor/item/exact amount. delivered_at=NULL is an event dispatch state, NOT a payment failure; missing/duplicate/wrong event is UNVERIFIED, inaccessible outbox is 503.
3. purchases.created_at and ledger_transactions.created_at are independently generated timestamps; never require equality. shop_items.name is a CURRENT mutable catalog label, NOT a purchase-time name snapshot. Display Current catalog name; do not imply historical title or derive paid WLD from current price. A later closed USER_CASH or SINK account does not retroactively invalidate its original posting; verify owner, type, system sink key and recorded posting, not present active status.
4. frontend/src/app/shop/my-receipts.tsx refreshes its private fetch on viewer?.signedIn but not on stable viewer identity. Signed-in-to-signed-in account switch can leave prior-user history in local component state. The new detail, list CTA and Android MUST clear private data, abort in-flight requests, and fence responses by authenticated actor/session generation. Client fences supplement, never replace, server owner-scoped DB authorization.
5. Migration 009 preserves nullable legacy paid_amount/idempotency_key/request_hash. A transaction FK alone is not payment proof. LEGACY_INCOMPLETE is distinct from a complete receipt with contradictory postings (RECEIPT_RECORDED_UNVERIFIED) and from unavailable verification source (503). No inferred zero, no current catalog price substitution. Prior read-only DEV inspection found 0 purchases, so the oracle is NOT proven on actual receipts.

### Exact evidence decision and UI/API contract
- VERIFIED_LEDGER_POSTINGS only when one consistent read confirms owner purchase, positive BIGINT WLD, exact receipt/transaction UUID, SHOP_PURCHASE actor, same idempotency key and recomputed request hash, EXACTLY two balanced postings USER_CASH credit (owner) / SINK debit (system_key sink), and exactly one matching outbox event aggregate/payload. This verifies a recorded virtual-WLD posting, NOT current item ownership, refund, real-money settlement or outbox delivery.
- RECEIPT_RECORDED_UNVERIFIED when the complete receipt exists but any ledger, actor, posting, hash, key or outbox predicate is false; no green badge. LEGACY_INCOMPLETE when immutable fields are missing/invalid; amount UNKNOWN if absent. Source unavailable/permission denied/timeout => 503, not an unverified business finding. Foreign and missing receipts => indistinguishable 404. 400 UUID, 401 session, 403 consent, 429 abuse quota; no error-to-empty.
- Authenticated consent-current owner enters /shop → My receipts → View payment evidence → /shop/purchases/:receiptId; default-OFF feature flag shop_purchase_ledger_detail. Loading, verified, unverified, legacy incomplete, offline, account switched, 400/401/403/404/429/503 and feature OFF have distinct accessible states. Show current catalog name (not historical), receipt ID, exact WLD string if known, KST purchase time, proof status, Retry and Back. 320px/200% zoom, WCAG 2.2 AA, keyboard, screen reader, text-not-color-only, focus on errors.
- Nest GET /api/v1/shop/purchases/:receiptId/evidence with SessionGuard/AuthenticatedGuard/ConsentGuard/ParseUUIDPipe, private Next BFF GET /api/shop/purchases/:receiptId/evidence, Android /app-api/v1/shop/purchases/:receiptId/evidence same typed enum/errors. No actor UUID from user input, no admin per-user bypass, no raw ledger/account IDs or sensitive analytics. Exact decimal BIGINT strings, ISO dates, verifiedLedgerAtUtc only if VERIFIED. GET makes ZERO ledger, balance, receipt or item writes.
- Add only forward migration after actual migration head for owner-scoped public.shop_my_purchase_evidence_v1(actor uuid,purchase uuid), SECURITY DEFINER, SET search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp, schema-qualified bounded joins, no dynamic SQL, REVOKE PUBLIC and app direct table DML/unauthorized SELECT, GRANT EXECUTE only. Nest authenticated session must bind actor; supplied UUID alone is NOT DB authentication. Read-consistent snapshot across receipt/transaction/postings/outbox. EXPLAIN(ANALYZE,BUFFERS) on isolated PG17.11 with 10,001 synthetic receipts before proposing any nonduplicate index.
- On HTML, Next BFF, Nest, Android gateway and real public Nginx edge enforce Cache-Control private,no-store; X-Robots-Tag noindex,nofollow; Referrer-Policy no-referrer on success, errors and redirects. No private route sitemap/canonical/OG; no receipt UUID, amount, user/account ID, outbox payload in logs/telemetry. Rate-limit UUID probing and same-signed-in account switches. Admin aggregate only >=20 members, verification-status counts, 404/429/503, p95, false-positive alerts. Acceptance targets (NOT observed): zero false VERIFIED, foreign leaks, financial writes or cache leaks; p95<300ms on synthetic load.

### QA, deployment and ownership
**PROPOSED, NOT EXECUTED:** 10,001 synthetic legacy receipts; 100 foreign/missing/nullable legacy; 100 idempotency/hash mismatch (including JSONB vs JS serialization); 100 actor/type/amount/direction/extra postings; 100 missing/duplicate/wrong-aggregate/wrong-payload outbox with delivered_at NULL positive case; 100 historical account closures; 100 web/Android same-signed-in account switches and delayed responses; 50 public edge 200/4xx/5xx privacy headers; 100 concurrent reads. Verify existing 30/100 list unchanged, no app direct DML and no financial mutation. Full runtime CI, independent backup restore, official Test exact-SHA auth/API/DB/finance/UI and public edge attestation BEFORE reviewed flag OFF→ON zero-downtime promotion. Rollback: flag OFF and code revert, never rewrite ledger.
**P0 4 / P1 6 backlog:**
- P0 OPS1526-01 (#1984/#2031) actual public Nginx SHA/digest, rollback artifact and isolated restore; don't confuse legacy v1515 with public v1935.
- P0 SEC1526-02 (#993/#2000) excessive DEV app-role DELETE/UPDATE, five prior migration checksum mismatches and clean-schema ACL parity.
- P0 FIN1526-03 (#1976/#1151) Treasury/MINT/SWF/bond proof plus SHOP_PURCHASE hash/idempotency/postings/outbox oracle.
- P0 REL1526-04 (#2031/#1088) Draft/HOLD mass-merge audit, protected-main required runtime CI, official exact-SHA Test and restore.
- P1 PURCHASE1526-05 (Backend B) owner SQL/ACL, hash and outbox oracle, 503 mapping, EXPLAIN and 10,001 fixtures.
- P1 PURCHASE1526-06 (Backend B) Nest guards/typed API, indistinguishable 404, rate limits and negative tests.
- P1 PURCHASE1526-07 (Development A) private Next detail/BFF, current-name label, accessible uncertainty and account switch fence.
- P1 PURCHASE1526-08 (Development C) Android gateway privacy, account generation, no disk cache, status parity.
- P1 PURCHASE1526-09 (A/B/C) edge privacy/SEO/no-referrer, admin >=20 KPI, default-OFF flag.
- P1 PURCHASE1526-10 (QA) isolated PG17.11 negative suites, exact-SHA Test, full CI, restore and rollback.

**QA source:** last independently documented v1396: two runtime checks failed, frontend lint parse failed, exact-SHA official Test and independent restore not certified. No full QA rerun this cycle. Prior DEV purchase count 0 (not proof of real payment). External independent reference approvals NEW 0, cumulative deduplicated/evidence-classified UNKNOWN / 10,000, BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD.


## v2026.10.30.1524 — F675-187 Owner Shop Purchase Receipt Ledger Evidence Detail (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Previous / Current / Next Cycle Type:** Feature Improvement v1522 → Feature Addition v1524 → Feature Improvement v1526 (provisional). **Authority:** `origin/main` `bf8d0d48f810932b43783d8605dce53954c60cff` rechecked at start/mid; `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, `docs/planning/PROJECT_PLAN.md` (English canonical), `PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md` (Korean parity). Draft planning chain #2036 → #2042 → #2048; engineering issue **#2046**; release incident #2031. Documentation-only DRAFT, not an implementation, merger or Production approval.

### Product evidence, scope and alternatives
- `frontend/src/app/shop/my-receipts.tsx` shows an owner-only legacy shop purchase list with search, KST date range and WLD minimum; `frontend/src/lib/shop-purchase-history.ts` validates purchaseId/itemName/amount/purchasedAt, not posting evidence. `backend/src/shop/shop.controller.ts` GET `/api/v1/shop/purchases` uses SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard, ConsentGuard and `shop_list_my_purchases` from migration 009. The list is **latest 30 by default, filtered over latest 100**; it is not a complete historical listing.
- `packages/database/migrations/009-shop-hardening.sql` extends legacy `public.purchases` with nullable `idempotency_key`, `request_hash`, `paid_amount` to retain prototype rows. New `shop_purchase` writes `SHOP_PURCHASE` with owner `USER_CASH credit` (balance decreases), system `SINK debit` (balance increases), and `shop.purchase.completed` outbox. `packages/database/init/001-economy-core.sql` defines the project's non-conventional posting direction. Receipt FK or positive paid_amount alone is NOT a verified posting.
- **Do not conflate** legacy `public.purchases`/`shop_items` with `public.shop_purchases`/`shop_catalog` (`072-shop-purchase-function.sql` transaction type `SHOP_CATALOG_PURCHASE`) or `shop_maintenance_receipts` (F675-186). Existing holdings are aggregate and can expire/be consumed; a purchase receipt does not prove current possession.
- Alternative A: expose raw ledger table from Next/Android (REJECT: leaks account IDs and violates least privilege). Alternative B: infer paid from the list amount/transaction FK (REJECT: false financial proof). **Chosen V1:** narrow owner-only read-only evidence projection, explicit VERIFIED vs UNVERIFIED, no new table, no payment or inventory write.

### Purpose, audience, entry and screen contract
- **Purpose:** let authenticated, consent-current members inspect whether one **legacy shop purchase** has a corroborating WLD ledger movement. No real-money payment proof, tax invoice, refund, current ownership, subscription, settlement schedule, or complete-history claim.
- **Entry:** existing `/shop` → `My receipts` → labelled `View payment evidence` CTA on each listed row → private `/shop/purchases/:receiptId` detail. Show immutable receipt ID (masked/copy with privacy warning), item display name, `Recorded amount (WLD)`, purchase timestamp in KST with timezone, `Ledger evidence`, optional verified transaction timestamp. Back → previous list state; Retry after transient failure. The detail may resolve a historical owner receipt by UUID even if it falls outside latest 100; do not advertise an all-history index.
- **States:** loading; `VERIFIED_LEDGER_POSTINGS` (one ledger movement verified, not current item ownership); `RECEIPT_RECORDED_UNVERIFIED` (receipt exists, posting proof absent/mismatched); `LEGACY_INCOMPLETE` (historical receipt missing positive paid amount/idempotency or other required immutable fields); 404 missing/foreign identical; 401 unauthenticated; 403 consent; 429 rate-limited; 503 database/verification source unavailable; offline; account switched; feature OFF. No fabricated zero WLD, green paid badge or success-shaped empty object on error. Unknown source is not an empty receipt.
- **Permissions:** session-derived actor only; no actorId query/path, no foreign user enumeration. New endpoint is read-only and does not mutate purchases, holdings, ledger, outbox, balances or admin state. Existing shop purchase command/CSRF/idempotency remains unchanged. Owner can see only own legacy receipt; administrators have no per-user bypass in V1.

### API and data truth contract
- Nest `GET /api/v1/shop/purchases/:receiptId/evidence`, guards SessionGuard + AuthenticatedGuard + ConsentGuard, UUID validation, rate budget, typed response `{receiptId,itemName,recordedAmountWld?:string,purchasedAt,ledgerTransactionId?:string,evidenceStatus:'VERIFIED_LEDGER_POSTINGS'|'RECEIPT_RECORDED_UNVERIFIED'|'LEGACY_INCOMPLETE',verifiedLedgerAtUtc?:string,sourceStatus:'VERIFIED_READ'}`. Omit `recordedAmountWld` if original amount is absent/invalid; do not invent 0. `verifiedLedgerAtUtc` only when full proof succeeds. API errors: 400 malformed UUID, 401 session, 403 consent, 404 missing/foreign, 429 quota, 503 DB/proof read unavailable. The proof query must distinguish a proven mismatch (UNVERIFIED) from inaccessible/unavailable proof (503 or explicit incomplete, never green).
- Next `GET /api/shop/purchases/:receiptId/evidence` private BFF passes session and allowlisted UUID only, validates response and preserves 400/401/403/404/429/503 (no `apiOrNull` false-empty). Android `/app-api/v1/shop/purchases/:receiptId/evidence` same enum and errors, no local disk cache, cancel/ignore stale requests on logout/account switch. Contract tests enforce exact WLD decimal strings and ISO timestamps; no JavaScript Number money arithmetic.
- PostgreSQL proposed additive `public.shop_my_purchase_evidence_v1(p_actor uuid,p_purchase uuid)` SECURITY DEFINER with `SET search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp`, schema-qualified joins and `WHERE purchase.user_id=p_actor` plus active-user policy aligned with existing `shop_list_my_purchases`. `REVOKE ALL ... FROM PUBLIC`; grant EXECUTE only to app role; reassert no direct `SELECT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE` on `purchases`, `ledger_transactions`, `ledger_postings`, `accounts`, `outbox_events`, `audit_logs`. UUID argument is **not** DB authentication; Nest verified session/consent must bind actor. Add forward-only migration after actual migration head (currently 312); do not reserve a number or edit applied migration 009.
- Proof predicate (single read-only REPEATABLE READ statement/transaction): purchase owner and recorded amount positive BIGINT; purchase transaction ID points to `ledger_transactions` with `type='SHOP_PURCHASE'`, `actor_user_id=p_actor`, matching idempotency when present; exactly **two** `ledger_postings` with amounts equal to receipt, one `USER_CASH` account owned by actor with `direction='credit'`, one system `SINK` with `system_key='sink'` and `direction='debit'`; balanced totals; corresponding `outbox_events` aggregate/type `shop.purchase.completed` and matching item/actor/amount payload where evidence is available. No cross-family `SHOP_CATALOG_PURCHASE` acceptance. If any field is absent, unexpected, contradictory or extra posting exists, fail closed UNVERIFIED or LEGACY_INCOMPLETE; if source cannot be read safely, 503 rather than broadening table privileges. Distinguish current account status from historical posting provenance; do not falsely invalidate valid old postings solely because an account was later closed.
- **DB constraints/indexes:** existing `purchases.id` PK and `ledger_postings.transaction_id` lookup must be inspected via EXPLAIN/BUFFERS on isolated PG17.11 with 10,001 receipts; add only justified nonduplicate indexes in coordinated forward migration. Read-only query uses per-actor quota, short statement timeout, no arbitrary joins, no dynamic SQL; no idempotency key required for GET. No new table or financial transaction. No cross-request snapshot claim.

### Privacy, mobile, SEO, analytics and acceptance
- Private server HTML and BFF, Android gateway and real public edge must override upstream cache with `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex,nofollow`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` for 200 and 4xx/5xx. Exclude detail routes from sitemap, social previews and canonical indexing; no UUID, receipt amount, posting payload or cursor in analytics/logs. Do not leak foreign receipt existence through timing/error text. Avoid CSRF write surface (GET only), protect against high-rate UUID enumeration, account-switch races and stale hydration.
- Responsive at 320px and 200% zoom; 44px targets, semantic definition list, visible evidence status text not color-only, keyboard and screen-reader focus management, aria-live errors, reduced motion; Korean/English copy parity and WCAG 2.2 AA. Explicit uncertainty copy: “Ledger postings verified for this purchase; current ownership/refund not assessed.” or “Purchase receipt recorded; payment ledger proof not verified.”
- Admin: **aggregate only** verification-status counts and 404/429/503, p95 latency, proof mismatch and false-verified alerts, cohort suppression below 20 members. KPI/acceptance targets (NOT observed): 0 foreign receipts, 0 false VERIFIED, 0 WLD writes, 0 private cache/index leaks; p95 <300ms on 10,001 synthetic receipts. Measure detail open→evidence viewed, error/retry rate and support-ticket deflection; no unobserved retention/revenue claims.
- QA regression **PROPOSED, NOT RUN:** isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 full migration parity and 10,001 synthetic legacy receipts; 100 foreign/missing/malformed/legacy-null, 100 wrong actor/type/direction/amount/extra posting/missing outbox/cross-family, 100 simultaneous requests and historical account-status changes, 100 logout/account-switch Android/web, 50 edge-cache/robots error cases; prove no table grants or financial writes, baseline list latest30/100 unaffected, default flag OFF. Verify complete CI, independent backup restore, exact-SHA official Test auth/DB/API/UI and public-edge digest before zero-downtime Production promotion; flag OFF and code-only revert on rollback, no retroactive ledger rewrite.

## v2026.10.30.1522 — F675-186 Upkeep Receipt Ledger-Proof & Coverage Truth Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Previous / Current / Next Cycle Type:** Feature Addition v1520 → Feature Improvement v1522 → Feature Addition v1524 (provisional). **Canonical source:** `origin/main` `bf8d0d48f810932b43783d8605dce53954c60cff` rechecked 2026-10-30 21:00–21:05 KST; `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, `docs/planning/PROJECT_PLAN.md` (English authority), `PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md` (equivalent). Draft #2042 is Draft #2042 stacked on Draft #2036, issue #2035; release incident #2031. This is a DOCUMENTATION-ONLY refinement, not runtime implementation, merge authorization or deployment approval.

### Source-corrected ledger semantics — critical acceptance change
- `packages/database/init/001-economy-core.sql:25-40` computes account balance delta as `+amount` for **`direction='debit'`** and `-amount` for **`direction='credit'`**. Thus this project's actual cash outflow uses `USER_CASH credit`, while the `SINK debit` increases the sink balance. **Do not import conventional accounting terminology or require USER_CASH debit / SINK credit.** This correction supersedes any earlier backlog or issue wording that inverted directions.
- `packages/database/migrations/104-item-lifecycle.sql:423-445, ~585-630` explicitly writes `SHOP_MAINTENANCE` ledger transactions with the authenticated actor, `USER_CASH credit` and `SINK debit`, both for automated charge (`shop.maintenance.charged`) and manual settlement (`shop.maintenance.settled`). The `amount` must match each posting and receipt; no extra postings or foreign cash account may be silently accepted. Check transaction actor/type, active account type/owner, sink system key/type, posting count/directions/amounts, balanced totals, and corresponding outbox event origin/payload when available. Any mismatch or absent evidence yields `RECEIPT_RECORDED_UNVERIFIED`, never a green payment badge. Verify with real PostgreSQL; do not rely on mocked repositories or synthetic fixture convention (the purchase fixture uses a different direction and transaction type).
- `075-shop-read-models-and-maintenance.sql:67-75` defines UNIQUE `(user_id,catalog_id,maintenance_week)`, `amount BIGINT>0`, `transaction_id` FK, but no receipt `paid_at` or per-covered-week allocation. In 104 the **automated** `v_total = least(previous_arrears + current_due, cap)` is recorded under **current** `v_week`; **manual** settlement records `first_missed_week` with the aggregate due. Therefore a receipt's `maintenance_week` is a **reference week**, not proof that precisely that week alone was paid or that all weeks between receipts were covered. Display `referenceWeek` and `recordedAmountWld`, with `coveredWeeks:'UNKNOWN'` unless an independently proven allocation source exists. A `VERIFIED_LEDGER_POSTINGS` row proves one specific money movement, **not** complete upkeep schedule compliance, absence of arrears, or service eligibility. `ledger_transactions.created_at` may be displayed as `verifiedLedgerAtUtc` only after the full proof passes; never derive payment time from `maintenance_week`.
- The weekly runner catches errors per holding and increments `failed_count`; it can leave a missing receipt even when a scheduled run was attempted. Missing receipts and `shop_my_items`' zero arrears fallback do not prove payment, billing execution, or debt-free status. Historical receipts must remain accessible to their original owner even if the holding expires; catalog joins must not filter away inactive items.

### User flows, permissions and exact response states
- Preserve F675-186 member-only `/inventory` → `View upkeep receipts` and `/shop` → `My upkeep receipts`; purchase receipts remain a separate view. Owner identity comes only from verified Nest session + current consent; never from URL/query/Android payload. `SECURITY DEFINER` function's `p_actor` is **not independent DB authentication**; combine server guard, role ACL and negative tests. Item names may be retained from catalog but cannot disclose other owners. Feature switch `shop_upkeep_receipt_history` defaults OFF.
- Card/table labels: **Reference maintenance week** (not "week paid"), **Recorded WLD amount** (exact bigint decimal string), **Ledger posting proof** (`VERIFIED_LEDGER_POSTINGS` or `RECEIPT_RECORDED_UNVERIFIED`), **Coverage** (`UNKNOWN`), and optional **Verified ledger transaction time**. A verified row may say "Ledger posting verified for this receipt; covered weeks and outstanding balance not assessed." No "fully paid", "all weeks settled", "charged this week" or "zero arrears" without an independent authoritative debt/schedule model. `VERIFIED_EMPTY` means only no matching receipts in the selected range, not a verified empty debt. Invalid filters, loading, partial/unverified, source changed, expired cursor, 401/403, 429, 503, offline and account-switch must be distinct with Refresh/Retry and preserved safe filter state.
- Filters: optional catalog UUID, inclusive `fromWeek/toWeek` KST calendar date (default 12 reference weeks, max 366 days); 20 rows default / 50 max, keyset `(maintenance_week DESC,id DESC)` with a 50+1 query. Validate date format and calendar validity, duplicate query keys, impossible intervals, unauthorized session and payload limits before DB call. On no owner matches, do not reveal whether a foreign catalog ID exists. No financial mutation from GET.

### API, PostgreSQL, snapshot and security contract
- Nest `GET /api/v1/shop/upkeep/receipts?catalogId=&fromWeek=&toWeek=&limit=&cursor=` guarded by Session/Authenticated/Consent. Response `items:[{receiptId,catalogId,itemName,referenceWeek,recordedAmountWld,ledgerTransactionId,evidenceStatus,coveredWeeks:'UNKNOWN',verifiedLedgerAtUtc?}],hasMore,nextCursor,asOfUtc,sourceStatus`. 400 malformed/duplicate, 401 unauthenticated, 403 consent, 409 source changed, 410 expired cursor, 429 rate, 503 source unavailable. Error must never become `VERIFIED_EMPTY`. Next BFF must preserve 409/410/429/503; Android `/app-api/v1/shop/upkeep/receipts` preserves equivalent contract, no disk cache and generation-fenced account switching.
- Proposed additive forward-only `public.shop_my_upkeep_receipts_v1` with fixed `search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp`, fully qualified tables, `receipt.user_id=p_actor`, `REVOKE PUBLIC EXECUTE`, `GRANT EXECUTE moneyverse_app` only. Review that caller cannot directly SELECT/UPDATE/DELETE receipts, ledger, accounts or audit logs. Read-only REPEATABLE READ for receipt+posting+outbox validation within each request; no cross-request MVCC promise. If `outbox_events` cannot be accessed safely, proof must remain UNVERIFIED rather than adding broad grants. Verify existing UNIQUE/FK indexes and EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) on isolated 10,001-row fixture before proposing `(user_id,maintenance_week DESC,id DESC)` and `(user_id,catalog_id,maintenance_week DESC,id DESC)` indexes; do not duplicate an adequate existing index.
- Cursor HMAC-SHA256 bound to actor, session generation, normalized filters, stable order key, first-page high-water and source fingerprint, expiry <=15 minutes; source changes require 409 and clean restart, expiry 410. A digest limited to the visible page does **not** certify the whole result set; either verify a bounded eligible-set fingerprint each page or explicitly downgrade the stability claim and prohibit "complete" history. Concurrent inserts/backdated receipts and account switches are required negative tests. SQL statement timeout and per-actor quotas prevent expensive digest/ledger-join abuse. Zero ledger/receipt writes; no historical migration edits.
- Privacy: real public-edge `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex,nofollow`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` for 200 and 4xx/5xx; private routes excluded from sitemap, canonical indexing and social previews. Do not log UUIDs, cursor, amount, idempotency key or posting payload. Keyboard/aria-live/semantic table, 320px, 200% zoom, reduced motion, 44px targets, WCAG 2.2 AA, Korean/English parity. Admin receives aggregate-only counts, source-unverified rate, 409/410/429/503, p95 latency, no per-user receipts, suppress cohorts <20 members. KPI targets (not measured): 0 wrong-direction verified badges, 0 false weekly-coverage claims, 0 foreign rows, 0 WLD writes, p95 <300ms on isolated 10,001 rows.

### Regression, operations and handoff (P0 4 + P1 6)
- **P0 OPS1522-01** (#1984/#2031): preserve actual public Nginx upstream provenance. QA v1396 corrected the prior claim: public 3564/3565 `moneyverse-live-prod-*` runs `/srv/moneyverse-data/releases/prod-v2026.10.30.1935-bf8d0d48f810` matching public SHA; separate legacy units still run deleted v1515 and are not the public upstream. Preserve both sets; no blind restart or rollback before independent restore.
- **P0 SEC1522-02** (#993/#2000): prove real app-role least privilege and fresh-migration parity; 5 historical checksum mismatches and broad DEV ACL unresolved. No inference about Production DB compromise.
- **P0 FIN1522-03** (#1976/#1151): Treasury/MINT/SWF/bond truth, plus receipt→`USER_CASH credit`/`SINK debit`/outbox oracle. Reject false verified-payment or covered-week assertions.
- **P0 REL1522-04** (#2031/#1088): investigate Draft/HOLD mass merge and live v1935 promotion despite exact deployed SHA `bf8d0d48` failing `verify/runtime-check` before deployment and `runtime-check` after. Official Test still `c2129625`; no exact-SHA Test, independent restore or full CI approval. Audit actor/policy, freeze unsafe promotion, do not disable ongoing development/QA.
- **P1 UPKEEP1522-05** (B/QA): forward-only owner-scoped SQL and actual posting-direction/actor/type/outbox oracle, EXPLAIN, 10,001-row isolated PG tests.
- **P1 UPKEEP1522-06** (B): guarded Nest typed API, correct reference-week semantics, HMAC/fingerprint, error differentiation and actor binding.
- **P1 UPKEEP1522-07** (A): Next member UI with no coverage/paid misstatement, exact WLD, 200% zoom, 320px, error/empty/partial states.
- **P1 UPKEEP1522-08** (C): Android app-api parity, no-store, session-generation switch fence, offline and Retry.
- **P1 UPKEEP1522-09** (A/B): edge privacy/SEO header precedence, quotas, admin aggregate KPI and default-OFF flag.
- **P1 UPKEEP1522-10** (QA): isolated PG17.11: 10,001 receipts, 100 foreign actors, 100 malformed/expired cursors, 100 KST/arrears week cases, 100 concurrent/backdated inserts, 50 inverted-posting/extra-posting/wrong actor/type/outbox/missing-ledger negatives, 50 Android account switches; verify 0 financial writes, exact-SHA Test and flag-OFF rollback. **Proposed, NOT run.**

## v2026.10.30.1520 — F675-186 Member Upkeep Receipt & Ledger Evidence History (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Previous / Current / Next:** Feature Improvement v1518 / Feature Addition v1520 / Feature Improvement v1522 (provisional). **Authority:** origin/main bf8d0d48f810932b43783d8605dce53954c60cff checked at start and midpoint 2026-10-30; AGENTS.md, README.md, canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean parity. Engineering issue #2035; independent release-integrity P0 #2031. All changes are planning-only. Existing Draft/HOLD PRs were unexpectedly mass-merged around 19:23–19:25 KST despite failed runtime checks; never treat their presence on main as approval. The current main after the merge has NOT passed an independently observed exact-SHA full runtime gate.

### Why, source truth, alternatives, boundaries
- **User problem:** inventory currently reports upkeep estimates and recorded arrears, but no member-facing history of what upkeep receipts exist and whether each receipt matches a ledger transaction. A reported arrears value of zero is NOT proof that the scheduled charge ran or that every past week was paid. Provide historical transparency without initiating a charge.
- **Actual schema:** migration 075 defines shop_maintenance_receipts(id UUID PK, idempotency_key UUID UNIQUE, user_id UUID FK users, catalog_id UUID FK shop_catalog, maintenance_week DATE, amount BIGINT CHECK >0, transaction_id UUID FK ledger_transactions, UNIQUE(user_id,catalog_id,maintenance_week)). Migration 104 writes the receipt in the same transaction as economy_post_transaction for automated KST Monday 04:30 charges, or for manual shop_settle_upkeep. The manual receipt records first_missed_week, not the settlement date. There is NO charged_at column; do not present maintenance_week as payment timestamp. Ledger event origins shop.maintenance.charged and shop.maintenance.settled distinguish paths, subject to exact schema/ledger validation. A foreign key to ledger_transactions alone does NOT certify cash/sink postings, provenance or completeness.
- **Current surfaces:** backend/src/shop/shop.controller.ts has GET /api/v1/shop/holdings and POST /api/v1/shop/holdings/:id/upkeep-settlements; GET /api/v1/shop/purchases is purchase history, NOT upkeep history. frontend/src/app/inventory and frontend/src/app/shop/my-receipts.tsx do not establish an owner upkeep receipt history. Do not repurpose purchase receipts or infer paid weeks from missing arrears.
- **Alternatives:** A) reuse purchase history: incorrect data source; reject. B) new invoice/receipt table: redundant, introduces financial migration risk; reject. **Chosen:** bounded read-only owner-scoped function over existing receipts and ledger postings, with a new authenticated Nest GET and private Next/Android views. No new tables, no financial writes, no changes to historical migrations.

### Audience, permission, navigation, screens, CTAs, states
- Authenticated, consent-current owner only. Entry /inventory item card -> View upkeep receipts; /shop -> My upkeep receipts; preserve purchase receipts, equip/unequip, settlement POST and F675-184/F675-185 behavior. Historical receipts remain visible after holding expiry/removal, but are never exposed to a different actor. No owner UUID query parameter or shareable public receipt route. Default last 12 maintenance weeks; explicit fromWeek/toWeek KST calendar dates, inclusive, maximum 366 days; optional catalogId UUID; 20/page, hard maximum 50.
- Display item name (from authoritative catalog), maintenance week (not paid date), exact recorded amount in WLD (BIGINT string), ledger-evidence status and optional **independently verified** transaction timestamp. Explicit copy: "Receipt recorded and ledger postings verified", "Receipt recorded; ledger verification unavailable", "No receipts recorded for this filter — this does not certify no debt", "Source unavailable; cannot confirm payment". Do not show settled badge for missing data, never imply all weekly charges completed. Loading, verified results, VERIFIED_EMPTY, RECEIPT_RECORDED_UNVERIFIED, INVALID_FILTER, AUTH_EXPIRED, CONSENT_REQUIRED, SOURCE_CHANGED(409), CURSOR_EXPIRED(410), RATE_LIMITED(429), SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE(503), offline and actor-switch states; Retry/Refresh/Clear filters; preserve entered dates on recoverable errors.
- Use semantically labelled table/cards, sortable date label only when supported by source, keyboard pagination, aria-live status, focus management, no color-only evidence, reduced motion, 320px and 200% zoom, 44px touch targets, WCAG 2.2 AA, ko/en parity.

### API, DB security, consistency, transaction and abuse
- Proposed Nest GET /api/v1/shop/upkeep/receipts?catalogId=&fromWeek=&toWeek=&limit=&cursor=; SessionGuard + AuthenticatedGuard + ConsentGuard, actor from verified session only. DTO: {items:[{receiptId,catalogId,itemName,maintenanceWeek,amountWld,ledgerTransactionId,evidenceStatus,verifiedLedgerAtUtc?}],hasMore,nextCursor,asOfUtc,sourceStatus:'VERIFIED'|'PARTIAL_UNVERIFIED'}. UUIDs strictly validated; WLD integer as canonical decimal string; timestamps RFC3339 UTC; no JavaScript floating-point monetary arithmetic. 400 invalid/duplicate/oversize date/limit, 401 no session, 403 consent, 409 stale source, 410 cursor expired, 429 throttled, 503 DB/ledger unavailable; no fallback success-shaped empty list. Foreign catalog ID is indistinguishable from no matching owner receipts (not an existence oracle).
- New additive forward-only public.shop_my_upkeep_receipts_v1(p_actor uuid, p_catalog uuid, p_from_week date, p_to_week date, p_limit integer, p_after_week date, p_after_receipt uuid) SECURITY DEFINER read-only function. Fixed search_path pg_catalog,pg_temp, fully qualified tables, explicit receipt.user_id=p_actor, REVOKE PUBLIC EXECUTE, GRANT moneyverse_app EXECUTE only, reassert NO direct app-role DML/SELECT on receipts/ledger/audit; role grants must be measured on real PostgreSQL and migration applied in independent test only. Existing receipt PK/UNIQUE and FK stay intact. Evaluate index on (user_id,maintenance_week DESC,id DESC) and (user_id,catalog_id,maintenance_week DESC,id DESC) via EXPLAIN ANALYZE/BUFFERS on 10,001 synthetic rows before adding a nonduplicate index; do not preempt other teams' migration numbers.
- For each receipt, ledger proof must match transaction id, expected actor, type SHOP_MAINTENANCE, amount, actual cash debit and sink credit in the project's posting direction convention, balancing and committed ledger; mismatch or permission/timeout -> RECEIPT_RECORDED_UNVERIFIED, not paid/verified. Require one consistent read-only REPEATABLE READ snapshot for receipt + posting verification. This is a **read**, not a billing or settlement transaction; no idempotency key or POST, no modification of receipts or WLD. If actual posting semantics cannot be proven by existing schema, downgrade all to UNVERIFIED and block VERIFIED copy. Distinguish the maintenance week from ledger transaction time and manual settlement timing; never fabricate a charge timestamp.
- Deterministic keyset (maintenance_week DESC, receipt.id DESC) with HMAC-SHA256 cursor bound to actor, session generation, normalized filters, asOf, last keys, source fingerprint and <=15 minute TTL; detect changed eligible source with 409 or mark snapshot unverified rather than promising cross-request MVCC. Bounded DB statement timeout, rate limits per actor, 50+1 rows, payload cap, strict input normalization, no sensitive logging or cursor echo.

### Privacy, mobile, admin, KPI, tests, rollout
- Private page/API at **actual edge** on success and error: Cache-Control private,no-store; X-Robots-Tag noindex,nofollow; Referrer-Policy no-referrer. Not in sitemap, public SEO, social previews or indexable query URLs. Next server-only BFF; Android /app-api/v1/shop/upkeep/receipts must preserve authenticated contract, no disk cache and generation-fenced logout/account switch, offline/Retry parity. Explicitly test global Next header override risk #1994/#2012.
- Admin: no individual receipt search in V1.
