## v2026.10.30.1518 — F675-185 Inventory Lifecycle Source-Truth Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Previous / Current / Next Cycle:** Feature Addition v1516 / Feature Improvement v1518 / Feature Addition v1520 (provisional). **Authority:** exact origin/main `9da6545c4ff4809b5bd84ab6a28b9d2f1db74cef` verified at start and midpoint on 2026-10-30; source code and canonical AGENTS.md, README.md, PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean parity inspected. Stacked on Draft #2025 (after #2018/#2014); existing engineering issue #2024; Draft #2029. This is a planning-only refinement, not a runtime implementation or release approval.

### Source-grounded correction: reported state is not a settled financial receipt
- **Actual SQL:** `packages/database/migrations/123-store-read-models-v2.sql` `shop_my_items_v2(p_actor)` returns `catalogue.maintenance_cost * held.quantity` as `weekly_cost`, and `catalogue.maintenance_cost * held.quantity * shop_upkeep_grace_weeks()` as `arrears_cap`. These are **current catalog-derived estimates**, not historical invoices, not ledger postings and not proof that a scheduled charge ran. `arrears_due`, `unpaid_weeks` and `suspended` are from `shop_upkeep_arrears` with `coalesce(owed.amount_due,0)`, `coalesce(owed.unpaid_weeks,0)`, `coalesce(owed.suspended,false)`; absence of a row becomes zero/false, so zero/false alone cannot certify 'fully paid', 'up to date' or 'never suspended'. The client must say **reported amount / recorded arrears / reported suspension**, not verified payment, settlement or financial compliance. No client-side derived payment or receipt badge.
- **Billing schedule:** migration `104-item-lifecycle.sql` charges by an idempotent KST Monday 04:30 weekly period; `shop_maintenance_receipts` is the charging receipt source. The existing holdings GET does **not** expose a last successful billing-run watermark, paid receipt, next-due date, or an independently verified ledger transaction. A missing arrears row may mean no arrears *recorded* at read time, not that the week's billing was completed. Do not invent 'next payment due', 'paid this week', 'all settled', or billing timestamp. A true receipt/history view requires a separately scoped, owner-authenticated, ledger-proven API and financial approval; not in V1.
- **Policy drift:** maintenance_cost is policy-adjustable (migrations 089/092); cap is recomputed using **current** cost/quantity/grace while stored arrears may originate under earlier policy. Never enforce `arrears_due <= arrears_cap` as an invariant or silently clamp/replace historical arrears; if recorded due exceeds displayed current cap, show both explicitly with an explanation ('current policy estimate versus recorded outstanding amount'), flag a diagnostic and request review. Monetary fields are canonical nonnegative integer WLD strings, parsed with BigInt; do not use JS Number or decimal floats for money.
- **Duration semantics:** `durable` is true when holding_duration_days is non-null **OR** maintenance_cost > 0, so it is not equivalent to 'maintenance applies', 'never expires', 'active effect' or 'permanent ownership'. Maintenance applicability is based on an explicit validated weekly_cost and policy classification; zero cost does not imply no expiry. `expires_at` is holding end, while `effect_expires_at` is the **maximum future effect receipt expiry** from `item_effect_receipts`, not a proven equipped/activated effect or an exhaustive receipt history. An absent active receipt yields null; do not claim permanent effects or 'effect running' without separate source proof.
- **Temporal consistency:** `shop_my_items_v2` calls `clock_timestamp()` independently for holding and effect receipt filtering; `STABLE` declaration does not pin those wall-clock reads to a single snapshot time. A GET 200 is a **read-time report**; label 'last refreshed' using server response time if available and avoid countdowns, cross-field atomicity claims or post-expiry 'active' claims. Refresh on focus, after equip/consume and account switch. Do not rewrite applied migration 123; if a single-asOf API is proven necessary, B owns an additive forward migration with reviewed signature, fixed search_path, PUBLIC revoke, explicit owner predicate, index/EXPLAIN and real-PG tests.

### Member contract, UI, access, error and data-flow
- **Purpose / audience:** authenticated, consent-current owner reviewing their own item condition; entry `/inventory` → `View condition / 상태 자세히` disclosure on the existing card. Preserve equip/unequip and F675-184 independent search/sort/equipped summary; do not substitute paginated search results for the complete equipped state. No new route, table, index, SQL migration, endpoint, payment CTA, financial transaction, notification, or elevated admin view.
- **Existing API:** Nest `GET /api/v1/shop/holdings` via SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard and ConsentGuard; repository `holdings(actor)` invokes `shop_my_items_v2($1)`. Frontend `page.tsx` uses `apiOrNull` and `holdingsData?.holdings || []`, incorrectly converting upstream failure to verified empty (separate #1977; dependency not duplicated). UI must fail closed: 401 AUTH_EXPIRED, 403 CONSENT_REQUIRED, 429 RATE_LIMITED, 503 SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE, invalid shape MALFORMED_RESPONSE, valid owner-empty VERIFIED_EMPTY, and partial nullable fields VERIFIED_UNKNOWN. Retry cannot turn a failed response into 'zero arrears'. Cache clear on logout, session switch, tab revalidation and feature OFF.
- **UI labels:** 'Weekly upkeep (current catalog rate)', 'Recorded unpaid weeks', 'Recorded outstanding WLD', 'Cap at current policy', 'Suspension reported by source', 'Holding end date', 'Latest active effect receipt expiry (if any)'. Display explicit 'Not reported / UNKNOWN' for missing/malformed fields; `0` from valid source may be shown as **reported 0**, never 'paid'. If policy-derived cap and stored due conflict, show both and 'requires review' without destructive correction. All amounts rendered as locale-grouped BigInt strings; no hidden number conversion, negative/unsafe values or XSS from preview_data.
- **Security / DB / API:** no member UUID in URL/query; bind actor to verified Nest session and consent; passing p_actor to SECURITY DEFINER is not independent DB identity proof. Review effective app-role ACL (#993) and migration mismatches 219/220/221/234/266; fresh install schema parity (#2000). Strict DTO parser for UUID, UTC timestamps, booleans, safe-integer quantities, exact money strings and bounded payloads; 400/401/403/429/503 do not leak owner data. No new DB transaction in read-only V1; do not call upkeep settlement POST. Idempotency remains with existing writes outside scope.
- **SEO / mobile / a11y / admin:** actual public edge `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow` and no-referrer on success/failure (#1994/#2012); private inventory absent from sitemap and telemetry. Android app-api must verify identical owner response, no disk cache, generation-fence account switches and offline state; never reuse another account's holdings. WCAG 2.2 AA at 320px/200% zoom, keyboard button/aria-expanded/aria-controls, semantic definition list, visible focus, status aria-live, no color-only warning, 44px targets, ko/en. Admin KPI aggregate cohorts >=20 distinct owners: disclosure-open, UNKNOWN, contradictory current cap vs stored arrears, source errors, privacy/ACL failures, a11y defects; no per-user arrears or serial numbers. Target 0 false payment claims, 0 cross-owner exposure, 0 WLD writes, 0 regression to equip flow.

### Prioritized implementation handoff and acceptance gates
- **P0 OPS1518-01 (#1984/#1979):** Production displayed v1523 SHA differs from deleted live v1515 process path; preserve processes, prove executable SHA/artifact and immutable independent rollback before restart. No restart by planning.
- **P0 SEC1518-02 (#993/#2000):** effective DEV moneyverse_app ACL (QA v1394: UPDATE 211/DELETE 211/TRUNCATE 233 of 253 tables), five migration SHA mismatches, fresh DB migration parity and owner-only function negatives. Production ACL unverified.
- **P0 FIN1518-03 (#1976/#1151):** Treasury truth tests 2 FAIL, bond payout fail-open 2 FAIL; verify finance journal, shop maintenance receipt vs current estimate and replay before any 'paid' claim. Preserve C-owned candidate branch.
- **P0 REL1518-04 (#1994/#2012):** exact-main QA v1394 backend 119 FAIL/2563 PASS/495 SKIP, frontend 53 FAIL/2607 PASS; main prepare CI FAIL, runtime skipped. Prove full CI, actual edge privacy, official Test exact frontend/backend/edge/app-api SHA, authenticated E2E and independent external backup restore before any Production promotion.
- **P1 LIFECYCLE1518-05 (B/QA):** compare SQL current rate/cap, stored arrears and coalesce cases against fixture oracle; policy changes, missing arrears row, weekly 04:30 KST boundary, max effect receipt, durable-but-zero-cost and quantity>1. No migration 123 rewrite.
- **P1 LIFECYCLE1518-06 (A):** strict owner DTO parser, BigInt display, source-failure vs verified empty, cap/due conflict display, unknown-as-zero negative tests and no false payment badges.
- **P1 LIFECYCLE1518-07 (A/C):** accessible inline disclosure, correct label copy and expiry/effect semantics, preserve full equipped summary, 320px/200% and keyboard/screen-reader regressions.
- **P1 LIFECYCLE1518-08 (C):** Android API parity, no cache, generation-fenced actor/session switch, offline/retry and 401/403/429/503.
- **P1 LIFECYCLE1518-09 (A/B):** edge privacy/SEO on all outcomes, feature flag OFF and staff-only rollout, aggregated admin KPIs (cohort>=20), no owner financial logs.
- **P1 LIFECYCLE1518-10 (QA):** isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 exact migration and owner/foreign-owner ACL; 10,001 synthetic holdings, 100 absent/zero/clock-boundary, 100 changed-policy cap<due, 100 account switches, 100 concurrent refresh, 50 effect receipts, 50 Android parity, 0 ledger/item writes, equip and flag-OFF rollback. **PROPOSED, NOT EXECUTED**. Verify actual edge and Test exact-SHA before any rollout.

**Latest independent QA v1394 (2026-10-30 18:35 KST): FAILED/P0.** Exact-main build/typecheck/lint passed, but full backend/frontend suites failed; Treasury reconciliation 2/2 FAIL, bond hold 2 FAIL; 293 applied DEV migrations with 5 checksum mismatches; Production v1523 symlink vs deleted live v1515 process, Test different SHA, independent restore not performed. QA DEV ledger 891 transactions/1829 postings, 0 imbalance/orphans/balance mismatch is not Treasury provenance proof. External independent reference approvals: new **0**, cumulative deduplicated classified **UNKNOWN / 10,000**, gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. Draft docs-only; no runtime/DB/WLD/Test/Production mutation or main merge.

**Rollout:** separate A/B/C/QA owner branches, `inventory_lifecycle_details` default OFF, official Test exact candidate SHA + full CI/real PG ACL/finance/edge/mobile/a11y + independent restore; staff canary, abort on false-verified payment/expiry/ACL/privacy or performance; rollback flag OFF and previous verified immutable app SHA, never edit historical migrations or ledger.


## v2026.10.30.1516 — F675-185 Member Inventory Lifecycle and Upkeep Transparency (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Previous / Current / Next Cycle Type:** Feature Improvement v1514 / Feature Addition v1516 / Feature Improvement v1518 (provisional). **Authority:** origin/main `9da6545c4ff4809b5bd84ab6a28b9d2f1db74cef`, rechecked start and midpoint 2026-10-30; planning parent Draft #2018 (stacked after #2014), engineering handoff [#2024](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/2024). English is canonical; Korean document has equivalent semantics. **DRAFT, no runtime/DB/WLD/Test/Production mutation or approval.**

### Product gap, choices, non-duplication
- **Observed source:** `frontend/src/app/inventory/page.tsx` authenticates via `requireMember()` and fetches `/api/v1/shop/holdings` through `apiOrNull`, converting a null response into `[]` (a false-empty risk, tracked separately in #1977). `inventory-view.tsx` displays name, description, rarity, quantity, serial number and equip state but not holding expiry, effect expiry, upkeep arrears or suspension. Its `HeldItem` type lacks lifecycle fields.
- **Existing authority:** `backend/src/shop/shop.controller.ts` GET `holdings` requires SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard, ConsentGuard; `shop.repository.ts` already returns `durable`, `weekly_cost::text`, `effect_expires_at`, `unpaid_weeks`, `arrears_due::text`, `arrears_cap::text`, `suspended`. Migration `123-store-read-models-v2.sql` exposes them from owner-scoped `public.shop_my_items_v2(p_actor)` and reads holdings, catalog, effect receipts and arrears. This is **source evidence, not Production data certification**.
- **New source-backed privacy gap:** frontend/src/app/app-api/v1/[...path]/route.ts explicitly forces private no-store/noindex for wallet/privacy/work/support/collections/notifications/account-security and shop/purchases, but **not** shop/holdings. The gateway forwards upstream Cache-Control and X-Robots-Tag if present, so a cacheable upstream response is not independently overridden for owner holdings; this is a conditional exposure risk, NOT proof of Production caching/indexing. frontend/src/app/inventory/page.tsx also lacks explicit robots noindex metadata. Require Android/app-api holdings success AND error 400/401/403/503 header regression with upstream deliberately returning public,max-age=3600; assert private no-store/noindex at the final HTTP edge. Treat as release-blocking privacy gate #2012 and LIFECYCLE1516-08/-09, not as a proven incident.
- **Alternatives:** A) reuse the existing owner read and show validated details on each card (selected V1: no new DB/API contract or financial writes); B) new lifecycle table or notification scheduler (reject: duplicate state, privacy and migration risk); C) auto-pay/settle CTA (reject: finance release gate incomplete). This is **not** F675-184 search/sort, #1977 false-empty remediation, shop purchases, or upkeep settlement. No new feature-specific table, endpoint, SQL migration, index, webhook, push notification or money movement in V1.

### Member journey, screen, actions and state model
- **Purpose / audience:** authenticated, consent-current owner can understand what an owned item is, whether its holding/effect has an expiration timestamp, whether its upkeep is reported overdue, and whether the source reports suspension. Avoid unexpected confusion without claiming certified billing or automatically charging WLD.
- **Entry:** `/inventory` existing item card → labelled `View condition / 상태 자세히` disclosure (button with `aria-expanded`, not an equip action) → in-place details for that item. Keep existing category tabs, equipped avatar preview, quantity and equip/un-equip action intact. If F675-184 search is later enabled, details open on its paginated results without replacing the independent equipped summary. No member UUID in route/query; no public deep link to personal holdings.
- **Fields:** distinct `holdingExpiresAt` from `expires_at` and `effectExpiresAt` from `effect_expires_at` (both ISO UTC, localized for display in Asia/Seoul); `durable` is a catalog maintenance classification, not a promise of lifetime ownership; `weekly_cost` is the source-reported aggregate for held quantity, `unpaid_weeks` nonnegative integer, `arrears_due` and `arrears_cap` exact nonnegative integer WLD strings, `suspended` explicit boolean. Show `Not applicable` only for an explicitly verified non-durable item; absent/null/malformed fields are `UNKNOWN`, never 0, paid, permanent, or active by inference. A null holding expiry means `No holding end date recorded`, not `permanent`. A null effect expiry means `No active effect-expiry receipt reported`, not proof of a permanent effect. A reported suspension is displayed regardless of arrears number; never infer suspension from arrears alone.
- **States:** initial closed, loading, VERIFIED_WITH_DETAILS, VERIFIED_NO_UPKEEP, VERIFIED_UNKNOWN_FIELDS, SUSPENDED, REFRESH_REQUIRED, AUTH_EXPIRED 401, CONSENT_REQUIRED 403, SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE 503 (Retry), MALFORMED_RESPONSE (Retry), VERIFIED_OWNER_EMPTY, account switch/logout (discard stale data), OFF (legacy cards). If existing GET fails, never display `0 items` or a successful lifecycle badge. Do not invent expired holdings history: the source function filters expired held rows out. Time-sensitive status must be labelled `as reported when refreshed`; a client clock is display-only and cannot certify current server state. Refresh on focus or explicit CTA; no countdown that claims guaranteed active status.

### API, data boundary, types, consistency and finance
- **Reuse exact existing API:** Nest `GET /api/v1/shop/holdings` returns `{holdings: ShopHoldingRow[]}`; Next server-only BFF calls the same route; Android existing app-api forwarding contract must be tested rather than assuming it supports a new path. No new endpoint or DB table. Additive UI parsing may require a shared runtime schema/DTO update, but it must preserve legacy clients and reject an unverified payload rather than silently coercing it to empty.
- **Validation:** UUID catalog_id, bounded strings, positive safe-integer quantity, valid UTC timestamp with offset and range, explicit booleans, nonnegative safe-integer `unpaid_weeks`, canonical decimal integer strings for `weekly_cost`, `arrears_due`, `arrears_cap` (use BigInt for comparison only, never JS Number or floating WLD arithmetic). Nullability from actual source is respected; cap response bytes/rows and protect rendering of `preview_data`/CSS from injection. Any invalid item field cannot be silently presented as authoritative; either item-level `UNKNOWN` with clear explanation if base identity is valid or whole-response `MALFORMED_RESPONSE` if ownership/list shape cannot be trusted.
- **DB authority:** migration 123 `shop_my_items_v2(p_actor)` uses owner predicate `held.user_id=p_actor`, quantity >0, and expiry `clock_timestamp()`; its effect receipt query uses a **second** `clock_timestamp()`, so holding/effect boundaries are not a single pinned instant. Do not claim atomic as-of lifecycle certification. If consistent expiry is later required, design a reviewed additive owner-scoped `SECURITY DEFINER` function with one DB `asOf` and forward-only migration, fixed search_path and PUBLIC revoke; coordinate B migration numbering and run effective app-role/cross-owner negative tests. V1 does **not** need such migration. Passing `p_actor` is not DB identity proof: Nest must bind actor to verified session and consent; never grant direct app SELECT/DML as a workaround (#993).
- **Transactions:** V1 is read-only, no explicit write transaction or idempotency key, no account balance/ledger mutation. Existing purchase, equip, consume and upkeep settlement POSTs remain outside this feature. In particular do **not** attach an upkeep payment CTA or claim a settled receipt until independent ledger/finance certification. A 200 GET is not proof that arrears were paid. Avoid cross-request snapshot claims; refresh after equip/consumption and account switch.

### Security, operations, SEO, mobile, accessibility and analytics
- **Access/security:** authenticated+consented owner only, no arbitrary actor param, no direct table read, no PII/owner inventory in logs, telemetry or URLs, CSRF unchanged for existing equip POST, bounded read rate and statement timeout, safe error DTO. No elevated admin item access. Review effective ACL and five migration checksums (#993), clean-schema parity (#2000), and Dev C bond read ACL #2022 separately.
- **Private HTTP contract:** `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` on success and 400/401/403/503 at **actual public edge**, not merely Next metadata; #2012 is unresolved. Exclude all personal inventory URLs and filters from sitemap. Android must discard in-flight results on logout/account change, forbid disk cache, validate equivalent fields and support offline/retry without stale verified status.
- **UX/a11y:** 320px/200% zoom, 44px targets, keyboard disclosure/close, visible focus, `aria-controls`/`aria-expanded`, semantic `dl` for values, status announcements `aria-live=polite`, screen-reader text distinguishing holding versus effect, no color-only suspended signal, reduced motion, ko/en copy. Do not block equip controls with an expanded disclosure.
- **Admin and KPI:** aggregated-only (minimum cohort 20 distinct owners) adoption, detail-open %, unknown-field rate, 401/403/503, false-empty prevented, source parity, accessibility defects, p95 page load. No per-owner inventory, exact arrears amount, search terms or serial numbers. Targets: 0 false paid/permanent claims, 0 cross-owner exposure, 0 WLD writes, 0 unknown-as-zero, no regression to existing equip flow; other conversion numbers remain `TEST TARGET` until measured.

### P0/P1 delivery backlog and gates
- **P0 OPS1516-01 (#1984):** prove Production executable SHA/digest equals edge/public identity, immutable rollback path, failure-independent restore before restart or promotion.
- **P0 SEC1516-02 (#993/#2000/#2022):** DEV app-role effective ACL, applied migration checksum mismatch and fresh-install schema/read parity; avoid direct grants, preserve B migrations.
- **P0 FIN1516-03 (#1976/#1735):** reconcile Treasury/MINT/SWF provenance, exploratory vs canonical balance results and shop upkeep settlement before any payment CTA.
- **P0 REL1516-04 (#2012):** remediate failing full suites, prove edge privacy, full CI, official Test frontend/backend/edge/Android exact candidate SHA and independent backup restore.
- **P1 LIFECYCLE1516-05 (B/QA):** source/DB DTO parity and owner ACL in isolated PostgreSQL 17.11; exact null, suspension and clock-boundary oracle; no schema change unless proven necessary.
- **P1 LIFECYCLE1516-06 (A):** runtime parser, safe integer-WLD handling, unavailable-vs-empty distinction, invalid payload negatives.
- **P1 LIFECYCLE1516-07 (A/C):** responsive accessible lifecycle disclosure, separate holding/effect fields, truthful arrears/suspension, preserve equipped avatar and existing equip flow.
- **P1 LIFECYCLE1516-08 (C):** Android app-api parity, no disk cache, generation-fenced actor/session change and offline states.
- **P1 LIFECYCLE1516-09 (A/B):** actual-edge noindex/no-store/privacy, aggregate admin KPI and feature-flag OFF/staff cohort.
- **P1 LIFECYCLE1516-10 (QA):** isolated PG 17.11 owner/foreign-owner ACL; 10,001 synthetic holdings for payload/performance, 100 malformed/absent/clock-edge, 100 account-switch, 100 concurrent refresh, 50 suspended/upkeep transitions, 50 Android parity, 0 financial/item writes, equip regression and OFF rollback. **Proposed tests, NOT RUN.**

**Current blockers:** QA v1392 worktree exists but final signed result not verified. Earlier QA v1391 reported backend 119 FAIL / 2,563 PASS and frontend 53 FAIL / 2,607 PASS; Dev C v1197 local candidate separately reports 106 backend FAIL / 2,576 PASS, 53 frontend FAIL / 2,619 PASS, not a current-main full-suite certification. Public Production `/api/version` reports `94d38f47603e99aabc42fc8f292b89441afb9663`; official Test reports `c2129625aa6629c616fb31ce790950618b8d92b2`; neither is current main. Existing #1984 runtime mismatch and #993 ACL remain OPEN. These observations do not establish a Production breach, financial loss or new release approval.

**Rollout and rollback:** docs-only planning branch stacked on #2018; implementation on separately owned A/B/C/QA branches and isolated Test Docker. Flag `inventory_lifecycle_details` default OFF, staff-only after verified focused/full CI, official Test exact-SHA signed user flows, DB ACL, edge/mobile/a11y, backup/restore, financial signoff; then small cohort and monitored broad release. Abort on any false-verified lifecycle state, ACL, privacy, finance or performance regression. Roll back flag OFF and previous verified app SHA without deleting or rewriting inventory, migrations or WLD.

**External independent reference gate:** newly evidence-approved **0**, cumulative independently sourced/deduplicated/classified approvals **UNKNOWN / 10,000**, **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**; never count synthetic fixtures, search-result totals or duplicates as verified references. No runtime implementation, database write, ledger movement, Test/Production deployment or main merge in planning.


## v2026.10.30.1514 — F675-184 Inventory Find/Sort Source-Equivalence and Safe Pagination (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Previous / Current / Next Cycle Type:** Feature Addition v1512 / Feature Improvement v1514 / Feature Addition v1516 (provisional). Authority origin/main `9da6545c4ff4809b5bd84ab6a28b9d2f1db74cef` checked at start and midpoint on 2026-10-30; parent Draft #2014 (unmerged, stacked on #2008), engineering handoff #2013. English canonical; Korean counterpart meaning-equivalent. This is a documentation-only hardening overlay on F675-184, **not** a second inventory search feature, table, endpoint or feature flag. No implementation or Production authorization.

### Source-backed correction and product acceptance
- **Actual source:** `frontend/src/app/inventory/page.tsx` calls `apiOrNull('/api/v1/shop/holdings')` and turns null into `[]`; `inventory-view.tsx` derives the equipped showcase, category counts and visible cards from the **entire** holdings array. A 20-row search page cannot replace that array without truncating equipped cosmetics and lying about totals. The default-OFF fallback still has a false-empty failure mode; coordinate the independent C #1977 fix rather than claim this feature repairs the old route.
- **Database oracle:** `packages/database/migrations/123-store-read-models-v2.sql:80-145` uses `public.shop_my_items_v2(p_actor)`, `held.quantity > 0`, unexpired `held.expires_at`, catalog join, arrears and effect receipts. It orders `acquired_at DESC, catalog_id ASC`. It invokes `clock_timestamp()` separately for holding eligibility and effect receipt expiry, so an arbitrary application-clock cutoff or reused HTTP cursor timestamp is **not automatically source-equivalent** near expiry. `packages/database/migrations/071-shop-catalog-schema.sql:14` defines `user_items PRIMARY KEY(user_id,catalog_id)` and `acquired_at NOT NULL`. Preserve exactly one row per catalog ID; never invent stack/serial pagination rows.
- **Decision:** V1 server-bounded owner search is retained, but replace initial unbounded holdings **only after** an independently owner-scoped, bounded equipped-summary/read model is implemented and verified against the existing full-list showcase (frame/background/effect/nameplate/title/badge slots). Otherwise keep existing page behind OFF and block rollout. Counts must be either explicitly verified owner-wide aggregates in the same read snapshot or labelled `items on this page`; never show `holdings.length` as total when it is page-sized. Existing equip POST remains separately authorized and may invalidate search/summary together.
- **Source parity oracle:** In isolated PG17.11, compare the old `shop_my_items_v2(actor)` catalog ID set, quantity, equipped, rarity, expiry, arrears, effect and preview fields against new V1 at a controlled single DB timestamp. For clock/expiry boundaries use a deterministic injected asOf in a *new* forward-only function and separately test behavior against the legacy oracle immediately before/at/after expiry; document any intentional behavior difference and block until product approval. Do not rewrite migration 123 or silently freeze expired entitlements.

### Entry, states, UX and contract (no second feature)
- Authenticated/consented member enters `/inventory`. Find (2–40 Unicode code points when nonempty), Search/Clear, existing category tabs, rarity filter from **actual** catalog enum values, equipped all/yes/no, Recent/Oldest/Name A–Z. Literal substring, NFC-normalized query and stored comparison policy, escape SQL LIKE %, _ and backslash, reject controls/duplicate or unknown params. Query is not a global catalog search. Keep avatar showcase independent of paginated search and preserve equip actions, focus, Korean/English copy, 320px/200% zoom, 44px touch, WCAG 2.2 AA and no keyboard focus theft.
- States distinguish verified owner-empty, verified filter-empty, loading, invalid 400, auth 401/consent 403, source changed 409 (Refresh), cursor expired 410 (Restart), throttled 429, DB/source failure 503 (Retry), logout/account switch and offline. **No `apiOrNull -> []` success fallback**, no stale equipped preview after actor switch, no aggregate count inferred from 20/50 rows. Search itself is GET-only, no inventory/ledger mutations.

### Exact API, DB, time and pagination gates
- Existing proposed endpoint only: Nest `GET /api/v1/shop/holdings/search`, Next server-only BFF and Android `GET /app-api/v1/shop/holdings/search`. DTO `{items: HeldItem[],pageSize,hasMore,nextCursor,sourceStatus:'VERIFIED',asOfUtcMicros,equippedSummaryStatus}`; full equip summary may be separate authenticated bounded read, but not inferred from a search page. Response header `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` on success and error; no indexed search URLs, raw q, owner UUID or cursor in analytics/logs.
- PostgreSQL forward-only function `public.shop_my_items_search_v1` must use pinned `asOfUtcMicros` **once per request**, one READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ transaction for initial page and summary, fixed search_path, fully qualified relations, `SECURITY DEFINER` reviewed owner, revoke PUBLIC EXECUTE, app role EXECUTE only, no direct SELECT/DML on holdings/catalog/arrears/receipts. `p_actor` is not database identity authentication: Nest verified session+consent actor binding and effective-role negative ACL tests are mandatory; DEV direct `user_items` SELECT/DELETE is currently reported allowed (#993), not Production evidence. No new table, no applied SQL rewrite, index only if EXPLAIN/BUFFERS justifies it; coordinate migration number with B's active stack.
- **Total order:** Recent `acquired_at DESC, catalog_id ASC` (match migration 123); Oldest `acquired_at ASC, catalog_id ASC`; Name `lower(name) COLLATE "C" ASC, catalog_id ASC`. Encode comparator direction, NULL policy (acquired_at nonnull), catalog UUID and exact PostgreSQL microseconds in a purpose-separated versioned HMAC cursor; never round-trip via JS Date milliseconds. Case-folded C collation is deterministic, **not** a promise of locale-aware Korean dictionary sorting. Prove duplicate-name, identical-timestamp and UUID-tie behavior. Cursor binds actor, session epoch, normalized filters, sort, asOf, full source-set fingerprint, last key, issued/expiry <=15m; mismatch/tamper -> 400, actor/session/source changed -> 409, expiry -> 410. No OFFSET; limit 20 default / 50 max with 50+1.
- **Expiry and consistency:** Holding and effect eligibility are time-dependent even without writes. Re-evaluate owner-scoped full source-set fingerprint including eligible membership, quantity, acquisition, catalog name/category/rarity, equipped slot/state, expiry, arrears/suspension and effect expiry; compare on every subsequent page. If any boundary expires, a write occurs or catalog metadata changes, return 409 and requery, never show an expired item as currently usable merely because cursor asOf was earlier. Cap cursor validity at the earliest relevant expiry boundary when practical; otherwise check and fail closed. MVCC snapshots do not survive between HTTP requests. If full scan exceeds seeded p95 <300ms target, BLOCK rollout and redesign revision mechanism rather than claim stable pages.
- No user-provided actor UUID or arbitrary SQL field names, no GET idempotency key, no equip/purchase bypass, per-actor rate limits, bounded statement timeout, bounded response bytes, safe error DTO, Android generated contract parity and cancellation/discard on account switch, no disk cache.

### Operations, admin, SEO, KPI, regression and release
- Admin has only cohort aggregates (>=20 distinct actors): search adoption, verified empty vs unavailable, 409/410/429/503, p95, false-empty prevented, equipped-summary mismatches and stale-session drops. No member search text or per-user holdings. SEO/private headers must be tested at **actual HTTP edge**, including 400/401/403/503, in light of #2012; do not equate Next metadata with response headers. Feature flag `inventory_find_sort` remains OFF.
- P0 unresolved: #1984 Production advertised SHA versus deleted-path processes/rollback provenance; #993 DEV app-role effective ACL and five applied migration checksum mismatches, #2000 clean-schema bank pocket drift; 552 Treasury-unlinked/MINT/SWF provenance and exploratory 24 vs canonical 0 balance mismatch; QA v1391 119 backend FAIL/2,563 PASS/495 SKIP and 53 frontend FAIL/2,607 PASS, private SEO #2012. QA v1392 worktree was observed but no newly authenticated final report for this run: **FINAL UNKNOWN**. No incident or successful release is inferred.
- **P0 4:** OPS1514-01 (#1984) executable SHA/edge/rollback assets and off-host restore before restart; SEC1514-02 (#993/#2000) effective ACL, checksum and forward-only schema parity; FIN1514-03 Treasury/MINT/SWF and discrepancy reconciliation in identical snapshot; REL1514-04 full failing-suite remediation, HTTP privacy, exact frontend/backend/edge/Android Test SHA, backup restore, independent signoff.
- **P1 6:** INVENTORY1514-05 B/QA controlled-time oracle + owner SQL/equip summary/EXPLAIN; INVENTORY1514-06 B typed API/HMAC microsecond cursor, 409 expiry/fingerprint and ACL; INVENTORY1514-07 A/C Next first-page/showcase separation, truthful counts/error states and WCAG; INVENTORY1514-08 C Android strict contract, session fencing, no disk cache; INVENTORY1514-09 A/C aggregate admin KPI, edge privacy/SEO, OFF rollout and non-destructive rollback; INVENTORY1514-10 QA PG17.11 source parity, 10,001 synthetic holdings, 100 cross-owner, 100 forged, 100 concurrent, 50 expiry/equip/catalog mutations, 30 duplicate names/microsecond/Korean cases, 50 malformed/unavailable, zero WLD/item writes.
- **Gate:** Tests are acceptance *proposals, NOT EXECUTED*. Development in separate B/A/C/QA branches and Test Docker; no overlapping code changes from planning. All CI, exact candidate SHA in official Test, independent restore, finance/ACL, edge/mobile/a11y and performance evidence required before canary OFF -> staff -> limited cohort -> broad. Rollback flag OFF and previous verified SHA without deleting holdings or indexes. External independently sourced, deduplicated, classified and evidence-approved new **0**; cumulative **UNKNOWN / 10,000**, **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD, no main merge or runtime/DB/WLD/Test/Production mutation.


## v2026.10.30.1512 — F675-184 Member Inventory Find & Sort (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Previous / Current / Next:** Feature Improvement v1510 / Feature Addition v1512 / Feature Improvement v1514 (provisional). Authority origin/main 9da6545c4ff4809b5bd84ab6a28b9d2f1db74cef rechecked at start and mid; stacked after planning Draft #2008 (which follows #2003/#1995/#1990/#1983); development handoff #2013; Draft PR #2014. English canonical; Korean meaning-equivalent. Documentation-only proposal, not an implemented or approved design. No separate distributed feature plan.

### Verified product gap, source and design alternatives
- Current frontend/src/app/inventory/page.tsx requires a member and fetches GET /api/v1/shop/holdings through apiOrNull. The client inventory-view.tsx has only category tabs, no keyword/rarity/equipped filter or acquisition/name sorting. It displays an empty inventory when the upstream returns null; this can misrepresent an outage as a genuine empty holding set. Current equip POST remains separate and is not part of this feature.
- Nest ShopController.holdings delegates the authenticated actor to ShopCatalogRepository.holdings, which reads public.shop_my_items_v2(actor) from migration 123. The SECURITY DEFINER function returns owner items with quantity > 0 and unexpired held rows, ordered by acquired_at DESC, catalog_id; it also exposes rarity, equipped, expiry, arrears and suspended status. Its app-role EXECUTE grant does not authorize direct table reads. Passing actor UUID alone is not independent DB identity authentication: Nest session and consent must bind the actor. This source does NOT prove Production row counts. A further READ ONLY DEV PostgreSQL 17.11 check found effective moneyverse_app direct SELECT=true and DELETE=true on public.user_items despite function EXECUTE=true (6 DEV rows, aggregate only). This contradicts intended least privilege and blocks rollout under P0 #993; it does NOT prove Production permissions or compromise.
- Alternative A, client-side filtering of the unbounded current holdings payload, is simple but cannot bound network/CPU for 10,001+ holdings and inherits the false-empty behavior. Alternative B, new search table/materialized index, creates unnecessary sync and migration risk. **Chosen V1 proposal: a bounded, read-only owner-scoped SQL function and typed search endpoint, with no new table and no financial mutation.** It depends on a forward-only migration sequenced after Backend B's existing 307–311 stack; no migration number is reserved here.

### Purpose, audience, permissions, entry and UX
- Purpose: help an authenticated, consent-current member find owned cosmetics/items without scanning an entire inventory. Entry /inventory, new labelled Find items search input, Search/Clear CTA, existing category tabs, rarity dropdown (all/common/uncommon/rare/epic/legendary/mythic only when represented by source), Equipped-only toggle and sort Recent first / Oldest first / Name A–Z. Preserve avatar preview and equip/un-equip actions; the search endpoint itself is GET-only and never equips, purchases, pays upkeep, mints or transfers WLD.
- Filter defaults: q empty, category all, rarity all, equipped all, sort recent, page size 20 (hard maximum 50). Search q is trimmed Unicode 2–40 code points when nonempty; literal substring matching, not regex; escape LIKE wildcard %, _ and backslash, reject control characters, duplicate parameters, unknown enum, oversize input and malformed UTF-8. Normalize filters identically on Nest/Next/Android; no hidden global catalogue results. Preserve input on 400 and provide a clear correction.
- States: initial (existing unfiltered view), loading, verified results, verified empty search (not zero owned), filter reset, in-flight account switch, source changed (409 with explicit Refresh), expired cursor (410 Restart), rate-limited (429), unavailable (503 with Retry), offline, unauthorized and consent expired. A source error must never become holdings=[] or a success-shaped count; do not silently mix stale equipped showcase with fresh search results. Page count and exact total are omitted unless verified in the same read transaction.
- Mobile/a11y: 320px layout, 200% zoom, 44px targets, WCAG 2.2 AA, visible labels, keyboard Enter search, tab/focus recovery after pagination and errors, aria-live status without focus theft, reduced motion, Korean/English strings, screen reader announces verified result count on current page. No autosubmit on each keystroke.

### API, DB invariants, pagination and concurrency
- Nest GET /api/v1/shop/holdings/search?q=&category=&rarity=&equipped=&sort=&limit=&cursor=; authenticated/consented actor only (SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard, ConsentGuard). Next private server BFF forwards allowlisted query keys through server-only API helper; Android GET /app-api/v1/shop/holdings/search uses existing authenticated gateway and generated contract parity. Return {items:[existing HeldItem DTO],pageSize,hasMore,nextCursor,sourceStatus:'VERIFIED',asOfUtc}; never return another owner's items or raw audit/financial details. Error codes 400 invalid, 401 unauthenticated, 403 consent, 409 changed source, 410 expired, 429 rate, 503 DB/source; foreign IDs are not accepted.
- DB: new reviewed forward-only public.shop_my_items_search_v1(p_actor uuid, p_query text, p_category text, p_rarity text, p_equipped boolean, p_sort text, p_limit integer, p_after_* cursor keys) SECURITY DEFINER, stable within one read-only REPEATABLE READ request, fixed search_path, schema-qualified relations, owner WHERE held.user_id=p_actor, quantity>0, expiry>asOf, same arrears/preview semantics as shop_my_items_v2. Revokes PUBLIC EXECUTE, grants app role EXECUTE only, reasserts no direct SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE on user_items, shop_catalog, shop_upkeep_arrears and receipts. No new table, foreign key, balance change or mutable transaction. Existing PK/FK/quantity and expiry constraints stay authoritative. Review indexes (user_id, acquired_at DESC, catalog_id DESC) and (user_id, catalog_id) plus catalogue category/rarity and lower(name) with EXPLAIN/BUFFERS on 10,001 seeded rows; only add justified nonduplicate indexes in coordinated forward migration.
- Stable keyset: recent/oldest uses acquired_at microseconds + catalog_id UUID; name uses lower(name) COLLATE C + catalog_id UUID, never locale-unstable OFFSET. HMAC-SHA256 cursor with purpose/version, owner, session generation, normalized filter/sort, asOf UTC microseconds, last keys, expiry <=15 minutes. Signature, actor, sort, filter or session mismatch -> 400/409; expired -> 410. HTTP requests do not share an MVCC snapshot. Re-evaluate an owner-scoped source-set revision/fingerprint for each page; changed eligible holdings/equip/expiry -> 409 REFRESH_REQUIRED rather than promise immutable results. If full fingerprint exceeds p95 300ms TARGET at 10,001 rows, block feature rollout and redesign revision strategy; never label an unverified page complete. Limit 50+1 and bounded SQL timeout/rate budget.
- No idempotency key or write transaction on GET; equip/purchase/upkeep retain their existing independently authenticated, CSRF/idempotency-protected write paths. New search must not bypass those guards. Android must cancel in-flight requests and discard responses on logout/account switch; no local disk caching.

### Admin, abuse, privacy, SEO, analytics and deployment
- Privacy: Cache-Control private,no-store and X-Robots-Tag noindex,nofollow plus no-referrer on success/errors, no sitemap/canonical search pages, no indexed private search query URLs. Confirm HTTP response headers, not only Next metadata, in light of Production private-route header contradiction #2012. Do not log q, item names, owner UUID, signed cursor or raw response; rate-limit per authenticated actor and bound query CPU, memory and payload. No administrator individual inventory search in V1.
- Admin KPI: aggregate-only weekly adoption, verified search completion, empty-result rate, 400/409/410/429/503, p95 latency, filter usage and account-switch stale-response drops, suppressed for cohorts below 20 distinct members; no raw query text or member identifiers. p95 <300ms, no cross-owner disclosures and zero ledger writes are acceptance TARGETS, not observed measurements.
- Feature flag inventory_find_sort defaults OFF. Developer B/C/A/QA use separate branch/test Docker, coordinate SQL numbering and contract. Test exact candidate SHA for frontend/backend/edge/Android with authenticated owner E2E, all required CI, security, negative grants, independent backup restore and finance invariants before approval. Rollout OFF -> staff cohort -> limited cohort -> monitor -> broader only after sign-off; rollback OFF preserves existing category tabs/equip flow and does not delete holdings, receipts or indexes. Production promotion is BLOCKED until all P0 gates pass; planning never deploys.

### P0/P1 developer backlog — 4 P0 + 6 P1
1. **P0 OPS1512-01** — #1984 Production advertised SHA 94d38f4 vs old deleted process path; prove executable/edge manifest, rollback artifacts and independent restore before restart or release.
2. **P0 SEC1512-02** — #993 broad DEV app-role DML, five applied checksum mismatches, clean-schema bank pocket #2000, notification DDL #1693, B 307–311 migration stack. Do not grant direct table privileges or rewrite applied migrations.
3. **P0 FIN1512-03** — Verify 552 Treasury-unlinked rows, MINT/SWF provenance and exploratory 24 vs canonical 0 balance mismatches under identical snapshot/scope; never infer financial settlement from inventory read.
4. **P0 REL1512-04** — QA v1391 backend 119 FAIL / 2,563 PASS / 495 SKIP, frontend 53 FAIL / 2,607 PASS, full audit nonzero, #2012 private HTTP robots; full CI, exact SHA official Test, independent restore, finance/security/edge gates.
5. **P1 INVENTORY1512-05** — B/QA source-equivalent owner-scoped SQL search function, stable keyset, conditional indexes, negative ACL/owner tests, EXPLAIN, migration number coordination after #2011.
6. **P1 INVENTORY1512-06** — B typed Nest GET, query allowlist, HMAC cursor and 400/401/403/409/410/429/503 fail-closed source/error contract, resource limits.
7. **P1 INVENTORY1512-07** — A/C Next inventory Find/Clear/rarity/equipped/sort, preserved category/equip flows, verified-empty vs unavailable, keyboard/a11y/320px.
8. **P1 INVENTORY1512-08** — C Android gateway and generated API contract, no-store/noindex, account-switch cancellation, stale response suppression and no disk cache.
9. **P1 INVENTORY1512-09** — A/C privacy-safe admin cohort >=20 KPI, HTTP robots/cache headers, no query/UUID logging, default OFF and reversible staged rollout.
10. **P1 INVENTORY1512-10** — QA isolated PostgreSQL 17.11: >=10,001 seeded holdings, 100 cross-owner, 100 forged cursors, 100 concurrent, 50 item/equip/expiry changes, 30 Korean/locale/time edge, 50 invalid/503; no ledger/item writes, official exact-SHA Test and rollback proof.

**Acceptance PROPOSED, NOT RUN.** Confirm source-equivalent item visibility, unexpired boundary, correct rarity and stable microsecond ordering, source-change 409, invalid/missing vs verified empty, cross-owner denial, HTTP privacy headers and default-OFF fallback. External independent references newly evidence-approved 0; cumulative deduplicated/approved UNKNOWN / 10,000 => BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. No runtime/DB/WLD/ledger/Test/Production change; DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD.


## v2026.10.30.1510 — F675-183 Weekly Quest Evidence Canonical-Parity Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Previous / Current / Next:** Feature Addition v1508 / Feature Improvement v1510 / Feature Addition v1512 (provisional). Main SHA 9da6545c4ff4809b5bd84ab6a28b9d2f1db74cef rechecked start/mid. Docs-only successor to Draft #2003, stacked on #1995 → #1990 → #1983; existing handoff #2002; Draft #2008. Authority main AGENTS.md, README.md, English canonical and Korean equivalent. No separate feature planning file.

### Verified gaps against migration 101 — parity gate before implementation
- The canonical public.early_game_weekly_goals(p_actor) in packages/database/migrations/101-early-game-progress.sql:437–566 computes the KST week with clock_timestamp(), but applies only created_at >= v_from to receipts and ledger transactions: **no next-Monday upper bound and no shared asOf cutoff**. The proposed [Monday, next Monday) detail can disagree for future-dated/backfilled rows or week rollover. Require same-transaction oracle equality for all four metrics; if the stricter bound is desired, update canonical and detail together through a separately reviewed forward-only migration. Never rewrite applied 101.
- Current job is highest-level user_job_progress ordered by level DESC, job_type (enum tie-break), NOT selected_at or job at receipt time. Distinct work counts DISTINCT assignment.task_id; job work counts paid work_reward_receipts whose task job equals the current highest-level job. A grouped task evidence row is not one receipt; changing job rank may change old weekly progress without a new receipt.
- Spend is only USER_CASH credit postings for SHOP_CATALOG_PURCHASE, SHOP_PURCHASE, BUSINESS_PURCHASE, CONSUMPTION_EVENT. Savings is USER_BANK debit minus credit for BANK_DEPOSIT and BANK_WITHDRAW, clamped with GREATEST(net,0). The canonical 101 query does not explicitly filter accounts.currency='WLD'; a new WLD-only detail must prove parity or coordinate a canonical change. A 50-row page cannot be summed into the full progress.
- NPC affinity is lifetime MAX(npc_relationships.affinity), self_reported=true: pressing the NPC order button is not a verified delivery. Return AGGREGATE_ONLY / SELF_REPORTED, no invented receipt, event time or verified-weekly badge.

### Corrected owner-only V1 contract
- **Purpose, audience, entry:** Authenticated/consented member can explain their own current quest board, never claim reward or certify settlement. /quests card CTA View evidence → /quests/weekly-evidence?goal=<active-catalog-code>&week=<current-KST-Monday>. NPC uses the week parameter only as board context and prominently labels its window lifetime. Feature switch quest_weekly_evidence default OFF; no historical archive, exports, cross-owner or administrator receipt browsing.
- **Snapshot and state:** Obtain DB KST week and asOfUtc once. In one read-only REPEATABLE READ transaction compare canonical 101 goal_code, metric, window, target, progress, self_reported and the new detail aggregate. Only identical results earn sourceStatus=CANONICAL_MATCHED; otherwise 503 PARITY_UNVERIFIED without success-shaped zero/rows. Week rollover yields 409 WEEK_CHANGED + Refresh. An MVCC snapshot cannot be held across HTTP pages.
- **Cursor:** Versioned purpose-separated HMAC-SHA256 (15m) binds actor, session epoch, goal, week, formula/catalog revision, current highest-level job, asOfUtc microseconds, full eligible source-set fingerprint, stable last (timestamp, UUID/group-key) and expiry. 50+1 keyset, max 50; distinct-task groups need deterministic tie-breaks. Recompute fingerprint over ALL eligible rows, not only visible page. Backfill/deletion/job change → 409 EVIDENCE_CHANGED; expired → 410. If 10,001-row fingerprint exceeds p95 <300ms seeded Test target, BLOCK performance gate and redesign; never promise stable pages from cutoff alone.
- **API and UI:** Nest GET /api/v1/engagement/early-game/weekly-evidence and Android GET /app-api/v1/engagement/early-game/weekly-evidence. Typed result {goalCode,weekStartKst,windowKind,weekEndExclusiveUtc,metric,target,progress,selfReported,sourceStatus,evidenceStatus,asOfUtcMicros,rows,nextCursor}. Exact decimal strings for BIGINT/WLD and UTC microseconds. Rows are partial evidence, not additive totals. NPC returns no list/cursor. Typed 400 invalid, 401 auth, 404 foreign/missing indistinguishable, 409 changed, 410 expired, 429 rate, 503 source/parity/DB. Loading, verified empty, aggregate-only, changed, retry and unavailable must never fabricate a completed goal.
- **DB and abuse controls:** SECURITY DEFINER owner-scoped read, qualified relations, fixed search_path, revoke PUBLIC EXECUTE, minimum app EXECUTE, negative direct DML/SELECT tests, EXPLAIN/BUFFERS before indexes. p_actor argument alone is NOT DB identity proof: Nest session/consent actor binding and least privilege are required. No new table in V1. No private UUID/transaction logs, no-store/noindex/no-referrer on success and errors, session-switch cancellation and no Android disk cache, 320px/200% zoom, 44px touch, WCAG 2.2 AA, keyboard/focus recovery.
- **Admin and release:** Admin sees only anonymous aggregate KPI when >=20 distinct members: opens, aggregate-only, parity-fail, changed, 503, p95. No individual evidence or searchable member IDs. p95 <300ms is a TARGET, NOT MEASURED. Separate developer branch/test Docker; full CI, exact-head official Test frontend/backend/edge authenticated E2E, independent restore and finance/security gates. Default OFF → limited cohort → observe → OFF rollback without ledger/data deletion. Planning never deploys.

### P0/P1 backlog — 4 P0 + 6 P1
1. **P0 OPS1510-01** — Preserve Production runtime/rollback: edge /api/version 94d38f4 while #1984 reports deleted v1515 process paths; verify binary/symlink/manifest/edge and independent restore before restart.
2. **P0 SEC1510-02** — Investigate 5 applied checksum mismatches, DEV broad app DML #993, clean-schema bank pocket #2000, notification DDL #1693, and B migration 307–310 stack; no ad-hoc grants or applied SQL rewrite.
3. **P0 FIN1510-03** — Reconcile 552 Treasury-unlinked entries, MINT/SWF provenance and exploratory 24 vs canonical 0 balance discrepancies on identical snapshot/scope before settlement claims.
4. **P0 REL1510-04** — QA v1390 backend 119 FAIL / 2,563 PASS / 495 SKIP; frontend baseline 53 FAIL; contract 41 PASS not release proof. Require full exact-SHA CI, official Test, independent restore and finance/auth/security/edge gates.
5. **P1 QUEST1510-05** — B/QA isolated PostgreSQL oracle for four detail metrics vs canonical 101 in one REPEATABLE READ transaction; forward-only joint correction if week bound/currency semantics change.
6. **P1 QUEST1510-06** — B owner EXECUTE-only SQL, active catalog, highest-level job tie, exact spend allowlist/bank clamp, task grouping, full source fingerprint, negative ACL and EXPLAIN.
7. **P1 QUEST1510-07** — A/B Nest session/actor/consent binding, strict DTO, current KST week, versioned HMAC cursor and 400/401/404/409/410/429/503 fail-closed parity errors.
8. **P1 QUEST1510-08** — A/C Next CTA/detail: weekly vs lifetime NPC, partial-page and non-additive explanation, loading/error/retry, 320px/200%/WCAG AA/keyboard/focus.
9. **P1 QUEST1510-09** — C Android contract/header parity, session-switch cancellation/no disk cache; admin >=20-member anonymized KPI, privacy/noindex and default-OFF rollout.
10. **P1 QUEST1510-10** — QA isolated PostgreSQL 17.11: >=10,001 synthetic, 100 cross-owner, 100 forged cursor, 100 concurrent, 50 backfill/job changes, 30 KST boundaries, 50 malformed/timeouts; oracle parity, zero ledger writes, exact-SHA Test/rollback.

**Acceptance proposed, NOT RUN:** Include future-dated rows, highest-level job ties, bank net-withdrawal clamp, cross-currency records, lifetime NPC and 50-row partial totals. External independent references: newly evidence-approved 0, cumulative UNKNOWN / 10,000, BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. QA v1390 evidence is not release certification. DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD.


## v2026.10.30.1508 — F675-183 Member Weekly Quest Evidence Breakdown (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Previous / Current / Next Cycle Type:** Feature Improvement v1506 / Feature Addition v1508 / Feature Improvement v1510 (provisional). Start and mid-run origin/main `9da6545c4ff4809b5bd84ab6a28b9d2f1db74cef`; planning-only stacked on unmerged Draft #1995 → #1990 → #1983. [P0/P1 handoff #2002](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/2002). Canonical requirements: AGENTS.md, README.md, this English PROJECT_PLAN.md; Korean equivalent PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md.

### Product rationale, existing evidence and non-overlap
- **Observed:** `frontend/src/app/quests/page.tsx` renders four early-game weekly goal cards from `GET /api/v1/engagement/early-game`. `packages/database/migrations/101-early-game-progress.sql` computes goals from verified work_reward_receipts, eligible ledger postings and NPC relationship affinity, but the UI exposes no drilldown showing why a weekly figure moved. This is separate from wallet transaction detail F675-182, calendar range F675-181, saved filters and Android banking pocket read work; no code is implemented in this planning cycle.
- **Purpose/target:** Give authenticated, consented members a transparent read-only explanation of their own current-week quest progress without allowing self-awarded rewards or creating an alternate financial balance. Success: members can trace contributing activity, distinguish aggregated from receipt-level evidence, and never see fabricated empty or zero progress on API failure.
- **V1 scope:** Current KST Monday–Sunday week only; no historical archive, exports, reward claims, transaction changes, profile disclosure or administrator access to individual receipts. Four metric kinds: `distinct_work_tasks` (unique work task IDs, not raw receipt count), `job_work_completions` (verified work receipts matching the user's selected job rule), `spend_or_save` (eligible cash spending + max(0, bank deposits minus withdrawals), WLD exact integer), and `npc_affinity` (current relationship aggregate only, no invented events). Preserve the exact eligibility/type allowlist in migration 101; do not treat gambling, stock buys or arbitrary debits as consumption.

### Permissions, entry, screens and CTA
- **Entry:** On `/quests` weekly goal cards, a visible `근거 보기 / View evidence` CTA links to `/quests/weekly-evidence?goal=<catalog-code>&week=<YYYY-MM-DD>`. Server-side `requireMember` and session/consent verification precede all data access; feature switch `quest_weekly_evidence` default OFF. Only catalogued goal codes; no raw SQL identifiers or arbitrary dates.
- **Screen:** Goal label, canonical KST week range, target/progress as exact decimal strings, metric explanation, evidence source and limitation, bounded list of contributing rows (timestamp, category, safe label, signed contribution where meaningful), and Back/Retry/Next page. For distinct-work counts, show unique task grouping; do not add row amounts to infer the total. For spend-or-save, show eligible spending and signed bank deposits/withdrawals, net-clamp explanation; no full counterparty identities. For NPC affinity show `AGGREGATE_ONLY`, no receipt list and a truthful note.
- **States:** LOADING, READY_WITH_ROWS, VERIFIED_EMPTY, AGGREGATE_ONLY, EVIDENCE_CHANGED (refresh required), INVALID_REQUEST, UNAUTHENTICATED, UNAVAILABLE and RATE_LIMITED. A failed read never renders zero, success or a reward receipt. On an expired cursor, offer Refresh without silently dropping filters. 320px mobile, semantic table/list, focus recovery, 44px controls, keyboard and screen-reader status announcements, WCAG 2.2 AA.

### API, database, exactness and transaction boundaries
- **API:** `GET /api/v1/engagement/early-game/weekly-evidence?goal=<code>&week=<date>&cursor=<opaque>` and Android parity `GET /app-api/v1/engagement/early-game/weekly-evidence`. Success: `{goalCode, weekStartKst, weekEndExclusiveUtc, metric, target, progress, sourceStatus, evidenceStatus, asOfUtc, rows, nextCursor}`. Money/counts serialized as canonical base-10 strings; timestamps retain PostgreSQL microseconds. Explicit typed 400 invalid goal/week, 401 unauthenticated, 404 not found/foreign indistinguishable, 409 EVIDENCE_CHANGED, 410 expired cursor, 429 throttled, 503 unavailable. No caching or success-shaped fallback. GET is read-only; all mutation methods rejected.
- **DB contract:** New reviewed `SECURITY DEFINER` owner-scoped read function(s) only if needed, schema-qualified relations, pinned search_path, PUBLIC EXECUTE revoked, only necessary app-role EXECUTE granted, no direct table DML/SELECT relaxation. `p_actor` is NOT an independent DB identity proof: Nest session binding and separately reviewed app-role least privilege are mandatory. No new table for V1; add index only after `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)` on a disposable seeded DB demonstrates need. Candidate indexes: work_reward_receipts(user_id,created_at,id), work_assignments(id,task_id), ledger_transactions(created_at,id), ledger_postings(account_id,transaction_id), account owner/type; check existing indexes before proposing migration.
- **Consistency:** Use a single read-only REPEATABLE READ DB transaction to calculate canonical summary and first page from one snapshot. Later pages bind actor, goal, KST week, as-of UTC, last key, expiry and a server-computed canonical source-row fingerprint in HMAC-signed cursor (15 min). Recompute fingerprint before later pages; any backfill, deletion, job-selection or NPC-affinity change returns 409 rather than silently mixing snapshots. Stable keyset ordering (`created_at DESC, stable UUID`); 50+1 per page, hard max 50; server must not hold a long-lived transaction between HTTP requests. If full fingerprint is too expensive, fail the performance gate and redesign before implementation; do not promise stable pagination without proof.
- **Correctness/security:** KST Monday 00:00 inclusive to next Monday 00:00 exclusive; no client clock trust, future week, or timezone ambiguity. Apply the same source cutoff and job selection as migration 101. DB BIGINT/NUMERIC never coerced through JS Number; protect UUIDs and raw identifiers in telemetry. Rate-limit by actor/session/IP, bounded statement timeout, input allowlist, deny cross-owner, no CSRF write path, anti-enumeration 404, no-store/noindex/no-referrer on success and errors. Android session-switch cancellation and private no-disk-cache parity.

### Operations, administration, KPI, release and rollback
- **Admin:** Aggregate-only feature adoption and failure rate, minimum 20 distinct members before displaying cohorts, no individual quest/ledger rows or searchable user IDs. Metrics: `quest_evidence_open_total`, `quest_evidence_verified_empty_total`, `quest_evidence_aggregate_only_total`, `quest_evidence_changed_total`, `quest_evidence_api_error_total`, `quest_evidence_p95_ms`. Proposed p95 <300ms on seeded Test is a target, not a measured result. No public SEO indexing; private routes use noindex/nofollow and private no-store.
- **Safe rollout:** Feature flag OFF until isolated PostgreSQL, security/contract/a11y/mobile, exact-SHA official Test frontend/backend/edge/auth and independent restore gates pass. Development in separate branch/worktree/test Docker; reviewed forward-only migration only if required, no applied checksum rewrite. Stage canary by member cohort, monitor 409/503/p95, rollback flag OFF and code to prior verified SHA without deleting evidence or ledger records. No planning-run runtime deployment.
- **Acceptance proposed, NOT EXECUTED:** >=10,001 synthetic receipts/postings, 100 cross-owner, 100 cursor tamper, 100 concurrent, 50 snapshot/backfill/late-commit races, 30 KST Monday/leap/year boundaries, 50 invalid/timeout responses, NPC aggregate-only, distinct-count non-additivity, net-withdrawal clamp, zero WLD/ledger mutation, full frontend/backend regressions and real Android gateway contract. All numbers are test design targets, not passed tests.

## v2026.10.30.1506 — F675-182 Wallet Detail Evidence Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Previous / Current / Next Cycle Type:** Feature Addition v1504 / Feature Improvement v1506 / Feature Addition v1508 (provisional). Main 9da6545c4ff4809b5bd84ab6a28b9d2f1db74cef checked start and midpoint. AGENTS.md, README, English canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean parity reviewed. Documentation-only successor stacked on unmerged Draft #1990 → #1983; existing engineering handoff #1988. No duplicate feature, runtime/DB/WLD/Test/Production writes.

**Source-backed design correction:** Existing wallet.repository.ts aggregates ledger postings by account.owner_user_id, but wallet.service.ts converts PostgreSQL timestamps through JS Date, losing microseconds. No detail route exists in main. Accounts currency is char(3), posting amount positive bigint. Detail SQL must explicitly filter owner USER_CASH/USER_BANK currency WLD, include historical frozen/closed accounts, sum signed NUMERIC exactly and return canonical integer strings. netDelta=cashDelta+bankDelta; zero net does not alone prove internal transfer. Only allowlisted authoritative bank-move type plus opposite pocket deltas may be called an internal move. Preserve original UTC microseconds separately from KST display. Type label must come from a safe server allowlist.

**Security limitation:** SECURITY DEFINER with p_actor does NOT independently authenticate the SQL caller. A compromised app role could invoke EXECUTE with another actor UUID without a separately proven DB-bound identity mechanism; DEV role also has direct ledger SELECT. Require Nest authenticated session/consent-to-actor binding, review auth_sessions id/user_id/expires_at/revoked_at and limits, independent least-privilege DB verification. A forgeable GUC is not an identity proof. Fixed search_path, qualified public tables, dedicated function owner, REVOKE EXECUTE FROM PUBLIC and reviewed app EXECUTE only. No new direct table grants. One statement snapshot; EXPLAIN existing indexes before new ones.

**Evidence semantics:** Successful owner ledger SELECT means row retrieval only, NOT globally reconciled postings, payment finality, certified receipt, live balance or verified settlement. Supersede ambiguous LEDGER_READ_VERIFIED with readStatus=READ_OK and reconciliationStatus=NOT_ASSESSED. UI says “원장 기록 조회됨 · 정산 검증서 아님”. Do not fabricate zero or counterpart data; inconsistent currency/amount/posting source must fail closed (503). Absent/foreign identical 404, malformed UUID 400, unauthenticated 401, throttled 429, unavailable 503.

**Member UX/API:** /wallet/activity “거래 상세 보기” to private /wallet/activity/transactions/:transactionId; Nest GET /api/v1/wallet/activity/transactions/:transactionId and Android /app-api/v1/wallet/activity/transactions/:transactionId. 200 strict DTO {transactionId,typeLabel,occurredAtUtcMicros,cashDelta,bankDelta,netDelta,currency:'WLD',readStatus:'READ_OK',reconciliationStatus:'NOT_ASSESSED'}. Validate unknown JSON, no URL actor, allowlisted Back filters, loading/empty-not-found/error/retry/copy/focus states. Session switching cancels stale requests. Private no-store, noindex, no-referrer on success and errors, no sitemap/ID logs. WCAG 2.2 AA, 320px, 200% zoom, 44px controls, semantic definition list and aria-live. Admin anonymous >=20-member cohort detail_open/unavailable/p95 KPI only. Default OFF wallet_transaction_detail_v1. Backward-compatible migration only by separate developer branch; feature-OFF rollback never deletes ledger/backups.

**QA v1388 (12:19–12:32 KST):** Production symlink v1523 vs live deleted v1515 PIDs reverified after 13:00; no confirmed user outage. DEV ACL 211 UPDATE/DELETE and 233 TRUNCATE of 253 tables; 5 checksums. Exploratory balance_mismatch=24 vs canonical 0 on 40 accounts is a probe discrepancy, NOT proof of tampering. Treasury 552 unlinked and Test synthetic 200. Backend 119 failed, frontend 53 failed; mobile contract 183 endpoints passed but full CI/Test/restore gates remain HOLD.
