## v2026.10.30.1504 — F675-182 Owner-Private Wallet Transaction Detail (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle and authority.** Previous Feature Improvement v1502 → Current Feature Addition v1504 → Next Feature Improvement v1506 (provisional). Latest `origin/main` `9da6545c4ff4809b5bd84ab6a28b9d2f1db74cef` checked at start and midpoint, with `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, `PROJECT_PLAN.md` English canonical and Korean parity. The v1502 Draft #1983 is unmerged; this proposal is stacked for documentation lineage only and must not be independently merged. [Engineering handoff #1988](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1988). No runtime/DB/WLD/Test/Production changes.

### User problem, scope and alternatives
- **Observed:** `frontend/src/app/wallet/activity/page.tsx` renders up to 50 `PostingStrip` rows but has no transaction detail CTA; Nest `wallet.controller.ts` exposes `GET wallet/activity` but no `wallet/activity/transactions/:id`; repository aggregates owner CASH/BANK postings and already returns transaction UUIDs. The wallet transfer action returns a transaction UUID, yet there is no unified owner-readable detail destination. Existing F675-176 saved filters, F675-177 monthly digest, F675-178 bookmarks and F675-181 custom date range are different features. No duplicate detail endpoint found in main.
- **Alternatives evaluated:** (A) expand each row inline (poor deep-link and mobile semantics); (B) export full ledger (unnecessary exposure); **(C) dedicated owner-only read-only detail (selected)**. It adds a small navigable surface without changing money or introducing unverified financial evidence.
- **Purpose/target:** an authenticated, consented member verifies the exact CASH/BANK deltas of one of their own transactions, including internal transfers whose net change is zero. Not an official tax document, payment certificate, audit attestation, or a statement of current balances.
- **Entry/CTA:** a keyboard-focusable “거래 상세 보기” action on each `/wallet/activity` row (and future bookmark/range results), linking to `/wallet/activity/transactions/{uuid}`; return to list preserving only validated filters, never trusting arbitrary redirect URLs. Transfer success may link to the same destination after verified receipt. No share-to-public or cross-member lookup.

### Member state machine and display contract
- Loading skeleton → verified detail; missing/other-owner → indistinguishable “거래를 찾을 수 없습니다” 404; malformed UUID → 400; signed-out → login gate 401; throttle → 429 with bounded retry; unavailable/invalid upstream → 503 with retry, **never** empty/zero or a fabricated “verified” receipt. Session switch clears owner-specific data before any fetch. Display transaction UUID, allow-copy ID button with accessible feedback, safe type label, KST timestamp with timezone, exact signed decimal WLD `cashDelta`, `bankDelta`, `netDelta`, and evidence status `LEDGER_READ_VERIFIED` / `UNVERIFIED` / `UNAVAILABLE` based on actual authoritative DB read. No invented counterpart, fees, tax, or sender name. Show “현금↔은행 이동은 순변동 0” when applicable. Unknown types display a neutral localized label.
- No raw counterparty user ID/name, system account IDs, ledger posting IDs, policy payload, idempotency key, request tokens, outbox or audit metadata. Read result is a point-in-time ledger view, not a live balance, and `createdAt` retains original PostgreSQL microseconds in a canonical UTC representation; UI localizes only for display.

### API, database, transaction and permission boundary
- **Nest:** `GET /api/v1/wallet/activity/transactions/:transactionId` with strict canonical UUID parsing and session/consent checks, 200 validated DTO `{transactionId,typeLabel,occurredAt,cashDelta,bankDelta,netDelta,currency:'WLD',evidenceStatus}`. Return exact decimal strings; `netDelta = cashDelta + bankDelta` as NUMERIC, no JS float. 400 malformed, 401 auth, 404 nonowner/absent, 429 rate limit, 503 DB unavailable; no 200 on failure. Denied/missing must have identical body and no existence oracle.
- **Database:** add one carefully audited `SECURITY DEFINER` owner-read function (separate migration via developer branch) with fixed `search_path`, `REVOKE ALL FROM PUBLIC`, `GRANT EXECUTE` only to app role; join ledger postings to owner `USER_CASH`/`USER_BANK` accounts by `owner_user_id` **not** `actor_user_id`, retain frozen/closed accounts' historical postings, group one transaction before returning. No direct new ledger SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE grants. Single SELECT/function invocation gives one PostgreSQL statement snapshot; no multi-query transaction needed. No new feature tables. Use existing transaction PK, posting `transaction_id` and account owner indexes; require EXPLAIN ANALYZE on isolated PG before adding indexes. Enforce `currency='WLD'`, signed NUMERIC and `net=cash+bank` invariant.
- **Security finding:** read-only DEV PostgreSQL 17.11 probe on 2026-10-30 confirms `moneyverse_app` has direct `ledger_transactions SELECT` and `audit_logs DELETE` privileges; this contradicts AGENTS.md's least-privilege contract and must be fixed in P0 #993 before feature release. It is NOT evidence of Production ACL or tampering.
- **Web/Android:** Next SSR route `/wallet/activity/transactions/[transactionId]`, runtime response-schema validation rather than `apiOrNull<T>`, safe error boundary, force-dynamic, no caching, noindex; Android app-api `/app-api/v1/wallet/activity/transactions/:id` must preserve session, status, headers and error semantics. The existing app-api gateway already preserves raw query and applies wallet private/no-store/noindex; test it, do not duplicate it. No service-worker/offline storage or persistent URL previews. CSRF not required for GET, but enforce Origin/CORS/session boundaries and ID enumeration rate limiting. Redact transaction UUIDs from telemetry and logs; prohibit private values in analytics.
- **Accessibility/mobile:** 320px minimum, 200% zoom, 44px target, WCAG 2.2 AA, focus-visible, semantic definition list, status announcements for copy/error, keyboard and screen reader tests, Korean labels with localization keys.
- **Admin/SEO/KPI:** admin gets only aggregated anonymized daily `detail_open`, `detail_unavailable`, `detail_retry` counts (minimum cohort threshold ≥20); no member-level transaction search. `Cache-Control: private, no-store` and `X-Robots-Tag: noindex` on success and all errors, no public sitemap, robots metadata, no query/referrer leak. KPI: validated detail open success ≥99% in healthy Test, unauthorized disclosure 0, zero WLD mutations; measured only after opt-in and consent.
- **Release:** `wallet_transaction_detail_v1` OFF by default. Separate developer branch and test-first RED→GREEN→REFACTOR. Deploy schema/function backward-compatibly and verify app-role grants on isolated PostgreSQL before enabling Test; Next/Android feature behind switch, staged Test exact frontend/backend/edge SHA with real auth/PG E2E, independent backup restore and rollback to feature OFF. Never enable Production or auto-promote until P0 gates and owner-approved GitOps. Rollback OFF hides CTA/route; DB function can remain inert until reviewed migration rollback; no deletion of ledger/backup data.

### P0 blockers (4) and P1 implementation handoff (6)
1. **P0 OPS1504-01:** QA v1387 [#1984](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1984): Production symlink v1523 `94d38f4...` advertises new SHA but actual backend/frontend PIDs 2608302/2608303 run from deleted v1515 directories; preserve evidence, independently recover rollback, prohibit restarts/prune/promotions.
2. **P0 SEC1504-02:** DEV app-role direct ledger SELECT/audit DELETE [#993](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/993), migration checksum mismatch 5 and sensitive MCP URL logs [#1972](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1972). Diagnose, no ad hoc production grants.
3. **P0 FIN1504-03:** MINT 18,000 WLD unverified provenance, 552 Treasury ledger links, SWF finance failures and Test synthetic Treasury HTTP 200 [#1917](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1917). Fail closed.
4. **P0 REL1504-04:** Full CI/lint/mobile contract, source-exact frontend+backend+edge official Test SHA, real PostgreSQL/auth E2E, independent restore and approved GitOps [#1979](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1979).
5. **P1 DETAIL1504-05:** SECURITY DEFINER function, owner SQL, privilege/index proof and exact decimal arithmetic.
6. **P1 DETAIL1504-06:** Nest controller/service/DTO, strict UUID/owner 404 parity, error/rate-limit contract.
7. **P1 DETAIL1504-07:** Next page, list CTA, schema validation, accessible success/error/return states.
8. **P1 DETAIL1504-08:** Android gateway parity, account switching, cache/noindex and logging checks.
9. **P1 DETAIL1504-09:** privacy, anonymized admin KPI, SEO, threat model and retention.
10. **P1 DETAIL1504-10:** isolated PostgreSQL and web/Android acceptance, exact-SHA Test, feature-OFF rollback.

**Acceptance criteria proposed, NOT RUN:** ≥10,001 synthetic ledger transactions; ≥100 cross-owner/forged IDs, ≥100 concurrent, ≥50 missing/frozen/closed cases, ≥30 cash↔bank net-zero, ≥50 malformed/503 responses, ≥30 account/session swaps, 0 unscoped data and 0 WLD/ledger writes. No fake VERIFIED state. External independently acquired/deduplicated/classified evidence: newly approved **0**, cumulative **UNKNOWN / 10,000**, gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. No runtime implementation or Production authorization.


## v2026.10.30.1502 — F675-181 Wallet Calendar-Range Evidence and Cursor Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle and authority.** Previous Feature Addition v1500 → Current Feature Improvement v1502 → Next Feature Addition v1504 (provisional). Latest `origin/main` `94d38f47603e99aabc42fc8f292b89441afb9663` rechecked at start and midpoint (2026-10-30 11:08–11:10 KST); v1500 PR #1974 **MERGED** at 11:08 KST, but the calendar-range runtime is **not implemented**. AGENTS.md, README.md, English canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md, Korean parity, Nest/Next/app-api, DEV PostgreSQL 17.11 and QA v1386 reviewed. Documentation-only; no runtime, database, ledger, WLD, Test or Production mutation.

### Source-backed corrections and scope
- `wallet.repository.ts:259–293` has owner CASH/BANK aggregation, direction `HAVING` before `LIMIT 51`, and keyset ordering `(created_at,id) DESC`, but only a lower `since` bound and a JS-Date `asOf`; there is **no exclusive calendar end**. `wallet.service.ts:393–414` converts `created_at` via `Date.toISOString()`, losing PostgreSQL microseconds. Existing `wallet-activity-cursor.ts` v1 expires at 60 minutes and is not a custom-range token.
- `frontend/src/app/wallet/activity/period.ts` silently normalizes unrecognized periods to `all`; `page.tsx` uses `apiOrNull<WalletHistoryPage>` (which collapses HTTP errors to null) and casts unvalidated JSON to a typed response. The page does distinguish null/unavailable from zero results, but **cannot distinguish 400/410/429/503** and may mishandle malformed 200. A strict custom-range path must not reuse that error-collapsing behavior.
- The current Android app-api proxy `frontend/src/app/app-api/v1/[...path]/route.ts` **already preserves the raw query string** (`target.search=request.nextUrl.search`) and forces wallet `private, no-store` plus `X-Robots-Tag: noindex`; do not create a redundant proxy or claim these headers are missing. Add allowlisted custom parameter validation, error/schema parity and session fencing instead.
- DEV PostgreSQL 17.11 read-only check found 10 indexes across accounts, ledger_transactions and ledger_postings, including `idx_ledger_transactions_created_at_id` and `idx_ledger_postings_transaction_id`. `moneyverse_app` has SELECT on ledger_transactions/postings **and** direct UPDATE on account_balances and DELETE on audit_logs (DEV only, P0). Reuse existing authorized read path or reviewed SECURITY DEFINER function; **never broaden app-role grants** or change applied migrations. Existing v1500 requirements remain in the integrated master; this section hardens them without creating a second feature.

### Implementable API, clock and SQL contract
- Keep member-only `GET /api/v1/wallet/activity?period=custom&from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD&direction=all|in|out|neutral&cursor=...`, existing 50+1 pages and legacy all/7d/30d unchanged. Explicitly reject duplicate/array/unknown query keys, invalid Gregorian dates, missing or mixed custom parameters, end before start, inclusive span >366 calendar days and future end relative to **the same authoritative DB KST date**. Do not silently fall back to all. Canonical deep links omit cursor when date/direction changes.
- The **first page** obtains one DB `statement_timestamp()` as a microsecond-preserving `asOf`, KST current date and the UTC half-open range `[from 00:00 KST, (to+1) 00:00 KST)` from the same SQL statement (or one pinned READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ client). Later pages reuse the signed first-page `asOf` and exact KST bounds; `created_at>=startUtc AND created_at<endExclusiveUtc AND created_at<=asOf`. Never compute a cursor bound through JS `Date` or 24h local arithmetic. Historical frozen/closed accounts remain visible to their owner; no status='active' history filter.
- Aggregate signed NUMERIC CASH/BANK postings **by transaction ID before** direction HAVING, keyset and `ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 51`. Preserve the exact `created_at` microsecond text from PostgreSQL (for example UTC `to_char(created_at AT TIME ZONE 'UTC','YYYY-MM-DD"T"HH24:MI:SS.US"Z"')`) in a separate cursor-only projection; retain ISO milliseconds for display if desired. Cursor SQL compares the original PostgreSQL timestamp, not a rounded display timestamp. Account transfers with zero net are neutral, not proof of an internal transfer; no double counting or WLD writes.
- New purpose-separated HMAC `wallet-activity-custom-v1` token binds owner UUID, authenticated session, range, direction, DB asOf, microsecond keyset timestamp and transaction UUID, version, issuedAt, expiry and key ID. TTL 15 minutes, max 2048 bytes, constant-time MAC and bounded key rotation. Invalid/signature/scope/legacy tokens → typed 400; expired → typed 410; never use cursor as authentication. No cross-request repeatable-read snapshot is claimed: late/backdated postings can change subsequent pages despite a fixed `asOf`; disclose this and offer restart, not a fictitious stable export.
- Next server component/BFF parses searchParams as `string|string[]|undefined` and rejects duplicates before API call. Replace `apiOrNull` for custom range with typed error handling; validate unknown HTTP 200 JSON (<=50 unique UUID transactions, canonical signed integer strings, cash+bank=net, valid timestamps, hasMore boolean, nextCursor iff hasMore, bounded range/asOf). Show verified-empty **only** for validated HTTP 200 with zero rows; distinct invalid/expired/throttled/unavailable/malformed/stale response states. Never show zero balance or no activity as a substitute for failure. Android generated EN/KO/JSON contract, account-switch cancellation, strict schema and 400/410/429/503 parity are release gates.
- UI: `/wallet/activity` date Apply/Clear/Next/Restart, preserved direction, KST inclusive labels, server asOf, no auto-query while editing, 320px/200% zoom/WCAG 2.2 AA, keyboard focus/aria-live/44px touch targets. ## v2026.10.30.1500 — F675-181 Owner-Private Wallet Calendar Range (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1498 → Current Feature Addition v1500 → Next Feature Improvement v1502 (provisional). **Authority:** origin/main a7f8730b9838b9324b6ece25e394dfcc8709aadf, rechecked start/mid on 2026-10-30; AGENTS.md, README.md, canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean parity. **GitHub:** [Draft #1974](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1974), [handoff #1973](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1973). Documentation-only proposal; no runtime/DB/WLD/Test/Production mutation. Prior v1498 Draft #1964 CLOSED without merge; preserve its integrated historical requirements.

### Problem, target and non-duplication
- Verified: frontend/src/app/wallet/activity/period.ts supports all/7d/30d and direction all/in/out/neutral; page.tsx displays owner ledger in pages of 50; backend/src/wallet/wallet.controller.ts rejects custom periods; wallet.service.ts computes rolling days; wallet.repository.ts aggregates owner CASH/BANK postings with SQL direction filter before LIMIT 51. The inspected path has no explicit calendar from/to. Source inspection is not a Production usage survey.
- Authenticated, consented members should inspect their own WLD transactions between two KST calendar dates. Private navigation only; not settlement certification, tax proof, export, financial mutation or automatic budget advice.
- Separate from F675-176 saved period presets, F675-177 monthly aggregation, F675-178 bookmarks, F675-173 privacy export and existing export-ledger-csv.tsx. No duplicate table, receipt, export or endpoint family.

### Entry, screen, CTAs and state machine
- Entry /wallet/activity; add “Custom dates / 기간 지정” beside existing periods. Canonical private deep link /wallet/activity?period=custom&from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD&direction=all. Existing all/7d/30d semantics remain unchanged.
- Native Start date/End date inputs, Apply range, Clear range, existing direction chips, Next 50, Restart from first. Suggest KST today and previous 29 calendar days, but do not query until Apply. Date/direction changes discard cursor; browser back restores canonical range.
- States: initial, editing, invalid, loading, verified-results, verified-empty, more-pages, expired-cursor, unauthenticated, consent-required, throttled, unavailable, stale-response-discarded. Empty only after schema-verified 200 with zero rows; network errors, malformed 200 or 503 must not masquerade as empty. Show inclusive KST dates, server as-of cutoff and warning that late/backdated postings can alter later reads.
- Strict Gregorian YYYY-MM-DD including leap days; start<=end, 1..366 inclusive calendar dates, end<=today KST. Invalid dates, arrays, duplicate or unknown params, non-custom with from/to and custom missing either date return 400. Do not silently truncate invalid ranges; 366 days is a proposed load cap.

### API, DB, transaction, index and cursor
- Extend guarded Nest GET /api/v1/wallet/activity with period=custom, from, to, direction=all|in|out|neutral and optional cursor. Preserve existing transactions,hasMore,nextCursor,asOf fields; custom adds range:{from,to,timezone:'Asia/Seoul',startInclusiveUtc,endExclusiveUtc}. Strict Next/Android runtime validation of amount strings, timestamps, arrays and cursor; verified empty has hasMore=false,nextCursor=null.
- Convert KST calendar days into UTC half-open [start 00:00 KST, day-after-end 00:00 KST) using timezone-aware database conversion (date::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Seoul'). Query created_at>=startUtc AND created_at<endExclusiveUtc AND created_at<=serverAsOf. Never use browser timezone or naive 24h offsets. Server asOf is a cutoff, not a fixed PostgreSQL snapshot across multiple HTTP pages.
- Reuse owner-scoped USER_CASH/USER_BANK joins and NUMERIC signed posting aggregation by transaction ID; net=cashDelta+bankDelta. Direction HAVING SIGN(net) before ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 51; return at most 50, derive hasMore from row 51. CASH↔BANK internal movement is neutral. Preserve historical postings on frozen/closed accounts. No JS Number for WLD, no ledger writes.
- No new persistent tables. EXPLAIN on isolated PostgreSQL checks indexes for ledger_transactions(created_at,id), ledger_postings(transaction_id,account_id), accounts(owner_user_id,account_type,id). Any extra index requires separately reviewed forward-only immutable-checksum migration, no new app-role direct DML grants. Proposed 2s statement timeout, subject to load evidence.
- Purpose-separated HMAC cursor wallet-activity-custom-v1 binds ownerId,sessionId,from,to,direction,asOf,afterCreatedAtUs,afterTransactionId,issuedAt,expiresAt,version; TTL 15m, <=2048 bytes, constant-time MAC, bounded key rotation. Preserve PostgreSQL microsecond timestamp text through keyset SQL; do not round via JavaScript Date milliseconds. Legacy cursor rejected for custom; malformed/forged/scope-changed 400, expired 410, UI restart offered. One read-only SQL statement preferred; if multi-statement, pin one PG client in READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ with rollback/release.

### Authorization, abuse, SEO, admin, Android and KPI
- Keep SessionGuard → AuthenticatedGuard → ConsentGuard → CsrfGuard. Owner identity only from server session, owner predicate inside SQL or reviewed SECURITY DEFINER function. No cross-owner existence oracle or raw SQL errors. Feature switch wallet_activity_calendar_range seeded disabled before gate; no automatic enable.
- Proposed 10 requests/minute/member, one in-flight custom query/member, 2s DB timeout, strict query allowlist. Cache-Control private,no-store, X-Robots-Tag noindex, Next robots noindex/nofollow, no sitemap/structured data. Do not log query strings, owner IDs, amounts, transaction IDs, raw cursors or sessions; consider QA v1385 MCP gateway URL logging [#1972](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1972).
- Admin sees aggregate-only cohort>=20 adoption, 7-day reuse, 200/400/410/429/503 rates, p50/p95 latency and timeouts; no member ledger or date drill-down. Proposed, not observed: zero cross-owner reads, zero false-empty, zero cursor scope bypass, p95<500ms under agreed load; rollback on violations.
- 320px, 200% zoom, WCAG 2.2 AA, explicit labels/errors, keyboard date Apply/Clear, error focus, aria-live result count, >=44px touch targets. Android app-api preserves custom query and strict schema, fences account switches and matches 400/410/429; regenerate EN/KO/JSON mobile API contract with negative tests.

- During v2026.10.30.1 production scheduling recheck, market timer active/running while executing its healthy oneshot could temporarily lose a next-trigger field. The base watchdog mistakenly sent CRITICAL unscheduled:running, despite later market.service MARKET_OK and valid next 2-minute calendar schedule. v2026.10.30.3 PR #1969 (source merge 7c4919d) correctly accepts transient active/running but separately monitors market.service Result as CRITICAL when the worker fails; active/elapsed and genuinely unscheduled failures remain CRITICAL.

Production Telegram bridge source hash matched merged GitHub source, market timer waiting with valid next 09:26 and market worker Result success, base bot CHECK_OK. Existing backend/frontend PIDs unchanged; no finance/database writes, no economic scheduler changes.

## v1482 / F675-176 — Wallet Saved Views: Verified History, Monotonic Generation and Replay Safety (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **Stacked Draft PR:** [#1920](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1920) — base #1916, unmerged; CI in progress at first check, not release certification.

**Cycle and authority:** Previous Feature Addition v1480 → Current Feature Improvement v1482 → Next Feature Addition v1484 (provisional). Latest main at start `62c6cd038951d65c94b709302bdaec6e75b080b6` (2026-10-30 01:05 KST); v1480 design remains unmerged Draft PR #1916, and this v1482 proposal is stacked on that branch, not an assertion that main implements it. Independent QA v1376 **FAILED/P0** (#1917); C v1172 wallet response validation (#1919) is local-only, not merged. All below is a proposed engineering contract; no runtime/DB/Test/Production change.

### Verified source deltas and design corrections
- `backend/src/wallet/wallet.repository.ts` already has a direction-filtered `recentTransactionsForUser` with SQL `HAVING` and `in/out/neutral`, but the **paged** `activityTransactionsForUser` has no direction predicate. `frontend/src/app/wallet/activity/period.ts` defines direction URL helpers, while the active page and Nest activity controller accept only period+cursor. Reuse the verified signed-delta semantics after real PostgreSQL fixture proof, **not** a second post-LIMIT filter or an assumption that the existing UI offers this feature.
- Legacy `wallet-activity-cursor.ts` v1 signs with a session-related key, expires after **60 minutes**, and uses `Date.toISOString()` (millisecond precision). Proposed saved-view cursor v2 expires after **15 minutes**, uses a distinct purpose-separated signing key, preserves PostgreSQL microsecond ordering, and must not reinterpret v1 tokens. The existing page uses `apiOrNull<WalletHistoryPage>` without runtime validation; malformed HTTP 200 can be mistaken for valid data or crash. C v1172 (#1919) has an independent local-only validator candidate: coordinate its contract without cherry-picking or changing its branch.
- Read-only DEV PostgreSQL 17.11 inspection at 01:08 KST: all three proposed saved-view/set/receipt tables are **absent** (not evidence about Production); `moneyverse_app` still has direct UPDATE and TRUNCATE on `support_threads`, confirming the ACL gate is unresolved. The app API gateway `frontend/src/lib/app-gateway.ts` accepts the `wallet` group but its `APP_API_REQUEST_HEADERS` does **not** forward `If-Match` or `Idempotency-Key`. Android saved-view writes therefore require an explicit reviewed header allowlist change, CSRF/auth preservation, and negative tests; do not claim mobile parity from path routing alone.
- P0 release truth from QA v1376: Test **public** `/api/treasury/overview` returned synthetic HTTP 200 with `disb-*` identifiers while Production **public** returned fail-closed 503; matching backend version SHAs did not attest frontend/edge artifacts (#1917; draft remediation #1918). This is a release blocker, not evidence of WLD movement or public Production synthetic responses.

### State ownership, atomic mutations, idempotency and retention
- Add a proposed `member_wallet_activity_view_sets` row per owner (`owner_user_id UUID PK`, `generation BIGINT NOT NULL CHECK(generation>0)`, `updated_at`), created under the same SECURITY DEFINER transaction if missing. `generation` **strictly increases** on every successful create/rename/filter/reorder/default change/delete/reset, including transitions to zero active views; never reset it to 1 after deleting the last view. Keep per-view `revision` for edit identity; use an owner-set ETag `"wv-g<generation>"` and require `If-Match` on **all** mutations, including create and default change (missing precondition 428, stale 409).
- Transaction order: authenticate owner from session; look up `(owner,idempotency_key)` receipt **before** checking current generation; same canonical operation+payload+expected generation returns original immutable receipt, different payload 409. Otherwise lock owner-set row `FOR UPDATE`, verify ETag and selected view revision, enforce max 8/unique normalized names/at most one default, clear old default and set new one in the same transaction, increment owner generation, persist view/tombstone and immutable receipt, commit once. On error roll back all. Never allow a delayed create/update to resurrect a deleted view or change the default after a later mutation.
- Do not assert permanent idempotency after deleting receipts. Receipt/tombstone retention and erasure remain **policy pending** (v1480 suggested 7-day tombstones); require a bounded, server-signed operation freshness token and reject expired replays even after receipt purge. Do not purge live proof needed for a permitted retry. A receipt attests preference mutation only, **never** a ledger transfer.
- SECURITY DEFINER functions pin `search_path`, check owner within SQL, revoke PUBLIC EXECUTE and all direct app-role table DML/SELECT/TRUNCATE, and grant only function EXECUTE. No finance table writes or applied migration edits; new migration numbering remains BLOCKED on five applied checksum mismatches and DEV ACL drift.

### Cursor, query correctness and API response proof
- Derive direction from the exact owner CASH+BANK signed posting aggregate in SQL **before** `ORDER BY (created_at,id) DESC LIMIT 51`; positive INCREASE, negative DECREASE, zero UNCHANGED, never label zero as guaranteed internal transfer. Check debit/credit sign with isolated real-PG fixtures, and compare against legacy owner history on ALL. Keep all amounts as canonical decimal strings/BigInt, not JS Number; require `cashDelta + bankDelta = netAmount` on the validated response.
- Cursor v2 HMAC-SHA-256 binds `v=2`, key-id, purpose, owner UUID, authenticated session/generation, filter digest, view UUID+revision **and owner-set generation**, database `asOf` cutoff, period-relative `since`, exact last `created_at` (microsecond string) + UUID, issued-at and expiry (<=15m). Use server SQL timestamp parsing/bind parameters that preserve six fractional digits; **never round the last tuple through JS Date**. Validate canonical encoding, size, signature in constant time, key rotation and bounds before DB access. Filter or default changes, account switch, logout, 409 stale view and 410 expired cursor restart at first page with a visible explanation. Reject malformed/oversized cursors with 400; **never silently discard a bad cursor and show page one as though it were page two**. Keep v1 60-minute period-only contract unchanged while feature OFF.
- Runtime-validate every Nest/Next/Android history response as `unknown`: `transactions` array <=50, unique UUID IDs, bounded valid timestamps, canonical exact integer WLD strings and pocket-sum consistency, `hasMore` boolean, `nextCursor` non-null **iff** `hasMore`, `asOf` and applied filter/revision matching the request. Reject malformed HTTP 200 as **unavailable/503**, not a fabricated empty history. Distinguish verified empty, zero matches, expired cursor, unavailable and unknown save result. Do not leak cursor, owner, filter label or amounts into logs/analytics.
- Next same-origin session/CSRF/server-only API and Android `/app-api/v1/[...path]` must expose the same typed errors and response validator; generated app-api EN/KO/JSON contract parity is a release gate. No `viewId+period/movement` mixed request, arbitrary userId, public caching or indexed pages.

### Acceptance, rollout, KPI and backlog handoff
- Proposed isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 tests **NOT EXECUTED**: 10,001 skewed synthetic ledger transactions; 100 cross-owner/forged cursor attempts; 100 simultaneous mutations including two competing defaults and empty→recreate ABA; 50 lost-response idempotency replays across later generations; 30 exact microsecond/UTC/zero-net/expired token/tombstone cases; 100 malformed HTTP 200 payloads and duplicate/oversized cursors. Assert filter-before-LIMIT, 50+1 stable keyset, v1 OFF compatibility, real app-role denied direct ACL, no finance writes, 320px/200% zoom WCAG 2.2 AA, noindex/no-store, web/Android parity and OFF rollback. p95 filtered read target <500ms is a **hypothesis**, measured only on isolated representative data.
- Admin receives minimum-cohort aggregate counts, errors and latency only, never view names, per-owner ledger, receipt keys or WLD amounts. Measure save success, 7-day reuse, 409/410 rates, bad-response rejection, 0 cross-owner reads and 0 duplicate preference mutations. Rollout: feature OFF; isolate new schema behind verified forward-only migration; separate A/B/C development branches; full CI and official Test authenticated exact-SHA including **separate frontend/backend artifact and edge attestation**, independent backup restore and owner-authorized Production GitOps. Rollback disables new reads/writes without altering the ledger or deleting saved preferences.
- **P0:** REPO1482-01 preserve 462 historic refs; SEC1482-02 reconcile DEV support/audit app-role DML and five checksum mismatches; FIN1482-03 SWF synthetic success, 18,000 WLD MINT provenance and 552 Treasury links; REL1482-04 repair Test synthetic Treasury public 200 / frontend SHA attestation (#1917/#1918), classifier/lint/app-api drift, full CI, off-host restore and official Test exact-SHA. All **BLOCKED / HOLD**, not fixed by this document.
- **P1:** VIEW1482-05 owner-set generation/unique schema/ACL; VIEW1482-06 SQL direction filter + microsecond v2 cursor; VIEW1482-07 runtime-validated Nest/Next/Android API, app-gateway If-Match/Idempotency-Key forwarding and error parity; VIEW1482-08 conflict-safe member UI/320px/a11y; VIEW1482-09 aggregate-only admin/privacy/SEO/KPI; VIEW1482-10 isolated PG and adversarial regression/rollback. Coordinate #1919 C v1172 as a **separate** candidate; no overlap with developer or QA branches.
- External independent, deduplicated, classified references: **0 newly verified/approved**, cumulative approved **UNKNOWN / 10,000**; gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. No new standalone feature-planning file; the v1480 section remains authoritative background for details not changed here.

## v1480 / F675-176 — Owner-private Wallet Activity Saved Views (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle and authority.** Previous Feature Improvement v1478 → Current Feature Addition v1480 → Next Feature Improvement v1482 (provisional). Start main `62c6cd038951d65c94b709302bdaec6e75b080b6` (2026-10-30 00:10 KST); QA v1375 FAILED/P0. [P0/P1 handoff #1915](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1915). This is a proposed documentation contract, not an implemented, tested, or released feature.

### Source-backed problem, user value and boundaries
- `frontend/src/app/wallet/activity/page.tsx` currently offers three periods `all/7d/30d`, 50-row keyset pages, no named saved view, no movement-direction selector. `backend/src/wallet/wallet.controller.ts` accepts only period and cursor; `wallet.service.ts` binds the cursor to owner/session/period; `wallet.repository.ts` aggregates owner CASH/BANK postings, but does not filter by net movement. `frontend/src/app/wallet/export-ledger-csv.tsx` is separate export functionality and must not be duplicated. Source inspection is not a Production usage survey.
- Target: signed-in members repeatedly inspecting their own WLD ledger activity. Outcome hypothesis: one-click return to a personally chosen period and net-movement filter without losing owner isolation or cursor correctness. Save up to **8** named views; choose one optional default; cross-device web/Android consistency. No WLD transfer, claim, trade, export, payout, balance mutation or admin read of personal ledger. The immutable ledger remains authoritative; saved views are preferences, not transaction proofs.
- Avoid duplication with F675-171 saved recipients, F675-172 dashboard shortcuts, F675-173 privacy export, F675-174 notification search and F675-175 support drafts. This feature stores only filter preferences and reads existing ledger records. Feature flag `wallet_activity_saved_views` defaults **OFF**, seed-before-gate and fail closed.

### Member entry, UI/CTA and exact states
- Entry: existing private `/wallet/activity` page, beneath the period buttons. Controls: Period (ALL, LAST_7_DAYS, LAST_30_DAYS), Net movement (ALL, INCREASE, DECREASE, UNCHANGED), `Apply filter`, `Save this view`, `Use saved view`, `Rename`, `Set as default`, `Remove`, `Reset to all`. Default means opening wallet activity selects the owner's saved view; it never auto-submits a financial operation. Labels are 1–40 NFC code points, trimmed, no markup; show original user-supplied label as escaped text only.
- Movement uses **signed net WLD change across this owner's USER_CASH + USER_BANK accounts** for a transaction, after verifying debit/credit sign on real-PG fixtures: positive = INCREASE, negative = DECREASE, zero = UNCHANGED. Zero-net must be labeled `순변동 없음`, **not** automatically `내부 이체` or `수수료 없음`; zero can have multiple causes. This is a ledger read classification, not an income/tax statement. No currency rounding or JavaScript Number for amounts.
- States: legacy/default, unsaved preview, saving, saved+revision receipt, applied, zero matching rows (distinct from no ledger data), loading next page, stale cursor/revision, rename collision, limit reached (8), duplicate replay, 401 session expired, 403 forbidden, 409 CAS conflict, 410 removed view/expired cursor, 429 throttled, 503 source/ACL unavailable, offline/uncertain result (re-query receipt, never claim saved), feature disabled (legacy history unchanged), account-switch/logout stale-response fence. Re-apply from first page after changing filters, default or session.
- Responsive 320px single-column, 200% zoom, 44px tap targets, visible focus/labels, keyboard and screen-reader announcements, reduced-motion and WCAG 2.2 AA. Korean primary copy and English parity; do not silently rewrite existing Korean wallet labels. Private page and all API responses `noindex,nofollow`/`Cache-Control: private, no-store`, no sitemap or public schema.org.

### API, ownership, auth, abuse and Android parity
- Proposed NestJS `GET /api/v1/wallet/activity/views` (list active views and revision), `POST /api/v1/wallet/activity/views` (create with UUID Idempotency-Key), `PATCH /api/v1/wallet/activity/views/:id` (rename/filter/default/reorder with `If-Match` revision and Idempotency-Key), `DELETE /api/v1/wallet/activity/views/:id` (tombstone with CAS and idempotent receipt), and extended `GET /api/v1/wallet/activity?period=...&movement=...` for unsaved preview OR `?viewId=...&cursor=...` for saved view; reject mixed viewId+period/movement, arbitrary userId and unknown enum fields. Legacy period+cursor contract remains byte-for-byte compatible when flag OFF. Response includes `transactions,hasMore,nextCursor,asOf,appliedFilters,viewRevision?` and mutation `receiptId,viewId,revision,outcome`.
- Next.js same-origin authenticated server actions call NestJS using server-only `lib/api.ts`, CSRF/session protection and `no-store`. Android app-api must use the established `/app-api/v1/[...path]` auth/route contract; generate EN/KO/JSON app-api docs together and check parity before advertising mobile availability. No raw session, bearer token, UUID or saved filter name in telemetry. All actor identity comes from verified auth context, never request body. User-provided view IDs are selectors, never authority.
- Proposed budgets: 30 read requests/minute/member and 10 mutations/minute/member plus IP ceiling; server-enforced 8 views, 40-code-point name and 2-second DB timeout; 400 malformed, 401 unauthenticated, 403 forbidden, 409 stale revision/name/key payload collision, 410 removed/expired, 429 rate, 503 DB/schema/security gate. Use parameterized SQL and strict DTO allowlists; refuse unsupported filters rather than silently returning incorrect history.

### DB schema, transactions, signed cursor and invariants
- After **forward-only migration and checksum/ACL gates**, propose `member_wallet_activity_views`: `id UUID PK`, `owner_user_id UUID NOT NULL` FK, `name TEXT NOT NULL`, `normalized_name TEXT NOT NULL`, `period ENUM`, `movement ENUM`, `sort_position SMALLINT`, `is_default BOOLEAN`, `revision BIGINT CHECK(revision>0)`, `created_at/updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ`, `deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ`. Unique partial index `(owner_user_id,normalized_name) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL`, unique partial default `(owner_user_id) WHERE is_default AND deleted_at IS NULL`, owner+position active index; database CHECK on enum/length/position; canonical Unicode normalization at API and DB validation. Do not assign a migration number until current 304 unique source SQL/293 applied and five mismatches are reconciled.
- Proposed immutable `member_wallet_activity_view_receipts`: `(owner_user_id,idempotency_key)` unique, canonical request SHA-256, outcome/target/revision/created_at; no raw session or transaction payload. Reusing a key with identical canonical payload returns **the original** receipt even if a newer view revision exists; different payload yields 409. Mutation transaction: lock owner row in stable order, enforce max 8/one default/name uniqueness, check `If-Match` CAS, write view/tombstone and immutable receipt atomically; rollback both on failure. Deletion must not re-create a removed view from a delayed request; monotonic revision/generation and tombstone fencing.
- SECURITY DEFINER owner-scoped functions with pinned `search_path`, no PUBLIC EXECUTE, explicit grant only to approved app role; no direct app-role SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE on preference/receipt tables. Read owner function checks `p_actor` against view owner inside SQL; no caller-supplied arbitrary owner. Test actual DEV/isolated grants rather than assuming migrations' REVOKE statements took effect. No modifications to financial tables, existing postings, or applied migration checksums.
- Filter in **SQL before LIMIT 51** (not in-memory after fetching 50), derive net movement by aggregating owner USER_CASH/USER_BANK postings per ledger transaction and apply HAVING/outer SELECT predicate. Keyset `(created_at,id)` descending, 50+1, bounded owner/date indexes, DB `asOf` upper cutoff; retain PostgreSQL microsecond precision in cursor tuple. New purpose-separated HMAC-SHA256 cursor version binds owner, session generation, exact period/movement or view UUID+revision+filter digest, asOf, last tuple, 15-minute expiry and key-id; constant-time signature validation and rotation. Legacy period-only cursor v1 remains supported for old endpoint. In-flight ledger corrections/backdated privileged inserts can change membership; never claim cross-request immutable MVCC snapshot. Stale view revision, account switch, logout or changed filter forces a fresh first page.
- No ledger write or balance read-modify-write, no balance conversion to JS Number, no grant relaxation to make a query pass. A saved-view receipt certifies only the preference mutation, never WLD settlement.

### Admin, privacy, SEO, KPI, test and rollout
- Admin sees only aggregate feature enablement, counts, error rates and p50/p95 latency (minimum cohort threshold); never individual view names, wallet records, balances, cursor, account identifiers or request hashes. Owner can delete views; soft-deleted tombstones retained only for defined idempotency/abuse window (proposed 7 days, policy review required), then privacy-safe purge with evidence. No email/push, no public indexing, no third-party sharing.
- KPI hypotheses: view-save success, 7-day repeat application, p95 filtered read <500ms on representative isolated PG, 0 cross-owner reads, 0 duplicate mutations, 0 financial ledger writes, 0 misleading zero-net labels, WCAG task completion. Collect coarse counters only, no personal names or WLD amounts. Budget/index query plans verified with `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)` in isolated synthetic DB, not Production.
- **Planned, NOT EXECUTED:** PostgreSQL 17.11 with 10,001 synthetic ledger transactions and skewed owners; 100 foreign-owner/forged cursor attempts, 100 concurrent create/update/delete/CAS races, 50 lost-response idempotency retries, 30 zero-net/negative/positive/UTC/DST/microsecond boundary cases; compare filter-before-limit and legacy cursor results; 320px/200% zoom keyboard/Android a11y, noindex/no-store, SQL ACL denial/EXECUTE, full transfer/ledger regression and OFF rollback. Do not run destructive SQL on DEV/Production.
- **P0:** REPO1480-01 historic 462 missing refs/346 nonancestor (#1663); SEC1480-02 DEV support/audit direct DML (#1908), five applied checksum mismatches 219/220/221/234/266; FIN1480-03 SWF synthetic SUCCESS (#1909), 18,000 WLD MINT provenance and 552 Treasury links; REL1480-04 classifier bypass Draft #1907, lint errors, app-api affinity drift, full CI, independent off-host restore and official Test exact-SHA authenticated E2E. QA v1375 FAILED/P0; do not promote.
- **P1:** VIEW1480-05 schema/owner functions; VIEW1480-06 filtered ledger query/signed cursor; VIEW1480-07 Nest/Next/Android APIs; VIEW1480-08 accessible member UI; VIEW1480-09 privacy/admin/SEO/KPI; VIEW1480-10 isolated PG/regression/rollback/exact-SHA acceptance. Separate development A/B/C branches and reviewed Test exact-SHA before owner-approved Production GitOps. Runtime code/DB/WLD/Test/Production **unchanged by planning**.
- External independent deduplicated references: newly verified and approved **0**, cumulative approved **UNKNOWN / 10,000**; gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. Existing historical specs remain in the integrated master; no new standalone planning file.



## v1486 / F675-177 — Monthly Wallet Digest Snapshot and Historical-Account Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **Draft PR:** [#1930](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1930) — OPEN / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD; stacked on unmerged #1926, not independently mergeable.

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1484 → Current Feature Improvement v1486 → Next Feature Addition v1488 (provisional). **Authority:** exact main `62c6cd038951d65c94b709302bdaec6e75b080b6` verified at start/mid; preceding [Draft #1926](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1926) remains unmerged. Independent QA v1378 **FAILED/P0**. This stacked documentation proposal does not imply an implemented endpoint, database change, Test certification or Production release. [Engineering handoff #1925](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1925).

### Source-verified defects and scope
1. `backend/src/wallet/wallet.repository.ts:259+` executes wallet activity reads through `Queryable.query` on an injected pool; the v1484 phrase `REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY` is **not achieved** by issuing BEGIN/SELECT/COMMIT as separate pool queries. Any multi-statement implementation MUST acquire one dedicated `pg.PoolClient`, set transaction isolation/read-only and statement/lock timeout, run all snapshot/aggregation queries on that same client, `ROLLBACK` on failure and `release()` in `finally`. Alternative: a **single SQL statement** that derives `asOf`, month boundaries, owner postings and results from one statement-level MVCC snapshot, with an explicit test of isolation semantics; never claim REPEATABLE READ for a single statement without opening that transaction.
2. `packages/database/init/001-economy-core.sql` defines owner accounts with `status=active|frozen|closed`, `currency char(3)`, `ledger_transactions.created_at`, and per-posting `bigint amount`. Existing activity SQL includes all owner USER_CASH/USER_BANK accounts **without** filtering status. Monthly history MUST NOT add `account.status='active'`, since that would erase historical postings for frozen/closed accounts. Include WLD-only owner accounts; account status must not authorize cross-owner reads. Do not filter by `ledger_transactions.actor_user_id`: inbound transfers may have another actor.
3. v1484 conflated transaction totals with gross money flows. Define **owner-net activity**, not gross economic volume: first aggregate every signed USER_CASH+USER_BANK posting for one owner and transaction ID, then classify `net>0`, `net<0`, `net=0`. A cash↔bank transfer contributes cash/bank deltas and one zero-net transaction but **zero owner-net inflow/outflow**. Multiple same-owner postings do not multiply transaction counts. UI labels must say 'Net increases/decreases of my WLD' (not total gross receipts/spending). `netWld = inflowWld - outflowWld = cashDeltaWld + bankDeltaWld` for every month and entire returned range. This is accounting arithmetic, **not** independent MINT/Treasury/SWF provenance or settlement certification.
4. A `transaction_timestamp()`-derived `asOf` and KST month boundaries MUST originate in the same database snapshot. Use half-open `[monthStartUtc, min(nextMonthStartUtc,asOf))`; never include transactions at/after `asOf`, future periods or rely on browser timezone. `periodEnd` remains next calendar month UTC for labeling; add `effectiveEndExclusive` for current partial month, with `effectiveEndExclusive <= periodEnd`. Exact month ordering, contiguous months and `asOf` within server-clock tolerance must be verified. Historical totals are revisable after backdated inserts; do not present a permanent month-close certificate.

### Concrete engineering contract
- **Entry and states:** Existing private `/wallet/activity` → `Monthly activity` (feature flag `wallet_monthly_activity_digest`, OFF) → select canonical integer 1..12, default 6 → `View monthly summary` → rows and existing `View history` link (do not claim month-filter deep links). LOADING, READY, VERIFIED_EMPTY (only successful authoritative read), PARTIAL, INVALID_MONTHS(400), SESSION_EXPIRED(401), FORBIDDEN(403), RATE_LIMITED(429), UNAVAILABLE(503), BAD_RESPONSE(503), OFFLINE and DISABLED. No cached previous-member data or zero fallback on error; stale responses are discarded on logout, session rotation or account switch.
- **API:** Proposed authenticated `GET /api/v1/wallet/activity/monthly-summary?months=6`; no `userId`, `asOf`, arbitrary timezone or date input. Nest/Next/Android validate `{asOf,timezone:'Asia/Seoul',monthsRequested,sourceStatus:'VERIFIED_READ',months:[{month,periodStart,periodEnd,effectiveEndExclusive,status,incomingCount,outgoingCount,zeroNetCount,inflowWld,outflowWld,netWld,cashDeltaWld,bankDeltaWld}]}`. Exact canonical signed integer strings for WLD, nonnegative safe-integer counts, distinct month IDs, expected contiguous newest-first months, exact per-month and range invariants. If DB returns NUMERIC, serialize as strings; never `Number` for WLD. Reject unknown/missing fields, malformed 200, impossible bounds or mismatched sums as unavailable (no partial success).
- **SQL and privileges:** Single owner filter from authenticated server session, WLD currency and USER_CASH/USER_BANK only; group by `ledger_transaction.id,ledger_transaction.created_at` before month classification, use posting debit +amount / credit -amount. No status filter and no actor-user filter. Evaluate account-owner and transaction timestamp index plans on **isolated** PG; no unbounded JS pagination/aggregation or direct user-controlled SQL. No new table, direct app-role write grant, ledger write, finance mutation or user-specific admin endpoint. If an owner-only `SECURITY DEFINER` read function becomes necessary, pin `search_path`, revoke PUBLIC EXECUTE, grant minimal EXECUTE, assert owner SQL predicates and recheck migration provenance before forward-only SQL.
- **Failure/abuse:** Member budget 10/min, one in-flight request, DB statement timeout 2s (targets subject to load testing), deterministic 400/401/403/429/503 with no PII. No CDN/ISR cache; `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex`, no sitemap/structured data. Never log amount, raw transaction ID, owner ID or receipt; admin only cohort-threshold adoption, latency and error aggregates. 320px/200%-zoom, keyboard and screen-reader error/partial labels, WCAG 2.2 AA and Android generated EN/KO/JSON contract parity. KPI p95 <500ms and accessible task completion are proposed thresholds, not observed results.
- **Acceptance / rollback (PLANNED, NOT RUN):** isolated PG 17.11 with 10,001 skewed synthetic transactions; 100 cross-owner/forged actor cases, 100 KST/UTC month and leap-boundary cases, 100 frozen/closed account and multi-posting cases, 100 concurrent backdated inserts and read-snapshot races, 50 timeout/malformed/partial network responses, 2^53+ exact WLD and negative cases. Assert all sums against independently recomputed owner postings, zero-net cash↔bank, closed/frozen history retained, one checked-out client transaction or verified one-statement snapshot, no app-role DML, no ledger changes, Next/Nest/Android parity and flag-off old-history regression. Feature OFF rollback hides new CTA/route; no data deletion. New index only as separately reviewed forward migration after P0 clearance. Full CI, off-host restore, official authenticated exact-SHA Test with frontend/backend/edge artifacts, user-approved GitOps promotion, smoke and rollback proof required.
- **P0 backlog (engineering handoff):** `REPO1486-01` preserve 462 historic missing refs/346 nonancestor commits, no deletion; `SEC1486-02` reconcile DEV support/audit DML/TRUNCATE and applied migration checksums 219/220/221/234/266 (304 unique source SQL, 293 applied); `FIN1486-03` block uncertified SWF/MINT/Treasury success, investigate 19 reconciliation transactions/18,000 WLD and 552 unlinked Treasury rows plus Test public synthetic 200; `REL1486-04` fix exact frontend/edge identity, finance focused 6 failures, lint 3 errors, contract drift, independent restore, exact-SHA Test and capacity (root 86%, data 92% at start; no unauthorized cleanup).
- **P1 backlog (engineering handoff):** `MONTH1486-05` historical owner-account/WLD SQL and 10,001-row index proof; `MONTH1486-06` dedicated-client snapshot/half-open boundary/effectiveEnd contract; `MONTH1486-07` strict Nest/Next API and sign/count invariant validation; `MONTH1486-08` Android contract, session fencing and 320px/WCAG UX; `MONTH1486-09` privacy, aggregate-only admin, SEO and measured KPI; `MONTH1486-10` isolated real-PG concurrency/precision/closed-account/regression/rollback proof. **P0 4 + P1 6 = 10**; acceptance cases specified, none executed.
- **External reference gate:** independently obtained, deduplicated and evidence-classified sources newly approved **0**; cumulative **UNKNOWN / 10,000**. **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**, never assert 10,000 achieved. Release **HOLD**; no runtime, DB, WLD, Test or Production mutation by planning.

## v1484 / F675-177 — Owner-Private Monthly Wallet Activity Digest (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **Draft PR:** [#1926](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1926) — OPEN / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD; unmerged, full CI not certified.

**Authority / scope.** Previous Feature Improvement v1482 → Current Feature Addition v1484 → Next Feature Improvement v1486 (provisional). Main `62c6cd038951d65c94b709302bdaec6e75b080b6` rechecked start/mid; QA v1377 FAILED/P0. v1480/#1916 and v1482/#1920 are unmerged and must not be represented as released. This is a new feature proposal in the existing integrated document, not a runtime or DB implementation. Development issue [#1925](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1925). English canonical; Korean equivalent adjacent.

### Purpose, evidence and non-duplication
- **Problem:** signed-in members can inspect `/wallet/activity` only as `all/7d/30d` pages of at most 50 records. `backend/src/wallet/wallet.controller.ts` exposes owner/session-bound period+cursor, and `wallet.repository.ts` already aggregates signed postings for `USER_CASH` and `USER_BANK` at the transaction level. No existing calendar-month digest endpoint or UI was found. `frontend/src/app/wallet/export-ledger-csv.tsx` exports only the supplied recent rows; it is not a complete ledger statement. Source evidence is not a Production usage survey.
- **Audience/value:** a member checking how virtual WLD moved across months without paging through every row. Success hypothesis: fewer history navigation steps and higher month-over-month comprehension, not real financial income/tax reporting. Keep distinct from F675-176 saved filter preferences (#1915/#1920), F675-173 privacy package (#1876), and existing recent CSV. No share link, trading recommendation, transfer, balance write, new financial claim or admin access to an individual's ledger.

### Member flow, CTA and exact states
- **Entry:** existing private `/wallet/activity` with a feature-gated `월별 활동 요약` / `Monthly activity` CTA. Default last **6** calendar months including current; selector accepts **1..12** integer months only. `월별 요약 보기` / `View monthly summary` reloads the private report; each row has `거래내역 보기` / `View history` linking to the existing history with a visibly disclosed period limitation (existing page does not yet support arbitrary month filters; do not pretend it does). Do not add a deep link with unsupported month query until a separate contract exists.
- **Each month:** `month` YYYY-MM in Asia/Seoul, `periodStart` inclusive and `periodEnd` exclusive ISO UTC, `status=PARTIAL|COMPLETE`, `incomingCount`, `outgoingCount`, `zeroNetCount`, `inflowWld`, `outflowWld`, `netWld`, `cashDeltaWld`, `bankDeltaWld` as canonical exact decimal strings. `asOf` server snapshot timestamp and `sourceStatus=VERIFIED_READ` required. Current month is PARTIAL; completed months never imply final immutable accounting if late/backdated transactions can be inserted—re-query can change historical totals, disclose that limitation. Counts are distinct transaction IDs **after** owner aggregation, not posting counts.
- **States:** LOADING, VERIFIED_EMPTY (successful query, no activity in selected months), READY, PARTIAL_CURRENT_MONTH, 401 SESSION_EXPIRED, 403 OWNER_FORBIDDEN, 400 INVALID_MONTHS, 429 RATE_LIMITED, 503 SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE, 503 MALFORMED_UPSTREAM, OFFLINE/UNKNOWN and FEATURE_DISABLED. Never show 0 WLD for an unavailable source. Distinguish zero **net movement** from internal transfer: it can represent multiple posting patterns. No opening/closing balance, investment performance, income, tax or certified financial statement labels.
- **Accessibility/mobile:** 320px single-column summary, responsive month list/table, 200% zoom without clipping, 44px touch targets, explicit focus order, headings, keyboard controls, aria-live nonduplicated error/partial announcements, reduced motion, WCAG 2.2 AA and Korean/English parity. No color-only sign cues. Logout/account-switch invalidates outstanding UI requests.

### API contract, permissions, transaction and data invariants
- **Endpoint:** proposed NestJS `GET /api/v1/wallet/activity/monthly-summary?months=6`; authenticated owner from server session only, no userId input, no client-selected `asOf`, no arbitrary time zone or future month. Strict canonical decimal 1..12 (default 6), duplicate/array/whitespace/float/negative/oversized query rejected 400; no silent coercion. Response `{asOf,timezone:'Asia/Seoul',monthsRequested,sourceStatus:'VERIFIED_READ',months:[...]}` exactly 1..12 months, most recent first; unknown fields and malformed money/timestamps rejected at Next/Android boundaries, not displayed as empty.
- **Read algorithm:** start one PostgreSQL `REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY` transaction, derive server `asOf` inside it, compute KST half-open month boundaries converted to UTC (never use naive browser locale or 30-day sliding windows), filter ledger_transaction.created_at in `[oldestStartUTC, asOf]`, join owner-only USER_CASH/USER_BANK postings, **GROUP BY transaction id and created_at before month grouping**. Reuse verified sign mapping from repository (`debit` positive, `credit` negative for owner) after isolated PG fixture tests. For each transaction, `net=cashDelta+bankDelta`; classify sign >0 incoming, <0 outgoing, =0 zero-net. Monthly inflow is SUM(positive net), outflow SUM(abs(negative net)), net=inflow-outflow. Sum cash/bank deltas separately and assert exact equality. Use NUMERIC and canonical signed integer strings end-to-end, never JS Number or float; overflow/invalid amount must fail closed. Zero-fill missing calendar months **only after** the authoritative query succeeds. A ledger arithmetic result is not proof that source transactions were financially authorized.
- **Database/indices:** reuse existing ledger read model and owner scoping; **no new tables, schema constraints or app-role write grants** are required for v1. Check existing `(account.owner_user_id, account_type)`, `ledger_postings(account_id,transaction_id)` and `ledger_transactions(created_at,id)` index plans under 10,001 skewed rows; do not assume all these indexes exist. If missing, propose an additive concurrent index only after migration checksum/ACL P0 clearance and lock/rollback review; do not edit applied migrations. SQL access must not expand `moneyverse_app` DML/TRUNCATE; if SECURITY DEFINER is introduced for least-privilege, pin search_path, revoke PUBLIC EXECUTE, grant narrow EXECUTE and test ownership in SQL. No DB writes during read; read-only timeout target <=2s, per-member quota 10/min and one concurrent request (performance/abuse targets subject to measured verification).
- **API/Android:** same-origin Next server session and Nest guard; Android `/app-api/v1/[...path]` wallet group must preserve authorization but never forward arbitrary user ID, secrets or unsupported request headers. Add generated EN/KO/JSON app-api contract for GET response and negative 401/403/429/503 cases. Runtime-validate `unknown` payload at each boundary: exact month count/order/uniqueness, valid ISO asOf/bounds, canonical money strings, counts nonnegative integers, `net=inflow-outflow=cash+bank`, no future data, and sourceStatus verified. Missing/invalid 200 -> 503 unavailable, not verified empty. `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex`, no CDN/ISR cache, no public sitemap or structured data.
- **Security/abuse/privacy:** member-only owner filter in SQL and auth layer; reject forged owner, session change and cross-account cached data. No amount, account ID, source receipt, or raw query logs/analytics. Admin gets cohort-threshold aggregate adoption/latency/errors only; never member monthly amounts. Monitoring: request volume, p95 query latency, timeout, bad-payload rejection, 401/403/429, owner leak **0**, inconsistent sum **0**. Performance p95 <500ms and 7-day adoption are **TEST TARGET/HYPOTHESIS**, not measured KPIs. SEO value none for private pages.

### QA acceptance, deployment and rollback
- **Planned, NOT EXECUTED:** isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 10,001 skewed synthetic transactions with multi-posting same transaction and cash↔bank zero net; 100 forged/cross-owner requests; 100 UTC/KST boundary, February leap, end-of-month, late/backdated and zero-net cases; 50 malformed HTTP 200/timeout/network failures; numeric >2^53 precision, duplicate transaction, 12-month cap, 429, 320px/200% zoom and Android parity. Verify monthly sums against independent raw owner posting reconciliation and that zero activity is only asserted after a successful query. No direct app-role DML, no ledger writes, no index/DB mutation during planning.
- **Rollout:** `wallet_monthly_activity_digest` OFF by default; no schema or data migration in baseline, so rollback hides CTA and endpoint without changing ledger or existing history/CSV. If later index is needed, separate migration and backward-compatible rollback reviewed. Separate developer A/B/C branch, full lint/test/build/app-api contract, official exact-SHA authenticated Test and separate frontend/backend/edge artifact proof, off-host encrypted backup restore, owner-approved Production promotion and smoke/rollback. Release HOLD until P0 all pass.
- **P0:** REPO1484-01 historic 462 missing refs; SEC1484-02 DEV app direct support/audit DML/TRUNCATE + five applied migration checksum mismatches 219/220/221/234/266; FIN1484-03 SWF synthetic success, 18,000 WLD MINT provenance and 552 Treasury direct-link gaps plus Test public synthetic 200; REL1484-04 frontend/edge SHA (#1917/#1918), lint 3 errors/app-api contract drift/full CI, exact-SHA Test, off-host restore and disk capacity 86% root/89% data. No destructive cleanup without reviewed retention/backup proof.
- **P1:** MONTH1484-05 owner SQL/index proof; MONTH1484-06 strict Nest read-only API; MONTH1484-07 Next UI/a11y; MONTH1484-08 Android contract; MONTH1484-09 admin/privacy/SEO/KPI; MONTH1484-10 isolated PostgreSQL adversarial/rollback evidence. All are engineering handoff only. External independent deduplicated classified evidence **0 newly approved**, cumulative **UNKNOWN/10,000**, gate **BLOCKED/IN PROGRESS**. No new standalone planning file, runtime, database, WLD, Test or Production change.
