## v1490 / F675-178 — Wallet Bookmark Consistency, Replay and Mobile Privacy Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1488 → Current Feature Improvement v1490 → Next Feature Addition v1492 (provisional). **Authority:** main 62c6cd038951d65c94b709302bdaec6e75b080b6, AGENTS.md, README.md, PROJECT_PLAN.md canonical, PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md parity; main and plans rechecked at start/mid. **Predecessor:** unmerged Draft PR #1937; do not merge this follow-up without predecessor review. **Handoff:** issue #1936. **QA:** independent v1380 FAILED/P0. Documentation only; no runtime/SQL/DB/WLD/Test/Production modification.

### Evidence-based corrections to v1488
1. The existing wallet activity cursor in backend/src/wallet/wallet-activity-cursor.ts serializes timestamps through JavaScript Date/ISO strings (millisecond resolution). Its repository keyset compares (created_at,id) in PostgreSQL, which can store microseconds. **Do not reuse this cursor for bookmarks:** two bookmark timestamps within the same millisecond may cause a duplicate/skip if rounded. New bookmark cursors carry an exact six-digit PostgreSQL fractional timestamp, never round-trip through Date; order by (bookmarked_at DESC,transaction_id DESC). Validate the exact UTC timestamp string and UUID before parameterized comparison, HMAC-SHA-256 with key ID, owner/session/filter/limit/state-generation binding, constant-time verification, TTL 10 minutes and ≤2048-byte bound. The existing history cursor defect is a separate regression handoff; do not claim it is fixed by this plan.
2. The Android app-api gateway at frontend/src/lib/app-gateway.ts APP_API_REQUEST_HEADERS **does not include Idempotency-Key**; the route forwards only allowlisted headers. A new bookmark mutation would silently lose the stable request key. Add a reviewed, wallet-bookmark-specific header forwarding rule with strict UUID validation and rejection of duplicate/conflicting values; preserve CSRF/session and never forward internal credentials. The wallet gateway sets private no-store but **does not force X-Robots-Tag: noindex**; add it for owner-private wallet paths, including errors, 304 and malformed upstream responses. Track independent Backend B privacy issue #1935; do not overwrite its code branch.
3. The existing frontend/src/app/wallet/activity/page.tsx displays 50-row history without bookmark controls; the page uses apiOrNull, distinguishing outage from an authoritative empty response. New UI must preserve that distinction, show verified server status only, and fence all async responses by authenticated session epoch. Existing activityPeriod helpers have direction/completeness options not wired into this page; do not invent a direction-filter dependency or claim those controls are deployed.
4. The v1488 receipt model is underspecified when a successful Save is followed by Remove and then the original Save response arrives late. An immutable replay receipt describes **the effect at original commit**, not current saved state. A replay must never reapply the original mutation or cause the client to assert currently saved; return original operationId/committedGeneration/effectAtCommit and require a fresh authoritative status read when generation is newer. Retain operation evidence ≥90 days but accept same-key retries only during a defined 24-hour replay window; an expired key returns 410 without a new write. Different fingerprint under any retained key returns 409.
5. Per-owner serialized cap alone does not protect multi-page reads from intervening save/remove. Introduce an owner-scoped monotonic bookmark **generation** that never resets when the last bookmark is removed. A first page returns generation; every continuation cursor binds it and the server compares against the current generation atomically. On change return 409 BOOKMARK_PAGE_CHANGED and offer Restart, not a silent missing/duplicated row or a false immutable snapshot. A generation-fenced keyset gives stable membership only while no writes occur, not a cross-request MVCC snapshot.

### Product, access, states and navigation
- Audience: authenticated, consented member saving up to 100 previously posted **own WLD** ledger transactions across devices, including inbound and frozen/closed historical accounts. This is private navigation metadata, never a certified receipt, public share, settlement attestation or financial mutation. Keep v1488 non-goals (no notes, tags, exports, payment, balance edit, automatic save).
- Entry: /wallet/activity → contextual Save transaction / Remove bookmark and View saved transactions; private /wallet/activity/bookmarks → Load earlier saved, Restart list, Back to activity. Buttons have transaction-context accessible names, not raw UUID. On cap: Remove one before saving another. 320px, 200% zoom, keyboard, 44px targets where feasible, screen-reader live feedback, WCAG 2.2 AA and Android TalkBack. No optimism on mutation; no cross-account flash on logout.
- Explicit states: DISABLED, LOADING, READY, VERIFIED_EMPTY, SAVING, REMOVING, ALREADY_APPLIED, LIMIT_REACHED (409), REPLAY_CONFLICT (409), PAGE_CHANGED (409), CURSOR_EXPIRED (410), REQUEST_KEY_EXPIRED (410), SESSION_EXPIRED (401), FORBIDDEN (403), NOT_OWNER_VISIBLE (404), RATE_LIMITED (429), OFFLINE, UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE (503), MALFORMED_RESPONSE (503), ACCOUNT_SWITCHED. A 200 with invalid shape is never treated as empty or success.

### API and exact semantics
- GET /api/v1/wallet/activity/bookmarks?limit=20&cursor=... returns {asOf, generation, items, nextCursor, hasMore, limit}; default 20/max 50; each item contains transactionId, bookmarkedAt and **owner-projected** wallet transaction fields only (transactionId, occurredAt, label, netAmount/cashDelta/bankDelta as exact decimal strings), not counterparty identities or global postings. A bookmarked transaction whose owner-visible ledger proof is no longer valid is withheld and counted in an internal privacy-safe consistency alarm; do not leak a foreign transaction through a stale bookmark row.
- POST /api/v1/wallet/activity/bookmarks/status accepts 1..50 distinct canonical UUIDs and returns only currently owner-visible saved IDs plus server generation. Do not expose existence of foreign or missing transactions. No write and no ledger effects.
- PUT/DELETE /api/v1/wallet/activity/bookmarks/:transactionId require authenticated actor, CSRF for web, mobile session/consent, canonical Idempotency-Key UUID and exact method/path/fingerprint. Response {operationId, transactionId, action, effectAtCommit, committedGeneration, alreadyApplied, serverTime, currentStateRequiresRefresh}; replay returns the original immutable receipt. A distinct request key is required for a new intended mutation, never generated automatically after an ambiguous network failure. 400 malformed, 401 unauthenticated, 403 policy, 404 unowned/unknown, 409 cap/fingerprint/generation, 410 expired key/cursor, 429 quota, 503 dependency. Stable machine-readable codes in Nest/Next/Android; never 200 on an uncommitted write.
- Client status after replay: compare committedGeneration to freshly read owner generation; if not current or request was replayed, fetch status before displaying Saved/Removed. On lost response, retry the same key within 24 hours. On 410 stop, show Cannot confirm earlier action and refresh status; do not mint a new key as an implicit retry.

### PostgreSQL, authorization and race proof
- Proposed additive tables after SEC gate: wallet_bookmark_owner_state(owner_user_id uuid PK REFERENCES users(id), generation bigint NOT NULL CHECK(generation>=0)); wallet_activity_bookmarks(owner_user_id,transaction_id,bookmarked_at timestamptz, PRIMARY KEY(owner_user_id,transaction_id), owner FK, ledger FK with reviewed retention semantics); wallet_bookmark_operation_receipts(owner_user_id,request_key PK composite, action,transaction_id,fingerprint,operation_id,effect_at_commit,committed_generation,created_at,expires_at, immutable response). Index bookmarks(owner_user_id,bookmarked_at DESC,transaction_id DESC), receipts(expires_at); owner generation never recycled, and account erasure needs an audited tombstone/restore-redelete strategy. Do not edit applied migration files or assign a migration number while five checksums differ.
- A transaction is owner-visible iff a ledger_postings row joins an account owned by authenticated actor, account_type USER_CASH/USER_BANK and currency WLD. Do not filter ledger_transactions.actor_user_id or account status; ensure one result per transaction even with multiple postings. Forbid direct app-role DML/SELECT/TRUNCATE on new tables and PUBLIC EXECUTE; reviewed SECURITY DEFINER owner functions with fixed search_path and explicit actor policy. **Security boundary:** the database function's actor argument alone is not cryptographic session proof; Nest must derive it from the verified server-side session, never from request body, and DB privilege/EXECUTE exposure must be independently threat-modeled.
- Under one transaction, lock/create the owner_state row, validate owner ledger visibility, check idempotency fingerprint and age, enforce count <100, apply INSERT/DELETE, increment generation **only if membership changes**, persist receipt and commit. Duplicate no-op Save/Remove records a deterministic receipt without advancing generation. Concurrent cap=99 + 100 Save attempts must never exceed 100; save→remove→old replay must not resurrect. Failure rolls back both membership and receipt. First page reads generation and rows in a single SQL snapshot; continuation compares generation and reads rows in one SQL statement/transaction to avoid TOCTOU. If generation changed, fail closed with PAGE_CHANGED, not partial results. Use PostgreSQL microsecond tuple comparison without JS Date conversion.
- Existing main history SQL reads directly from ledger owner accounts and uses JS Date millisecond cursors; avoid modifying financial reads in the planning task. Any new function/SQL must be separately reviewed and tested with the real moneyverse_app role, not superuser mocks.

### Security, privacy, observability, release and tests
- Private no-store/noindex for Next, Nest and Android gateway, including error paths. No sitemap, robots indexing, public share, counterparty metadata, bookmark IDs in logs or analytics. Admin may inspect k≥20 cohort aggregates and error/latency counts, never member bookmark contents. Per-member target 30 reads/min, 10 writes/min, 1 concurrent mutation; enforce after load tests, rate-limited synthetic concurrency uses an isolated bypass fixture. KPI hypotheses only: owner leak 0, false success 0, p95 page <500ms/write <700ms, 30-day adoption ≥5%; not observed results.
- Planned **NOT RUN** isolated PostgreSQL 17.11: ≥10,001 synthetic ledger transactions, same-millisecond/different-microsecond bookmarks, 100 foreign/forged actor cases, 100 concurrent saves at cap 99, 50 lost response/late replay after Remove, 30 session/account switches, cursor generation change, 24h/90d retention boundary, 2^53+ WLD strings, non-WLD exclusion, multiple owner postings, deleted/restricted account, malformed 200, Android header propagation/noindex, 320px a11y, zero financial changes, app-role ACL and restore/redelete. Do not claim PASS until independently executed.
- P0 backlog: REPO1490-01 preserve 462 missing historic refs (346 nonancestor) and enforce branch protection; SEC1490-02 reconcile five applied checksums (219/220/221/234/266), DEV support/audit app DML and owner-function ACL; FIN1490-03 resolve 18,000 WLD MINT provenance, 552 unlinked Treasury rows, SWF fail-closed 6 FAIL and Test synthetic 200; REL1490-04 QA v1380 FAILED/P0, full lint/test/contract, exact backend/frontend/edge SHA, independent off-host restore, GitOps and disk root 92%/data 97% capacity safety without deleting other work.
- P1 backlog: BOOKMARK1490-05 microsecond owner/generation keyset and SQL indexes; BOOKMARK1490-06 owner-row serialization/receipt replay/current-state reconciliation; BOOKMARK1490-07 Nest contract/strict UUID and Android Idempotency-Key/noindex forwarding; BOOKMARK1490-08 Next owner UX, account-switch fencing and accessible error recovery; BOOKMARK1490-09 privacy/admin aggregate/SEO/analytics and retention/restore; BOOKMARK1490-10 isolated PG 10,001-row adversarial tests, regression, rollback and exact-SHA Test. **P0 4 + P1 6 = 10**.
- External independently acquired/deduplicated/classified references: newly approved **0**, cumulative **UNKNOWN / 10,000**; gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. Feature OFF. Developer work only on separate branch after P0 resolution; RED/GREEN → reviewed SQL/isolated PG → full CI → official authenticated Test exact-SHA → off-host restore → approved GitOps promotion/smoke/rollback. This planning change never deploys or changes WLD, ledger, shared DB or Production.


## v1488 / F675-178 — Owner-Private Wallet Transaction Bookmarks (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
**Draft PR:** [#1937](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1937) — OPEN / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD, independent main-based branch (unmerged prior #1926/#1930 preserved).

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1486 → Current Feature Addition v1488 → Next Feature Improvement v1490 (provisional). **Source authority:** main `62c6cd038951d65c94b709302bdaec6e75b080b6` and `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, English canonical `PROJECT_PLAN.md` with Korean parity; start and mid main/plan hashes rechecked. **Engineering handoff:** [#1936](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1936). QA v1379 finance 6 FAIL / 3 PASS, full lint 3 errors, five applied migration checksum mismatches. **Proposal only:** no implementation, DB migration, feature switch activation, WLD movement, Test or Production deployment.

### Product outcome, scope and source evidence
- **Goal / audience:** a signed-in member can save up to 100 of *their own* previously posted WLD transactions, revisit the saved list across devices and remove a bookmark. This is a personal navigation aid, **not** an audit certification, ledger annotation, complaint, saved search, receipt, payment instruction or accounting edit.
- **Verified gap:** `frontend/src/app/wallet/activity/page.tsx` displays up to 50 owner transactions per page with `all/7d/30d` and a signed cursor, but no save/unsave CTA. `backend/src/wallet/wallet.controller.ts` exposes `GET wallet/activity` but no bookmark endpoint. Read-only DEV PostgreSQL 17.11 returned `to_regclass('public.wallet_activity_bookmarks')=NULL` and 891 ledger transactions; DEV figures are **not** Production figures. Repository search did not find a deployed wallet-bookmark implementation. F675-176 saved **filters**, F675-177 monthly **aggregation**, F675-03 explanatory **receipts** and F675-173 privacy **export** are separate; do not duplicate or silently rewrite them.
- **Non-goals:** no free-text notes, user-defined financial categories, automatic saving, public share links, CSV/PDF export, transaction changes, transfers, balance/loan/treasury mutations, mutable ledger labels or data claims that bypass provenance checks.

### Member flow, permissions, states and exact CTAs
- **Entry:** private `/wallet/activity` (existing period/cursor remains unchanged) adds a `Save transaction` button on each verified owner transaction. Saved item shows `Remove bookmark`; a separate `View saved transactions` link opens private `/wallet/activity/bookmarks`. No anonymous/administrator impersonation path; no client-supplied `userId` or actor field.
- **Saved view:** newest bookmark first (`bookmarkedAt DESC, transactionId DESC`), 20 per page by default, maximum 50; `Load earlier saved`, `Back to activity`, and per-item `Remove bookmark`. Show 100-item cap and `Remove one before saving another` on limit; avoid presenting bookmarked transaction as verified settlement/provenance. Read-only history remains available when feature is OFF.
- **States:** DISABLED, LOADING, READY, VERIFIED_EMPTY (only authoritative successful empty response), SAVING, REMOVING, ALREADY_SAVED, ALREADY_REMOVED, LIMIT_REACHED (409), SESSION_EXPIRED (401), FORBIDDEN (403), NOT_FOUND (404 for unowned/nonexistent transaction), CONFLICT (409 reused key with different request), CURSOR_EXPIRED (410), RATE_LIMITED (429), OFFLINE, UNAVAILABLE (503), MALFORMED_RESPONSE (503). Never treat an API failure as empty, never optimistically claim a write succeeded, never render a previous member's saved items after logout/account switch.
- **Accessibility:** 320px width, 200% zoom, keyboard focus visible, 44×44 CSS-pixel target where feasible, accessible status/live regions, button names including transaction context without exposing full UUID, no color-only state, screen-reader feedback and Android TalkBack parity, WCAG 2.2 AA.

### Proposed authenticated API and error contract
- `GET /api/v1/wallet/activity/bookmarks?limit=20&cursor=<opaque>` → `{asOf,items:[{transactionId,bookmarkedAt,transaction:{occurredAt,label,netAmount,postings...}}],nextCursor,hasMore,limit}`. Reuse the authoritative owner wallet activity row schema, exact integer WLD strings, not raw unfiltered ledger rows. `limit` is canonical decimal 1..50; `cursor` ≤2048 bytes, HMAC-SHA-256 signed, owner+session+limit+issuedAt+last bookmarkedAt microsecond+UUID bound, TTL 10 minutes. Revoke on logout/session rotation. New writes may change paging; invalidate local cursor after save/remove and restart. No false snapshot guarantee when set changes between pages.
- `POST /api/v1/wallet/activity/bookmarks/status` with `{transactionIds:[uuid,...]}` (1..50 unique) → `{bookmarkedTransactionIds:[uuid,...]}` for **owner-visible** transactions only. Unknown/unowned IDs are omitted without an existence oracle; do not return their metadata. This POST is read-only, CSRF/session guarded and no-store; explicit payload bounds prevent unbounded query lists.
- `PUT /api/v1/wallet/activity/bookmarks/:transactionId` and `DELETE` with `Idempotency-Key: <UUID>`, same-origin CSRF and authenticated consented member session. PUT saves, DELETE removes; no toggle endpoint. Response `{transactionId,bookmarked:boolean,bookmarkedAt?:string,operationId,alreadyApplied:boolean,serverTime}`; same key + same owner/action/transaction returns original receipt, same key + different fingerprint returns 409. Never accept actor/owner from body. A lost response is retried with the **same** key, never a new key. A stale request >24h returns explicit 410; receipt/tombstone retention ≥90 days with a documented retry boundary.
- Validate strict UUID, RFC3339 timestamp, signed cursor, response fields and owner/session identity in NestJS, Next.js server action and Android app-api. Error codes stable across clients; 400 invalid, 401 expired session, 403 policy, 404 not owner-visible, 409 limit/key conflict, 410 cursor/request expired, 429 quota, 503 unavailable. HTTP 200 with malformed data must fail closed, not silently produce an empty list.

### Database, authorization, concurrency and transaction design
- **Proposed additive tables** (names tentative until SEC1488-02): `wallet_activity_bookmarks(owner_user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id), transaction_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES ledger_transactions(id), bookmarked_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), PRIMARY KEY(owner_user_id,transaction_id))` and `wallet_bookmark_operation_receipts(owner_user_id uuid NOT NULL, request_key uuid NOT NULL, action text NOT NULL CHECK(action IN ('save','remove')), transaction_id uuid NOT NULL, request_fingerprint bytea NOT NULL, response_json jsonb NOT NULL, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), PRIMARY KEY(owner_user_id,request_key))`. Index `wallet_activity_bookmarks(owner_user_id,bookmarked_at DESC,transaction_id DESC)` and receipt expiry/cleanup index; avoid indexes on WLD amounts. Only the approved retention job can purge expired receipt records, never ledger transactions.
- **Owner proof:** a transaction is eligible iff an `EXISTS` query finds `ledger_postings.transaction_id=:id` joined to `accounts.owner_user_id=:authenticatedActor` where account type is `USER_CASH` or `USER_BANK` and currency `WLD`. Do **not** filter `ledger_transactions.actor_user_id` (inbound transfers belong to recipient's history) or `accounts.status='active'` (frozen/closed historical account records remain visible). If ledger is not owner-visible, respond 404 without revealing its existence. No cross-owner data in error/log/response.
- **Functions:** `SECURITY DEFINER` owner-scoped `wallet_my_bookmark_page(actor,limit,cursorTuple)`, `wallet_my_bookmark_status(actor,ids)`, `wallet_my_bookmark_apply(actor,transactionId,action,requestKey,fingerprint)` with fixed `search_path=public,pg_temp`, explicit role/owner checks, `REVOKE ALL ... FROM PUBLIC`, minimal `GRANT EXECUTE TO moneyverse_app`, no direct app-role SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE on bookmark/receipt tables. Never regrant unsafe `economy_post_transaction` or write to financial tables. Validate real DEV and isolated PG privilege boundary before any migration.
- **Atomic mutation:** begin one DB transaction, serialize by owner (reviewed advisory transaction lock namespace or equivalent row lock), verify owner-visible ledger transaction, check prior receipt fingerprint, enforce max 100 *under the same lock*, INSERT ... ON CONFLICT for save or DELETE for remove, persist immutable operation receipt, commit. Identical retry returns receipt; divergent key reuse 409. Concurrent saves across 100 boundary cannot exceed 100; save/remove races have deterministic commit order. Transaction errors roll back both bookmark and receipt. `asOf` and cursor tuple must originate in one consistent read statement; preserve microsecond precision. Cleanup never races with in-flight idempotency receipts.
- **Migration/rollback:** **no SQL migration number assigned now** because 5 applied checksums disagree and app-role DML is unsafe. Separate forward-only reviewed migration after SEC gate, isolated PG 17.11 replay and index EXPLAIN. Rollback feature flag OFF hides routes/CTA, leaves additive tables intact for safe later re-enable, and never deletes bookmarks or edits the ledger.

### Administration, privacy, SEO, mobile, abuse and observability
- **Privacy:** member-only bookmark membership is personal behavioral metadata; account deletion must purge or restrict per approved retention policy, with explicit backup-restore re-deletion proof. No free text, no public profile/leaderboard/analytics row-level export, no third-party tracking. Private API and Next responses `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex`; no sitemap, structured data, external share links or ISR/CDN cache.
- **Android:** app-api route allowlist must forward `Idempotency-Key`, cookie/session/CSRF context without forwarding secret headers to clients; strict generated EN/KO/JSON contract and 401/403/409/410/429/503 parity. Explicit no-store/noindex even if upstream omits it. Reject stale asynchronous results after session change.
- **Abuse controls:** target 30 reads/min, 10 writes/min per member, 1 concurrent write per member and 2s DB statement timeout, subject to real load validation. Batch ≤50, max saved 100, HMAC key rotation and constant-time verification; no high-cardinality user/transaction identifiers in logs. Admin sees only aggregated opt-in adoption, 409/429/503 counts and p50/p95 latency with k≥20 cohorts, never private saved IDs.
- **KPI/test targets (not observed):** ≥5% of active wallet-history viewers try save in 30 days (hypothesis); p95 saved-list <500ms and write <700ms, owner leakage 0, incorrect successful write indication 0, old-history regression 0. Instrument aggregate `bookmark_save_attempt/result`, `bookmark_remove_result`, `bookmarks_view_loaded` without transaction IDs; do not claim real measured lift.

### Acceptance, P0/P1 development handoff and release hold
- **PLANNED NOT RUN:** isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 with ≥10,001 synthetic ledger transactions (including inbound transfers, frozen/closed accounts, duplicate postings, same timestamps), 100 forged-owner IDs, 100 concurrent saves at 99/100 cap, 50 lost-response retries with stable UUID, 30 account-switch/session-revocation cases, invalid cursor/key/malformed-200, 2^53+ amount serialization, expiry/cleanup races, 320px/200% a11y and Android parity. Assert zero ledger/balance changes, zero app-role direct DML, no cross-owner disclosure, no false success, exact rollback.
- **P0:** `REPO1488-01` preserve 462 missing historic refs/346 nonancestor commits and protect main; `SEC1488-02` five checksum mismatches 219/220/221/234/266 and DEV support/audit direct DML; `FIN1488-03` 18,000 WLD MINT provenance, 552 Treasury links, SWF false success, Test synthetic 200; `REL1488-04` full CI (QA1379 finance 6 FAIL, lint 3), off-host restore, frontend/backend/edge exact SHA, official authenticated Test, GitOps and disk capacity (root 87%, data 94%; no destructive cleanup).
- **P1:** `BOOKMARK1488-05` owner SQL/schema/index/ACL; `BOOKMARK1488-06` serialized idempotent mutations and cap; `BOOKMARK1488-07` Nest endpoints and strict contract; `BOOKMARK1488-08` Next CTA/bookmarks/a11y; `BOOKMARK1488-09` Android/admin/privacy/SEO/KPI; `BOOKMARK1488-10` real-PG regression/rollback. **4 P0 + 6 P1 = 10**. Each runtime change must be on its own developer branch, with RED/GREEN tests, CI, isolated DB, official Test exact application SHA, independent restore and explicit approved Production promotion + smoke + rollback.
- **External independent evidence gate:** newly independently acquired/deduplicated/classified approved sources **0**, cumulative **UNKNOWN / 10,000** → **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. No claim of 10,000. Feature `wallet_activity_bookmarks` defaults **OFF**. This planning run does not merge or deploy and does not modify runtime code, SQL, DB, WLD, Test or Production.

## v2026.10.30.1498 — F675-180 Release-Readiness Evidence Matrix: Duplicate-Plan Reconciliation and Trust-Boundary Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**GitHub handoff:** [Draft PR #1964](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1964) (dependent on #1960); [planning issue #1959](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1959), historical duplicate [#1829](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1829), DEV ACL P0 [#993](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/993). **CI in progress at initial check; no final CI success claim.**

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1496 → Current Feature Improvement v1498 → Next Feature Addition v1500 (provisional). **Authority:** latest remote main 62c6cd038951d65c94b709302bdaec6e75b080b6 rechecked at start and mid-work; this is a dependent, docs-only successor to Draft PR #1960, not an implementation or merge authorization. English PROJECT_PLAN.md is canonical; Korean counterpart is equivalent.

### New source-backed P0 and duplicate-plan discovery
- **Previously missed duplicate:** [F675-170 / issue #1829](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1829) already specifies an evidence-bound admin release readiness board (v1456). F675-180 / issue #1959 (v1496) covers substantially the same operator intent. Do **not** build two dashboards, API families, ingestion pipelines or conflicting release policy engines. Treat F675-180 as the current **read-only V1 consolidation**, link F675-170 as historical requirements and potential separately approved V2 append-only evidence storage. Keep both issue histories; do not close or overwrite #1829 before its owner confirms the supersession. Canonical V1 entry is /admin/operations/release-readiness and Nest GET /api/v1/admin/operations/release-readiness. The old /admin/releases/readiness and /api/v1/admin/releases/readiness paths are **not** independently implemented, aliased or promised.
- **Policy conflicts resolved:** F675-170's WAIVED_WITH_AUTHORITY must never silently equal VERIFIED; hard financial provenance, DB privilege integrity, Test identity, independent restore and signed capacity gates are **non-waivable**. Optional/advisory gates may be NOT_APPLICABLE only with a signed, versioned release-policy rule, authorized reviewer, reason and expiry; an arbitrary UI waiver or missing source remains HOLD. F675-170's three proposed immutable DB tables are **deferred**, not silently adopted: V1 adds **zero** DB objects/grants/migrations and consumes verified existing artifacts. A separately reviewed V2 migration would require fixed ACL lineage, owner-safe SECURITY DEFINER reads, append-only receipts and unique (candidateSha,environment,policyVersion,gateId,artifactDigest) plus expiry indexes. F675-170's Android admin parity is explicitly unsupported in V1; no public/app-api release route.
- **Fresh independent QA v1384 evidence, not final QA verdict:** /srv/moneyverse-data/qa-reports/qa1384-evidence/dev-db-acl-summary.txt (08:19 KST) reports DEV PostgreSQL 253 base tables, effective moneyverse_app UPDATE on **211**, DELETE on **211**, TRUNCATE on **233**; SET ROLE moneyverse_app confirms effective UPDATE/TRUNCATE on account_balances and DELETE on audit_logs/users. This expands the previously observed audit_logs UPDATE discrepancy and conflicts with the least-privilege design stated in AGENTS.md. Counts are **effective privileges in DEV**, not proof of exploit, Production permissions, or unauthorized balance movement. No GRANT/REVOKE was executed; capture grant provenance, default privileges, schema owners, migration ordering, role inheritance and effective session role in a dedicated P0 investigation before any correction. Avoid automatically revoking grants in a live environment.
- Host 09:01 KST Debian 13.6 ROOT 97% (2.9 GiB free), DATA 97% (5.7 GiB free), later DATA 98% (5.4 GiB free): still a P0 capacity risk, but not the 08:00 snapshot's 663 MiB; no outage inferred. QA v1384 collected 50 HTTP probes and Treasury public 503 fail-closed evidence; no signed final QA v1384 verdict confirmed. Existing unresolved finance MINT 18,000 WLD, Treasury 552 rows, SWF settlement, five migration checksums, 462 historic refs and official Test exact-SHA/independent restore remain HOLD unless individually re-certified.

### Release-stage semantics and operator flow
- **Preflight and post-release are different stages.** Input: exact 40-hex candidate SHA, target environment test|production and explicit stage PREFLIGHT|POST_RELEASE. PRE-FLIGHT verifies exact candidate's full CI, official Test frontend/backend/app-api/edge image digests and authenticated negative E2E, independent off-host restore, real DB ACL/checksum, finance receipts, signed host ROOT/DATA headroom, security/privacy/a11y/SEO/mobile contracts and current protected-main/ref policy. Production baseline identity is a separate comparator; a *future candidate's* Production runtime cannot be a prerequisite for preflight or it creates an impossible circular gate. POST_RELEASE is only applicable after a separately authorized GitOps promotion and verifies the actually running Production candidate, edge/origin parity, smoke, rollback identity and monitoring. Preflight EVIDENCE_READY never means DEPLOYED or RELEASE_APPROVED.
- UI at /admin/operations/release-readiness: explicit SHA and stage selectors; gate cards show policyVersion, required/advisory, stage, environment, issuer, artifact SHA256, source-run identity, measuredAt/expiresAt, signed source revision, verdict and redacted evidence link. CTAs: Refresh (read-only), Inspect allowlisted metadata (recent step-up), Open incident, Copy redacted summary. No Approve, Merge, Deploy, Waive, Delete, Shell, DB or WLD action. States: invalid/no candidate, loading, verified, HOLD_FAIL, HOLD_UNKNOWN, STALE, SOURCE_REVOKED, POLICY_CHANGED, 401, 403, 409, 429, 503, offline and session/account switch. A prior green snapshot must be invalidated on source revocation, new blocking QA, changed policy or changed main.
- **Snapshot consistency:** Bind every observation to candidateSha, stage, environment, immutable policy digest and issuer identity. Never mix a passing backend version with an unverified frontend or ingress route (see QA #1917); require independently verified frontend, backend, app-api, edge and Test DB fingerprints. Capture source revision before/after aggregation; on revision mismatch or contradictory gate identities return 409/HOLD_UNKNOWN. No claim of global atomicity across GitHub, Test, DB, host and backup. Require a fresh final pre-promotion revalidation at the promotion boundary; a 2-second UI snapshot is not a durable release ticket.
- **Evidence trust:** GitHub required checks must be for exact candidate SHA, verified workflow/runner provenance, immutable run ID and required-check policy; document-only CI and SKIPPED jobs do not satisfy runtime gates. Reject self-reported JSON, unsigned screenshots, mutable branch names, old reruns, wrong-environment artifacts, expired/revoked keys and source time skew >30 seconds. Storage signed collector from Dev A #1956 is an upstream dependency, not reimplemented. If its key/service is unprovisioned, report UNKNOWN; never treat a fabricated sourceVerified boolean as attestation. Host evidence TTL <=120s, CI recheck <=15m, other TTL explicit and versioned; no silent extension.
- **Verdict precedence:** any mandatory verified FAIL -> HOLD_FAIL; otherwise any missing, stale, untrusted, mismatched, waived-hard-gate, conflicting or unmeasured mandatory evidence -> HOLD_UNKNOWN. Only every mandatory signed PASS in the same stage/policy/candidate yields EVIDENCE_READY, which is informational and cannot trigger promotion. Post-release status uses POST_RELEASE_VERIFIED only after independently observed actual Production identity; otherwise HOLD. Optional NOT_APPLICABLE requires signed policy rationale. Preserve failing evidence and append-only incident history.

### API, DB, security, SEO, metrics and regression
- Existing proposed Nest read-only GET accepts candidateSha (exact 40 hex), environment and stage; admin-only Session/Authenticated/Consent/Admin/AdminSession guards with recent reauth for sensitive metadata; Next same-origin private proxy. Strict response max 64 KiB, no arbitrary URL/host/path/shell input, issuer allowlists, source-specific bounded timeouts, per-admin/IP throttling and sanitized 400/401/403/409/429/503. Server derives admin identity, not a caller-provided actor. No Android/public API. Cache-Control private,no-store and X-Robots-Tag noindex on all success/error paths, no sitemap/JSON-LD, no secrets or member/WLD data in analytics, logs or copied summaries. Keyboard/focus/44px targets, WCAG 2.2 AA and 320px English/Korean admin UX.
- V1 DB: **no schema, index, migration, table grant, data transaction or WLD ledger change**. Read-only aggregation is not a PostgreSQL cross-provider transaction. Audit authorized admin reads through an existing approved redacted mechanism; no direct app-role access to audit_logs. KPI hypotheses only: false-green=0 in controlled tests, missing-proof recall=100% in fixtures, stage-misclassification=0, p95 <=2s in isolated fixtures, incident time-to-identification and issuer coverage; no observed KPI claim.
- **Planned, NOT EXECUTED:** 10,001 synthetic evidence records; 100 forged/role/cross-SHA/key-revoked cases; 100 concurrent refresh and policy flips; 50 stale/response-loss/rollback races; 30 stage/clock/edge/DB-boundary cases. Include negative tests: 211 DEV update / 233 truncate grants are not silently approved; 200 synthetic Treasury Test vs 503 public Production (#1917) must HOLD; signed host evidence missing must HOLD; doc-only CI success must HOLD; preflight must not demand future Production runtime; post-release must demand actual Production artifact; hard gate cannot be waived; F675-170 legacy route not accidentally exposed. Official Test exact-SHA, independent restore, full CI/security/real PG and authorized GitOps promotion remain separate gates. Feature flag OFF by default, rollback hides UI/API without deleting evidence; no runtime deploy by planning.

### Evidence, problem, intended users, non-duplication

- Target: authenticated operations administrator deciding what **evidence is still missing** for a specific candidate SHA. This is a *read-only evidence inspection* surface, not a deployment controller, CI orchestrator, compliance certification, financial attestation generator or automatic approval mechanism. Value hypothesis: shorten time to identify release blockers without ever showing a false green state.

### Entry, UX, CTA and state machine
- Admin console → **/admin/operations/release-readiness**. Candidate SHA input accepts exactly 40 lowercase/uppercase hexadecimal characters, canonicalized to lowercase; environment selector Test/Production (informational, cannot switch environment); gate cards show mandatory/optional policy version, source, scope, artifact digest, measuredAt, expiresAt, exact candidate SHA, verdict and linked evidence. Never default to a branch name or a previous candidate.
- Actions: **Inspect evidence** (authorized allowlisted artifact metadata only), **Refresh** (bounded read-only re-verification), **Open incident/PR** (server allowlisted GitHub links), **Copy redacted summary** (no secrets, raw host paths, member data, tokens or private payloads). No Approve, Deploy, Merge, Override, Delete, Shell, Restart, Backfill or Money actions.
- States: no candidate, invalid SHA, loading, evidence verified, FAIL, UNKNOWN, STALE, conflicting evidence, source unavailable, 401 login, 403 denied, 409 candidate/generation changed, 429 throttled, 503 verification unavailable, network offline, and logout/account-switch stale response. A missing gate is **UNKNOWN/HOLD**, never PASS or empty-green. No cross-request atomic snapshot is claimed.
- 320px mobile admin, 768px and desktop layouts, keyboard focus, visible text and not color-only statuses, 44px touch targets, screen-reader live region, reduced motion, WCAG 2.2 AA; equivalent Korean and English labels. No public sitemap entry, structured data, or search indexing.

### Evidence contract and release verdict
- Required evidence gates: (1) main SHA and protected-ref policy; (2) complete required GitHub CI with successful *required* checks on the **exact candidate SHA** and verified workflow identity; (3) authenticated official Test frontend/backend/app-api artifact digests and DB migration lineage bound to that same SHA; (4) independent off-host backup restore, integrity and rollback rehearsal; (5) PostgreSQL application ACL and applied migration checksum reconciliation; (6) MINT/SWF/Treasury provenance, real settlement receipts and negative finance tests; (7) signed host ROOT/DATA byte/inode/rollback headroom from the storage evidence contract; (8) full security, privacy, accessibility, SEO and mobile API schema contracts; (9) independent external-reference 10,000 gate **only where applicable under the reviewed release policy**. Explicitly show NOT_APPLICABLE with signed policy rationale; never silently omit.
- Each gate evidence record: gateId, policyVersion, required, environment, candidateSha, sourceKind, sourceIdentity, evidenceDigest (sha256), capturedAt, expiresAt, sourceRunId, sourceArtifactSha, signatureVerification, verdict PASS/FAIL/UNKNOWN, reasonCode, incidentRefs. The verifier binds each record to exact SHA, environment, policy and source, checks issuer allowlist, signature/key rotation, run conclusion, time window, nonces/replay, digest and immutable artifact availability. Unverified client JSON, branch names, green UI, HTTP 200 alone, cached status from another SHA, screenshots or document CI alone are **not proof**.
- Reducer: any mandatory FAIL → HOLD/FAIL; otherwise any missing/expired/unsigned/contradictory/mismatched/UNMEASURED gate → HOLD/UNKNOWN; only all mandatory verified PASS → EVIDENCE_READY (not deployment approval). A human-controlled authorized GitOps promotion remains separately required. Any new QA failure or source revocation invalidates a prior green evidence snapshot. Source-specific freshness: signed host capacity ≤120 seconds, GitHub check evidence ≤15 minutes (re-verify immutable run status), all other sources explicit policy TTL; never silently extend. Clock skew >30 seconds or unverifiable time => UNKNOWN.
- Data freshness and cross-system races: one response has server observedAt and per-gate timestamps; it does **not** assert global MVCC consistency across GitHub, DB, Test, host and backups. If source identity changes during verification, fail closed and require Refresh. Financial claims require ledger receipts and provenance, not frontend success labels.

### API, permissions, DB, transactions, security
- Proposed Nest **GET /api/v1/admin/operations/release-readiness?candidateSha=40hex&environment=test|production**, strict DTO, response {candidateSha,environment,policyVersion,observedAt,overall: HOLD_FAIL|HOLD_UNKNOWN|EVIDENCE_READY,gates:[...],nextReviewAt}. Responses: 400 invalid SHA, 401 unauthenticated, 403 not authorized, 409 candidate/policy generation conflict, 429 budget exceeded, 503 evidence verifier unavailable. 2-second response budget; no arbitrary URL, path, query, shell command or external fetch controlled by request. Next same-origin admin proxy passes only canonical allowlisted fields and auth context. No public or Android member endpoint.
- Reuse SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard, ConsentGuard, AdminGuard, AdminSessionGuard and CSRF/session protections as appropriate for the existing admin console. Step-up reauthentication is required before exposing sensitive artifact metadata; read-only redacted status remains admin-only. Bind all responses to current admin session; prevent cross-session cache reuse, replay and forged issuer. Per-admin/IP throttling and bounded 64-KiB response; sanitized errors, constant-time digest/signature comparison where relevant, no source secrets in logs.
- **V1 DB changes: NONE.** No table, index, migration, grant, SECURITY DEFINER function or WLD/ledger write. Read existing signed/immutable evidence via least-privilege source adapters, with no raw unrestricted DB table reads; a future historical receipt store requires a separate forward-only migration, explicit unique(candidateSha,environment,policyVersion,gateId,artifactDigest) and expiration index, only after the five migration lineage conflicts are resolved. No application-role broad grants.
- Read-only aggregation has no write transaction or false cross-system snapshot. No audit payload may include member balances, private session IDs, tokens, raw host paths, signing keys, full backup manifests or arbitrary GitHub private content. Aggregate telemetry only; administrative access and policy changes logged through existing approved redacted audit mechanism. Cache-Control: private, no-store; X-Robots-Tag: noindex on 200/4xx/5xx and Next proxy; Content-Security-Policy inherited, links validated.
- SEO: admin page noindex/nofollow and excluded from sitemap, no JSON-LD. KPI hypotheses: false-green count **0**, missing-evidence detection recall **100% in controlled tests**, p95 snapshot rendering <2s in isolated fixtures, mean time to locate a P0 blocker, verified source coverage and audit denial count; no invented observed baseline.

### Acceptance, deployment, rollback and coordination
- **Planned, NOT EXECUTED:** 10,001 synthetic evidence records (mixed mandatory/optional, SHA/policy/TTL), 100 forged signature/role/cross-SHA cases, 100 concurrent refreshes/candidate switches, 50 stale/timeout/response-loss cases, 30 mount/clock/rollover/rollback boundaries. ## v2026.10.30.1494 / F675-179 — Fail-closed Storage Evidence and Reserved-Block Truth (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
**GitHub:** [Draft PR #1954](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1954); depends on [v1492 PR #1949](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1949), which remains unmerged. [Development handoff #1948](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1948); [P0 capacity #1170](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1170).

**Cycle and authority.** Previous Feature Addition v1492 → Current Feature Improvement v1494 → Next Feature Addition v1496 (provisional). Latest remote main at start/mid: `62c6cd038951d65c94b709302bdaec6e75b080b6`; v1492 is a stacked draft, not merged truth. Latest independently confirmed QA v1381 FAILED/P0; later QA verdict not verified. This is a documentation-only enhancement, not an implemented or approved release.

### Fresh operational evidence and correction to v1492
- Debian 13.6 host read-only measurements 2026-10-30 07:02–07:05 KST: root **94%** used, ~**5.8 GiB** unprivileged available; DATA `/srv/moneyverse-data` **100% as rounded by df**, but ~**1.1 GiB** unprivileged available. `stat -f` on DATA: `f_blocks=51459497`, `f_bfree=2650519`, `f_bavail=287189`, fragment size 4096 bytes; free inodes ~9.15M. Reserved blocks (~9.7 GiB) are **not** general-worker/backup free space. Never interpret a rounded 100% as literally zero bytes or `f_bfree` as worker headroom. Root and DATA are distinct ext4 filesystems; no inode exhaustion was observed.
- Prior v1492 root 92%/DATA 99%/3.1 GiB were valid earlier observations, not current readings. Capacity is now **P0 EMERGENCY / RELEASE HOLD**; no verified customer outage and no cleanup performed. The dashboard itself does not recover space. Preserve all worktrees, backups, Git references, release rollback assets and owner rights; capacity recovery requires operator inventory/approval and independent restore proof.
- Existing main `backend/src/admin/api-health.controller.ts` reads `os` CPU/memory and DB ping, not host DATA mount. `frontend/src/app/api/admin/api-health/status/route.ts` returns `NextResponse.json` without explicit private/no-store/noindex headers on its success/error paths. This is a **source gap**, not proof of an observed cache leak. New storage proxy must set the headers on **every** status and test them; do not modify Development C's public status work.

- **Rapidly changing capacity:** an additional read-only `df -B1` at 2026-10-30 **07:08:30 KST** showed DATA unprivileged available **233,713,664 bytes (~223 MiB)**. The earlier ~1.1 GiB was not a durable reserve. This is an urgent P0 capacity incident, not permission for automated cleanup.

### Source-authenticated collector, freshness, arithmetic and release gate
- The response's `sourceVerified:true` is only a claim, never proof. A dedicated host-side non-root read-only collector samples fixed ROOT/DATA mount identities, captures host boot identity + monotonic sequence + UTC time, and signs canonicalized sample bytes with a collector-only protected key (e.g. Ed25519). Backend verifies a pinned public key, key id, signature, unique aliases, expected host identity/mount namespace, and stable mount IDs/devices against an operator-controlled allowlist. A container-local snapshot, replaced/missing DATA mount, duplicate alias, unexpected device or mismatched boot identity must yield **503 UNKNOWN / HOLD**. Do not leak actual host IDs or mount paths to clients. Signed data must be bound to collector identity; an unsigned `sourceKind:'HOST'` is insufficient.
- Replay/freshness: sample interval 60s, max age 120s, reject future timestamps >30s, invalid clock, stale/duplicate sequence per collector boot, replayed envelopes and source-key mismatch. In-memory last-seen sequence is best-effort; after collector/backend restart mark UNKNOWN until a newly authenticated sample arrives. If local channel or signing fails, never fall back to container `statfs` or previous green. Bounded local channel, 2s timeout, rate limits 6/min/admin and 30/min/IP; no arbitrary path input, filesystem traversal, shell, cleanup or persistent writes to the full DATA volume.
- Calculate with arbitrary-precision integers: `usedBytes=(f_blocks-f_bfree)*f_frsize`, `availableBytes=f_bavail*f_frsize`, `reservedBytes=(f_bfree-f_bavail)*f_frsize`, `totalBytes=f_blocks*f_frsize`; require `0<=f_bavail<=f_bfree<=f_blocks`, `f_frsize>0`, and finite nonzero `usedBytes+availableBytes`. `usedPercent=100*usedBytes/(usedBytes+availableBytes)` as a bounded display number. Return exact decimal strings for byte/inode fields; never round availableBytes to zero. `f_favail=0` is inode EMERGENCY; unsupported/invalid inode counters are UNKNOWN/HOLD rather than healthy. UI explicitly labels **df-rounded 100% with nonzero available bytes** and **reserved/unavailable-to-worker blocks**.
- Separate **measurement severity** from **release readiness**. EMERGENCY if used >=95% or unprivileged available <5 GiB (either mount); UNKNOWN on unverified source, missing sample, mount mismatch or invalid arithmetic. Independent release-headroom gate requires verified build peak + isolated restore working set + rollback artifact size + operator-approved safety margin on the **correct target filesystem**, compared to `f_bavail` (not `f_bfree`). Missing size evidence is UNKNOWN/HOLD, never zero. A green disk card cannot bypass finance provenance, DB ACL/checksums, full CI, independent backup restore, exact-SHA Test E2E, frontend/edge attestation or authorized Production promotion.
- Backend `GET /api/v1/admin/operations/storage` and Next proxy must enforce real admin/session/consent/admin-session checks; return only `storage-v1` allowlisted ROOT/DATA fields, server-verified severity, no paths/devices/keys/PII. All 200/401/403/429/503/flag-OFF responses: `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`, `Vary: Cookie` where cookies are used, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`; no sitemap, Android member gateway or public endpoint. Next proxy must not collapse 503 to an empty/healthy card. No HTML/script injection through errors.
- UI keeps v1492 CTAs **Refresh evidence / View read-only details / Open incident #1170**, with loading, fresh, stale, unknown, offline, unauthorized, forbidden and throttled states; after tab wake, account switch or response race discard old evidence. Korean/English equal content, 320px and 200% zoom, WCAG 2.2 AA, keyboard and aria-live, text+icon severity, 44px targets. Admin audit logs only event category, verified freshness and severity buckets; no raw mount paths or sensitive identities. KPIs (hypotheses): false-green=0, stale/forged accepted=0, unauthorized reads=0, emergency detection lag and freshness p95.
- V1 remains **ephemeral, read-only, no SQL tables/indexes/migrations/grants, no WLD/ledger writes**. Future time-series storage remains separately gated after safe capacity recovery and migration checksum repair. Flag `admin_storage_headroom` OFF by default. Rollback disables collector/route/UI without modifying host data; never auto-delete or auto-promote.

### Updated executable handoff — 4 P0 + 6 P1
- **P0 DISK1494-01:** #1170 immediate safe operator-owned capacity inventory, ownership/retention map, protected rollback/backup, explicit cleanup approval, remeasure DATA `f_bavail` and independent restore before releasing HOLD; no unowned deletion.
- **P0 SEC1494-02:** verify app-role support/audit ACL drift and five applied migration checksum mismatches against isolated DB; forward-only reviewed remediation and negative privilege tests.
- **P0 FIN1494-03:** MINT 18,000 WLD source, 552 Treasury ledger links, SWF settlement receipt and synthetic Test Treasury 200 truth; financial writes stay held.
- **P0 REL1494-04:** preserve 462 historic refs; full CI, frontend+backend+edge identity, independent restore, exact-SHA official Test and operator-signed Production gate.
- **P1 STORAGE1494-05:** host-only non-root collector, mount identity, protected signing key and source proof; reject container namespace fallback.
- **P1 STORAGE1494-06:** BigInt statfs arithmetic, reserved vs unprivileged blocks, inode unknown, 100%-rounding, threshold precedence and verified rollback-headroom inputs.
- **P1 STORAGE1494-07:** Nest admin API + Next proxy, signed-envelope verification, replay/freshness, all-response no-store/noindex, timeouts/rate limits and privacy.
- **P1 STORAGE1494-08:** bilingual admin UI with explicit 100%-but-nonzero capacity, UNKNOWN/HOLD, error/race/a11y/SEO states and read-only CTAs.
- **P1 STORAGE1494-09:** audit/KPI minimization, key rotation, Android/public negative exposure tests, flag OFF and no-schema rollback.
- **P1 STORAGE1494-10:** isolated real-host read-only contract tests + 10,001 synthetic snapshots, 100 forged/source/role, 100 concurrent, 50 stale/replayed, 30 reserved-block/mount/threshold boundaries; independent backup restore and exact-SHA Test required. **Tests specified, not executed.**
- External independent verified/deduplicated references: new approved **0**, cumulative **UNKNOWN / 10,000** ⇒ **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. No claim of 10,000 acquired. Coordinate with Gemini, Development A/B/C and QA; documentation branch only, no runtime or Production mutation.

### Source-backed gap, target and separation
- Debian 13.6 miniPC read-only `df -h` at 2026-10-30 06:09 KST: root 92% and data mount `/srv/moneyverse-data` 99% with approximately 3.1 GiB available. These are point-in-time host measurements, not forecasts or Production customer outage proof. Do not delete unowned worktrees, Git refs, backups or rollback releases.

### Entry, screen, CTA and UX states
- Admin-only `/admin/operations/storage`, linked from existing `/admin/api-health` and admin navigation. No Delete, Cleanup, Restart, Stop, shell execution, migration, file-browser, auto-remediation or approval button. A capacity card never by itself authorizes release; release gate is an independent conjunction of finance, CI, backup/restore, exact-SHA Test and authorized GitOps.
- Explicit states: loading, measured healthy, warning, critical, emergency, missing collector, stale evidence, invalid signature/source, mount-namespace mismatch, insufficient inode headroom, 401 sign-in, 403 forbidden, 429 throttled, 503 unavailable, feature OFF and offline retry. Unknown/stale is **UNKNOWN / RELEASE HOLD**, never zero or green. Retain prior verified reading only with an unmistakable stale label, not as current evidence.
- Korean/English parity, 320px single-column, 768px+ grid, 44px touch targets, keyboard focus, aria-live status, text and icons in addition to color, screen-reader units, 200% zoom, reduced motion, WCAG 2.2 AA. No indexable public route; sitemap excluded.

### Evidence collector, API and authentication
- Proposed restricted host-side collector reads only a fixed allowlist of host mount identities via `statvfs`/`statfs`; no arbitrary path or shell argument from clients, no symlink traversal, no root-command API, no scanning of backup files. Bind to host mount namespace and expected host identity, with authenticated local Unix socket or equivalent signed channel; reject container-only mount snapshots and unverified source. Bound sample size to exactly ROOT/DATA, collection interval 60s and source freshness <=120s. Collector cannot write, delete, rotate, compress or alter artifacts.
- `GET /api/v1/admin/operations/storage` behind `SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard, ConsentGuard, AdminGuard, AdminSessionGuard`; server-side admin authorization never relies on client flags. Response: `{schemaVersion:'storage-v1',observedAt,sourceKind:'HOST',sourceVerified:true,mounts:[{alias:'ROOT'|'DATA',totalBytes,usedBytes,availableBytes,usedPercent,inodeAvailable,inodeTotal,severity,headroomPolicyStatus}],freshness:'FRESH'}`. Byte counters are decimal integer strings (avoid JS unsafe integers); percentages are bounded numeric values, timestamps ISO UTC, mount list unique and exactly two. No source path, host credentials, token, member data or free-form diagnostics. No public/Android member gateway exposure.
- Errors: 401/403 access control, 429 (proposed 6 refreshes/min/admin and 30/min/IP), 503 unavailable/stale/invalid source, 2s upstream timeout; `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`, no referrer leakage. Never replace a failed read with a synthetic successful payload. Feature switch `admin_storage_headroom` defaults OFF and supports immediate code-only rollback.
- Accurate `df`-style used%: `used=f_blocks-f_bfree`, `available=f_bavail`, `usedPercent=100*used/(used+available)` when denominator positive, computed with overflow-safe integer arithmetic. Report `totalBytes=f_blocks*f_frsize`, `availableBytes=f_bavail*f_frsize`; distinguish reserved blocks. Inodes use `f_favail` when available, never assume 100% free if unsupported. Severity proposal: WARN >=80%, CRITICAL >=90%, EMERGENCY >=95% **or** unprivileged available <5 GiB; at exactly 95% emergency. Inode exhaustion independently raises EMERGENCY; unknown statfs/inode data remains explicit UNKNOWN. These are proposed thresholds pending operator approval and calibration.
- No new SQL schema, index, DB grants, migrations or WLD ledger operations in v1: evidence is ephemeral live host data, not a fabricated PostgreSQL metric. Existing audit infrastructure may record only redacted access/feature configuration metadata. If a historical series is separately approved **after disk and checksum recovery**, require a new forward-only migration with unique `(mount_alias,observed_at)`, check `0<=available<=total`, B-tree `(mount_alias,observed_at DESC)`, bounded 30-day retention and no app-role direct DML. This future schema is **not authorized by this plan**.

### Security, operations, analytics and rollout
- No arbitrary path enumeration, directory listing, file content, deletion token, backup filename, raw host identity or shell access. Protect against forged HMAC/source identity, replay, timestamp skew, SSRF, cross-tenant admin impersonation, stale UI response races, resource exhaustion and untrusted metric units. Operator access is audited without sensitive telemetry; administrators see operational aggregates, members and anonymous users see nothing.
- KPI hypotheses (not measured): >=99% of scheduled snapshots fresh, 0 false-green on stale/forged evidence, time from EMERGENCY observation to incident acknowledgement, 0 unauthorized exposure. Log only counts, latency, source verification result and severity bucket, not paths, byte-level secrets or user IDs. No email/push dispatch in v1.
- P0 disk recovery must be independently authorized with inventory, owner approval, rollback-preservation and post-action remeasurement; the dashboard **cannot** free space. Test exact-SHA gate requires independent full CI, isolated real-host read-only collector tests, backup restore and authenticated official Test E2E before any Production promotion. Rollback: disable feature, revert admin UI/API/collector, retain existing API-health and independent release HOLD; no schema down-migration.

### Concrete P0/P1 engineering backlog and acceptance
- **P0 DISK1492-01:** incident #1170 safe headroom inventory, ownership, backup/rollback retention and operator-approved recovery; no automated cleanup.
- **P0 SEC1492-02:** application DB ACL drift and five applied migration checksum mismatches, reviewed forward-only fix and negative permission tests.
- **P0 FIN1492-03:** MINT 18,000 WLD provenance, 552 Treasury linkage gaps, SWF financial failures and Test synthetic 200 truthfulness gate.
- **P0 REL1492-04:** 462 missing historic Git refs, full lint/contract/CI, independent restore, official Test exact SHA and authorized Production promotion.
- **P1 STORAGE1492-05:** fixed allowlist read-only host collector, source and namespace proof, no shell or mutation.
- **P1 STORAGE1492-06:** byte/inode arithmetic, threshold precedence, freshness and schema fail-closed.
- **P1 STORAGE1492-07:** guarded Nest API, timeout/rate/privacy response headers, no SQL grants.
- **P1 STORAGE1492-08:** Next admin route/nav and all UI/CTA/unknown/a11y states.
- **P1 STORAGE1492-09:** audit, private SEO, aggregate KPI, Android/public negative tests, flag OFF and rollback.
- **P1 STORAGE1492-10:** isolated collector and admin contract tests, 10,001 synthetic offline snapshots, 100 forged roles/sources, 100 concurrent reads, 50 stale/unavailable, 30 mount/threshold boundary cases, backup/restore and exact-SHA Test acceptance. **All acceptance tests are planned, not run.**
- Gate: external independent references new verified/deduplicated **0**, cumulative **UNKNOWN / 10,000** ⇒ **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. Do not claim 10,000 achieved. Coordinate with Development A/B/C, Gemini and QA; no overlap branch edits.
