## v1478 / F675-175 — Support Draft ACL, Deduplication and Verified Send Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Authority, cycle and coordination.** Previous Feature Addition v1476 → Current Feature Improvement v1478 → Next Feature Addition v1480 (provisional). Origin/main `54b182d2214a15974350701174175f589789613b` at start/midpoint; v1476 Draft PR #1901 is **OPEN / CONFLICTING** with main, not merged. QA v1374 candidate PR #1902 is **DRAFT / FAILED-HOLD** (broad finance selection 15 FAIL / 135 PASS / 18 SKIP after candidate patches); not an exact-SHA official Test certificate. This document extends v1476 without changing code, SQL, DB, WLD, Test or Production. [P0 support ACL #1908](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1908), [v1476 handoff #1900](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1900).

### 1. Evidence, overlap resolution and canonical ownership
- **Verified DEV-only P0:** PostgreSQL 17.11 `woldeok_moneyverse_dev` has 3 support threads, 6 support messages and no draft table. `information_schema.role_table_grants` shows `moneyverse_app` directly holds SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE, TRUNCATE, REFERENCES and TRIGGER on **both** `public.support_threads` and `public.support_messages`, grantor `postgres`. This conflicts with explicit `REVOKE ALL` in migration 196 and 302. No support content was read; Production ACL, exploit and cause remain **UNKNOWN**. No blind SQL revocation: snapshot role/default grants, actual migration history and effective privileges; independently test a forward-only remediation in isolated PG before authorized release. App-role direct table access must be denied and owner-scoped `SECURITY DEFINER` functions must remain usable.
- **Verified source send gap:** `frontend/src/app/support/actions.ts` generates `randomUUID()` **inside each** `createSupportThread`/`replySupportThread` invocation. A lost response followed by another invocation uses a different key and can create a duplicate. Migration 196's `support_create_thread` same-key replay checks actor only, **not subject/body**; `support_add_message` checks body, but its pre-insert lookup has a concurrent unique-key race requiring explicit testing. Migration 302's `support_my_message_receipt` requires a known message UUID; it cannot resolve an unknown-result send by idempotency key. These are source/contract gaps, **not evidence of duplicate Production messages**.
- **Three overlapping plans must become ONE implementation:** earlier F675-139 / issue #1456 already specified draft recovery plus atomic send, F675-164 / issue #1758 proposed private drafts, and F675-175 / issue #1900 repeated draft persistence. Do not create three draft tables, independent send endpoints or flags. F675-175 is the latest **hardening overlay**; reconcile against F675-139's atomic send and F675-164's schema. Reuse one owner-scoped `support_private_drafts` rollout flag and one canonical API. Earlier plans proposed **30-day** retention; v1476 proposed **7-day**. Proposed privacy-minimizing default is 7 days, but retention/notice/legacy migration is **DECISION PENDING** (product/privacy owner), not silently approved. No migration or deletion until resolved.

### 2. Member flow, UI and explicit send states
- Entry remains `/support` new-thread and `/support?thread=<owned UUID>` reply. CTAs **Save draft**, **Resume**, **Discard**, **Send**, plus **Check send status** and **Retry same send** only after an uncertain result. Opt-in 800ms autosave remains OFF by default. An existing draft with unsaved edits must be explicitly saved/verified before **Send saved draft**, or user may select **Send current text without consuming saved draft** (no implicit cleanup). Never silently transmit an autosave or substitute stale server text.
- States: `not_saved`, `saving`, `saved(revision,time)`, `conflict`, `expired`, `offline`, `unavailable`, `disabled`, `send_pending`, `send_uncertain`, `send_committed(receipt)`, `send_rejected`. On 409 show local and remote revision/timestamps and non-destructive review; on timeout preserve input and send intent; on 401/logout/account switch clear in-memory plaintext and invalidate pending response. Do not label a 404/timeout as an unsent guarantee. 320px, 44px touch targets, keyboard/screen reader status, WCAG 2.2 AA, reduced motion, Korean/English copy and Android parity; noindex/nofollow and private no-store for member pages/APIs.

### 3. API and replay contract (proposed; no deployed endpoint)
- Preserve owner-only GET/PUT/DELETE `/api/v1/support/drafts` with `contextType,threadId?,expectedRevision` (decimal-string bigint), `requestId` (UUID), bounded subject/body and receipt. Add **one** POST `/api/v1/support/drafts/send` (not a second independently operating send path) with `{contextType,threadId?,expectedRevision,sendRequestId,subject?,body}` and owner-scoped GET `/api/v1/support/send-receipts/:sendRequestId`. A verified session derives actor; no `userId` payload. Next same-origin BFF and Android app-api use the same DTO, identity/consent, CSRF or bearer and error semantics. Existing direct support send routes must either delegate to the same receipt contract or remain explicitly separate without claiming draft cleanup.
- Generate a cryptographically random `sendRequestId` **once per logical user-confirmed send**, before network submission, bind it to actor/session generation/context and canonical payload digest, and reuse the identical key for uncertain retries; do not generate a fresh UUID inside a retried server action. Same key+same payload returns the **original immutable** `threadId/messageId/committedAt` receipt; same key+different payload/context/actor is rejected (409 or non-disclosing 404 for foreign owner). Server receipt lookup by owner+key must not require the client to know a thread/message UUID. A `NOT_FOUND` receipt check while the original request is in flight is **not proof of non-commit**; retry only with the same key and payload. Expired receipt retention cannot guarantee dedupe beyond its published 30-day window; do not automatically reuse an expired key.
- Error mapping: 400 invalid canonical input; 401 unauthenticated; 403 consent/active-user denial; 404 unknown/foreign context or receipt without existence oracle; 409 revision/key/payload conflict; 410 expired owned draft; 413 oversized; 429 rate limited; 503 unavailable key/DB. Enforce strict DTO allowlist, Unicode NFC/trim normalization with consistent JS/PostgreSQL length checks, request size/time limits, per-owner/IP budgets, response `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, redacted logs and no raw message text/keys in analytics. Keep existing support history read API backward-compatible.

### 4. DB, encryption, atomicity and retention (implementation contract)
- **No migration number reserved** until the five applied DEV checksum mismatches and #1878 lineage are reconciled. One forward-only schema: `support_message_drafts` owner/context unique, revision BIGINT monotonic, ciphertext/nonce/tag/key_version, owner+expiry and expiry+owner indexes; one owner-scoped send-intent/receipt store with `UNIQUE(owner_id,send_request_id)`, canonical request HMAC, immutable result identity, status and 30-day receipt retention. Scope request IDs by owner rather than globally to avoid cross-owner existence leakage; enforce 20 active contexts under owner-row lock. Preserve tombstone/generation after send/discard/expiry to reject stale autosave (no ABA resurrection).
- Backend AES-256-GCM encryption uses a fresh 96-bit nonce and 128-bit tag. AAD binds actor, context and the **proposed next revision = expectedRevision+1** before encryption; DB CAS checks that expected revision still matches before accepting ciphertext. On conflict discard the attempted ciphertext; never reuse nonce. Key loss fails closed; key version/rotation and backup-restored tombstones require proof. No admin decrypt/search, no app-role table DML/SELECT/TRUNCATE, no PUBLIC function EXECUTE; grant narrow owner-checked functions only.
- **Atomic send of saved draft:** lock owner → context/draft → send-intent row in a consistent order, verify active member/owned reply thread, expected revision, payload digest and send key; write the existing support thread/message, immutable send receipt and matching draft tombstone in **one DB transaction**. If draft revision advanced, return 409 with **no send and no deletion**; if send already committed, return original receipt without writing another message; if response lost, owner-bound receipt lookup/retry returns original result. A direct send of unsaved current text must not delete a newer saved draft. Fix/cover `support_create_thread` same-key different-subject/body replay and concurrent insert races without editing applied migration 196; avoid granting broad DML as a workaround.
- Deletion/expiry/consent withdrawal/owner deactivation must prevent plaintext read immediately; ciphertext purge and tombstones survive backup restore under approved retention. No silent shortening from earlier 30-day plans to 7 days until retention policy is signed off. Never infer that encryption is end-to-end or that app compromise cannot access drafts.

### 5. Admin, security, SEO, analytics, tests and release
- Admin sees only privacy-thresholded aggregate save/restore/send uncertainty/failure counts and rollout status, not draft contents, member IDs, keys, receipts or plaintext. KPI hypotheses: zero duplicate send under replay, zero foreign-owner draft/receipt reads, zero unintended sends, p95 save <500ms on representative isolated Test, reduced uncertain-send support tickets; not measured. Security alarms redact content and tokens. Preserve SEO noindex/no-store, no sitemap, and accessible conflict/retry UI.
- **Proposed acceptance, NOT EXECUTED:** isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 with 10,001 synthetic draft contexts; 100 forged-owner/CSRF/access checks; 100 concurrent save/discard/send races; 50 lost-response replays; 30 same-key/different-payload and simultaneous same-key inserts; 30 expiry/key-rotation/account-switch/backup-restore cases; test original receipt after later revision, no duplicate support message, stale autosave tombstone, 20-context cap, foreign-owner non-disclosure, app-role table privilege DENIED, ADMIN content DENIED, and zero WLD/ledger changes. Verify Nest/Next/Android contracts and 320px/a11y. Explicitly test migration 196/302 source-vs-DEV privilege discrepancy in isolated DB.
- **P0 backlog:** `REPO1478-01` preserve historic refs and reconcile conflicting predecessor PR #1901 without force-push/overwrite; `SEC1478-02` investigate/remediate support-table ACL drift #1908 and five migration checksums; `FIN1478-03` retain Treasury/Mint #1895/#1896 and Central Bank/SWF #1902 fail-closed, 18,000 WLD MINT and 552 Treasury provenance; `REL1478-04` full CI, independent backup restore, authenticated official Test exact-SHA, GitOps approval and rollback. **P1:** `DRAFT1478-05` deduplicate F675-139/164/175 and resolve 7-vs-30-day policy/schema; `DRAFT1478-06` atomic saved-draft send, stable key, owner receipt and ABA fencing; `DRAFT1478-07` Nest/Next/Android API contract; `DRAFT1478-08` member conflict/uncertain-send/a11y; `DRAFT1478-09` admin/privacy/SEO/KPI; `DRAFT1478-10` real PG concurrency, privilege, replay and rollback tests. All engineering in separate branch; feature OFF, clean exact-SHA official Test and authorized Production promotion only. No runtime or DB changes in this planning cycle. External independently deduplicated approved references **new 0; cumulative UNKNOWN / 10,000**, gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**.


## v1476 / F675-175 — Owner-Private Support Message Drafts (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Cycle, evidence and limits.** Previous Feature Improvement v1474 → Current Feature Addition v1476 → Next Feature Improvement v1478 (provisional). Authoritative origin/main at start and midpoint: `0dffb939676e6cfd861d19f96208f8929902d1f9`; v1474 Draft #1892 is not assumed merged. Latest completed independent QA v1373 FAILED/P0 (2026-10-29 21:35 KST), and new financial fail-open regression #1895. Source/DEV DB were inspected read-only. [Development handoff #1900](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1900). This is **planning only**; no runtime, SQL, WLD, feature switch, official Test or Production change.

### Source gap, target, value and scope
- `frontend/src/app/support/member-support-forms.tsx` has `useActionState` forms for new-thread and reply with no persisted draft action or autosave; `backend/src/support/support.controller.ts` has thread/message send, list and transcript but no draft API. Migration `196-user-admin-support-chat.sql` stores submitted threads/messages. DEV PostgreSQL: 3 support threads, 6 messages, `support_message_drafts` table absent; **not** a Production count or proof of unmet demand.
- Target: authenticated member composing a sensitive administrator inquiry on mobile or across devices. F675-175 stores only **unsent** new-thread/reply drafts. Distinct from existing submitted support threads, proposed F675-104 case escalation, F675-105 evidence vault, and F675-173 privacy export. No support-agent draft access, attachments, automatic send, escalation, notification dispatch, balance change, refund or claim.
- Options considered: browser-only localStorage (simple but leaks across shared-device profiles and cannot sync), server-side plaintext draft table (simple but broadens sensitive DB exposure), and **recommended** server-encrypted owner-scoped drafts (cross-device sync with explicit TTL and access gate). Crypto/key operations remain backend-only, outside PostgreSQL; key management must be proven before enabling.

### Member UI, entry, actions and states
- Entry: existing `/support` new inquiry form and `/support?thread=<owned-uuid>` reply form; no new public route. CTAs **Save draft**, **Resume**, **Discard**, **Send** (existing send contract unchanged). Autosave is **opt-in**, 800ms debounce, and visibly labeled; off by default. Never turn Save into Send. Max 20 active contexts per owner, exactly one new-thread draft and one per owned reply thread, 7-day expiry after last verified save; body 1–2,000 Unicode characters, new-thread subject 1–120. Empty form is discarded explicitly, not silently uploaded.
- Display `not_saved`, `saving`, `saved` with server timestamp/revision, `conflict`, `expired`, `offline`, `unavailable`, `disabled`, `sending`, `sent_verified` and `send_uncertain`. A network timeout must **not** say saved or sent; preserve local input and offer retry using the same request UUID. 401 sign-in, 403 owner/consent denial, 404 deleted/foreign thread, 409 stale revision, 410 expired, 413 oversized, 429 throttled, 503 backend/key unavailable. On account switch/logout, cancel requests, clear in-memory sensitive content and fence stale responses.
- On two-device conflict show remote update time, offer **Review server draft** or **Keep local copy** (explicit user action to reconcile); never silently last-write-wins or overwrite remote draft. A confirmed send may clear only the matching draft revision, not a newer concurrent edit; an uncertain send must retain the draft until existing immutable support-message receipt verifies completion. No WLD receipt is involved.

### API, identity, encryption and abuse controls
- NestJS: `GET /api/v1/support/drafts?contextType=new_thread|reply&threadId=<uuid>`; `PUT /api/v1/support/drafts` with `{contextType,threadId?,subject?,body,expectedRevision,requestId}`; `DELETE /api/v1/support/drafts` with `{contextType,threadId?,expectedRevision,requestId}`. `expectedRevision` and returned `revision` are decimal **strings** (bigint); first save expects `"0"`. Response includes `contextType,threadId?,revision,updatedAt,expiresAt,receiptId`; GET additionally includes decrypted own subject/body. Server derives actor from verified session, never accepts `userId` in payload. Strict DTO allowlist; reject extraneous keys, invalid UUIDs, oversized strings, disallowed Unicode control characters. Same-origin Next BFF under `/api/support/drafts` requires session/consent and CSRF for writes; Android `/app-api/v1/support/drafts` uses its verified bearer identity, not web cookie trust. Route-map and app-api schema parity required.
- Retry with identical `requestId` and payload returns the original immutable receipt even if a later revision exists; same key/different payload returns 409. GET returns 404 for foreign or missing reply context without revealing existence; 410 for an expired own draft only when authorized. Per-owner proposed 30 writes/minute and 60 reads/minute, per-IP cap, size limits, request-body timeout, and encryption service circuit breaker; tune from measured traffic. All API responses `Cache-Control: private, no-store`; private page noindex/nofollow, sitemap excluded. Never log plaintext drafts, ciphertext, raw receipt keys, tokens or message content.
- Backend encrypts UTF-8 JSON `{subject?,body}` with AES-256-GCM using random 96-bit nonce, 128-bit tag and versioned key reference from an approved secret manager; authenticated associated data binds owner, context and revision. No key stored in PostgreSQL or browser. Fail closed if key unavailable; define key rotation, lost-key recovery behavior and purge of expired ciphertext. Do not claim end-to-end encryption or admin-resistant encryption against a compromised application server.

### Database, transaction, ownership and cleanup
- **Proposed only**, forward-only migration after source/applied migration parity and five checksum mismatches are resolved. `support_message_drafts(owner_id uuid REFERENCES users(id), context_type CHECK IN ('new_thread','reply'), context_id uuid NOT NULL, ciphertext bytea NOT NULL, nonce bytea CHECK octet_length=12, tag bytea CHECK octet_length=16, key_version text NOT NULL, revision bigint NOT NULL CHECK(revision>0), updated_at timestamptz, expires_at timestamptz, PRIMARY KEY(owner_id,context_type,context_id))`. Use all-zero UUID sentinel for new-thread and CHECK enforcing sentinel iff new_thread; reply context must refer to an existing `support_threads.id` owned by actor. Index `(owner_id,expires_at)` and bounded cleanup `(expires_at,owner_id)`; no global raw-content search index.
- `support_draft_operation_receipts(request_id uuid PRIMARY KEY, owner_id uuid NOT NULL, context_type,context_id, operation CHECK IN ('save','discard','sent_cleanup'), request_hmac bytea NOT NULL, resulting_revision bigint, receipt_id uuid UNIQUE, committed_at timestamptz)` is append-only; keep 30 days for retry proof even after 7-day draft expiry, then approved retention purge. No raw text in receipts. SQL `SECURITY DEFINER` functions with pinned search_path and explicit actor ownership; `REVOKE ALL FROM PUBLIC,moneyverse_app` on tables, grant only narrow EXECUTE. DB transaction locks the owner account row to enforce the 20-context cap across concurrent new contexts, then the owner/context row; validates revision, verifies active account and reply ownership, updates ciphertext/expiry and inserts immutable receipt atomically; same-key replay reads receipt before mutation. Never grant app direct table DML.
- Deletion and send cleanup require compare-and-swap revision. Concurrent save vs discard vs send must produce one committed revision outcome; stale requests 409 with no data loss. Expired/deleted account drafts are unavailable immediately; asynchronous retention cleanup is idempotent, auditable by counts only and backup-restore aware. Privacy access/export may include own draft only under separately reviewed lawful scope; administrator never sees draft text.

### Admin, analytics, accessibility, SEO and acceptance
- Admin: only anonymous aggregate draft-save failure/latency/expiry counts and rollout switch; no per-member draft read, search, impersonation, content export or automatic case creation. Security alerts redact IDs and content. KPIs (hypotheses, not measured): resumed-draft completion, save p95 <500ms on representative Test, conflict/429/503 rate, zero foreign-owner reads, zero unintended sends. Analytics never stores subject/body/ciphertext.
- Web 320px single column, 44px tap targets, visible label and keyboard focus, screen-reader `role=status` for saved/conflict, clear draft expiry copy, reduced motion, WCAG 2.2 AA; Android native secure storage only for pending in-flight input and purge on logout/account switch. Prevent cached draft display on browser Back navigation after logout.
- **Proposed tests, NOT RUN:** isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 with 10,001 synthetic drafts; 100 cross-owner/forged-thread/CSRF probes; 100 concurrent save/delete/send races; 50 lost-response/idempotency replays; 30 TTL/account-switch/key-rotation cases; verify no app-role direct SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE, no admin read, no WLD/ledger change, Next/Nest/Android parity, a11y, noindex/no-store, receipt exact replay, backup restore and feature-OFF fallback. Do not allocate a migration number until #1878 and applied checksum lineage are verified.
- **Release HOLD:** QA v1373 P0 #1895/#1896 unverified Treasury/Mint activation, pre-existing Central Bank/SWF fail-closed failures, 18,000 WLD MINT provenance, 552 Treasury links, five DEV migration checksum mismatches and audit_logs app-role DML/TRUNCATE, historic branch preservation #1663, independent backup restore and official authenticated Test exact-SHA. New code only via independent development branch, clean full CI, real PG security/finance regression, exact-SHA official Test, authorized GitOps and rollback. Feature OFF by default, rollback hides draft UI/API while preserving encrypted data until controlled purge; never silently drop user drafts.
- **P0 backlog:** REPO1476-01 historic refs/PR preservation; FIN1476-02 Treasury/Mint fail-closed, Central Bank/SWF and provenance; SEC1476-03 migration checksum/ACL/schema lineage; REL1476-04 full CI, restore, official exact-SHA Test and GitOps. **P1 backlog:** DRAFT1476-05 schema/ACL/TTL; DRAFT1476-06 transaction/idempotent receipt; DRAFT1476-07 Nest/Next/Android API; DRAFT1476-08 member UX/a11y; DRAFT1476-09 privacy/admin/KPI; DRAFT1476-10 isolated PG/security/rollback. Ten unique identifiers mirrored in Korean. External independent deduplicated verified references: newly approved **0**, cumulative **UNKNOWN / 10,000**, gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**.


## v1474 / F675-174 — Notification Search Snapshot, Unicode and Cursor Safety (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
**GitHub:** [Draft PR #1892](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1892) OPEN / unmerged; [development issue #1887](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1887) updated. Rebasing onto latest main `ba18116a0f94ee4c2018cbb37fc7e161b1cd97c5`; documentation CI policy/classify SUCCESS, runtime-check SKIPPED. RELEASE HOLD.

**Cycle / authority.** Previous Feature Addition v1472 → Current Feature Improvement v1474 → Next Feature Addition v1476 (provisional). Baseline and mid-run `main` `f9fd777458b89b3c33593ae0cad46a37b216474b`; QA v1372 FAILED/P0 (evidence from its earlier main `69c4867…`, not a test of the present main). v1472 design was merged as PR #1888, but **not implemented**. Source inspection: `backend/src/notification/{notification.dto.ts,notification.controller.ts,notification.service.ts}`, `frontend/src/app/{api/notifications/route.ts,account/notifications/notification-tabs-bar.tsx}`; no new search endpoint/SQL observed. Work remains docs-only; [handoff #1887](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1887).

### Failure modes corrected in the v1472 design
1. **An as-of timestamp is not a cross-request database snapshot.** The v1472 keyset cursor excludes ordinary new rows with later `created_at`, but a privileged backdated insert, deletion, or `unreadOnly`/mark-read race can alter membership. Guarantee only deterministic `(created_at DESC,id DESC)` traversal of a *stable matching set*, not immutable results or exact-once delivery under concurrent writes. Return `asOfUtc` as an informational server cutoff, never label it a PostgreSQL MVCC snapshot. A frozen result set requires an independently approved, bounded materialized snapshot design; it is **not** silently added here. On an explicit member read action, category/keyword/date change, session change or account switch, discard the cursor and restart. Where a verified owner notification read/change epoch exists, bind it to the cursor and return 409 `RESULT_SET_CHANGED` when it advances; until then display “results may change; refresh search” and do not claim stable unread-only pagination.
2. **Short Hangul/literal substring queries are a query-plan hazard.** The proposed 2–80 Unicode-scalar keyword contract remains an input *intent*, not a promise of indexed performance. NFC-normalize on the server; trim/collapse Unicode whitespace, reject control/bidi override/invisible-only input, enforce 2–80 code points and ≤320 UTF-8 bytes, and define literal case-insensitive title/body matching with `%`, `_` and `\\` escaped (never regex/SQL concatenation). Compare canonicalized filters, not raw JSON. `pg_trgm` is absent from inspected DEV; 2-character or punctuation-heavy patterns may not have selective trigrams. Require a measured indexed EXPLAIN plan per supported query class, with hard per-owner/date bound and 2-second statement timeout. When a safe plan is unavailable, return coded 422 `QUERY_TOO_BROAD` (with accessible suggestion to narrow dates or add characters) or 503 `SEARCH_UNAVAILABLE` for missing index/extension. Never silently scan all users, truncate hits, or report a false empty result.
3. **Cursor must bind a single canonical request and precise DB tuple.** Versioned base64url HMAC-SHA-256 envelope `{v,kid,ownerHash,sessionGeneration,filterDigest,asOfUtc,anchorCreatedAt,anchorId,lastCreatedAt,lastId,expiresAt}`; `ownerHash` is server-side and not reversible to the user ID. Reject unknown versions/kids, oversized cursor (>2048 bytes), malformed base64, noncanonical timestamps, bad signatures (constant-time comparison), expiry and revoked session. Rotate signing keys with bounded 10-minute verification overlap, never accept an untrusted `kid` as an algorithm selector. Canonical filter digest includes keyword, category, unreadOnly, inclusive local-date intent resolved to UTC `[from,to)`, limit and stable sort; server controls `asOfUtc` and anchor. Preserve PostgreSQL microsecond timestamp precision end-to-end (do not round-trip through JS Date milliseconds). Query `LIMIT pageSize+1`, emit exactly pageSize, `hasMore` only from extra row, cursor from the last **emitted** tuple. A `created_at` tie must use UUID `id` comparison in the same PostgreSQL order; no offset fallback.
4. **Authentication and privacy boundaries.** Nest `POST /api/v1/notifications/search` inherits `SessionGuard`, `AuthenticatedGuard`, `ConsentGuard`; Next same-origin POST BFF verifies viewer, JSON content type, Fetch Metadata/CSRF, and never forwards user-provided actor IDs. Android uses bearer-bound session identity and its own anti-replay controls, not web cookies. Read-only POST is never confused with the existing `POST /api/notifications` read mutation. Unknown/foreign cursor and owner/session mismatch return a uniform coded rejection without exposing whether a notification exists. Private no-store/noindex/nofollow, strict response-size cap, no query/body/cursor in URL, referrer, logs, metrics or analytics; internal destination links require the current allowlist and access check at click time. Search does not mark read, claim WLD, send a notice or write a ledger entry.
5. **Read-only query and rollout integrity.** Search executes in a bounded READ ONLY transaction; owner-scoped `SECURITY DEFINER` with pinned search_path and reviewed EXECUTE-only grants, not a blanket table GRANT. No new search-history table. Confirm the existing `in_app_notifications` DDL lineage and DEV role permissions before any forward SQL; independently test query plan with skewed owners and Korean two-syllable terms. Missing provenance/index, failed privilege negative, 429/503 overload, or unverified 90-day retention must keep feature OFF. Legacy offset inbox and mark-read flow remain unchanged on rollback; search cursors become invalid without dropping existing notices.

### Member UX, admin, SEO, KPI and tests
- Search UI preserves existing inbox as default, explicit Search/Clear/Load more; initial/loading/empty/results/error/offline/logout/expired cursor/changed set/too-broad/index-unavailable states must be distinct. A stale response from a prior account/filter/generation must never append. On `409 RESULT_SET_CHANGED` show “Refresh search” rather than silently continuing; on `410 CURSOR_EXPIRED` restart from first page with same visible filters; on 422 preserve input and suggest narrowing. Accessible Korean/English copy, 320px layout, 44px targets, keyboard focus, TalkBack/VoiceOver, WCAG 2.2 AA. Never show unverified exact totals or financial success from notification text.
- Administrator sees coarse, k-anonymized query-plan/latency/error counters only, with suppression for small groups and no keyword, user ID, cursor, body, raw IP or joinable fine-grained buckets. Member page and API excluded from sitemap/index; `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, robots and browser-cache negatives. Hypothesis KPIs: p95 ≤500ms in isolated representative data, zero cross-owner rows, 0 ledger delta, 0 unsafe query plans, user task success and accessible recovery, with no claim of measured adoption.
- **Proposed, NOT EXECUTED:** isolated PostgreSQL 17 with 10,001 synthetic notifications and skewed owner distributions; 100 owner/cursor forgery tests; 100 concurrent read/insert/delete races; 50 expiry/key-rotation/revocation/network cases; 30 timezone/DST and timestamp-microsecond tie cases; 30 Korean 2-syllable, punctuation, wildcard and Unicode confusable cases; query-plan and timeout negatives; 320px/keyboard/screen-reader tests; Android/Next/Nest parity, feature OFF/rollback, legacy read-all receipt and zero WLD regression. Explicitly verify stable-set pagination has no duplicate/skip, and document changed-set behavior as best-effort unless read epoch certified.

### Current P0/P1 engineering handoff and release gates

Public Test/Production `/api/version` observed `c2129625aa6629c616fb31ce790950618b8d92b2`, not current main. No candidate release approval.
- **P1 NOTIFSEARCH1474-05:** owner-only SQL/index provenance and 2-char Hangul query-plan gate; **P1 NOTIFSEARCH1474-06:** canonical filters, HMAC cursor, timestamp precision and read-state race contract; **P1 NOTIFSEARCH1474-07:** Nest/Next/Android authentication and coded API; **P1 NOTIFSEARCH1474-08:** member UX/a11y/error recovery; **P1 NOTIFSEARCH1474-09:** privacy/admin/noindex/KPI/abuse controls; **P1 NOTIFSEARCH1474-10:** real PostgreSQL adversarial regression, feature-OFF rollback and exact-SHA Test handoff.
- **External evidence gate:** independently obtained/deduplicated/approved new 0, cumulative UNKNOWN / 10,000; `BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS`. ## v1472 / F675-174 — Verified Member Notification Search & Stable History (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Authority, scope and cycle.** Previous Feature Improvement v1470 → Current Feature Addition v1472 → Next Feature Improvement v1474 (provisional). Authoritative main at start and mid-run: `69c486777d023ee45a216528e7a0c8b7dd1d7ea7`; latest completed independent QA v1371 FAILED/P0; v1372 collecting, not yet a completed verdict. Source and DEV database inspected read-only. This is **documentation only**: no app, database, WLD, feature-switch, Test or Production change. Development handoff: [issue #1887](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1887).

### Source-backed gap, scope and non-duplication
- `frontend/src/app/account/notifications/page.tsx` offers an authenticated notification centre and a goal/NPC toggle; `notification-tabs-bar.tsx` offers category tabs, mark-read, 20-item offset pages and a 5,000 offset cap, but no keyword/date search. `backend/src/notification/notification.dto.ts` only validates category, unreadOnly, limit and offset; `notification.service.ts` directly queries `in_app_notifications` ordered by `created_at DESC,id DESC`. No inspected search/cursor contract exists. This is a source gap, **not** evidence of Production user demand or privacy violation.
- DEV PostgreSQL 17.11: `public.in_app_notifications` exists, **0 rows at inspection**; columns `id,user_id,category,title,body,link,dedupe_key,is_read,read_at,created_at`; indexes `idx_in_app_notif_user_created`, `idx_in_app_notif_user_unread`, partial unique `idx_in_app_notif_dedupe` and PK. Existing `moneyverse_app` SELECT/INSERT privileges are observed, **not approved to expand or silently revoke**. `pg_trgm` is **not installed** (only `pgcrypto`, `plpgsql`). No defining CREATE TABLE migration for this table was found in inspected init/migrations; reconcile actual schema lineage before forward SQL. DEV 0 rows does not imply Production 0.
- F675-101 verified inbox, F675-102 consent/quiet-hours and F675-103 opt-in digest are distinct existing plans. F675-174 adds **read-only search and reliable deep history**, not push/email dispatch, quiet-hour changes, notification creation, claim-all, automatic mark-read, financial transfers or retention deletion. Never treat notification text as verified WLD settlement evidence.

### Persona, entry and complete screen/CTA states
- Target: authenticated member searching their own historical notices. Entry: existing `/account/notifications` (not a public indexable page). UI: keyword field (2–80 NFC code points), category dropdown (existing allowlist), unread-only checkbox, UTC date range with local-time display (maximum 90-day inclusive user selection translated into `[fromUtc,toUtc)`), Search, Clear, Load more, and link to the underlying verified destination. Default view remains the existing inbox; search is explicitly user-initiated.
- States: initial/default, validating, loading, results with matched count **only if DB has verified total**, no results, no notifications, partial network failure (no fabricated empty state), offline, 401 re-login, 403 access denied, 409 filter/cursor mismatch, 410 cursor expired (restart with same filters), 429 throttled, 503 search/index unavailable, feature disabled (fall back to old inbox), and logout/account-switch stale-response fencing. Search never auto-marks read. A search result displays actual category, timestamp, read state and a server-allowlisted internal link; no fabricated amount, status or action CTA.
- Responsive: 320px single-column and 768px/desktop; 44px touch targets, keyboard focus, visible labels, accessible status/live region, screen-reader result counts and error recovery, reduced motion and WCAG 2.2 AA. Korean/English labels; Android app-api must expose the same authenticated read-only semantics without relying on web cookies.

### API, authorization and search semantics
- NestJS `POST /api/v1/notifications/search` **read-only** JSON body `{query,category?,unreadOnly?,fromUtc,toUtc,limit?,cursor?}`; no sensitive keywords in URL/query strings, referrers or access logs. Next same-origin `POST /api/notifications/search` with authenticated session, CSRF and no-store; Android bearer/session-bound app-api route with its platform auth and anti-replay controls. Response `{items:[{id,category,title,body,link,isRead,createdAt}],nextCursor,hasMore,asOfUtc}`. Default 20, max 50; no false total count. Strict DTO allowlist; reject HTML/SQL syntax as **data**, never interpolate SQL; normalize NFC, trim whitespace, enforce length 2–80, 90-day max, dates ISO UTC and `fromUtc<toUtc`. No arbitrary sort, cross-account userId, wildcard regex or unbounded offsets. Existing inbox endpoint remains unchanged.
- Errors: 400 invalid input, 401 unauthenticated, 403 owner/consent denied, 409 cursor filter/session generation mismatch, 410 expired cursor, 429 rate budget, 503 unverified schema/index/query plan. Per-member and per-IP rate budget (proposed 20 searches/minute/member, 60/minute/IP; tune with measured load), 2-second DB statement timeout, bounded returned text and payload. All responses `Cache-Control: private, no-store`; redact keyword, body, member ID, raw cursor and PII from logs, analytics and exception messages.
- Server obtains actor from verified session, **never** accepts actor/userId in payload. Design reviewed `SECURITY DEFINER` owner-scoped read function with pinned search_path and least-privilege EXECUTE; do not grant new app-role table DML/SELECT. Validate current direct SELECT/INSERT provenance separately. Every returned ID and link is owner-scoped and link must pass the existing internal route allowlist; no arbitrary URL or external redirect.

### DB, indexes, cursor, transaction and concurrency
- First reconcile schema provenance, existing permissions and duplicate migration IDs 291–295; no edits to applied migrations or production checksum history. In a **new uniquely numbered forward-only migration only after #1878 is resolved**, add reviewed owner/date/id B-tree index `(user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` if missing; preserve existing PK, dedupe index and notification writes. For Korean substring matching, evaluate `pg_trgm` in isolated PG and index title/body with a reviewed extension/GIN plan; **DEV does not currently have pg_trgm**. Do not silently scan an unbounded owner history or truncate matches; if safe indexed search cannot be proven, return 503 `SEARCH_UNAVAILABLE` and keep legacy inbox. Benchmark explain-analyze on 10,001 synthetic rows and skewed owner distributions.
- Read-only function uses `WHERE user_id=actor`, category/unread/date filters, normalized literal keyword semantics and keyset `(created_at,id)<(cursorCreatedAt,cursorId)`, ordered `created_at DESC,id DESC`, LIMIT `pageSize+1`. Cursor is authenticated HMAC (rotatable key-id) binding actor, session generation, exact filter digest, `asOfUtc`, last tuple and 10-minute expiry; verify constant-time before SQL. Cursor is opaque, never stored in analytics. Initial `asOfUtc` is DB server time, and rows with `created_at>asOfUtc` are excluded. New post-snapshot notifications cannot shift earlier pages; read/unread state changes or backdated privileged inserts can change membership, so **do not claim a full immutable MVCC snapshot across requests**. Return honest reset guidance on changes.
- Search has **no write transaction, mutation receipt or ledger effect**; a signed response/cursor is a read-continuation proof, not a financial or delivery receipt. DB functions execute in a read-only transaction with statement timeout and query plan gate. No new per-member search history table; optional aggregate telemetry uses coarse buckets without keyword/body/user IDs. Rollback removes new route/feature flag but preserves old inbox and existing notifications; forward schema objects remain until independently reviewed cleanup.

### Admin, privacy, SEO, observability and KPI
- Admin may see only aggregated usage, query latency/error rate and feature availability; no member keyword, notice body, per-member result or cursor. Audit access-denied and configuration/rollout events with redacted metadata; abuse defense includes rate limiting, cursor tampering rejection, input/CPU limits, and session revocation fencing. Treat notification text as personal data, including security/transactional content; retention follows existing privacy policy, no new automatic purge.
- Private member page/API: `noindex,nofollow`, sitemap excluded, `no-store`, robots negative test, no public structured data. KPI hypotheses (not measured): successful search completion, zero cross-account rows, p95 search latency <500ms in isolated representative data, error/429/503 rates, time to locate a notice and accessible task completion. No keyword collection for analytics and no assertion of real user benefit until measured.

### Acceptance, dependencies and exact-SHA release gate
- Planned (NOT EXECUTED): isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 with 10,001 synthetic notifications, skewed owners and Korean/English terms; 100 cross-owner/cursor-forgery cases, 100 concurrent inserts/mark-read races, 50 network-loss/expiry/replay cases, 30 timezone/DST boundaries, invalid Unicode, index-missing fail-closed, query-plan/statement-timeout tests; Next/NestJS/Android contract tests, 320px keyboard/screen-reader/a11y and private SEO negative tests. Compare old inbox count/read actions unchanged, validate no WLD/ledger writes, real PG ACL denial and function EXECUTE allowlist. These are **future acceptance criteria**, not PASS results.
- **P0:** REPO1472-01 preserve historic 462 removed refs/344 non-main ancestors (#1663); SEC1472-02 resolve 291–295 duplicate migrations and five existing checksum mismatches (#1878, Draft #1885), confirm schema lineage; FIN1472-03 verify 19 MINT backfills / 18,000 WLD and 552 Treasury rows (#1816), no financial mutation; REL1472-04 fix latest-main QA v1371 lint/typecheck (#1883/#1884/#1886), prove independent off-host backup restore, full CI/security/isolated real PG, official authenticated Test exact-SHA and authorized GitOps.
- **P1:** NOTIFSEARCH1472-05 reviewed owner-only function and safe indexes; NOTIFSEARCH1472-06 HMAC keyset cursor; NOTIFSEARCH1472-07 Nest/Next/Android contracts; NOTIFSEARCH1472-08 accessible UX; NOTIFSEARCH1472-09 privacy/admin/KPI; NOTIFSEARCH1472-10 real PG/security/regression/rollback.
- Feature flag **OFF** by default. Separate A/B/C development branches, no planning deployment, never overwrite QA/Gemini branches. No main merge/Production promotion without reviewed migration parity, full green exact-SHA CI, official Test same SHA authenticated/finance E2E, backup/restore, operator approval, immutable image/GitOps evidence, smoke and rollback proof. Missing evidence = `BLOCKED` not `PASS`. External independent deduplicated evidence newly approved **0**, cumulative **UNKNOWN / 10,000**, gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**.
**GitHub:** [Draft PR #1888](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1888) OPEN / unmerged; [P0/P1 issue #1887](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1887). Documentation only, release HOLD.


## v1470 / F675-173 — Personal-data export snapshot, identity and download-proof hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Planning Draft PR:** https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1882 (unmerged / RELEASE HOLD).

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Addition v1468 → Current Feature Improvement v1470 → Next Feature Addition v1472 (provisional). **Authority:** remote main 76d63a5840a4a185fcaeb02f8b1132dd145f19e4 checked 2026-10-29 19:09 KST; QA v1370 FAILED/P0. This is a planning specification, not an implementation or release. Previous v1468 specification was merged in Git history (6930c76ac) but its leading integrated-master and project-plan sections are absent from the latest main after v270 consolidation; this change restores those sections without reverting unrelated main edits.

### Source-backed defects and boundaries
- Existing Next.js /account/privacy is a manual request form and list; NestJS privacy/requests supports only access, correction, restriction, withdrawal and deletion, all received. There is no owner-bound export job, artifact or download route in the inspected source. Do not treat a legal access request as fulfilled by self-service export.
- Existing backend ReauthGuard uses REAUTHENTICATION_WINDOW_SECONDS = 900 (15 minutes). The v1468 export requirement is at most 600 seconds (10 minutes). Reusing that guard alone violates the proposed export-specific policy. Require a distinct server-side 600-second proof tied to the active session and user, with verified reauthentication timestamp; no client-provided auth_time, local clock, cookie-only or stale OAuth callback as authority. If unavailable, return coded reauthentication_required or 503, never silently widen the window. Android token flows need their own verified proof and account binding.
- The latest main contains 303 SQL migration files with duplicate numeric IDs 291×3, 292×3, 293×2, 294×2 and 295×4. QA v1370 earlier inspected 300 files and identified #1878; the present count is a fresh read-only check at the newer main. Applied DEV migration count 293 is QA evidence, not a fresh database replay. Five historical checksum mismatches (219,220,221,234,266) remain. No new privacy-export SQL may be assigned a number or applied until author-coordinated, forward-only unique lineage is established. Draft developer fixes #1880/#1881 are not a release certificate.
- QA v1370 also records 462 missing historical branch names (344 original heads not ancestors of main), developer role able to TRUNCATE audit_logs beyond approved finance DML, unresolved 18,000 WLD MINT provenance, and no official Test exact-SHA pass. Preserve existing owner-approved finance grants; do not imply ledger arithmetic parity proves provenance.

### Snapshot completeness and data classification
- Introduce a versioned export-class registry with explicit source-function identifier, stable source primary key, schema version, allowed columns, per-column ownership/redaction rule, retention rule, and reviewed exclusion reason. Never SELECT * or dynamically accept table names/joins/columns from clients. Only export owned profile/consent/request receipts, owned verified ledger activity, and owned work/quest activity after each source's authorization and provenance have been independently certified. Cross-member counterpart names, emails, chat bodies, security credentials and raw audit events remain excluded.
- Each class must have a server-derived as_of cutoff, stable keyset ordering, source high-water mark, included row count, redacted/omitted row count and reason, canonical UTF-8 record digest and source schema revision. Prefer a bounded PostgreSQL READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ snapshot for all classes when feasible; cap snapshot transaction duration to 60 seconds, terminate on exceeded budget, and never extend it for streaming downloads. If a single consistent snapshot cannot be proven, publish explicit per-class cutoffs and a NOT_ATOMIC_ACROSS_CLASSES marker; do not label the bundle as one coherent account snapshot.
- Export generation is all-or-HOLD for any selected mandatory class with unverifiable ownership, missing source, broken pagination, schema drift or digest mismatch. Optional omitted classes require explicit, user-visible omission codes and separate user acknowledgement before requesting; never silently mark a truncated 25 MiB archive READY. Reject oversize with TOO_LARGE, retain a minimal receipt and provide the existing manual privacy-request route. Finance projections must use authoritative read-only ledger functions and decimal strings; export must never create postings, adjust balances or invent missing treasury links.
- The signed manifest binds owner pseudonymous export identifier, source cutoffs, class registry revision, included/excluded classes, exact byte lengths, per-class row counts/digests, artifact SHA-256, generation and expiration. Document the signature algorithm/key identifier and publish a versioned public verification key through a controlled non-user-specific endpoint; provide a deterministic offline verifier and key-rotation/revocation policy. A hash or signature establishes artifact integrity and provenance only, not statutory completeness or proof that the recipient saved the file.

### Artifact encryption, download and deletion semantics
- Distinguish encrypted-at-rest object storage from the downloaded archive: ZIP is plaintext after authenticated server decryption unless an independently reviewed client encryption design exists. Use per-export envelope AES-256-GCM with KMS-managed data-key wrapping, authenticated metadata and no key/token/PII in URL, logs, metrics, email or third-party analytics. Fixed registry filenames only; no zip-slip paths, symlinks, nested archives, executable content or CSV formulas. Cap both compressed size (25 MiB), uncompressed bytes, entry count and memory/CPU budgets. Download page must warn that the resulting ZIP contains sensitive plaintext and advise safe storage.
- A one-use download grant is session-, owner-, export-, generation- and artifact-digest-bound, valid at most 10 minutes, and consumed atomically before the first response byte. No HTTP Range/resume or automatic browser retry with the same grant; reject Range with 416 and require fresh reauthentication and a new grant after interruption. A stream failure must not be represented as successful client receipt. Track distinct statuses GRANT_CONSUMED, STREAM_STARTED, STREAM_FINISHED_SERVER and USER_VERIFIED_DIGEST (voluntary); server bytes sent cannot prove local persistence.
- Every grant and download rechecks the 600-second proof, account status, owner, cancellation/deletion hold, generation and artifact expiry. Cross-account switching, logout, session revocation, concurrent cancel and key rotation invalidate grants without revealing whether a foreign export exists (404). After deletion/restriction request, new jobs and grants HOLD; cancel pending jobs, revoke grants, purge ciphertext under reviewed retention/legal-hold policy. Restored backups must replay revocation/purge tombstones before exposing any object. Record object-version-specific purge proof; do not call a deletion complete from an object-store DELETE acknowledgement alone.

### API, database, UI, operations and acceptance
- Proposed NestJS endpoints remain POST/GET /api/v1/privacy/exports, GET/POST cancel on /:exportId, POST /:exportId/download-authorizations, and GET /:exportId/download. Use 202 QUEUED, 200 original replay receipt, 401 coded reauthentication_required, 403 policy denied, 404 unknown/foreign, 409 conflicting digest/active job, 410 revoked/expired, 413 TOO_LARGE, 416 Range unsupported, 429 quota, 503 source/KMS/migration authority missing. All private no-store/noindex/nosniff; CSRF for browser mutations, bearer/session validation for Android, no redirects to public signed URLs. Use canonical owner-locked SQL SECURITY DEFINER functions with EXECUTE-only grants and no direct table DML for moneyverse_app.
- Proposed schema retains owner+request UUID uniqueness, active-job partial unique, worker lease/generation fencing, event append-only, per-class manifest rows, grant digest and consumed_at, revocation epoch, object-version purge tombstone and index on (owner_id,created_at DESC,export_id). Do not assign a migration number while lineage is ambiguous. Serialize quota and replay checks in one owner-locked transaction; retries with the same key and digest return original receipt even if subsequently cancelled, but cannot regenerate expired artifacts.
- Next.js account/privacy/export and Android show NOT_REQUESTED, REAUTH_REQUIRED, QUEUED, BUILDING, READY, HOLD, TOO_LARGE, FAILED, EXPIRED, CANCELLED and DOWNLOAD_INTERRUPTED distinctly. Accessible Korean/English status and retry guidance, keyboard/VoiceOver/TalkBack, 320px, 44px targets, WCAG 2.2 AA, no silent downloads. Admin sees only aggregates, status, lag and failure-code counts with audited access; no raw owner IDs or file contents. Public SEO limited to privacy-policy explanation, private route never indexed.
- KPIs: class-completeness validation rate, snapshot aborts, p95 generation time, verified digest opt-in rate, stream interruptions, 600-second reauth rejection rate, purge verification lag, zero foreign access and zero ledger delta. Avoid collecting private archive contents or individual browsing behavior.
- Acceptance in isolated PostgreSQL 17: 10,001 synthetic records spanning owner/third-party rows, 100 concurrent identical/different idempotency requests, 100 forged owner/session cases, 50 interrupted streams, 30 deletion/restore/worker races, 30 reauth boundary cases at 599/600/601/899/900 seconds, 30 snapshot pagination mutations, 25 MiB ±1 byte and uncompressed limits, KMS outage, checksum tamper, signature rotation, object restore, Android/web parity, 0 WLD postings. These are proposed tests, NOT EXECUTED. Additive schema only after #1878 reconciliation, flag OFF, exact-SHA official Test authenticated E2E and independent off-host restore before any Production promotion; rollback disables requests/downloads and revokes grants without dropping audit or source data.

### P0/P1 developer backlog and ownership
- P0 REPO1470-01: preserve/reconcile 462 historical missing refs and SHA recovery evidence (#1663); no destructive cleanup.
- P0 SEC1470-02: resolve 303-file migration collisions 291–295, five checksum mismatches and audit_logs TRUNCATE exposure (#1878, #1880, #1881); do not modify applied SQL.
- P0 FIN1470-03: audit 18,000 WLD MINT and 552 treasury link provenance (#1816); no money mutation by planning.
- P0 REL1470-04: independent restore, exact-SHA authenticated official Test, CI/security/route ledger and signed Production/rollback gate; retain HOLD.
- P1 EXPORT1470-05: export-specific 600s step-up, session binding, owner authorization, browser/Android guard tests.
- P1 EXPORT1470-06: class registry, snapshot cutoff, completeness manifest, redaction and signed offline verification.
- P1 EXPORT1470-07: envelope encryption, fenced worker, immutable idempotency receipts, cancellation and purge tombstones.

## v1468 / F675-173 — Verified owner-private personal-data export (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Planning Draft PR:** https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1877 (unmerged, release HOLD).

**Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1466 → Current Feature Addition v1468 → Next Feature Improvement v1470 (provisional). **Authority:** remote main 9ebc5613845ba2e8179a0051e86db996593c8a19 checked 2026-10-29 18:06 KST; QA v1369 FAILED/HOLD. **Developer handoff:** GitHub issue #1876. Planning only, not a deployed product, legal conclusion, or export certification.

### Existing source and decision
- Next.js account/privacy/page.tsx offers a list and manual form for data-subject requests. NestJS privacy.controller.ts has GET/POST privacy/requests. privacy.repository.ts permits access, correction, restriction, withdrawal, deletion; statuses currently only received. privacy.service.ts explicitly says it does not generate exports, choose delivery addresses or mutate accounts. No verified downloadable personal-data artifact, owner-scoped export job, or Android equivalent was found in inspected main.
- Do not silently reinterpret the existing access request as an automatically fulfilled right. Keep manual privacy request and administrator review separate; optional self-service export may cover a subset of portable data and must say so. An export never erases, edits or transfers any WLD, ledger, tax, account or consent record. It does not imply that all personal information has been provided or that a statutory request has been satisfied.
- Alternative rejected: direct browser aggregation of APIs leaks other-member data and omits provenance. Alternative rejected: emailing an archive creates uncontrolled copies and account-takeover exposure. Alternative rejected: a public pre-signed object-store URL bypasses owner reauthentication and revocation. Use server-authoritative, encrypted, short-lived, same-origin retrieval.

### Purpose, audience, roles, entry and states
- Purpose: let an authenticated adult/eligible member request a bounded, auditable copy of their own selected in-service data and verify what was included, without waiting for a support agent for routine requests. Guests get sign-in prompt only. Suspended, deleting or unverifiable accounts must use existing manual access process; do not expose partial secrets.
- Entry: Account → Privacy Center → Export my data → /account/privacy/export. Explain archive contents, exclusions, generation delay, expiry, and that it is not a full legal access determination. Explicit unchecked opt-in; CTA Request export; status CTA Refresh status; READY CTA Verify identity and download; EXPIRED CTA Request new export after quota; FAILED/HOLD CTA Contact privacy support. A cancelled job cannot be resurrected.
- State machine: NOT_REQUESTED, REAUTH_REQUIRED, QUEUED, BUILDING, READY, FAILED, EXPIRED, CANCELLED, HOLD, DOWNLOAD_AUTH_EXPIRED. UI must never label QUEUED/BUILDING/HOLD as completed. A request timeout is RECEIPT_UNKNOWN until the original idempotency receipt is queried; do not create a new job/key automatically. Separate transient API outage from no jobs. On logout/account switch immediately discard in-memory job details and download authorizations.
- One active QUEUED/BUILDING/READY job per member; at most one new job per rolling 24h, at most 25 MiB generated artifact, maximum seven days from READY to expiry. Existing ready archive can be downloaded multiple times only with fresh, one-use authorization; every authorization expires in 10 minutes. Expiry/purge is server-authoritative, not browser clock.

### Canonical data-class contract and manifest
- Server allowlist v1: owned profile/public display fields; member's own consent choices and privacy-request receipts; own cash/bank ledger activity and owned task/quest participation (read-only projections with stable source IDs). Explicit class list in UI and export manifest; record source table/function revision, source as-of timestamp, row count, included fields, redaction reason, schema_version and SHA-256 for each class.
- Exclude password hashes, password reset/email verification tokens, session IDs/cookies, OAuth credentials, internal security/admin audit evidence, other users' identities/contact details, private chat/support message bodies, unapproved third-party data, raw payment credentials and unverified/ambiguous data. Use server-owned export projection functions; never export raw SELECT * rows or join arbitrary user-controlled IDs. For partially-owned records, redact foreign fields and record redaction in manifest. If provenance or ownership cannot be proven, omit with explicit reason or HOLD; never guess.
- Archive format: UTF-8 JSON files and a machine-readable manifest.json inside deterministic ZIP; stable ISO-8601 UTC timestamps, string decimal WLD, no CSV formula injection, no HTML/active content, filenames from a fixed registry. Manifest digest covers canonical bytes, archive digest SHA-256 and signer/key version; checksum validates integrity, not legal completeness. Signed receipt binds owner, export_id, class schema version, source cutoff, archive digest, expiry, and status; no raw PII in receipts or analytics.

### API, permissions, backend and Android
- New NestJS endpoints: POST /api/v1/privacy/exports (body {requestId: UUID, selectedClasses: server-approved enum[]}); GET /api/v1/privacy/exports?limit=20; GET /api/v1/privacy/exports/:exportId; POST /api/v1/privacy/exports/:exportId/cancel; POST /api/v1/privacy/exports/:exportId/download-authorizations; GET /api/v1/privacy/exports/:exportId/download (one-use server-side authorization). Exact path names are proposed and require route-map/contract verification by developers.
- Next.js server actions/BFF under account/privacy/export and same-origin authenticated streaming route; never expose INTERNAL_API_TOKEN, storage keys, object-store paths or authorization token to public CDN. Android app-api must implement identical status semantics and auth proof without sharing browser CSRF cookies; Android download uses an authenticated app-bound authorization. POST uses SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard, ConsentGuard, CsrfGuard and recent step-up reauthentication ≤10 minutes. Downloads recheck active session, owner, current account status, auth age, artifact expiry, checksum and one-use authorization atomically. No public signed URLs.
- HTTP: 202 QUEUED, 200 existing idempotent receipt/status, 400 invalid fields, 401 missing session, 403 owner/reauth policy failure, 404 indistinguishable unknown/foreign export, 409 same key different digest or active-job conflict, 410 expired/revoked download, 413 size cap, 429 quota, 503 missing authority/KMS/storage/worker. Responses Cache-Control private,no-store and X-Robots-Tag noindex; download Content-Disposition attachment, application/zip, nosniff and no sensitive redirects. CSRF for browser mutations; constant-shape non-owner failures and bounded timing.

### PostgreSQL ownership, indexes and transaction/worker
- Proposed new migration above the current contiguous migration head (never edit applied SQL): privacy_export_jobs(export_id UUID PK, owner_id UUID NOT NULL FK users, request_uuid UUID NOT NULL, request_digest bytea NOT NULL, classes_version integer NOT NULL, selected_classes jsonb validated, status enum, created_at, started_at, ready_at, expires_at, finished_at, lease_until, attempt_count, artifact_ref opaque nullable, artifact_sha256 bytea nullable, manifest_sha256 bytea nullable, failure_code allowlist nullable, generation bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1). Unique(owner_id,request_uuid), partial unique(owner_id) WHERE status IN (QUEUED,BUILDING,READY), index(owner_id,created_at DESC,export_id), worker index(status,lease_until,created_at), check timestamps/status coherence and byte length 32 for hashes. No archive bytes or PII in DB row.
- privacy_export_events(event_id UUID PK, export_id FK, actor_role enum, transition enum, happened_at, request_digest, receipt_digest), append-only. privacy_export_download_grants(grant_id UUID PK, export_id FK, owner_id FK, token_digest bytea UNIQUE, expires_at, consumed_at, session_binding_digest bytea, generation bigint); unique request and state fencing. privacy_export_purge_receipts(export_id UNIQUE, purged_at, object_version_digest, verifier_digest) to prove expiration cleanup; keep only minimal non-content audit under reviewed retention.
- SECURITY DEFINER owner functions for create/list/status/cancel/grant/consume, app role EXECUTE only; explicitly REVOKE table direct read/write and PUBLIC EXECUTE. Derive actor from verified session/bearer at service boundary; SQL p_actor alone is not authorization. Use advisory transaction lock keyed by (owner,request_uuid), then replay lookup and request_digest comparison before work; owner row lock and one-active quota check in same transaction. Worker claims with SKIP LOCKED and expiring lease; compare generation on completion to prevent late worker from reviving cancelled/expired job. Artifact is written to temporary encrypted object, verified, then atomically marked READY with digest and expiry. Failed write or missing KMS must not yield READY.
- Purge worker removes encrypted object after expiry or cancellation, records independent purge verification, then retains minimal audit for reviewed period. Backup policy must exclude/secure ephemeral export artifacts, and restoration must not resurrect an expired download. Legal hold/deletion request must suspend or cancel affected export jobs; no automatic deletion of authoritative source data.

### Admin, privacy, SEO, accessibility and metrics
- Admin-only metadata dashboard: counts by status, age, error code, purge lag, failed/held reasons and anonymized cohort; no artifact browsing or download impersonation. Two-person authorization and audit for any exceptional retention/legal-hold override. No content, filename, owner UUID, token, archive URL or manifest field values in analytics/logs. KMS and access logs independently protected; avoid exporting audit trails to third-party trackers.
- Private route robots noindex/nofollow, no public sitemap/SSR data cache, Content-Security-Policy and restrictive Referrer-Policy. Mobile 320px, 44px controls, visible focus, aria-live polite status changes, no countdown-only expiry cue, Korean/English copy parity, screen reader/TalkBack/VoiceOver, keyboard-only flow and WCAG 2.2 AA. Public SEO impact limited to clear privacy policy/help explanation, not member archive pages.
- KPIs: export request-to-READY p50/p95, verified download completion, user-cancel rate, 503/HOLD rate, purge lag p95, zero cross-owner accesses, zero leaked artifact URLs, zero WLD/ledger delta. Measure only aggregated, consent-compliant metrics; no per-user download behavior sent to analytics.

### Acceptance, rollout, rollback and backlog
- Isolated real PostgreSQL 17 test on 10,001 synthetic owner rows; 100 concurrent same-key requests yield one job and same receipt, 100 forged cross-owner requests leak zero data, 50 interrupted downloads never consume another grant, 30 expiry/cancel/worker races never resurrect READY, 30 auth/session-switch races fail closed, 25 MiB boundary, KMS outage, artifact checksum mismatch, restore/purge and no finance posting tests. All must be executed, not inferred from spec. Verify Android/web parity and authenticated exact-SHA official Test; never use production data for fixture.
- Feature flag OFF by default, additive migration, staged isolated worker, no destructive backfill; require independent privacy/legal approval of data classes, reviewed retention, KMS and off-host restore, clean PostgreSQL privilege regression, immutable image/source digest and Test E2E before GitOps Promotion. Canary limited eligible members, monitor no PII leakage, then controlled enable. Rollback disables new requests and downloads, cancels active grants, preserves required audit, safely purges artifacts after hold review, does not drop tables, alter existing privacy requests or change finance ledger.
- P0: REPO1468-01 historical branch preservation (#1663); FIN1468-02 MINT 18,000 WLD and treasury 552 unlinked (#1816); SEC1468-03 chat-report clean PG failure (#1874), five checksum mismatches and authorized grants; REL1468-04 independent restore, exact-SHA official Test, build-only auto-merge blocker (#1088). P1: EXPORT1468-05 schema/manifest; EXPORT1468-06 worker/idempotency; EXPORT1468-07 API/web/Android; EXPORT1468-08 UX/a11y; EXPORT1468-09 admin/privacy/KPI; EXPORT1468-10 real PG/security/rollback.
- QA v1369: HTTP 48/50 (two intentional 404), mobile 36/36, 48/48 targeted and 24/24 security PASS, route-ledger CLI evidence FAIL, one moderate dependency audit finding, 5 migration checksum mismatches, development ledger arithmetic drift 0 but MINT provenance unverified, 147 open Draft PR, 58 conflicts. Existing Production v1502 is not this feature's deployment. External independent deduplicated/approved evidence newly 0, cumulative UNKNOWN/10,000, gate BLOCKED/IN PROGRESS. No 10,000-source completion claim. Documentation-only draft, RELEASE HOLD.
