## v1448 / F675-168 — Verified member blog reading queue (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Development handoff:** [Draft PR #1801](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1801) / [Issue #1800](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1800). Documentation only, RELEASE HOLD.

**Authority and cycle.** Previous Feature Improvement v1446 → Current Feature Addition v1448 → Next Feature Improvement v1450 (provisional). Read main f05efcb5d7c21d9553f68df227f9771217a30671 at start; mid-run main advanced to 8ba355374a2553926f67140febbf85c114f00ba1 (implementation_plan.md only; planning authority unchanged); QA v1359 FAILED/HOLD. This is a proposal, not a deployed feature. Development handoff: GitHub issue #1800. No separate planning specification file is authorized.

### Source-backed gap and non-duplication
- The existing Next.js blog article component has a Share button but no Save/Unsave action; the public blog hub renders verified articles and legacy static entries. BlogPost has slug, country, PUBLISHED/WITHDRAWN status and optional verificationReceipt. The public verified API exposes receipt-bearing PUBLISHED articles; no inspected owner-scoped saved-article API, persistence table, or Android queue contract exists. GitHub issue search for a blog reading queue returned no prior exact match; existing board reading-list issue #1086 is a separate domain.
- **Additional direct-source risk:** migration 288 creates blog_verified_articles with receipt UUID and grants moneyverse_app direct SELECT; migration 290 REVISE updates title/excerpt/content_digest while retaining that same receipt. The publisher sets evidence.humanReviewRequired=false. Therefore receipt existence is not independent human editorial approval and is not a stable content-revision identifier. A safe developer migration must introduce reviewed immutable publication revision/epoch and compatible SECURITY DEFINER reads before revoking legacy SELECT, preserving existing public API behavior. QA v1359 lists 288-290 as applied but 278/279 as unapplied; independently verify migration dependency/parity before any schema change.
- The verified blog source migrations 278/279 are in QA v1359's unapplied set; existing source data or static AI content must not be represented as independently approved. Eligible means an immutable independently reviewed publication receipt, approved source provenance, published revision and an authoritative publication registry entry, not merely a matching slug or client-provided flag. Until the publication authority is proven, saving is UNAVAILABLE, not simulated.
- Alternative rejected: localStorage-only bookmarks would be forgeable, device-specific, difficult to delete centrally and unable to respect editorial withdrawal. Alternative rejected: automatic read-tracking would infer behavior without explicit consent. Choose server-authoritative opt-in, manual read state, no public sharing.

### Purpose, target, entry and UI states
- Purpose: enable signed-in members to explicitly save approved economics/coding/computer articles and resume on web or Android without inflating public engagement counts. Target: members who use multilingual educational blog articles. Guests may browse public posts but cannot save; Save prompts login without claiming success.
- Public entry: verified article page /blog/[slug] and its existing ko/en/ja/zh country-edition routes, with **Save for later** / **Remove from reading list** CTA only after server-proven eligibility. Private entry: /account/reading/blog, linked from Account and article save confirmation. List shows approved title, locale/country, last editorial revision, saved date, manually selected unread/read, and Open / Remove actions. No cross-account cached cards.
- States: GUEST_SIGN_IN_REQUIRED, INELIGIBLE_UNVERIFIED, READY_UNSAVED, SAVING, RECEIPT_UNKNOWN, SAVED_VERIFIED, UNSAVING, READ_MARKING, CONTENT_REVISED_REVIEW, WITHDRAWN_TOMBSTONE, EMPTY, OWNER_CONFLICT_409, RATE_LIMIT_429, SOURCE_UNVERIFIED_503, ERROR. A timeout is RECEIPT_UNKNOWN, never a success; query the operation receipt before retrying the same request UUID. Unavailable/withdrawn articles are never linked to their former body.
- A saved item is not proof of reading, educational attainment, endorsement, content accuracy or financial advice. No WLD, quest, ad impression, referral credit, notification, public popularity or ledger effect. Max 100 active saved items/member; 20 items/page; sort by server saved_at DESC, stable UUID tie-breaker. Explicit read/unread toggle only, no passive tracking.

### Publication authority and identity
- Existing article slug is a route alias, not a trustworthy immutable key; country/locale can alter article representation. Introduce approved immutable article UUID, edition (ko|en|ja|zh), country (KR|US|JP|CN), publication revision, source proof reference, approved content SHA-256, verification receipt UUID, published_at, and status PUBLISHED|WITHDRAWN|REVOKED in a server-authoritative publication registry. Only an independently approved PUBLISHED revision is eligible. Client-provided slug, receipt, locale, or title is never sufficient to grant eligibility.
- If static and verified posts share a slug, resolve through verified publication ID first and never silently bind a static post. A new revision changes the review badge and prompts member confirmation before showing a changed body; do not silently treat a withdrawn/revoked article as current. Re-publication after withdrawal requires a new publication epoch/receipt; a stale bookmark cannot resurrect automatically.
- Publication withdrawal must atomically update the authoritative registry and invalidate the public read and saved-queue projection. Preserve a minimal owner-only tombstone (title withheld where legally necessary) until member removes it or deletion retention expires; no content snapshot is persisted in bookmarks. Missing authority, signature mismatch or backend outage: fail closed 503.

### PostgreSQL constraints, ownership, transactions
- Developer migration (not written by planning) proposes blog_publication_registry (immutable publication_id UUID, edition, country, revision, content_sha256, source_proof_ref, verification_receipt, publication_epoch, status, approved_by/reviewed_by distinct, timestamps) with unique (publication_id,edition,revision), unique (verification_receipt), valid locale/country checks and immutable audit. Add blog_member_reading_items (owner_id,publication_id,edition,saved_revision,publication_epoch,saved_at,read_marked_at nullable,revision_counter) with PK(owner_id,publication_id,edition), FK to authoritative publication identity, owner/saved_at/UUID keyset index, check read timestamp <= current valid transaction time. Add blog_reading_operation_receipts (owner_id,request_uuid,operation,payload_digest,result_snapshot,created_at) unique(owner_id,request_uuid) and short bounded replay retention; no PII or raw article text in receipt.
- Use SECURITY DEFINER functions for list, save, unsave, mark_read and receipt lookup; app role gets EXECUTE only, no direct table INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/SELECT. Derive owner from trusted session or verified Android bearer at service boundary; SQL p_actor alone is not proof of authentication. Member lock then registry row lock in a consistent order; enforce <=100 active items atomically. Save only current approved publication revision and epoch, reject stale expected revision 409. Unsave and read toggle require owner row and CAS revision_counter; duplicate same request UUID+canonical payload returns original receipt, reused UUID with different digest returns 409, never double counts. Do not mutate finance tables, grant public bank EXECUTE, or rewrite historical migrations.
- Private cursor signed/HMAC-bound to owner, sort key, query filter and expiration; reject cross-user, expired or tampered cursor with 422. No raw DB errors, secrets or source credentials in client bundles, API payloads, analytics or logs.

### API, web, Android and administrator
- Proposed NestJS authenticated /api/v1/blog/reading-queue: GET list (limit <=20, cursor), POST /items {publicationId,edition,expectedRevision,requestId}, DELETE /items/:publicationId?edition=... {requestId,expectedVersion}, PATCH /items/:publicationId/read {edition,isRead,requestId,expectedVersion}, GET /operations/:requestId receipt recovery. Use existing Next server-only API bridge for web; Android through existing /app-api/v1 proxy with equivalent DTO, bearer/session semantics and response codes. Mutation requires same-origin CSRF on web, Android token and per-account/IP throttles. Response: 200/201 verified receipt; 400 invalid shape; 401 guest; 403 unauthorized; 404 missing owner item; 409 stale revision/UUID mismatch; 410 withdrawn/expired epoch; 422 invalid locale/cursor; 429 throttled; 503 unverifiable publisher/DB. Always Cache-Control private,no-store.
- Admin can revoke a publication and view privacy-safe aggregate saved counts and deletion completion; admin cannot impersonate a member, inspect arbitrary private lists or restore withdrawn content through the queue. Record audit with operator, reason, source receipt and epoch; use existing single-superadmin authorization boundary, no unapproved new roles. Data deletion removes owner rows and operation receipts through authorized functions, with documented backup retention; unsave tombstone metadata expires after 30 days, active list until explicit remove or account deletion. No user-generated article text.
- SEO: keep public article canonical/hreflang and verified publication robots policy unchanged. Private /account/reading/blog noindex/nofollow and no sitemap or public OpenGraph previews; prevent leakage through referrer, caching and analytics. WCAG 2.2 AA: keyboard toggles, aria-pressed/labels, focus restoration, role=status receipts, 320px viewport, 200% zoom, reduced motion, TalkBack and four-locale accessible copy.
- KPI: eligible verified publication count, unique opt-in saves, verified save success, 409 conflicts, 503 provenance failures, manual open-from-list and voluntary removals, withdrawal suppression latency. Report only privacy-safe aggregates with minimum group size; no fabricated engagement, no auto-read inference or personalization/ad targeting.

### Test, release and blockers
- P0 ENV1448-01: root 99% and data 99% (data reached 100% during this planning session), preserve co-worker assets, secure capacity and independently decrypt/restore off-host encrypted backup. P0 FIN1448-02: QA v1359 18 USER_CASH mismatches / +18,000 WLD; no synthetic adjustment, prove zero finance delta. P0 SEC1448-03: app writable relations 239, three PUBLIC bank EXECUTEs, five checksum mismatches and 20 unapplied SQL (including blog 278/279), prove least privilege and approved publication provenance. P0 REL1448-04: full exact-main CI, official Test exact-SHA DB/API/web/Android E2E, release identity and authorized no-downtime promotion/rollback.
- P1 BLOG1448-05 publication registry/constraints/indexes; BLOG1448-06 atomic owner operations and immutable receipts; BLOG1448-07 NestJS/Next/Android parity and auth; BLOG1448-08 accessible web/mobile flows; BLOG1448-09 admin, privacy, SEO and KPI; BLOG1448-10 isolated real-PG regression. Acceptance: 10,001 synthetic saved/receipt rows, 100 concurrent save/unsave/mark-read races, 100 forged owner/CSRF/UUID/cursor negatives, withdrawn/republished/revised/locale collision, no repository fallback, account switching, 320px/TalkBack, no WLD/ledger changes, migration parity and rollback. These are proposed tests, not executed.
- Feature flag OFF by default. Expand/contract additive migration only after ownership/QA approval; read-old/write-new compatibility, staged rollout to authenticated Test exact SHA, abort on publication provenance or authz failure, disable new writes on rollback while preserving historical receipts; no direct Production or Test mutation in planning. External independent evidence gate: newly approved/deduplicated 0; cumulative UNKNOWN / 10,000, BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. No claim of achieving the gate.


## v1446 / F675-167 — Quiz/quest reward boundary and receipt integrity (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Handoff:** [Draft PR #1794](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1794) (stacked on #1790/#1784/#1780); [development issue #1789](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1789).

**Cycle / authority.** Previous Feature Addition v1444 -> Current Feature Improvement v1446 -> Next Feature Addition v1448 (provisional). Main `35971c600f25869c96f1c666d4b9f58927202730` checked at start and midpoint, QA v1358 FAILED/HOLD. This proposal extends unmerged F675-167 Draft #1790, itself stacked on #1784/#1780. It is a documentation-only candidate; no verified glossary quiz exists in inspected main.

### Source-backed conflict and decision
- `frontend/src/components/daily-economic-quest-station.tsx` advertises **`quest_financial_quiz` for 1,000 WLD**, with `targetUrl: '/#financial-quiz'`. A search of main frontend found only that anchor reference, not an element with the target ID. `backend/src/engagement/dopamine.repository.ts` maps the quest to 1,000 WLD and its `claimDailyQuest` checks the requested quest ID and prior-day claim but **does not verify a completed question/answer receipt** in the inspected path. `backend/src/engagement/dopamine.controller.ts` has a synthetic success fallback if its repository is absent. This is a source-path concern, **not proof of a successful Production payout or exploitation**. Existing financial-quest issue [#1321](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1321) owns reward settlement remediation; do not duplicate A/B/C/QA runtime work.
- F675-167 is **voluntary, non-rewarded educational practice** with an isolated `glossary_practice` namespace. It MUST NOT call `/app-api/v1/engagement/dopamine/daily-quest/claim`, emit `quest_financial_quiz` completion, satisfy `all_clear`, award achievements/WLD, or touch wallet, treasury, `ledger_transactions`, `ledger_postings`, `account_balances` or outbox finance events. Never reuse daily-quest idempotency keys or claim receipts as practice receipts. The existing daily-quest CTA must remain separate; do not silently retarget `/#financial-quiz` to this practice or imply it earns 1,000 WLD. Existing broken-anchor observation goes to #1321 owner for independent review.
- If a future owner explicitly requests rewarded quiz integration, require a **separate approved financial spec**: trusted server-side completion proof bound to actor/quest/day/revision, one-use eligibility, atomic SECURITY DEFINER mint posting, ledger parity, rate and fraud controls, idempotent original receipt, authorized Test and Production gates. No reward integration or retroactive payouts are authorized by this plan.

### Entry points, UX, authorization, state and copy
- Public `/guide/glossary` and verified canonical term pages may show an optional **Practice 5 terms (no WLD reward)** CTA. Guest CTA signs in without starting; active consenting member opens `/account/learning/glossary/practice`, chooses `ko|en|ja|zh`, and explicitly starts. Preserve 5 distinct reviewed finance terms, 4 choices each, optional Skip, 30-minute expiry, at most 10 starts per rolling UTC 24h, one active attempt per locale and 90-day private retention. Do not auto-mark READ or mastery; 0–5 score is a practice result, not an investment certificate.
- States: SIGN_IN_REQUIRED, UNAVAILABLE_NO_APPROVED_BANK, READY, STARTING, IN_PROGRESS, SUBMITTING, RECEIPT_UNKNOWN, SUBMITTED_VERIFIED, EXPIRED, REVOKED, CONFLICT_409, RATE_LIMITED_429, SERVER_UNVERIFIED_503, ERROR. Never render success, reward, or a score from optimistic client state. On network timeout query original receipt using the **same** request UUID; only re-submit the exact original payload after verified absence. Account switch/logout fences in-flight responses and clears memory, session-bound cursors and sensitive caches. Use no-store/noindex and no private result in share links, sitemap or analytics.
- Preserve accessible keyboard radio groups, four-locale reviewed text, 320px and 200% zoom, focus recovery, TalkBack, status live regions and no color-only feedback. An expired attempt cannot be extended by UI timers; offer a new attempt subject to quota. Explain that a quiz is independent of the separate rewarded daily-quest station.

### Bank authority, key secrecy and publication proof
- Split **public safe metadata** (bank ID/revision, locale, term IDs, editorial source citations, content SHA and deployed release SHA) from **server-only answer material** (correct choice IDs, scoring mapping, explanations until submit). A digest of four low-entropy choices or a hashed correct-choice ID is **not** adequate secrecy: do not publish keys, answer hashes, grading branches, admin previews or answer-bearing sourcemaps in Next static/ISR payloads, Android bundles, CDN, logs or analytics. Use server-side restricted access with least-privilege functions, never rely on obfuscation.
- Editor and independent reviewer approve each locale's immutable question revision, 4 distinct nonempty choices, exactly one correct ID, factual source and licensed wording. Bind approval to `term_id`, `term_revision`, `locale`, `bank_revision`, question+choice digest and actual deploy SHA; invalidate starts if provenance is missing, stale or revoked. Reviewer cannot approve their own draft. Use allowlisted source URLs and no server-side fetch of member-submitted arbitrary URLs. On revoke, block new starts and unsubmitted attempts; preserve committed historical receipts with their original bank revision.

### API/DB contract, concurrency, replay and errors
- Retain v1444's private web/NestJS/Android routes under `/api/v1/guide/glossary-practice` and `/app-api/v1/...`; no endpoint in the engagement/dopamine reward namespace. `POST /attempts` uses member-derived actor, requestId UUID, expectedBankRevision; `POST /attempts/:id/submit` uses the same attempt's 5 question IDs, server-issued choice IDs or null, requestId UUID, expectedVersion and expectedBankRevision. `GET /receipts/:requestId` returns the original immutable result, scoped to owner/session; `GET /attempts` uses HMAC owner+session+filter+expiry cursor. Never accept a userId, score, reward flag, correctness bit or source version as authority from the client.
- Additive PostgreSQL tables/functions proposed in v1444: owner+attempt composite FK, unique `(user_id,request_id)` operation+canonical-payload-digest receipt, `(attempt_id,position)` unique 1..5, exactly five distinct term IDs and snapshotted choice IDs, immutable approved question revision and server-only key storage. `SECURITY DEFINER` fixed `search_path`, no PUBLIC EXECUTE or direct app DML. Lock actor -> attempt -> item in stable order, serialize rolling quota and submit; reject changed request UUID payload with 409, stale revision with 409, revoked/expired with 410, invalid choice with 422, quota with 429 and unverified bank/deployment with 503. Missing backend repository or scoring authority must **fail closed 503**, never fabricate a successful receipt or score. Commit result+receipt atomically and return the original receipt for exact retry, including across concurrent clients.
- Prevent pre-submit answer oracle: identical error shape/timing class for valid vs wrong options; do not return per-choice correctness until a committed submit; constrain question exposure with member/IP throttling and randomized but server-snapshotted selection/order. Keep anti-scraping separate from scoring correctness; never punish accessibility retries. Admin moderation and revoke are audited with independent reviewer and recent reauth. Aggregate KPI only for groups >=20; private answer selections never enter general analytics.

### Regression, rollout and P0/P1 developer handoff
- **P0 ENV1446-01:** QA v1358 root 98% (~2.0GiB), data 99% (~3.4GiB), `/tmp` 97%; capacity and encrypted off-host independent decrypt/restore before migration/build, preserve all other workers' assets.
- **P0 FIN1446-02:** 18 USER_CASH account +18,000 WLD historical mismatch and existing daily quest proof/settlement [#1321](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1321). Prevent quiz practice from triggering any WLD reward; independent finance owner must certify quest eligibility and ledger integrity before reward feature changes. No synthetic journal fixes.
- **P0 SEC1446-03:** 239 app-writable relations, 3 PUBLIC bank EXECUTEs, 5 checksum mismatches/20 unapplied; prove least-privilege practice owner boundary, server-only keys, fail-closed absent repository and no unreviewed migration edits.
- **P0 REL1446-04:** main runtime CI SKIPPED, official Test SHA `cfb13272...` != main, Production version unknown; exact-head full CI + real isolated PostgreSQL + authenticated Test exact-main E2E, backup/rollback and authorized GitOps only. Build-only success never authorizes merge/promotion.
- **P1 QUIZ1446-05:** bank/term/deploy revision and server-only key split, independent editor/reviewer, revoke, no low-entropy answer hashes in public manifests.
- **P1 QUIZ1446-06:** PostgreSQL owner FK, five-item immutable snapshots, 24h start cap, atomic grade/receipt and concurrent UUID digest replay, strict lock order.
- **P1 QUIZ1446-07:** NestJS/Next/Android DTO and no-store owner receipt parity, fail-closed 503, stale 409/410, 422/429, session/CSRF/Android bearer tests.
- **P1 QUIZ1446-08:** separate non-rewarded CTA, existing daily quest anchor review (#1321), 4-locale accessible member UI, account-switch/timeout recovery.
- **P1 QUIZ1446-09:** independent editorial admin, revocation audit, private-data erasure, safe SEO and aggregate KPI, answer-oracle/scraping controls.
- **P1 QUIZ1446-10:** isolated PG 10,001 synthetic attempts, 100 concurrent start/submit and 100 forged owner/CSRF/UUID negatives; missing repository, answer leakage, revoked bank, ISR drift, 30min expiry, exact receipt replay, daily-quest claim isolation and **zero practice-caused WLD/ledger changes**. Test reward isolation against a controlled fixture, not aggregate Production totals during concurrent unrelated activity.
- Flag OFF by default; preserve historical receipts on rollback, disable new start/submit without destructive DB changes. Runtime work belongs in separate developer branch with full exact-SHA Test gate and independent authorization. QA v1358 public HTTP 50/50 and mobile 36/36 are **not** authenticated practice/finance E2E. No Production promotion.

**External independent evidence gate:** new approved/deduplicated external records **0**, cumulative **UNKNOWN / 10,000**, **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. Glossary terms, quiz questions, GitHub issues, synthetic test rows and publisher families are not counted as approved independent records. This is a draft proposal, not implemented or deployed.


- Development handoff: [Issue #1789](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1789); [Draft PR #1790](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1790). Stacked on unmerged #1784 / #1780; no release.

## v1444 / F675-167 — Verified financial glossary self-check practice (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

**Authority and cycle.** Previous Feature Improvement v1442 -> Current Feature Addition v1444 -> Next Feature Improvement v1446 (provisional). Remote main 35971c600f25869c96f1c666d4b9f58927202730 checked at start and midpoint; QA v1357 (05:20 KST) FAILED/HOLD. This docs-only candidate is stacked on unmerged glossary learning-list Draft #1784 (itself on #1780), not deployed code. Do not treat a proposed content registry as already present in main.

### Product intent, scope, existing implementation and alternatives
- Main publishes 50 static PSEO financial glossary slugs in frontend/src/config/pseo-glossary.config.ts, plus 12 separate system terms in frontend/src/app/guide/components/glossary-search.tsx. Korean and en/ja/zh term pages exist, with 86400-second ISR. There is no glossary-specific quiz, approved question-bank, scoring receipt, owner attempt API or matching database model in the inspected main frontend/backend/migrations; GitHub issue search for glossary quiz found no matching existing task. These are source/search findings, not proof of exhaustive absence in all deployments.
- Goal: give a consenting signed-in member a voluntary, low-stakes five-question self-check tied to five distinct, editorially verified glossary terms, without pretending to assess investment competence. Success = five reviewed questions in the selected language, understandable feedback with source/revision, private receipt and safe replay. Target: novice members studying PER/PBR/ROE and other glossary concepts; exclude the 12 unrelated system terms.
- Considered (A) generated questions from glossary prose, (B) client-only quiz, (C) curated server-verified bank with private attempts. Select C: A risks amplifying inaccurate finance claims and invented answers; B cannot prove review/version or private cross-device receipt. No AI-generated questions, market predictions, public leaderboard, achievements, push, automatic reading marks, trading advice, bonus WLD, wallet or ledger writes. Editorial review is a hard prerequisite. The existing v1440/v1442 Save/Read proposal is separate and must not be assumed implemented.

### Entry points, screens, CTAs and user-visible state machine
- On public /guide/glossary and canonical term pages show an optional Practice these terms link. Guests see a non-mutating sign-in CTA; verified active members may open /account/learning/glossary/practice and choose ko/en/ja/zh. Never enroll on page load, scroll, dwell time or analytics events. For locale with fewer than five independently approved questions for distinct valid term IDs, show Quiz temporarily unavailable; do not substitute mock questions.
- Member chooses Start 5-question practice; each question has four mutually exclusive options, a clearly labeled Skip, Previous/Next, and Submit answers. The client stores unsent selections only in volatile component memory; no auto-save or unverified success toast. After Submit, server returns 0–5 correct count and each answer's reviewed explanation/source term and question revision; skipped counts as unanswered. Retake creates a fresh attempt and does not silently mark glossary entries READ or mastered.
- Explicit states: SIGN_IN_REQUIRED, REGISTRY_UNAVAILABLE, LOADING, READY, STARTING, IN_PROGRESS, SUBMITTING, RECEIPT_UNKNOWN (timeout), SUBMITTED_VERIFIED, EXPIRED, CONTENT_REVOKED, CONFLICT_409, RATE_LIMITED_429, ERROR. Preserve answer selection and keyboard focus on transient errors, recover by receipt lookup with same UUID and payload digest, and never show a result before a committed server receipt. If question withdrawn during an active attempt, fail closed with CONTENT_REVOKED and allow restart; do not grade with silently substituted answers. Logout/account switch clears private caches, selections, request IDs and stale responses.

### Question-bank authority, editorial review, localization and safety
- Immutable reviewed bank manifest per locale and release: bank_revision, release_sha, canonical_manifest_sha256, ordered question_id + question_revision, term_id, locale, approved_at, approved_by, reviewer_id, cited canonical glossary revision and editorial evidence identifiers. Require different editor and reviewer for approval, traceable fact verification, licensed original questions and no unsupported return/risk claims. Do not treat the 10,000 external independent-reference target as satisfied by a handful of quiz citations.
- Each question must have exactly four distinct nonempty localized choices, exactly one reviewed correct choice, an explanation and canonical term link. Correct choice and answer key are server-only and excluded from start/list responses, client bundles, public static pages, analytics and logs. Questions and options have stable IDs; render choices in deterministic attempt order to avoid index ambiguity. Do not create incorrect equivalences (notably volatility versus Sharpe ratio). Invalid aliases, stale/revoked glossary terms, unverified locale revisions and mismatched deployed manifest disable the bank, not silently remap questions. Published glossary remains public even when practice is OFF.
- Proposed content lifecycle: DRAFT -> REVIEW_PENDING -> APPROVED -> ACTIVE -> REVOKED/RETIRED. Admin may inspect/edit draft and revoke; cannot rewrite an approved question revision or alter historical attempt receipts. Revision change creates a new immutable revision. Publish only after source provenance and independent reviewer approval. No unreviewed external URL fetch, HTML injection, arbitrary question submission or financial endorsement.

### Versioned web, NestJS and Android API contract
- Proposed authenticated routes: GET /api/v1/guide/glossary-practice/availability?locale=ko|en|ja|zh; POST /api/v1/guide/glossary-practice/attempts (requestId UUID, locale, expectedBankRevision); GET /api/v1/guide/glossary-practice/attempts/:attemptId; POST /api/v1/guide/glossary-practice/attempts/:attemptId/submit (requestId UUID, expectedVersion, selections [{questionId,choiceId|null}], expectedBankRevision); GET /api/v1/guide/glossary-practice/receipts/:requestId; DELETE /api/v1/guide/glossary-practice/attempts/:attemptId (owner withdrawal). List GET /api/v1/guide/glossary-practice/attempts?cursor=...&limit<=20 is private, bounded and excludes answer keys before submission.
- Start returns attemptId, expiresAt (30 minutes), version, bankRevision and exactly five reviewed question/choice payloads without correct-answer markers. Submit accepts only the five attempt question IDs and one of the four server-snapshotted choice IDs or null for each, validates no duplicates and scores atomically against immutable server answer keys. Final receipt: requestId, owner-scoped attemptId, bankRevision, score, total=5, answeredCount, per-item correct/incorrect/skipped with source explanation, version and committedAt. No scores in public URLs. User may request deletion; legally required minimal de-identified audit retention is documented separately.
- Web Next server actions/BFF use verified HttpOnly session and CSRF; Android /app-api/v1 routes require real mobile bearer authentication and identical owner-scoped semantics. Server derives actor from verified credentials; never trust request-supplied userId or p_actor alone. 400 malformed, 401 unauthenticated, 403 inactive/CSRF/consent, 404 foreign or missing, 409 attempt version/reused UUID with different canonical payload, 410 expired/revoked attempt, 422 invalid choices/locale, 429 quota, 503 unverified bank/release. No-store/private responses, no sensitive analytics, no answer key in error responses. Authenticated /app-api and web parity must be tested rather than assumed.

### PostgreSQL model, transaction order, concurrency and idempotency
- Additive, forward-only migrations only after isolated real-PostgreSQL approval. Proposed glossary_practice_questions(question_id UUID, question_revision BIGINT, term_id UUID, locale CHECK IN ko/en/ja/zh, bank_revision, source_revision, approved_editor, independent_reviewer, status, answer_choice_id, explanation, immutable_payload_digest, created_at; PRIMARY KEY(question_id,question_revision), FK to authoritative term registry and published locale revision). Choices table PRIMARY KEY(question_id,question_revision,choice_id), exactly four validated at publication transaction, unique choice identifiers and one correct choice enforced by publish function. No direct app role DML, no PUBLIC EXECUTE, restricted SECURITY DEFINER owner/admin functions with fixed search_path.
- glossary_practice_attempts(attempt_id UUID PK,user_id FK,locale,bank_revision,created_at,expires_at,version,status CHECK,submitted_at,score CHECK 0..5, deleted_at); glossary_practice_attempt_items(attempt_id,position 1..5,question_id,question_revision,choice_order_snapshot,selected_choice_id nullable,correct_at_submission, FK composite to immutable question revision); glossary_practice_receipts(user_id,request_id UUID,operation,canonical_payload_digest,result_snapshot,committed_at, PRIMARY KEY(user_id,request_id)). Composite owner/attempt FKs prevent cross-user association; question snapshot immutable after start. Index (user_id,created_at DESC,attempt_id DESC), (user_id,status,expires_at), (attempt_id,position); no volatile now() partial-index predicate.
- One attempt has exactly five distinct canonical term IDs and five question IDs; select with bank revision locked, then insert attempt and five items atomically. Member limit: max 10 starts per rolling UTC 24h, at most 1 active attempt per locale, serialized by member lock; 30-minute expiry is checked transactionally. Submission locks owner then attempt and item rows in deterministic order, checks version/bank and canonical selections, grades, stores immutable result and receipt in the same transaction. Same user/requestId + same operation/payload digest returns the ORIGINAL receipt; reuse with different operation/payload/attempt returns 409 without mutation. Timeout => GET receipt then retry identical request. Withdrawals are idempotent and owner-only. Retention: private attempt details 90 days then purge by approved job; do not retain raw answer history indefinitely.
- Read-only result pagination uses signed owner/session/filter/expiry cursor; no cross-user or tampered cursor leakage. Administrator has approved aggregate-only counts (bucket >=20 eligible users), editorial bank review and revoke with audit, never member-level attempt scores by default. Revoke fences new starts and unsubmitted attempts while preserving previously committed historical receipts without retroactive rescore.

### Security, SEO, mobile/a11y, analytics and operations
- Rate-limit per account/IP/device and editorial APIs; prevent enumeration, answer-key exposure, timing-based correctness or question-bank scraping. Validate UUID, length, locale, choice allowlist, CSRF, session freshness and account switching; strict cache-control no-store and robots noindex/nofollow on private routes, exclude from sitemap and ad targeting. Keep existing public canonical/hreflang/DefinedTerm SEO unchanged; no indexable private scores. Admin content actions require separate admin role, CSRF, recent reauth for publish/revoke and independent reviewer.
- WCAG 2.2 AA: semantic radio groups, labels and keyboard navigation, accessible answer review, no timer pressure on reading/selection (expiry explained and extendable only by restarting), visible progress, focus management, live regions for submit/errors, no color-only correctness, reduced motion, 200% zoom and 320/360/390/768/1024/1280 widths, Android TalkBack. Localized copy for ko/en/ja/zh is reviewed; never fake a locale by machine translating an unreviewed answer key.
- KPI: consented start-to-submit completion, reviewed question coverage by locale, invalid answer rejection, stale bank 409/503, verified receipt success, voluntary repeat engagement, privacy incidents target zero; never label score as certification, mastery, financial skill or investment return. Avoid storing member selections in general event analytics.
- Gate: feature flag OFF by default. Unit, contract, migration, security and real isolated PostgreSQL tests must cover five approved distinct terms per locale, unavailable locale, 10,001 synthetic attempts for index/cursor, 100 concurrent starts/submits, 100 forged owner/CSRF/replay requests, stale 24h ISR, question revoke mid-attempt, duplicate UUID same/different payload, 30-minute expiry, no answer leakage before submit, web/Android parity, keyboard/TalkBack and no WLD/ledger changes. Independently verified encrypted off-host backup restore, migration parity/ACL, finance ledger reconciliation, full CI and exact commit SHA official Test E2E, explicit authorized GitOps promotion and rollback proof precede any Production release. Rollback disables starts/submits, preserves receipts, never erases other workers' artifacts or modifies balances.

### Concrete developer backlog (P0 4 / P1 6)
- **P0 ENV1444-01:** Root 98% (~2.0 GiB free), data 99% (~3.5 GiB free) at 06:04 KST; expand capacity/owner-approved retention, independently decrypt/restore encrypted offsite backup before schema work. No arbitrary cleanup.
- **P0 FIN1444-02:** QA v1357 confirms 18 USER_CASH accounts with +18,000 WLD mismatch, Treasury zero. Forensic provenance and authorized forward-only reconciliation; never replay payout or fabricate ledger.
- **P0 SEC1444-03:** QA v1357 reports 239 app-writable public relations, three PUBLIC bank EXECUTEs, five migration checksum mismatches and 20 unapplied SQL files. Verify ACL and bank manifest provenance; no privilege broadening or unreviewed migration.
- **P0 REL1444-04:** Current main runtime CI SKIPPED, official Test SHA differs from main, Production SHA unknown; full authenticated exact-SHA Test, approved GitOps promotion, security/backup/rollback proof required. Do not interpret HTTP smoke as full acceptance.
- **P1 QUIZ1444-05:** Approved four-locale immutable question/choice/term manifest, independent editorial review, correct-answer secrecy and versioned content retirement.
- **P1 QUIZ1444-06:** Additive owner-only attempt/items/receipt DB functions, 5-question/10-per-day cap, lock order, 30-minute expiry, 90-day retention, original digest replay.
- **P1 QUIZ1444-07:** NestJS DTOs, Next BFF and Android app-api parity, 400/401/403/404/409/410/422/429/503 contract, receipt lookup and cache fencing.
- **P1 QUIZ1444-08:** Public optional entry, member quiz and private review UI with explicit statuses, four-locale copy, 320px/WCAG/TalkBack and no fake progress.
- **P1 QUIZ1444-09:** Admin review/revoke, separate editor/reviewer, private-data lifecycle, public SEO preservation, aggregate-only KPI, abuse and answer-key leak defense.
- **P1 QUIZ1444-10:** Real isolated PG 10,001-row, 100-way races, 100 authorization negatives, revocation/replay/expiry, exact-SHA web/Android E2E and flag-off rollback with zero ledger delta.

**External evidence gate:** Newly accepted independently sourced/deduplicated records 0; cumulative UNKNOWN / 10,000, BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. A glossary term, editorial question, publisher family, GitHub issue or synthetic fixture is not an independently approved external reference record. This is a proposed design, not implementation, Test or Production deployment.


## v1442 / F675-166 — Canonical glossary identity, locale reading and replay hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- GitHub handoff: [Draft PR #1784](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1784) is stacked on unmerged [#1780](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1780); [development issue #1779](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1779) updated. Docs only; release HOLD.

**Authority and cycle.** Previous Feature Addition v1440 -> Current Feature Improvement v1442 -> Next Feature Addition v1444 (provisional). The latest remote main is 35971c600f25869c96f1c666d4b9f58927202730 (start/mid checked), predecessor planning Draft PR #1780 remains unmerged; this is a docs-only stacked change, not a deployed glossary feature. The latest independently available prior QA baseline is v1355 (FAILED/HOLD); live miniPC Debian 13.6 and development PostgreSQL were read-only inspected. Never infer Production safety from active services or source-only tests.

### Concrete source findings and product correction
- frontend/src/config/pseo-glossary.config.ts defines **50 distinct canonical financial glossary slugs**. frontend/src/app/guide/components/glossary-search.tsx separately defines **12 in-app/system glossary IDs**; the sets have **zero overlap**. They are different content namespaces, not 62 interchangeable financial study terms. Do not infer membership or auto-save from the in-app search widget.
- frontend/src/app/[locale]/guide/glossary/[term]/page.tsx defines **five aliases** (dca, mdd, beta, volatility, water-down). The current alias **volatility -> sharpe-ratio** is not a synonym: volatility and risk-adjusted Sharpe ratio are different concepts. Treat this mapping as an editorial semantic-integrity P0 issue; do not silently save/read a Sharpe term when a user requested volatility. Existing public pages are not changed by this planning run.
- Locale pages support en/ja/zh, Korean pages use /guide/glossary/[term], and detail pages revalidate after 86400 seconds. The v1440 single read_revision field cannot represent which language the member explicitly read. A public alias or locale switch must never fabricate a private read mark or overwrite another language's progress.

### Identity, canonicalization, source manifest and content retirement
Use an immutable glossary_term_id (UUID) as the internal identity, independent of slug, locale and public URL. Canonical financial term manifest records term_id, canonical_slug, source_namespace=PSEO_FINANCE, release_sha, manifest_sha256, per-locale content_digest/revision for ko/en/ja/zh, availability and publication proof. Stable SHA-256 input is canonical UTF-8 JSON with explicitly ordered fields, normalized Unicode, and versioned schema; reject duplicate term IDs/slugs, missing locale fields and unsigned/unreviewed artifacts before enabling writes. Publish the same reviewed immutable manifest to Next, Nest and Android contract generation; verify deployed exact SHA, never use an arbitrary client-provided digest as authority. Do not couple the feature to unmerged F675-165 correction PRs.
Alias table is separate and versioned: alias_slug, locale_scope, target_term_id, editorial_equivalence_approved, effective_revision, retired_at. Only reviewed semantic equivalents may redirect with an appropriate canonical public URL. An alias that changes concept (notably volatility -> sharpe-ratio) is QUARANTINED, with no private write or automatic remap; return a safe invalid-alias response (422 on authenticated mutation), and offer a real glossary search. On canonical rename retain the same term_id and historical saved entry; on removal keep a private tombstone with explicit Remove. Never create a new saved term from an unknown slug or alias. A verified stale client revision returns 409 CONTENT_CHANGED with safe current revision metadata; absent or inconsistent manifest/release proof returns 503 REVISION_UNAVAILABLE, feature writes OFF.

### Member flows, locales, CTA and observable states
Public financial term pages keep their normal SEO and guest access. A signed-in active member explicitly chooses Save/Remove and Mark read/Mark unread. The private /account/learning/glossary list shows the canonical title in the chosen locale, a visible language tag for each voluntary read mark, and optional "read in Korean; not marked in English" information. Marking read is **per locale and exact content revision**, not global mastery; a revision change displays NEEDS_REREAD for that locale while preserving historical evidence. Switching locale never converts another locale's read state. Never auto-mark by scrolling, dwell time, analytics, locale redirect, or opening a term. Guests see Sign in to save; no guest localStorage backfill.
States include UNAUTHENTICATED, LOADING, UNSAVED, SAVING, SAVED_UNREAD, SAVED_READ_IN_LOCALE, NEEDS_REREAD, UNAVAILABLE_TERM, INVALID_ALIAS, NETWORK_UNKNOWN, CONFLICT_409, REGISTRY_UNAVAILABLE_503, RATE_LIMITED_429, ERROR. Server receipt must precede success UI; timeout => lookup original receipt, then retry same request UUID and payload. Account switch/logout invalidates private caches, request keys and in-flight responses. The member cannot infer other members' saved lists or read locales.

### API contract, authentication and Android parity
Keep the v1440 authenticated NestJS route family GET /api/v1/guide/glossary-learning, GET /:slug, PUT /:slug, POST /:slug/read-state, DELETE /:slug, GET /receipts/:requestId; add locale (ko|en|ja|zh), canonicalTermId and localeRevision to the request/response DTO and preserve old clients with an explicit versioned contract rather than silently changing semantics. Canonical slug is the only accepted mutation path; an alias must be resolved by the server's reviewed mapping, not trusted from the client. PUT save is term-level, POST read-state requires locale and exact localeRevision; DELETE removes the term regardless of read languages and preserves an immutable receipt. An unchanged same-key replay returns the original response, while requestId reused with different term, locale, operation, expectedVersion or payload returns 409 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED. Receipt includes requestId, owner-scoped termId, canonicalSlug, locale when applicable, original content revision, operation, resulting version, committedAt and canonical payload digest; never leak other users' receipts.
Authentication: web Next BFF uses real HttpOnly session, CSRF and server-derived actor; Android /app-api/v1 uses verified mobile bearer credentials and identical response semantics. Do not trust p_actor alone in a shared database role. 400 invalid format, 401 unauthenticated, 403 inactive/consent/CSRF, 404 foreign/not found, 409 version/content/key conflict, 422 quarantined alias, 429 limit, 503 unverified registry. Every private response is no-store; no private term activity in URL, SSR public metadata, logs or analytics payloads.

### PostgreSQL data model, transactions and cursor
Retain registry and entry/receipt model from v1440 but key entries by (user_id,term_id), not (user_id,slug). Add glossary_term_locales(term_id,locale,revision,content_digest,publication_sha,active) UNIQUE(term_id,locale), and glossary_learning_read_marks(user_id,term_id,locale,read_revision,read_at,version,removed_at) UNIQUE(user_id,term_id,locale), with composite FKs enforcing owner/term consistency and a CHECK on supported locales and nonempty digests. The term entry has immutable term_id, owner-specific save_seq and saved_at, removed_at, version; per-locale read marks do not consume additional slots. The 100-active-saved-term limit is serialized by locking the verified member row, then registry/entry/mark rows in a consistent order. No direct DML grants to moneyverse_app, revoke PUBLIC EXECUTE and use narrowly granted SECURITY DEFINER functions with explicit actor authorization. Account deletion/erasure must preserve only legally justified de-identified audit material; do not retain readable member study history indefinitely.
List pagination uses a signed opaque cursor bound to user/session generation, filter, sort, registry epoch, max_save_seq snapshot and expiry, with HMAC key rotation. Order by (save_seq DESC, term_id DESC), limit <=50; re-save after deletion receives a new save_seq. A page excludes newer saves than its snapshot, may omit entries removed during pagination, and never promises a fully transactional snapshot across requests. Tampered, expired, cross-user or filter-swapped cursors fail without revealing saved-term existence. Index (user_id,removed_at,save_seq DESC,term_id DESC), (user_id,term_id,locale), and receipt UNIQUE(user_id,request_id); confirm EXPLAIN under 10,001 synthetic rows. Avoid volatile time predicates in partial indexes. Request UUID + canonical payload digest are checked inside the same DB transaction as state mutation and immutable receipt write.

### Administrator, security, privacy, SEO, accessibility and KPI
Administrator sees only cohort-thresholded aggregates (at least 20 eligible accounts per bucket), alias rejection and revision mismatch counts, not member-level term lists or read history. Enforce member/IP/device abuse limits, input length and slug allowlist, no arbitrary URL fetches, 100-term cap, audit without raw sensitive payloads. Public canonical/hreflang/DefinedTerm metadata and sitemap must stay on reviewed canonical financial slugs; quarantined alias should not create duplicate indexed pages. Private learning page is noindex/nofollow and excluded from sitemaps and ad personalization. WCAG 2.2 AA, keyboard, 320/360/390/768/1024/1280/1440 px, 200% zoom, reduced motion, focus restoration and TalkBack; locale-specific labels and explicit status text are mandatory. KPIs: verified save receipts, voluntary seven-day revisit, per-locale reread rate, invalid-alias rejection, 409/503 rates, cross-device consistency, privacy incident target zero. Never equate read clicks with comprehension or investment returns.

### Test, rollout and rollback acceptance
Real isolated PostgreSQL must verify 50 canonical finance IDs, 12 separate system IDs, all five aliases (especially volatility not silently mapping to Sharpe), four locale revisions, rename/retire, stale 24h ISR A/B, exact SHA mismatch, original receipt replay/digest conflict, 100 concurrent saves/read/remove, 100 forged owner/CSRF/cursor requests, 100-term cap, and 10,001 synthetic rows for cursor/index load. Test cursor snapshot under concurrent delete/re-save and account switch; run authenticated web/Android E2E, a11y and SEO regression, and assert **zero WLD/ledger mutation**. No skipped real-DB test counts as PASS. Feature OFF until independent encrypted off-host restore, security/finance reconciliation, official Test exact-SHA, authorized promotion and rollback proof. On rollback disable writes, preserve receipts and public glossary; no data purge or hidden success. Planning-only: no runtime, schema, balances, Test or Production change.

### Updated developer backlog (P0 4 / P1 6)
- **P0 ENV1442-01:** Root disk 98% (2.6 GiB free) and data disk 99% (3.7 GiB free) at 05:08 KST; preserve other workers, prove off-host encrypted backup and independent full restore before migrations.
- **P0 FIN1442-02:** QA v1355 prior evidence of 18 member cash/ledger variances totaling 18,000 WLD; reconcile authoritative history, never fabricate postings or fund a zero-balance Treasury.
- **P0 SEC1442-03:** Actual read-only development DB shows 235/247 public tables with direct moneyverse_app INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE; close privilege and manifest/alias identity gaps, 3 previously reported PUBLIC bank EXECUTEs and migration parity issues; fail closed.
- **P0 REL1442-04:** Release HOLD pending official exact-SHA Test, independent restore, real DB ACL/finance/security E2E, source/QA approval and explicit authorized GitOps promotion; #1088 build-only auto-merge is not proof.
- **P1 GLOS1442-05:** Immutable term_id, distinct namespace, reviewed aliases and four-locale exact release manifest with schema/unique/FK/index contracts.
- **P1 GLOS1442-06:** Per-locale read mark CAS and original digest-bound receipt, serial 100-term cap, rename/tombstone and race-safe rollback.
- **P1 GLOS1442-07:** NestJS/Next/Android versioned DTOs, 409/422/503 separation, signed owner/session/filter cursor, no-store and receipt recovery.
- **P1 GLOS1442-08:** Save/Read/Remove/Needs-reread/Invalid-alias member flows, account-switch fencing, 320px/TalkBack/locale accessibility.
- **P1 GLOS1442-09:** Aggregate-only admin threshold, private-data erasure, canonical/hreflang/SEO preservation, KPI and abuse controls.
- **P1 GLOS1442-10:** Isolated real-PG 10,001-row, 100-way race, 100 negative ACL/CSRF/cursor, four-locale ISR, exact-SHA, zero-ledger-delta and rollback acceptance.

**External reference gate:** Newly approved independently verified, deduplicated records **0**; cumulative **UNKNOWN / 10,000**. **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. Source slugs, aliases, synthetic fixtures, publisher mentions and GitHub issues are not external independent reference records. ## v1440 / F675-166 — Verified private glossary study list (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- GitHub handoff: [Draft PR #1780](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/pull/1780), [development issue #1779](https://github.com/wtrdd1-hash/Woldeok-Moneyverse-Migration/issues/1779). Unmerged / release hold.

**Cycle and authority.** Previous Feature Improvement v1438 → Current Feature Addition v1440 → Next Feature Improvement v1442 (provisional). Fresh remote main 35971c600f25869c96f1c666d4b9f58927202730 at start and midpoint; QA v1355 read-only evidence; independent branch from main. Earlier F675-165 glossary correction feedback is a distinct, unmerged proposal (#1770/#1775), not a deployed dependency. This design changes no runtime, DB, WLD, Test, Production, or feature switch.

### Product gap, purpose and source of truth
Main currently publishes public /guide/glossary and /guide/glossary/[term] from frontend/src/config/pseo-glossary.config.ts GLOSSARY_TERMS; detail pages use 86400-second revalidation and indexable DefinedTerm/FAQ structured data. No owner-private glossary saved-list, manual read marker, or matching NestJS/DB owner endpoints was found in main. Existing CalculatorSaveAction stores calculator simulations in localStorage and advertises account sync; it does NOT prove that a glossary learning list exists. GitHub issue search found no matching glossary bookmark/learning-list issue. This feature enables active members to save a real glossary term for later study and voluntarily mark it "read"; it must never infer comprehension, educational achievement, investment outcome, financial reward, or automatic reading activity.

### Target, entry points, CTA and states
Public glossary and term pages remain readable and indexable for guests. On /guide/glossary/[term], show explicit "Save term" / "Remove saved term" and "Mark as read" / "Mark unread" for authenticated active consenting members; guests see "Sign in to save" without background persistence. A private /account/learning/glossary screen provides saved-only list, filter SAVED/READ/UNREAD, search over verified public titles, remove, reopen and a non-authoritative self-marked progress counter. Do not silently save on view, backfill localStorage, or treat a click as mastery. States: UNAUTHENTICATED, LOADING, UNSAVED, SAVING, SAVED_UNREAD, SAVED_READ, REMOVING, NETWORK_UNKNOWN (receipt lookup), CONFLICT (409 revision/version), UNAVAILABLE_TERM (tombstone with Remove), RATE_LIMITED (429), ERROR (retry without false success). Always wait for server receipt before displaying "saved" or "read". On logout/account switch, invalidate cache, in-flight response and stored request keys; never show another user's list. No push, email, points, achievements or WLD change.

### Source revision and content retirement
Use the exact deployed immutable glossary slug manifest, generated in the reviewed build from GLOSSARY_TERMS and bound to release SHA plus per-term editorial digest; a frontend-only array cannot be trusted by the backend without a reviewed artifact. This can share the manifest contract proposed by F675-165 after independent review but must not assume that PR is merged. If no trusted manifest/registry exists, feature remains OFF while public glossary works. A stale but verifiable term revision yields 409 CONTENT_CHANGED and asks the user to reopen/reconfirm; unknown frontend/backend registry consistency yields 503 REVISION_UNAVAILABLE and disables writes. On slug removal/rename, preserve owner-only unavailable tombstone and allow Remove without redirecting to an unrelated term. Existing "read" mark is tied to the exact term revision; a content change resets its *current-revision* presentation to "needs reread" without erasing historical self-mark. Do not index private activity or expose private count in public metadata.

### API and Android parity
NestJS authenticated endpoints: GET /api/v1/guide/glossary-learning?state=&cursor=&limit= (owner keyset, max 50/page), GET /api/v1/guide/glossary-learning/:slug, PUT /api/v1/guide/glossary-learning/:slug (save with termRevision, expectedVersion, requestId), POST /api/v1/guide/glossary-learning/:slug/read-state (read:boolean, termRevision, expectedVersion, requestId), DELETE /api/v1/guide/glossary-learning/:slug (requestId, expectedVersion), GET /api/v1/guide/glossary-learning/receipts/:requestId. Return original immutable operation receipt {requestId, slug, operation, version, committedAt, state, termRevision}; on uncertain timeout read receipt before retry. Distinguish 400 malformed, 401 guest, 403 inactive/consent/CSRF, 404 foreign or absent, 409 revision/version/reused-key payload mismatch, 429 quota, 503 unverified manifest/DB. Browser uses Next server actions/BFF and CSRF with HttpOnly same-origin session; Android /app-api/v1 uses independent verified mobile authentication and equivalent DTO/status semantics, not the internal web API token. All private responses Cache-Control: private, no-store and Vary: Cookie where appropriate; avoid secret-bearing URLs. Admin can only inspect aggregate anonymized counts, not individual saved terms.

### PostgreSQL constraints, ownership, transactions and abuse prevention
Add additive glossary_term_registry (slug PK, schemaVersion, revision, contentDigest, releaseSha, active), glossary_learning_entries (user_id, slug, saved_at, read_revision NULLABLE, read_at NULLABLE, version positive, removed_at NULLABLE, updated_at) with PK(user_id,slug), FK(slug) to immutable/retained registry keys, and glossary_learning_receipts (user_id, request_id, operation, slug, canonical_payload_digest, original_result, committed_at) UNIQUE(user_id,request_id). CHECK allowed operations/states, nonnegative version and canonical slug format; owner index (user_id,removed_at,saved_at DESC,slug) and (user_id,read_revision) as justified by EXPLAIN. No volatile now() in partial index. 100 active saved terms per member (not 10,001: that is isolated synthetic test volume); removals do not erase immutable receipts, and repeat saves cannot bypass cap. A locked authenticated owner row followed by term/entry rows serializes save/read/remove; CAS expectedVersion and uniqueness enforce one winner in concurrent changes. Same requestId+canonical payload digest replays original receipt; different operation/body returns 409, including after logout/relogin. Do not use client-provided actor IDs; DB SECURITY DEFINER functions must check owner/role and narrowly grant EXECUTE, revoke PUBLIC, no direct table grants to moneyverse_app. Shared DB principal p_actor alone is not proof of authenticated user: enforce SessionGuard, AuthenticatedGuard, ConsentGuard, CsrfGuard, session-derived actor, strict Android token boundary and real-DB negative ACL tests. Rate limit per member/device/IP without leaking cross-member existence; reject slug injection, oversized payloads and arbitrary URLs; avoid storing finance/account identifiers.

### Admin, SEO, privacy, analytics, accessibility
Admin dashboard may show only coarse aggregates (saves, voluntary read marks, 409/503 errors, 95p response time, receipts verified, unavailable-slug removals), with minimum cohort threshold and no member-level browsing. Public glossary metadata, canonical URLs, FAQ/DefinedTerm JSON-LD, sitemap and OG must remain identical except reviewed public editorial changes; private account screen is noindex/nofollow, no sitemap, no public SSR data and no ad targeting. Minimize retained activity, document data retention and user deletion/erasure with receipt de-identification compatible with audit requirements. Keyboard-only, 320px, 200% zoom, WCAG 2.2 AA, aria-live operation outcomes, stable focus after list removal, non-color status, reduced motion, Android TalkBack and offline retry; no fabricated success. KPI: verified save receipt rate, seven-day voluntary revisit rate (aggregate), cross-device consistency, stale revision rate, privacy violations (target zero), duplicate mutation rate; never claim learning mastery from self-marks.

### Engineering acceptance and rollout
Developers own a separate branch, write RED tests first, then implement and prove with isolated actual PostgreSQL: 10,001 synthetic entry rows for pagination/index load (NOT 10,001 independent external sources), 100 simultaneous save/read/remove and replay requests, 100 cross-account/CSRF/invalid slug/reused requestId negatives, 100-term cap under race, removed/renamed slug, stale 24h ISR build A/B, exact SHA manifest mismatch 409 vs 503, owner logout/account switch, mobile/API/a11y/SEO/no-store and zero-ledger-delta regression. No skipped DB tests may count as PASS. Feature OFF by default; additive reviewed migrations, off-host encrypted backup and independent restore, read-only shadow then canary, verified Test exact-SHA authenticated owner/admin/finance E2E and explicit authorized promotion/rollback. No build-only auto-merge (#1088). On rollback disable writes, preserve private receipts and public glossary; never delete records to conceal failed writes.

### P0/P1 developer backlog (10 tracked items)
- **P0 ENV1440-01:** QA v1355 disk root/data 98%/98%, preserve worker assets, establish capacity and independently restored off-host encrypted backup; no arbitrary cleanup.
- **P0 FIN1440-02:** QA v1355 18 USER_CASH differences totaling 18,000 WLD; independently reconcile account/ledger provenance, do not fabricate corrections; Treasury 0 WLD.
- **P0 SEC1440-03:** QA v1355 app-writable 239 tables, PUBLIC bank EXECUTE 3, migration checksum mismatch 5/unapplied 20; owner/manifest boundary and least privilege must fail closed.
- **P0 REL1440-04:** Official Test exact-SHA, isolated DB ACL, security/finance E2E and independent restore before any production promotion; build-only merge gate #1088 remains blocked.
- **P1 GLOS1440-05:** Immutable deployed glossary manifest and additive owner entry/receipt schema, constraints, indexes and retention.
- **P1 GLOS1440-06:** Serializable owner locks, 100 cap, CAS, original request receipts, stale revision 409 vs 503 and tombstone semantics.
- **P1 GLOS1440-07:** Session/CSRF-protected NestJS + Next BFF + authenticated Android app-api parity, no-store, pagination and receipt lookup.
- **P1 GLOS1440-08:** Term and account-list CTA/states, 320px/a11y/TalkBack, account switching and offline UNKNOWN handling.
- **P1 GLOS1440-09:** Aggregate-only admin, privacy erasure, public SEO unchanged, safe analytics and abuse controls.
- **P1 GLOS1440-10:** Real isolated PG 10,001 synthetic rows, 100 concurrent + 100 BOLA/CSRF negatives, mixed release/ISR and rollback proof, zero WLD change.

**External evidence gate:** Newly accepted independent verified references 0; cumulative deduplicated verified accepted UNKNOWN / 10,000. BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. A glossary slug, synthetic row, GitHub issue or external publisher mention is NOT an approved independent reference. No new distributed planning file; absorb this into the existing integrated English canonical and equivalent Korean documents.
