## v2026.10.24.1210 — F675-109 Board Reading List Hardening: Deletion, Idempotency, Cursor and Resume (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, verified baseline and explicit corrections
- Previous **Feature Addition v1208**; Current **Feature Improvement v1210**; Next **Feature Addition v1212** (provisional). Authoritative remote main `54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede` and canonical main EN/KO plans rechecked start/mid; v1208 Draft PR #1087 is an **unmerged documentation dependency**, not deployed source. Latest QA v1213 (2026-10-24 08:25 KST) FAILED / HOLD; no runtime, production-linked DB, WLD, feature switch, Test, Production, or other worker changes.
- Source-backed: `044-member-board.sql` creates `member_board_posts(id,deleted_at)` and `member_board_delete` soft-deletes; `175-member-board-public-read.sql` and `259-board-author-user-id-and-chat-search.sql` define `member_board_public_get` returning only `deleted_at IS NULL` posts. The actual post page's comment heading has `id="comments-title"`, not `id="comments"`. No reading-list table/API was found in inspected main. Prior v1208 `ON DELETE RESTRICT` FK would prevent future hard-purge of posts while bookmarked; prior generic 404 mapping could prevent an owner from removing a now-unavailable saved post. These are **design defects to resolve before implementation**, not observed production incidents.
- Product remains voluntary member-only Save/Remove and explicit TOP/COMMENTS resume, no passive scroll history, automated interest inference, advertisements, notification, or WLD transfer. Static multilingual blog remains excluded pending an authoritative publication/visibility contract. Reuse existing public board and account UX; no overlap with A/B/C/Gemini/QA branches.

### Precise member, web and Android state contracts
- Entry: `/board/[postId]` Save/Remove and `/account/reading-list` owner list. States GUEST, LOADING, NOT_SAVED, SAVING, SAVED_CONFIRMED, UNAVAILABLE_SAVED, REMOVING, CONFLICT, UNKNOWN_COMMIT, ERROR, SIGNED_OUT. Buttons: Save, Remove, Open post, Resume at comments, Mark TOP, Mark COMMENTS, Refresh saved status, Retry after sign-in. A deleted/unpublished item remains removable by its owner **without public visibility**; owner GET/status may return `available:false` and redacted metadata, while Save of a deleted post returns indistinguishable 404. Unowned or never-saved status and DELETE are 404, not a post-existence oracle. REMOVE returns committed tombstone receipt even when source is unavailable; replay of same key returns original receipt, not current state.
- Actual `/board/[postId]` has `h2#comments-title`; the proposed resume URL is exactly `/board/<canonical-UUID>#comments-title` after fresh visibility check. No arbitrary fragments, query-supplied redirects or untrusted DOM selectors. Heading must be focusable for programmatic keyboard navigation (`tabIndex=-1` or equivalent) with a visible focus indicator and scroll margin below sticky navigation; TOP uses plain canonical URL. If source is unavailable, show redacted card with Remove, not a broken deep link. Reduced-motion preference must not be overridden. Android uses native navigation to comment section, same explicit-only marker and no background reading analytics.
- Auth/session generation fences: on logout, account switch, consent revocation or session expiry, discard in-flight GET/write response and cached title/list data; private BFF/API/edge responses `Cache-Control: private, no-store` and private pages `noindex,nofollow`. A historical idempotency receipt cannot make UI claim SAVED_CONFIRMED after a later removal; reconcile with owner GET after replay/unknown. Never persist another account's list in browser-wide localStorage.

### API, SQL authority, referential integrity and race resolution (PROPOSED ONLY)
- Preserve v1208 owner-only `GET /api/v1/board/reading-list`, `GET .../:postId`, `POST`, `PUT .../:postId/marker`, `DELETE .../:postId` and proposed Android `/app-api/v1/board/reading-list/*`. Session actor, consent and CSRF are server-derived; request body cannot select user. POST/PUT/DELETE require UUID `Idempotency-Key`; PUT/DELETE require `If-Match: "vN"` (return strong ETag and committedVersion). Error mapping: 400/422 malformed, 401 unauthenticated, 403 denied consent/CSRF, 404 not-owned/nonexistent or unavailable-to-SAVE, 409 version/key-digest conflict or cap, 429 throttle, 503 unknown commit; no false 2xx on timeout. DELETE of an owner-saved but unavailable source is **allowed**, with redacted 200 receipt.
- Proposed forward-only table `member_board_reading_list`: `(user_id,post_id)` PK, `user_id REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE`, `post_id REFERENCES member_board_posts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE` **instead of RESTRICT**; `saved_at`, `updated_at`, `removed_at`, `marker CHECK IN ('TOP','COMMENTS')`, positive monotonic `version`. Soft-deleted posts keep rows as UNAVAILABLE_SAVED; a separately authorized **hard purge** cascades active bookmarks (no stale title/body remains). The migration must test existing deletion/account-erasure behavior and avoid unapproved hard deletion. A hard purge may make the old receipt non-resolvable; it never recreates the post. `member_board_reading_requests` and `member_board_reading_events` require explicit `user_id REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE` or separately reviewed anonymized security retention, UUID `post_id` without blocking post purge, 32-byte request digest CHECK, unique `(user_id,idempotency_key)`, immutable receiptId/operation/resultVersion; retention/tombstone policy subject to privacy review (90 days is a proposal, not authorization). No copied post title/body, token, IP or passive reading trail.
- Transaction lock order is **actor users row → source post row FOR SHARE → owner reading-list row → idempotency insert/lookup** consistently for SAVE/MARK/REMOVE when source exists; unavailable REMOVE must still lock actor+saved row and not require visible source. For SAVE, acquire source `FOR SHARE`, re-evaluate `deleted_at IS NULL` after lock, serialize owner cap count (max 500) under actor lock, CAS row version, commit receipt/event atomically; concurrent post soft-delete UPDATE must wait on the row lock. Do not claim an already-started read can retract data after a later deletion commits; enforce no metadata disclosure for reads **started after** the deletion commit. For same-key concurrent requests use unique conflict-safe insert/re-read under the same transaction (not SELECT-then-INSERT without conflict handling); identical digest replays exact original receipt, mismatched digest 409. Re-SAVE after REMOVE needs a **new key plus current tombstone version**; a stale SAVE/MARK cannot resurrect.
- Cursor: HMAC-authenticated opaque payload with owner/session-generation scope, stable `saved_at,post_id` ordering, filter/version and short expiry; reject cross-user, tampered, expired or filter-mismatched cursor (400/422, never another member's data). Explicit initial `asOf` boundary stabilizes paging against new saves; concurrent deletes may reduce results and must not leak unavailable metadata. The 10,001 same-timestamp fixture is a **dedicated isolated DB synthetic test with cap overridden for the fixture only**; production 500-item cap remains enforced. No raw bookmark history in telemetry.

### P0/P1 executable backlog, QA and release gate
- **P0 ENV1210-01** — Isolate shared Production/dev `woldeok_moneyverse_dev` and constrain 5433 wildcard network exposure; verify exact connection identities and encrypted isolated restore before any write/test fixture. Root disk 93% requires capacity guard; never remove another agent's worktree.
- **P0 SEC1210-02** — Replace app-role UPDATE/DELETE grants (235/241 tables) and three PUBLIC bank EXECUTE grants with reviewed forward-only least privilege; isolated real-PostgreSQL negative tests, no ad hoc live REVOKE.
- **P0 REL1210-03** — QA v1213 full lint fail (wallet CSV), frontend Saturday blog test fail, pnpm audit Critical4/High10 and bot Critical1/High4, migration266 checksum mismatch, route-ledger missing inventory, isolated restore and exact-SHA Test all HOLD. **New P0 #1088:** auto-integrate build-only Test Candidate success cannot authorize ready-PR merge; require independently attested exact-head deployed Test backend/API/DB + authenticated login/finance E2E and security PASS before merge/branch deletion, with negative workflow tests.
- **P0 BOARD1210-04** — Resolve public visibility/delete/save race and redacted unavailable REMOVE; test 100 SAVE-vs-soft-delete interleavings, 100 foreign-owner probes, no post metadata in reads begun after deletion commit, no title leakage in private list/SEO.
- **P1 BOARD1210-05** — Forward-only FK CASCADE and account/post erasure parity, owner-scoped SECURITY DEFINER, deterministic actor→post→list lock order, request digest and 100-way original receipt; hard-purge/account-delete fixtures.
- **P1 BOARD1210-06** — HMAC owner-bound expiring snapshot cursor, 500 cap, 10,001 synthetic same-timestamp fixture, concurrent insert/delete pagination and key-rotation invalidation.
- **P1 BOARD1210-07** — Source-accurate `#comments-title` resume, explicit marker CAS, focus/scroll-offset/reduced-motion/320px/200%-zoom and Android native deep-link regression.
- **P1 BOARD1210-08** — Account switch/session invalidation, private no-store/noindex, aggregate-only admin with small-cell suppression, CSRF/BOLA/rate-limit, privacy/erasure and consent withdrawal.
- **P1 BOARD1210-09** — Web+Android confirmed receipt/unknown-commit/unavailable saved/remove UX, negative authorization, conflict retry, KPI success vs false-success, no passive tracking.
- **P1 BOARD1210-10** — Real isolated PostgreSQL TDD, 100-way concurrent SAVE/MARK/REMOVE and deletion, immutable migration/checksum parity, flag OFF and rollback preserving tombstones, receipts, privacy and existing board access.
- Release: `board_reading_list` defaults OFF. Separate engineering branch only; exact-head full CI/security, isolated PostgreSQL ACL+backup restore, official Test authenticated web/Android/financial regression, independently attested deployment and approved GitOps canary before Production. Build Test Candidate SUCCESS alone is not a release/merge gate. No runtime deployment from planning. No main merge, Test/Production change, or other worker branch changes in this cycle.
- Independent reference gate: consulted PostgreSQL explicit locking (https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/explicit-locking.html), OWASP authorization (https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html), W3C focus visibility (https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/focus-not-obscured-minimum). **Three publisher families reviewed, zero newly approved/deduplicated records, cumulative approved UNKNOWN / 10,000; BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS.** Reviewing pages is not collecting 10,000 independent evidence records.


## v2026.10.24.1208 — F675-109 Verified Member Board Reading List and Manual Resume Marker (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, authority, evidence, and product boundary
- Previous Feature Improvement v1206; Current Feature Addition v1208; Next Feature Improvement v1210 (provisional). Source-of-truth main `54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede` rechecked start/mid; v1206 Draft #1082 is unmerged and is the stacked documentation base, not main. Latest QA v1211 (2026-10-24 07:28 KST) FAILED / Production HOLD. No runtime, DB, WLD, Test, Production, feature switch, or other worker branch changes.
- Confirmed current code: `frontend/src/app/board/[postId]/page.tsx` renders public and member board posts; `backend/src/board/public-board.controller.ts` and `public-board.service.ts` call `member_board_public_get` for public visibility; `packages/database/migrations/044-member-board.sql` has UUID post IDs, `deleted_at`, and a visible-post index. There is no verified owner reading-list API or bookmark table in the inspected main migration/source paths. `frontend/src/app/blog/blog-page-server.tsx` serves locale-specific articles from frontend data, without a verified NestJS public-content authority: blog saving is OUT OF SCOPE until a reviewed publication manifest or canonical backend visibility contract exists. Do not reuse collectible `collections` favorites as article bookmarks or duplicate C's reading-scale UI.
- **Purpose/personas:** Allow authenticated members to manually save visible public board posts, revisit them across devices, and explicitly mark whether to resume at post top or comments; never infer scroll tracking, reading completion, or user interests. Guests can read public posts but must sign in to save. Member, Android app member, aggregate-only content operator, and authorized security/privacy reviewer are the actors. No reward, WLD credit, trade, promotion, algorithmic profiling, or automatic email/push send.

### Navigation, screen contract, and state machine
- Entry: `/board/[postId]` action **Save to reading list** / **Remove from reading list**; account shortcut and board list link to `/account/reading-list`. A saved item shows **Open post**, **Resume at comments** (only when explicitly selected), **Mark start/comments**, **Remove**, and **Refresh**. Anonymous Save opens login with safe same-origin return path, never creates a server record.
- States `guest|loading|not_saved|saving|saved|removing|unavailable|conflict|error|unknown`; no optimistic success or URL-query success flag. A confirmed DB receipt alone shows `saved`; network uncertainty shows **Check saved status**, not **Saved**. Empty list has a link to `/board`; deleted/moderated/inaccessible posts show only **Post unavailable** and **Remove**, never a cached title, author, body or screenshot. Unavailable rows may be counted in the owner's private list but are never externally exposed.
- No automatic scroll capture. Resume marker enum `TOP|COMMENTS` is saved only on explicit button activation. Do not accept arbitrary CSS selectors, DOM paths, URLs or free-text anchors. Opening a saved item rechecks current public visibility and resolves only the canonical `/board/<UUID>` link.

### Proposed API, auth and mobile contract — not implemented
- `GET /api/v1/board/reading-list?cursor=&limit=20` member session+consent; max 100, opaque signed keyset cursor `(saved_at DESC,post_id DESC)`; return `items:[{postId,savedAt,marker,version,available,publicTitle?}]`, `nextCursor`, never private body or foreign post metadata. `GET /api/v1/board/reading-list/:postId` owner-only status, 404 for not saved/foreign. `POST /api/v1/board/reading-list` authenticated+CSRF+consent with `{postId:UUID}`, `Idempotency-Key:UUID`; `PUT /api/v1/board/reading-list/:postId/marker` with `{marker:TOP|COMMENTS}`, `If-Match:version` and idempotency header; `DELETE /api/v1/board/reading-list/:postId` same guards and idempotency header. Successful writes return committed `{receiptId,postId,version,state,savedAt?}`, not an inferred client state.
- Server actor derived from session, never userId in body; 400 malformed UUID/marker, 401 missing login, 403 invalid CSRF/consent, 404 unavailable or not-owned, 409 stale version/key reused with different payload or max saved items, 429 throttled, 503 uncertain persistence (offer status recheck). No membership or existence oracle for another member's saved item. Next server-only BFF forwards session and CSRF without exposing internal API token. Android `/app-api/v1/board/reading-list/*` is a proposed contract requiring route-map/allowlist parity and authenticated device-session checks; it is NOT currently implemented. Private responses always `Cache-Control: private, no-store`.

### Proposed schema, SQL authority, atomicity and retention
- New forward-only reviewed migration (number assigned by engineering only after reconciling unmerged 280/281 and main migration history): `member_board_reading_list(user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,post_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES member_board_posts(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,saved_at timestamptz NOT NULL,updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL,removed_at timestamptz,marker text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'TOP' CHECK(marker IN ('TOP','COMMENTS')),version bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK(version>0),PRIMARY KEY(user_id,post_id))`; partial owner list index `(user_id,saved_at DESC,post_id DESC) WHERE removed_at IS NULL`; avoid copying private post title/body into list storage. Post deletion remains authoritative and never cascades into a public cache.
- `member_board_reading_requests(user_id uuid NOT NULL,idempotency_key uuid NOT NULL,request_hash bytea NOT NULL,receipt_id uuid NOT NULL UNIQUE,post_id uuid NOT NULL,operation text CHECK IN ('SAVE','MARK','REMOVE'),result_version bigint NOT NULL,created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(user_id,idempotency_key))` plus append-only `member_board_reading_events(id uuid PK,user_id uuid,post_id uuid,event_type text,version bigint,created_at timestamptz)`; index `(user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` for redacted audit. Never store raw session tokens, IPs, browsing histories, reading time or arbitrary anchors. Retention of removed-row tombstones and audit: initial 90-day proposal pending legal/privacy review; no silent destruction of mandatory security records.
- Owner-scoped `SECURITY DEFINER` functions with fixed `search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp`, explicit actor/active-session validation, reviewed `EXECUTE` allowlist, `PUBLIC EXECUTE` revoked and no direct `moneyverse_app` table DML. SAVE transaction checks public post visibility using the same authoritative predicate as `member_board_public_get`, locks post visibility against concurrent deletion/moderation, serializes per-owner cap checks by locking the actor users row (SELECT ... FOR UPDATE) before counting active items; enforces max 500 active saves atomically, locks/updates existing owner+post row, persists original idempotency receipt and event together. Same key+same fingerprint returns exact original historical receipt (not a promise that the item is still saved); client separately rechecks current status; same key+different request 409. REMOVE records a versioned tombstone; concurrent stale SAVE/MARK must not resurrect removed rows without a new explicit key and version. Read rechecks public visibility at response time; no stale title/author leakage if post was removed. A transaction must not issue WLD/ledger writes.

### Admin, security, SEO, a11y, telemetry and abuse controls
- No member reading list in ordinary admin screens. `GET /api/v1/admin/board/reading-list/metrics` returns only sufficiently aggregated totals (saved/removed, stale-source suppression, API 409/429/5xx, p95 read latency), with small-cell suppression; no per-member titles or identity joins. Emergency privacy investigation requires separate authorized role, recent reauthentication, reason and immutable audit; bulk export prohibited by default. Abuse budgets proposed 30 writes/member/minute and 500 active saved items/member, server-enforced and adjustable after observed baseline; input length caps, rate limits, CSRF, BOLA, replay, ID enumeration and permission-negative tests required.
- Private list and member save state `noindex,nofollow`, no sitemap entry, no public OpenGraph or structured data derived from saved state, `private,no-store`. Public board canonical/SEO unchanged; a removed/unpublished post is never resurrected via reading list. At 320px and 200% zoom use full-width controls, 44px touch targets, semantic buttons, visible focus, `aria-pressed` for saved toggle, `role=status` only for confirmed receipt and `role=alert` for failures, keyboard navigation and screen-reader state. Android native UI uses equivalent labels, no background reading telemetry or notification permission inference.
- KPIs: confirmed save success / eligible CTA impressions, 7-day explicit return-to-saved-item (aggregated), unavailable-source suppression, conflict/unknown receipt rate, median list load and p95, privacy complaints, wrong-owner access failures; no raw browsing events or member IDs in analytics. Targets require real baseline; safety thresholds: 0/100 foreign owner exposures, 0 leaked removed-post titles, 0 false-success receipts.

### P0/P1 executable backlog, test and release hold
- **P0 ENV1208-01** — Separate Production/dev DB currently sharing `woldeok_moneyverse_dev`, contain PostgreSQL 5433 wildcard exposure with approved network policy; prove separate exact connection identities without touching live balances.
- **P0 SEC1208-02** — Resolve broad app-role DML (QA: 235/241 tables), three `PUBLIC` bank EXECUTE grants; reviewed forward-only least privilege, real-PG negative ACL tests; no ad hoc production revoke.
- **P0 REL1208-03** — QA v1211 full lint/frontend failure, main pnpm audit 4 Critical/10 High and bot 1 Critical/4 High, migration266 checksum provenance, exact-SHA CI/Test, backup isolated restore and approved GitOps release gate. QA #1076 CI success is not official Test/Production authorization.
- **P0 BOARD1208-04** — Prove canonical public board visibility/deletion/moderation race and zero stale metadata in saved-list reads, including 100 save-vs-delete races and foreign-post oracle tests.
- **P1 BOARD1208-05** — Forward-only schema, owner-scoped SECURITY DEFINER functions, atomic 100-way same-key receipt, 100 conflicting fingerprints, versioned tombstones and 500-item cap.
- **P1 BOARD1208-06** — Member web Save/Remove/list/empty/unavailable/unknown UX with true committed receipts and stable keyset cursor for 10,001 same-timestamp saves.
- **P1 BOARD1208-07** — Explicit TOP/COMMENTS marker, CAS/If-Match, stale-session isolation, safe canonical resume and no automatic tracking.
- **P1 BOARD1208-08** — Android App API contract and authorized aggregate-only admin diagnostics, role/CSRF/reauth negative tests.
- **P1 BOARD1208-09** — Privacy retention/erasure policy, noindex/no-store/public SEO isolation, WCAG 2.2 AA 320px/200% zoom and privacy-safe KPIs.
- **P1 BOARD1208-10** — RED/GREEN unit/real PostgreSQL/contract/web+Android/QA regression, migration parity, concurrency, idempotency, 404/409/429/503, deleted-post race, 10,001 cursor, flag-off rollback.
- Release: new feature switch `board_reading_list` default OFF, reviewed separate development branch and migrations only after source/PR-stack reconciliation. Exact commit SHA CI full lint/typecheck/build/test/security audits, isolated real-PG grants and backup restore, authenticated official Test web/Android/finance regression and controlled canary must pass before authorized GitOps zero-downtime Production promotion. Flag OFF halts new saves/updates without deleting private records; rollback keeps tombstones, receipts and old clients from resurrecting saves. Never deploy from planning; do not merge #1082 or other agents' drafts without independent review.
- **Independent reference gate:** OWASP API1 BOLA (https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/), PostgreSQL ON CONFLICT (https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/sql-insert.html), W3C WCAG 2.2 (https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/). Three independent publisher families consulted; this is NOT 3 approved reference records. Newly accepted/deduplicated = **0**, cumulative accepted = **UNKNOWN**, target **10,000**, gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**. No fabricated count.


## v2026.10.24.1206 — F675-108 Accessibility Preference Hardening: Identity, Prepaint and Durable Sync (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, verified source and purpose
- Previous Feature Addition v1204; Current Feature Improvement v1206; Next Feature Addition v1208 (provisional). Latest canonical main 54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede unchanged at start/mid; v1204 is an unmerged Draft, not main. QA v1209 (2026-10-24 06:45 KST) FAILED/HOLD. Documentation only; no runtime, DB, WLD, Test, Production, feature-switch or other-agent branch changes.
- frontend/src/app/layout.tsx runs POINT_PREFERENCE_SCRIPT before SiteShell and uses next-themes ThemeProvider; frontend/src/lib/theme.ts owns wdmv.theme/wdmv.point; globals.css and cosmetics.css already honor OS reduced motion. Account page has no accessibility link. backend/src/account/account.controller.ts uses SessionGuard/AuthenticatedGuard/ConsentGuard and mutation CsrfGuard. No new accessibility preference API or schema was found. Preserve existing theme, point, session/device center and native OS ownership.
- Refined hazards, not confirmed incidents: an in-flight member response could overwrite a new account's settings; server values could leak into shared localStorage; unversioned DELETE could allow stale re-enable; site high contrast or motion could override OS forced-colors/reduced-motion.

### Entry, CTA, states and precedence
- Guest /accessibility and member /account/accessibility; shortcuts from account and common utilities. Text scale 100/125/150/175/200%, contrast standard/high, motion device/reduce. CTA Preview, Save on this device, Sync across my devices (explicit opt-in default OFF), Compare versions, Use this device, Use other device, Retry, Reset device, Stop syncing. Do not block login, WLD transaction confirmations or security alerts.
- Distinguish PREVIEW_UNSAVED (in-memory), LOCAL_SAVED (device only), SERVER_CONFIRMED (durable receipt), SYNC_PENDING, CONFLICT, OFFLINE, SAVE_FAILED and SYNC_DISABLED. aria-live announces actual persistence, not merely a sent request. Offline edits remain local; reconnection never silently queues sync.
- OS forced-colors overrides app high contrast and point palette; OS reduced-motion overrides any app motion choice. App may only further reduce motion. Use paired system colors Canvas/CanvasText and ButtonFace/ButtonText, visible focus/borders, no blanket forced-color-adjust:none. Browser zoom and Android native font scaling are not capped; preserve next-themes class and existing point-color storage. Test OS forced-colors plus prefers-contrast and light/dark combinations.
- Prepaint only allowlisted root attributes data-a11y-scale (100|125|150|175|200), data-a11y-contrast (standard|high), data-a11y-motion (device|reduce). Parse wdmv.accessibility.v1 JSON only when UTF-8 <=512 bytes, version=1, exact keys/enums; reject arbitrary CSS/HTML/scripts. Run before interactive shell, do not modify wdmv.theme/point; storage disabled/invalid JSON falls back to OS-safe defaults. SSR and first client render match; controls are unselected until mounted; no-JS baseline remains readable.
- Device-local preference can survive account switch, but server-synced values/account identifiers NEVER persist in browser-wide localStorage. Server snapshot is session-memory only. On logout, account change, consent withdrawal or expiry, increment auth-generation, discard pending GET/PUT/DELETE responses, remove server-derived root attributes and reapply only explicit device-local values. Cross-tab signals carry no IDs/values/receipts. If local and server values differ on login, require explicit comparison; never silently overwrite.
- Conflict UI shows local preview and server version/time. Reset device does not revoke server sync; Stop syncing is a versioned server operation and preserves local preferences. Error and retry paths remain keyboard and screen-reader accessible.

### API, DB, concurrency, privacy (PROPOSED; NOT IMPLEMENTED)
- GET /api/v1/account/accessibility-preferences: authenticated owner only, 200 {syncEnabled,textScalePct,contrastMode,motionMode,version,updatedAt}, strong ETag "vN"; absent row -> 200 {syncEnabled:false,values:null,version:0}, ETag "v0". Cache-Control private,no-store across API/BFF/edge. Never trust body/query userId. Web session/consent + mutation CSRF, Android native-equivalent auth and owner checks.
- PUT requires If-Match:"vN", Idempotency-Key UUID and exact allowlisted {textScalePct:150,contrastMode:"high",motionMode:"reduce",syncEnabled:true}; no implicit opt-in. Durable response {receiptId,operation:"UPSERT",committedVersion,serverConfirmedAt,replayed}. DELETE with If-Match/UUID means Stop syncing, returns same receipt fields with operation:"DISABLE"; no physical row deletion/version reset. Replay same key+same digest returns ORIGINAL receipt even if later version exists; it does not claim current state.
- Errors: 401 missing session; 403 denied/consent revoked; 409 VERSION_CONFLICT with currentVersion only (GET to compare) or IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED for different payload/operation; 422 invalid ETag/key/enum/scale; 429 per-user write throttle; 503 unavailable. No server write on preview/local save; no “synced” label before receipt.
- Additive member_accessibility_preferences: user_id UUID PK FK users(id), text_scale_pct SMALLINT CHECK IN(100,125,150,175,200), contrast_mode CHECK standard/high, motion_mode CHECK device/reduce, sync_enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE, version BIGINT NOT NULL CHECK>0, updated_at timestamptz, disabled_at nullable. Separate receipts(id UUID PK,user_id UUID FK,idempotency_key UUID,operation UPSERT/DISABLE,request_digest bytea length32,committed_version bigint,created_at timestamptz,UNIQUE(user_id,idempotency_key)). PK covers owner reads; no redundant user_id index. No raw preferences in receipts/logs/analytics.
- SECURITY DEFINER function SET search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp with qualified public objects, authenticated actor and consent, no PUBLIC EXECUTE or app direct DML. Serialize first-write via user lock/advisory lock, then row lock and version CAS; validate canonical digest, update preference and receipt atomically. DISABLE increments version and persists a tombstone(sync_enabled=false, disabled_at) to fence stale clients. 100 same-key concurrent PUT -> one mutation/receipt; 100 distinct stale-version PUT -> at most one advancement; 100 PUT/DISABLE races -> monotonic version and zero stale resurrection. Real isolated PostgreSQL role-negative tests required.
- No disability inference, fingerprinting, ad targeting, user-level admin access or account-linked client analytics. Retention of tombstone/receipt and account-deletion cascade need legal/privacy approval; no unreviewed indefinite retention. Never affect WLD balances or financial permissions.

### Admin, mobile, SEO, a11y, rollout and backlog
- Admin /admin/experience/accessibility shows only aggregated opt-in, errors and regression rates with k>=10 per cohort and differencing-resistant suppression; never member settings/mutations. KPI: preview-to-local-save, opt-in, confirmed receipt, conflict, unauthorized disclosure (target zero), WCAG failure, 200% text/320px task completion, p95 first-paint shift; baselines UNKNOWN. /accessibility, /account/accessibility and /admin/experience/accessibility noindex/nofollow and excluded from sitemap; member APIs private,no-store.
- Android app-api must use native font scaling, contrast and OS Reduce precedence; no WebView localStorage dependency; same opt-in/ETag/UUID receipt/disable tombstone. TalkBack/switch control/offline/account-switch tests. WCAG 2.2 AA: text 4.5:1, large 3:1, 200% resize, 320 CSS px reflow (1280px at 400% zoom), 1.4.12 spacing, visible unobscured keyboard focus; preserve WLD sign/amount, order confirmation and error meaning. Check existing horizontal scroll lock does not hide essential content; Safari/Chrome/Firefox, forced-colors, no-JS and blocked storage regression.
- P0 ENV1206-01 — separate Production/dev/Test DB, restrict wildcard 5433, prove backup isolated restore (#1053/#1060/#1066).
- P0 SEC1206-02 — app-role least privilege and PUBLIC bank EXECUTE revocation, isolated PostgreSQL negative tests (#993).
- P0 REL1206-03 — migration266 checksum, full pnpm audit (2 Critical/8 High #1075), bot npm audit (1 Critical/4 High #1077), route-ledger actual 3-pass E2E (#1078), exact-SHA CI/Test/GitOps gates.
- P0 A11Y1206-04 — critical wallet/order/support/admin contrast, 320px/400% reflow, OS motion/forced-colors and focus baseline; block rollout on failures.
- P1 A11Y1206-05 — bounded prepaint parser, SSR hydration, no-JS/storage fallback, preserve theme and point.
- P1 A11Y1206-06 — auth-generation cross-account isolation, conflict UI, offline and cross-tab non-leaking signals.
- P1 A11Y1206-07 — owner-only API, ETag/CAS, versioned disable tombstone and atomic original receipt.
- P1 A11Y1206-08 — semantic CSS, OS priority, 200%/320px and financial meaning regression.
- P1 A11Y1206-09 — Android parity, thresholded admin aggregate, SEO/no-store, privacy/abuse/KPI.
- P1 A11Y1206-10 — 100-way races/owner negatives, browser/TalkBack/no-JS/offline and Test exact-SHA rollback tests.
- Release feature OFF. QA v1209 local candidate lint/typecheck/build/test PASS is NOT full release PASS: shared live DB, 235/241 app DML, 3 PUBLIC bank EXECUTE, migration266 mismatch, full/bot audits and exact-SHA authenticated Test E2E/isolated restore remain blocked. No direct deployment from planning; separate engineering branch, forward-only reviewed migration, isolated Test exact-SHA, authorized GitOps canary, flag rollback preserving receipts and OS behavior. Gemini/A/B/C/QA branches untouched.
- Independent evidence gate: 10,000 external deduplicated classified references required. W3C and MDN publisher families reviewed (2), newly verified approved 0, cumulative approved UNKNOWN => BLOCKED/IN PROGRESS. Reviewed: https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/ ; https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/reflow.html ; https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/CSS/Reference/At-rules/%40media/forced-colors ; https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/CSS/Reference/At-rules/%40media/prefers-reduced-motion .


## v2026.10.24.1204 — F675-108 Member Accessibility Reading & Interaction Preferences (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, source of truth and verified gaps
- Previous: Feature Improvement v1202; Current: Feature Addition v1204; Next: Feature Improvement v1206 (provisional). Start/mid canonical main 54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede, canonical EN/KO SHA256 unchanged. QA v1205 FAILED/HOLD; no code, DB, Test, Production or WLD changes.
- Source-backed gap: frontend/src/app/account/page.tsx links profile, security, notifications and privacy but no accessibility-preference screen. frontend/src/lib/theme.ts already owns browser-local theme/point choices, and globals.css/cosmetics.css already respect OS prefers-reduced-motion; do NOT reimplement or override those contracts. frontend/src/app/layout.tsx permits browser zoom (maximumScale 5). The account security page already manages active sessions, so device revocation is explicitly OUT of scope.
- Purpose: give readers, including low-vision, motor and vestibular-sensitive members, an optional accessible way to control text scale, stronger contrast and reduced nonessential motion without affecting virtual balances or accessibility defaults. Target: guests (this browser only), authenticated members (local first, explicit cross-device sync), Android clients (native accessibility precedence), administrators (redacted aggregate health only). No disability inference, diagnosis or profiling.

### Entry, user flow, screen and state contract
- Entry: /account -> /account/accessibility; persistent accessibility shortcut in site utilities and sign-in page; guest /accessibility reachable without sign-in. Controls: Preview, Text 100/125/150/175/200%, Contrast Standard/High, Motion Follow device/Reduce, Save on this device, Sync across my devices (explicit opt-in), Reset, Restore device settings. Existing light/dark/point preferences stay independent and local.
- Preview updates immediately but does not claim server save; status LOCAL_ONLY, SYNC_PENDING, SYNCED(version), CONFLICT, OFFLINE, SAVE_FAILED. Distinguish unsaved preview from saved browser value and confirmed server receipt; error preserves local readable presentation and offers Retry/Keep only here. Never block login, payment review or critical alerts behind the settings page.
- On first visit, OS/browser reduced-motion and forced-colors/contrast are respected. OS Reduce ALWAYS wins over an app preference to allow motion; app may only further reduce. High contrast uses audited semantic palettes rather than arbitrary foreground/background inputs; never disable forced-colors. Text scale is additive to browser zoom and must not cap browser zoom. Existing theme palette and browser zoom remain independently controllable.
- Guests have local-only choices; account switching/logout removes the prior account's synchronized snapshot, without erasing deliberately local-only settings. Cross-device sync is opt-in and off by default. Offline/conflict fallback retains usable local preferences; authenticated server preferences do not silently overwrite explicit local choices. Conflict screen offers Use this device / Use other device with clear timestamps and version.
- Mobile 320px to 1280px, keyboard/screen reader/large text; visible focus, labeled sliders/radio groups, minimum 44 CSS px touch targets where practicable, no color-only statuses, accessible live confirmation, reduced-motion preview without moving animation. Protect login, WLD amount/sign, order-book and admin confirmation layout at 200% text + browser zoom; no truncation of transaction meaning.

### Proposed API, data model and consistency (NOT IMPLEMENTED)
- Member GET /api/v1/account/accessibility-preferences returns only the caller's allowlisted values, version and updatedAt; PUT with If-Match version and Idempotency-Key UUID updates explicit sync preference; DELETE with If-Match resets synchronized values and revokes server sync. Unauthenticated guest uses localStorage only; no guest API. Errors 401 unauthenticated, 403 forbidden, 409 stale version/different replay payload, 422 invalid enum/scale, 429 throttled, 503 unavailable. GET is private/no-store. API must authenticate from server session and consent, never trust userId from request body; mutation CSRF for web and equivalent native authorization.
- Proposed forward-only table member_accessibility_preferences: user_id UUID PRIMARY KEY FK users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, text_scale_pct SMALLINT CHECK IN (100,125,150,175,200), contrast_mode CHECK IN ('standard','high'), motion_mode CHECK IN ('device','reduce'), sync_enabled BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE, version BIGINT NOT NULL CHECK(version>0), updated_at timestamptz. Separate member_accessibility_preference_receipts with UNIQUE(user_id,idempotency_key), request_digest bytea(32), committed_version, created_at; index on (user_id,updated_at DESC) only if needed by measured read plans. No free-form CSS, arbitrary URLs, device fingerprints or inferred disability attributes.
- One transaction in SECURITY DEFINER function with fixed search_path, session/owner check and row lock/CAS: validate enum/range, compare expected version, bind idempotency key to canonical request digest, update one row, insert receipt and commit atomically. Same key/same digest returns confirmed receipt; same key/different digest -> 409; 100-way stale-version race allows one version advancement, all others stable 409 or confirmed replay. No app direct table INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE and no PUBLIC EXECUTE; negative permission tests must prove boundary on isolated PostgreSQL.
- Guest localStorage key wdmv.accessibility.v1, strict JSON schema/size bound and no scripts or arbitrary CSS; prepaint allowlisted root data attributes to avoid flash. Storage failure is nonfatal. Server preference is never a credential, never used to authorize finance and never logged with account identity. On account deletion, cascade and retention review; audit only redacted state/version/operation, not sensitive inferred traits.

### Admin, SEO, security, analysis and release
- Admin /admin/experience/accessibility shows only aggregate opt-in, save error and regression counters, no per-member settings and no admin mutation of member preferences. Aggregate disclosure threshold >=10; no cross-tenant analytics, fingerprinting or raw values. Metrics: setting reach, local preview success, opt-in sync adoption, confirmed-save rate, conflict rate, WCAG test failure count, 200%-zoom task completion, p95 UI hydration shift; baselines UNKNOWN, target unauthorized disclosure 0.
- Private settings endpoints and member/admin pages: robots noindex/nofollow, no sitemap, Cache-Control private,no-store and no CDN/shared cache. No security claims from CSS alone; use W3C WCAG 2.2 AA contrast 4.5:1 regular text/3:1 large, resize 200%, reflow at 320 CSS px, focus visibility and reduced motion. W3C WAI-Adapt is exploratory guidance, not a claim that the product implements the specification.
- Android app-api parity: native font scale/OS Reduce/forced contrast always take precedence; allow explicit sync without webview localStorage dependency, screen-reader labels and TalkBack regression. No automatic advertising personalization from accessibility preferences.
- Rollout OFF by default; existing UI unaffected. Block any DB migration while Production and development share 5433 and wildcard binding (#1053/#1060); never run fixtures on Production-connected DB. Before integration: resolve #993 broad app DML/PUBLIC bank EXECUTE, #1067 Next.js Critical2/High1, main lint/frontend failure, migration266 checksum, #1066 legacy backup, isolated restore and exact-SHA official Test authenticated checks. Additive reviewed migrations only; Test exact SHA with 100-way races, owner negatives, screen reader, forced-colors, dark/light, OS motion, 200% zoom and Android; then authorized canary and reversible flag. Rollback turns off server sync and new CSS attributes, preserves safe local OS behavior and durable receipts; no WLD/ledger mutation.

### Prioritized development backlog and acceptance
- P0 ENV1204-01 — #1053/#1060: separate Production/dev/Test identities and close 5433 wildcard network exposure via authorized change; prove off-host deny, authorized API and encrypted backup health, isolated restore.
- P0 SEC1204-02 — #993: revoke unsafe app table DML and PUBLIC bank function EXECUTE with reviewed forward-only security changes; owner-negative real-PG and existing finance regression tests.
- P0 REL1204-03 — #1067/#1066 plus main lint/test, migration266 and exact-SHA CI/Test/backup/rollback release blockers; no release claim before green evidence.
- P0 A11Y1204-04 — establish contrast/reflow/keyboard/OS-precedence baseline for account, wallet, stock order, support and admin-critical flows before rollout; fail closed on inaccessible critical confirmations.
- P1 A11Y1204-05 — guest/member local settings screen, accessible preview, reset, strict localStorage and prepaint safe defaults.
- P1 A11Y1204-06 — owner-only sync API, SECURITY DEFINER schema, version CAS, 100-way idempotency receipts and 409 conflicts; opt-in only.
- P1 A11Y1204-07 — semantic CSS tokens, OS/forced-colors precedence, high-contrast audited palette and 200% resize without changing theme storage ownership.
- P1 A11Y1204-08 — Android App API/native parity and redacted thresholded admin aggregate.
- P1 A11Y1204-09 — CSRF/owner/privacy/SEO/cache/abuse and KPI instrumentation without disability profiling.
- P1 A11Y1204-10 — 100 simultaneous PUT replay/stale conflicts, 100 cross-owner denies, 320px/200%/400% browser zoom, forced-colors, screen-reader, mobile, offline, account-switch, exact-SHA Test and flag rollback regression.
- Ownership: planning docs-only branch stacked after Draft #1065; engineering A/B/C, Gemini and QA branches unchanged. New code requires separate engineering branch and Test exact-SHA gate. 10,000 external independent reference gate remains BLOCKED: reviewed W3C and MDN publisher families (2), newly approved deduplicated evidence 0, cumulative approved UNKNOWN; no invented count.
- Reference guidance (reviewed, NOT registered as approved evidence): https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/ ; https://www.w3.org/WAI/adapt/ ; https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/CSS/Guides/Media_queries/Using_for_accessibility .


## v2026.10.24.1202 — F675-107 Private Data Access Package: Receipt, Isolation and Disclosure Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle and verified evidence
- Previous: Feature Addition v1200. Current: Feature Improvement v1202. Next: Feature Addition v1204 (provisional). Main at start and midpoint: 54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede. Read canonical main AGENTS.md, README.md, PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean comparator, stacked v1200 integrated plans, latest QA v1203 (04:39 KST), GitHub open PRs and actual privacy/controller/repository/migration 015 source.
- Existing privacy/requests GET+POST is an intake and receipt workflow, not a generated data copy. In migration 015, RETURNS TABLE includes created_at while INSERT RETURNING id, created_at is unqualified. Development C Draft PR #1064 independently reproduced SQLSTATE 42702 on isolated PostgreSQL 17.11 and proposes additive migration 280 plus stable form idempotency keys; this work is NOT in main and MUST NOT be duplicated or treated as released. The v1202 export design depends on verified, merged, exact-SHA-tested intake behavior.
- QA v1203: Production API and development backend share 5433/woldeok_moneyverse_dev (#1053); Docker binds the Production-connected PostgreSQL port to 0.0.0.0 and [::] (#1060), but internet reachability is unverified. App role can UPDATE/DELETE 235/241 public tables; three bank SECURITY DEFINER functions permit PUBLIC EXECUTE (#993). Migration 266 checksum mismatch; main lint/frontend test and Next.js security audit fail; Test DB schema differs; full exact-SHA Test and restore unverified. No write-capable testing, export worker or migration on shared 5433.
- Decision: preserve v1200 F675-107 scope, but add an explicit legal/technical release barrier, server-issued request receipts, source-specific disclosure contracts, artifact atomicity, credential fencing and post-release revocation. This is DOCUMENTATION ONLY; no runtime change, actual privacy export, legal approval, DB write or release.

### Product behavior, permissions, states and response truth
- Member entry remains /account/privacy -> /account/privacy/packages; accessible CTA: Request copy, Verify identity, Check status, Review included/excluded categories, Download, Report a problem. Show three distinct milestones: ACCESS_REQUEST_RECEIVED (existing intake), PACKAGE_QUEUED (new job committed), PACKAGE_READY (object verified + authorized release); NEVER show downloaded/fulfilled merely because a request row exists or a URL includes success.
- Intake dependency: on retry reuse a stable per-form UUID for unchanged normalized type/detail, verify server receipt ID, owner, request type, status and idempotency key before displaying success; unknown outcomes show Check request history and safe retry. Different payload under same key -> 409; 42702/500 -> failure, never success. Coordinate with C #1064 and forward-only migration 280; do not cherry-pick its runtime changes into planning.
- Package states: VERIFY_REQUIRED -> QUEUED -> SNAPSHOTTING -> POLICY_REVIEW -> APPROVED -> ENCRYPTING -> READY -> DOWNLOADING -> DOWNLOADED or EXPIRED; terminal REJECTED_WITH_REASON, CANCELLED; recoverable RETRY_PENDING, REVOKED, OBJECT_UNAVAILABLE. APPROVED is NOT READY; READY is invalid if object missing, hash mismatched, review revoked or encryption key disabled. Do not promise download completion if connection breaks after a one-use grant is consumed.
- Owner session + recent step-up (proposed <=10 min) for request and each download; separate privacy.review and privacy.release admin capabilities, distinct approvers for sensitive disclosure, recent admin reauth and CSRF. Account closure/restriction uses legally reviewed alternate rights channel. No default identity-document upload. Show category-by-category inclusion, omission reason, observed-at, expiry, redacted receipt; no private counterparty data in UI, logs, telemetry or manifest.

### Source, API, DB, transaction and concurrency contracts
- Field allowlist contract per source version: own profile; consent/request history; authored public content; own WLD ledger projection as exact decimal strings (not Number); notification preferences. Default-deny DMs, support evidence, OAuth tokens, bank secrets, admin notes, other-member identifiers, deleted/redacted data. Explicit legal-review overrides require case reference and audit; no implicit all-tables dump or SELECT *.
- Proposed APIs (not implemented): POST /api/v1/privacy/access-packages {privacyRequestId, idempotencyKey, requestedCategories, disclosurePolicyVersion}; 202 only after durable owner-scoped job/event/outbox commit. GET /:id owner-only, GET list signed keyset (created_at,id), POST /:id/download-intent with fresh step-up+CSRF, GET /:id/download with atomic single-use grant. Admin /api/v1/admin/privacy/access-packages/:id/review and /release require expectedVersion, reason, reviewer identity and separation-of-duties. Error mapping: 401 unauthenticated, 403 failed authorization, 404 unknown or foreign-owned object indistinguishable, 409 digest/version collision, 410 expired/revoked, 423 review hold, 503 unavailable; no raw object path in any response.
- Additive proposed tables only after migration-tip review: packages UNIQUE(owner_user_id,idempotency_key), request_digest bytea(32), source_policy_version, state_version, owner+privacy_request composite FK; events append-only UNIQUE(package_id,version); artifacts UNIQUE(package_id,object_id) with SHA256, encrypted key version, size and verified_at; grants UNIQUE(grant_hash) with consumed_at, expires_at, session_fingerprint and revoked_at. Index (owner_user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC), partial (state,lease_until) for worker claims and (expires_at) for purge. Enforce owner/request identity inside SECURITY DEFINER fixed search_path, no app direct table DML or PUBLIC EXECUTE.
- Transaction: request+event+outbox atomic; lease/epoch fencing for workers; bounded consistent-snapshot reads with source-level authorization; encrypt and upload privately, verify bytes/hash/manifest/policy version and reviewer decision, then compare-and-swap READY with artifact+event. Crash between object upload and DB CAS -> orphan cleanup, not READY. Re-review/revoke increments version and invalidates all grants before any new byte stream. A download grant is atomically consumed before streaming; retry requires reauth and a new grant. Already delivered bytes cannot be clawed back; UI must not claim recall.
- Export hardening: JSON UTF-8 primary, bounded CSV with formula neutralization, fixed-path ZIP without symlinks/traversal, compressed/uncompressed quotas, anti-zip-bomb checks, no EXIF/secret leakage, object encrypted at rest with versioned KMS keys, HTTPS transport, private/no-store/nosniff/no-referrer; exclude data from browser analytics and shared caches. Set artifact TTL only after legal/retention review; log deletion evidence without claiming backup erasure.

### Operations, mobile, accessibility, SEO, analytics and rollout
- Admin shows redacted counts, source policy version, case SLA age, audit of approve/release/revoke and object verification; not raw package contents by default. Kill switch blocks new jobs and grants while preserving requests and audit. Android app-api mirrors owner/step-up/CSRF-equivalent security and uses OS-private storage. 320px, WCAG 2.2 AA, keyboard/focus and live statuses; noindex/nofollow, no sitemap, private/no-store on API/BFF/edge/object; never log PII.
- KPI: intake receipt correctness, package READY integrity, time-to-review p50/p95, denied cross-owner attempts, stale policy versions, grant replay count, expired object deletion lag, unauthorized disclosures (target zero). Baselines UNKNOWN; legal-response deadlines require privacy officer sign-off, not invented SLAs.
- Rollout OFF by default; environment identity proof host/port/db/user and isolation before any real PG fixture, then reviewed forward-only migrations, owner-negative tests, full security audit, exact-SHA CI, official isolated Test authenticated five-pass, encrypted backup restore, canary and reversible flag. Never modify applied migration 015/266. On rollback disable jobs/grants, revoke capabilities, preserve immutable audit and pending rights cases, reconcile orphans without wallet writes. A/B/C/QA/Gemini code and PR ownership untouched.

### Prioritized engineering backlog and acceptance
- P0 ENV1202-01: #1053 and #1060 separate live/dev DB and close wildcard 5433 exposure with authorized network change; prove off-host deny, authorized API/backup health, and Test identity. No live changes in this cycle.
- P0 PRIV1202-02: integrate C Draft #1064 only after review: isolated PG reproduction of migration 015 SQLSTATE 42702, additive 280 receipt repair, stable idempotency/verified receipt and unchanged-key replay; no duplicate migration.
- P0 SEC1202-03: #993 app role direct DML and PUBLIC bank EXECUTE removal, per-object owner access and privacy.review/release RBAC, isolated real-PG negative tests.
- P0 REL1202-04: QA v1203 Next.js Critical2/High1, lint/blog, migration 266 provenance, exact-SHA Test parity and backup restore; HOLD all releases.
- P1 PRIV1202-05: owner/request composite constraints, versioned disclosure policy, source allowlist and immutable manifest; human review for third-party/legal exceptions.
- P1 PRIV1202-06: lease-fenced bounded export, object+DB READY CAS, corruption/revocation checks and orphan cleanup.
- P1 PRIV1202-07: one-use session-bound download, revoked/stale capability rejection, truthful retry receipt and no-cache semantics.
- P1 PRIV1202-08: member/admin/Android UX, WCAG/320px, SEO privacy, redacted audit and KPI.
- P1 PRIV1202-09: regression gate: 100 same-key parallel submits => one job/receipt; 100 changed-digest replays => 409; 100 cross-owner requests => no data; 100 parallel grant uses => <=1 consume; 10,001 equal-timestamp keyset entries => no loss; missing/corrupt object => zero READY; policy revoke racing download => no NEW stream after revocation; 38-digit WLD strings exact; CSV/ZIP payloads fail closed; restore/flag rollback/owner-negative isolated Test pass.
- External evidence gate: independently reviewed publishers this cycle PostgreSQL documentation, OWASP, EDPB (3 families), newly approved independently classified/deduplicated evidence records 0; cumulative accepted UNKNOWN/10,000 => BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. Design references: https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql-implementation.html ; https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Authorization_Cheat_Sheet.html ; https://www.edpb.europa.eu/documents/guideline/guidelines-012022-on-data-subject-rights-right-of-access_en . References are guidance, not legal advice or 10,000 approved records.


## v2026.10.24.1200 — F675-107 Verified Private Data Access Package (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, authoritative baseline and decision
- Previous Cycle: Feature Improvement v1198. Current Cycle: Feature Addition v1200. Next Cycle: Feature Improvement v1202 (provisional). Main at start/mid: 54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede; canonical main AGENTS.md, README.md, docs/planning/PROJECT_PLAN.md (English authority), PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md checked. The name "dev" is NOT a safe environment designation. Official public Test API at 3363 connects separately to 5585/wdmv_v761_test_acl. All writes, migrations, fixtures, privilege experiments and export jobs against the shared 5433 database are forbidden in this planning cycle; #1053 requires approved topology separation before integration testing. The prior "Production DB unverified" description in v1198 must be read in light of this newer evidence: 877 transactions/1,784 postings/0 imbalance were measured read-only on the DB connected to Production API, but authenticated Production finance workflows remain unverified.
- Product decision: reuse the existing privacy access-request intake, then provide an explicitly requested, reviewable and verifiable package containing ONLY the requester's permitted personal data. This is a user-facing access-copy feature, not a claim that all GDPR portability rules automatically apply to a Korean service. Human/privacy officer review decides legally restricted or third-party information; never export private counterparty records, passwords, access tokens, internal administrator notes or raw private support attachments by default. No WLD mutation.

### Purpose, roles, entry, UX and state contract
- Member goal: obtain a comprehensible copy of their own account information without emailing identity documents or sending raw data to an arbitrary address. Entry /account/privacy -> View access requests -> Request private copy -> /account/privacy/packages. The existing privacy request form and its idempotent receipt remain unchanged until reviewed migration/API changes; the package is a separate, explicit action linked to an authenticated owner request of type access.
- Member roles: active authenticated owner with current consent and recent step-up reauthentication (target freshness <=10 minutes) can request/download; inactive/deleted account is routed to the reviewed legal request channel, not silently denied with a fabricated success. Administrator roles privacy.review and privacy.release are distinct; fresh elevated session, CSRF and reason-coded audit required. No admin can bulk-download by using a member's link or approve their own privileged request without dual review for sensitive categories.
- Screens and CTAs: Request copy, Verify identity, Check status, View included categories, Download once, Re-request, Ask for review. States NOT_REQUESTED -> VERIFY_REQUIRED -> QUEUED -> COLLECTING -> REVIEW_REQUIRED -> READY -> DOWNLOADED / EXPIRED; exceptional REJECTED_WITH_REASON, FAILED_RETRYABLE, CANCELLED, UNAVAILABLE. The page distinguishes request received from package ready; only server-signed committed receipt and verified object availability can show READY. No optimistic URL query-parameter success, no invented delivery SLA. Present excluded categories and human appeal/contact route.
- Export categories: basic profile and identity-provider names (no provider tokens); recorded consents and privacy request history; the owner's authored public posts/comments where still available; owner-only WLD ledger posting projections and balances as VIRTUAL WLD strings, excluding counterparties, hidden memos and internal account IDs; own notification preferences where safely scoped. Private messages, support evidence, other users' personal data, moderation intelligence and security secrets require separate itemized privacy/legal review and default exclusion. Include per-category source, observation timestamp, record count, field allowlist and exclusions in manifest. CSV optional only for approved flat data, spreadsheet-formula neutralization mandatory; JSON UTF-8 primary, ZIP with fixed safe paths and size bounds (proposed <=50MiB, subject to real measurements).

### API, data constraints, transaction, object lifecycle
- Proposed GET /api/v1/privacy/access-packages?cursor&limit=1..25: authenticated owner-only list, signed keyset (created_at DESC,id DESC), no-store. POST /api/v1/privacy/access-packages: {privacyRequestId, idempotencyKey UUID, categories enum[]} with CSRF+recent reauth; server resolves owner, request type access, active status, canonical digest; 202 {packageId,state:QUEUED,receiptId,createdAt} only after atomic commit. Same owner+key+digest returns same receipt; changed payload 409. GET /api/v1/privacy/access-packages/:id: owner-only status/manifest redacted. POST /api/v1/privacy/access-packages/:id/download-intent: fresh step-up, CSRF and short-lived one-use session-bound download capability (never an open object URL); GET /api/v1/privacy/access-packages/:id/download with server-side consumed capability, no token in query string, Cache-Control private,no-store, Content-Disposition attachment, nosniff. Admin GET /api/v1/admin/privacy/access-packages?state&cursor and POST /:id/review or /release with elevated permission, expectedVersion and reason. Android App API mirrors statuses and authorization; mobile save must use OS private storage.
- Proposed additive forward-only schema (not migrated): privacy_access_packages(id uuid PK, owner_user_id FK users, privacy_request_id FK privacy_data_subject_requests, state enum/check, version bigint>0, request_key uuid, request_digest bytea length 32, categories jsonb constrained allowlist, requested_at, expires_at, reviewed_at, released_at); UNIQUE(owner_user_id,request_key); index (owner_user_id,requested_at DESC,id DESC), (state,requested_at,id); composite owner/request FK or SECURITY DEFINER cross-check so a request from another user cannot be attached. privacy_access_events append-only with UNIQUE(package_id,version) and actor/reason. privacy_access_artifacts(package_id UNIQUE FK, private_object_id UNIQUE, sha256 bytea length32, manifest_hash, byte_length CHECK <=52428800, encryption_key_version, ready_at, expires_at); no public bucket, raw object key or signed object URL in client response. An optional one-use download grant stores a hash and consumed_at; grants expire within minutes and are tied to session + owner + package.
- Transaction boundaries: (1) create job+event+outbox intent atomically under owner/active-account lock and idempotency key; (2) worker claims job by lease/version, collects bounded actor-scoped reads from approved source functions using a consistent read snapshot, never performs wallet writes, and streams encrypted artifact outside a long-held SQL write transaction; (3) verify SHA256, size, manifest, ownership and review, then CAS commit READY + event; object-store upload alone is NOT success; (4) timeout/crash leaves retryable state and orphan cleanup; one-time download grant is consumed atomically before streaming and failure can issue a fresh grant after step-up. TTL proposal: ready artifact 24h, delete object within 48h after expiry, retain minimal event metadata per approved retention policy, never promise physical deletion of legally retained backup copies.
- DB role boundary: no direct app SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE on sensitive tables; actor-bound fixed-search_path SECURITY DEFINER functions with least EXECUTE, revoke PUBLIC; privileged reviewer path independently checks capability, session age and version. Do not treat the live shared 5433 DB as disposable. No arbitrary URL, external email recipient, path traversal, ZIP symlink, cross-tenant read, SQL interpolation, CSV injection or unbounded collection. Throttle requests (proposed one active package and <=2 per seven days per owner; exception process for statutory rights), byte/time budget and worker concurrency; limits must not unlawfully block access rights and need legal review.

### Admin, security, SEO, mobile, analytics and acceptance
- Admin privacy queue displays state, age, redacted category counts and reason-coded release decisions, not raw exported contents by default; privileged preview is separately authorized and audited. Incident kill switch disables new generation/download without deleting request/event history; notify affected owners through verified channels only after review.
- Private pages /account/privacy/packages and /admin/privacy/access-packages are noindex/nofollow, absent from sitemap; BFF, API, edge, object responses no-store, Referrer-Policy no-referrer, no analytics payloads with PII. Accessible at 320px, keyboard, visible focus, labelled progress/status live region, safe failure messages, WCAG 2.2 AA; Android uses the same owner/step-up semantics, does not trust spoofable client headers.
- KPI: valid request -> committed receipt, median/p95 queue and review age, approved-category coverage, download completion, artifact expiry/delete lag, false READY count target 0, cross-owner disclosures target 0, failed step-up, staff overrides, byte budget; baselines UNKNOWN until measured. Do not optimize for download count at expense of privacy.
- Regression acceptance: 100 concurrent same-key submits -> exactly one package/event/outbox and one receipt; 100 same-key different digests -> 409; 100 other-owner package/request/download attempts -> no disclosure; stale/revoked sessions and expired reauth -> 401/403; one-use grant consumed at most once under 100 concurrent downloads; 10,001 same-timestamp jobs paginate without omissions; crash before artifact CAS never READY; corruption/missing object never READY; 38-digit WLD strings preserved without floating-point conversion; CSV cells beginning =,+,-,@ or control-prefix cannot execute as formulas; ZIP bomb/path traversal and >=50MiB fail closed; legal exclusion/appeal and data retention/expiry verified; Android and WCAG/SEO/no-store tests; isolated encrypted-backup restore and exact-SHA five-pass Test with synthetic accounts only after #1053 environment separation.
- Deployment: OFF by default; do not deploy runtime from planning. Development A owns wallet data projection and receipts; B owns admin privilege and ACL, C owns account UI where assigned; QA owns environment topology and exact-SHA gate; Gemini and existing PRs untouched. New code requires independent feature branch, reviewed migration after current tip (never rewrite 266), Test database isolation verified by hostname/port/db/user/credential fingerprint, CI security/lint/test/audit, encrypted restore drill, canary + feature flag + rollback proof. On rollback stop jobs/downloads, retain immutable requests/audit, expire objects safely, no WLD changes. P0 #1053, #993, Next.js critical/high, lint/frontend test, migration 266 and backup/release identity remain BLOCKED.

### P0/P1 engineering backlog and external evidence gate
- P0 ENV1200-01: #1053 prove Production/Test/dev DB topology and isolate all write-capable fixtures; verify credentials, port, actual backend runtime and immutable connection identity before any integration test.
- P0 SEC1200-02: #993 revoke broad app DML and PUBLIC privileged EXECUTE via reviewed forward-only migration; negative real-PostgreSQL tests in isolated Test.
- P0 PRIV1200-03: design per-field owner/third-party disclosure matrix, recent step-up and human exception review; zero unauthorized raw export or misleading READY.
- P0 REL1200-04: unblock current main lint, frontend test, Next.js 16.3.8 Critical 2/High 1, migration266 checksum, backup restore, exact-SHA Test/Production gates; no promotion while failing.
- P1 PRIV1200-05: additive package/event/artifact/grant schema, immutable audit, UNIQUE/partial indexes and owner-scoped SECURITY DEFINER API.
- P1 PRIV1200-06: bounded snapshot worker, encrypted object store, SHA256 manifest, TTL deletion, fail-closed CAS and durable receipts.
- P1 PRIV1200-07: member privacy center, admin release review, Android App API, 320px/WCAG 2.2 AA, private/noindex/no-store UX.
- P1 PRIV1200-08: 100-way concurrency, 10,001 keyset, legal exclusion, CSV/ZIP abuse, step-up, restore, staged flag rollback regression.
- External independent references reviewed: Korean PIPC privacy access portal, KLRI Korean Personal Information Protection Act, EDPB access/portability guidance, OWASP Cheat Sheets (4 publisher families, URLs below). These are comparative design sources, NOT four accepted independently deduplicated evidence records. Newly approved = 0; cumulative verified approved = UNKNOWN; 10,000 gate = BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS. https://www.privacy.go.kr/front/wcp/dcl/per/persnalInfo.do ; https://elaw.klri.re.kr/eng_service/lawTwoView.do?hseq=62389 ; https://www.edpb.europa.eu/system/files/2023-04/edpb_guidelines_202201_data_subject_rights_access_v2_en.pdf ; https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/


## v2026.10.24.1198 — F675-106 Wallet Dispute Evidence and Review Integrity (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, source authority and boundaries
- **Previous:** Feature Addition v1196; **Current:** Feature Improvement v1198; **Next:** Feature Addition v1200 (provisional). Start/mid origin/main 54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede; reviewed AGENTS.md, README.md, canonical PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean counterpart. Latest independent QA v1199 (2026-10-24 02:25 KST) FAILED/HOLD. This is a planning-only child of unmerged Draft #1048 (which itself depends on #1043), not an implementation, financial incident, or Test certification.
- **Verified implementation:** backend/src/wallet/wallet.repository.ts recentTransactionsForUser reads owner-owned USER_CASH/USER_BANK postings, groups by ledger transaction, orders (created_at DESC,id DESC) and limits to 50; frontend/src/app/wallet/activity/page.tsx renders the same 50 and no dispute action. backend/src/wallet/wallet.controller.ts exposes GET wallet, not a dispute endpoint. backend/src/support/support.repository.ts uses support_create_thread, support_add_message and admin_support_set_status with generic threads. App API proxy exists. QA v1199 DEV read-only ledger 877 transactions/1,784 postings and 0 imbalance, but **Production ledger and authenticated exact-SHA user flows are unverified**. Draft #1049 proposes a private receipt for the *latest 50*; it is unmerged, so older-transaction eligibility must not be silently excluded or treated as implemented. No new dispute schema is claimed deployed.
- **Risk decision:** member-facing dispute creation must re-verify committed ledger ownership inside the database transaction, not rely on a UI receipt, notification, transaction actor, browser-supplied WLD amount, or a recent-50 list. A member with postings on an older transaction may still be eligible through a bounded, separately authorized lookup. A zero-net cash↔bank movement may still be disputed; the signed cash/bank components and ledger posting proof, not net alone, establish the receipt. Never change WLD in the dispute path.

### Hardened product and API contracts
- **Entry and states:** /wallet/activity and a future owner-scoped transaction history or receipt (Draft #1049 is a dependency, not a release assumption) → Review transaction → /wallet/disputes/new?transactionId=UUID. If the transaction is outside the recent-50 window, show “Not in recent activity” and a secure lookup/history path; never show “transaction not found” solely because of the window. Member CTAs: Submit dispute, View receipt, Add information, Request re-review. Operator: /admin/support/transaction-disputes, Assign, Request information, Record decision, View redacted audit. UI states DRAFT, SUBMITTING, SUBMITTED, TRIAGED, NEEDS_MEMBER_INFO, UNDER_REVIEW, RESOLVED_NO_CHANGE, RESOLVED_CORRECTION_REFERENCED, REJECTED, CLOSED, UNAVAILABLE and RETRY_UNKNOWN. A timeout is not proof of failure or success: query the receipt by the same idempotency key.
- **Owner lookup:** GET /api/v1/wallet/transactions/:transactionId/dispute-eligibility checks current authenticated actor against at least one committed USER_CASH/USER_BANK posting via accounts.owner_user_id, regardless of recent-50 position. 404 for missing/other owner, uniform no-store/error timing where feasible; 503 for source unavailable. Response is minimal {transactionId,eligible,existingOpenDisputeId?,receiptAvailable,observedAt}; do not expose counterparty IDs, private memo or internal ledger metadata. A new bounded history cursor may be proposed separately after A/#1049 ownership review; no unbounded transaction scan.
- **Submission:** POST /api/v1/wallet/disputes requires authenticated session, consent, CSRF and UUID idempotency key; body includes transactionId, category and normalized summary only. Atomic function recomputes posting ownership and stores canonical request digest. Same actor/key/digest returns the same committed receipt (200); same key/different digest is 409; concurrent second open dispute for same owner+transaction is 409 or an explicitly owner-scoped existing-case redirect, never another case. Non-owner and nonexistent transaction both 404. Return 201 only after dispute+support thread+first event+outbox intent commit; unknown commit outcome returns RETRY_UNKNOWN with safe same-key receipt lookup. No debit/credit/freeze/refund/reversal.
- **Review:** POST /api/v1/admin/wallet/disputes/:id/decisions requires elevated support.dispute.resolve, recent reauthentication, CSRF, mandatory reasonCode, expectedVersion and idempotencyKey. Under row lock verify state/version, write immutable decision event and version once. Staff can never approve their own disputed transaction or directly invoke financial posting through this API. A correction reference is accepted only if a separately authorized finance decision and committed, balanced ledger transaction are independently proven; allow multiple affected owners to reference the same correction, but disclose only their own posting projection. F675-104 owns re-review lifecycle; F675-105 owns private evidence; no second escalation or public image store.

### Data, security, operations and acceptance
- **Schema contract (additive, forward-only):** retain v1196 wallet_disputes, wallet_dispute_events, wallet_dispute_resolution and owner-scoped partial unique open-case index. Store immutable ledger transaction UUID, owner, support thread, request digest, decision reason, event version, timestamps and receipt ID. Add an owner-scoped receipt lookup index (owner_user_id,request_key), history keyset (owner_user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC) and reviewer queue (state,updated_at,id). Do not use a global UNIQUE on ledger_transaction_id or correction_transaction_id. Re-review must lock the existing case, increment version and preserve history rather than create a competing open case. Prevent orphan support threads/outbox events on rollback; outbox dispatch is an intent, not evidence of delivery. DB app role gets EXECUTE on actor-bound fixed-search_path SECURITY DEFINER functions only, no broad finance/support DML or PUBLIC EXECUTE.
- **Cross-agent dependencies:** A wallet receipts #1049 and P2P replay #1025; B elevated-admin and finance grants (#1050), C onboarding/status changes (#1051/#1046), QA #1199, Gemini and F675-104/105 Drafts remain separate owners. Do not modify their runtime files or assume their Draft PRs merged. QA v1199 measured app UPDATE/DELETE grants on 235/241 DEV tables, three bank functions with PUBLIC EXECUTE, Next.js 16.3.8 audit 2 Critical/1 High, Migration 266 checksum mismatch, main lint failure and one frontend assertion failure. No production exploitability or ledger incident has been proven. Release remains HOLD until reviewed forward-only privileges, migration reconciliation, dependency patch, complete CI, backup restore and authenticated official Test exact-SHA gate.
- **Privacy, mobile, SEO, analytics:** member/admin routes and receipt endpoints noindex/nofollow, private/no-store at backend, Next BFF, App API and edge; stable 404 without identity leak. Android parity for authorization, CSRF/session binding, offline retry with same key, minimum version and 409/503 messaging. Keyboard, 320px layout, status live region and WCAG 2.2 AA. Audit privileged reads, exports and decisions without logging raw WLD memo, counterparty, evidence URLs or member identifiers in analytics. Track real eligible→submit conversion, first-response p95, unresolved age, idempotent replay count, correction-reference verification failures, non-owner denial count and false-success rate (target 0); targets for latency require measured baseline.
- **Regression matrix:** (1) 100 same-key simultaneous submits → one case/thread/event/outbox and one receipt; (2) 100 same-key different payloads → 409; (3) 100 cross-owner IDs including shared P2P transaction → only owner projections, no counterparty leak; (4) 51st/older real owner transaction eligible, while Draft #1049 recent-50 receipt boundary remains truthful; (5) internal cash↔bank transfer with net 0 retains exact signed components, 38-digit amount strings never float; (6) 100 concurrent reviewer updates → version conflicts, no lost decisions; (7) 10,001 same-timestamp events keyset complete; (8) crash between support/case/event/outbox rolls all back; (9) timed-out submit followed by same-key retry returns existing receipt; (10) unrelated/unbalanced/unapproved correction rejected; (11) app-role direct DML, PUBLIC EXECUTE, cross-owner admin and cache negative tests; (12) Android/web/a11y/SEO, encrypted backup isolated restore, rollback and 5-pass authenticated exact-SHA Test.
- **Rollout and rollback:** flag wallet_transaction_disputes OFF. Additive schema + functions behind reviewed privilege migration, shadow-read owner proof on restored Test with synthetic users, exact-SHA CI and authenticated Test/QA sign-off, allowlisted canary and reversible flag. On failure disable new submissions and privileged decisions, preserve cases/events/support evidence and read-only receipts; never rewrite applied Migration 266, delete financial history or post compensating WLD automatically. **No runtime, DB, Test, Production or flag change in this planning cycle.**

### P0/P1 engineering backlog and evidence gate
- **P0 WAL1198-01:** prove committed posting ownership beyond recent-50 and preserve signed cash/bank receipt truth; coordinate with #1049; tests older owner/foreign owner/net-zero/38-digit.
- **P0 WAL1198-02:** isolate P2P #1025 replay metadata/notification from ledger proof and reject uncommitted evidence; 100 retries no duplicate side effects.
- **P0 SEC1198-03:** forward-only app-role least privilege, no PUBLIC bank EXECUTE without reviewed actor checks, elevated reviewer session/CSRF/reauth; real-PG negative tests.
- **P0 REL1198-04:** QA v1199 lint/frontend/audit/migration266/branch preservation/backup restore/exact-SHA CI+Test release gates; no Production promotion.
- **P1 WAL1198-05:** atomic owner-bound case+thread+event+outbox with canonical digest, same-key replay, open-case uniqueness, versioned re-review.
- **P1 WAL1198-06:** verified separately approved correction reference and immutable decision, no balance-changing support API.
- **P1 WAL1198-07:** older-history discovery, truthful receipt CTA/states, Android App API, noindex/no-store, WCAG/mobile.
- **P1 WAL1198-08:** F675-104 re-review, F675-105 evidence linkage, reviewer audit/retention/legal hold without data leakage.
- **P1 WAL1198-09:** rate limits, privacy-safe KPI and operator workload/aging indicators, zero false-success.
- **P1 WAL1198-10:** 12-scenario concurrency/security/rollback matrix, exact-SHA 5-pass Test, staged canary and rollback proof.
- **Independent reference gate:** OWASP API1 BOLA, PostgreSQL 17 explicit locking, W3C WCAG 2.2 status messages reviewed (3 publishers, **0 newly approved records**, cumulative independently deduplicated approved count **UNKNOWN**, target **10,000**, gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**). URLs: https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/ ; https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/explicit-locking.html ; https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/status-messages.html . Do not claim 10,000 achieved.


## v2026.10.24.1196 — F675-106 Verified Wallet Transaction Disputes & Resolution Receipts (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

### Cycle, authority, observed baseline and non-goals
- **Previous:** Feature Improvement v1194. **Current:** Feature Addition v1196. **Next:** Feature Improvement v1198 (provisional). Canonical origin/main at start/mid: `54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede`; AGENTS.md, README.md, PROJECT_PLAN.md (English canonical), PROJECT_PLAN.ko.md reviewed. This proposal stacks on unmerged planning Draft PR #1043; it is NOT merged, implemented, tested, deployed or a finding of an actual Production financial incident.
