- **Source-verified gap:** `frontend/src/app/wallet/activity/page.tsx` displays at most 50 recent entries from `GET /api/v1/wallet?recent=50`, without a transaction-specific dispute action. `backend/src/wallet/wallet.repository.ts::recentTransactionsForUser` derives transaction IDs and cash/bank deltas from owner-owned postings. `backend/src/support/support.controller.ts` offers general member/admin support threads and messages but no transaction-bound dispute, reviewer decision receipt or finance-specific queue. Android uses `frontend/src/app/app-api/v1/[...path]/route.ts`. The admin support inbox exists at `/admin/support`. DEV read-only inspection: 877 ledger transactions, 1,784 postings, 3 support threads, 6 support messages, **0 tables matching *disput***. `moneyverse_app` has direct UPDATE/DELETE on `account_balances` and support tables; ledger_transactions/postings have SELECT but no UPDATE/DELETE in the inspected DEV snapshot. QA v1197 (2026-10-24 01:25 KST) FAILED/HOLD, and Production ledger is not certified.
- **Important dependency:** GitHub #1025 documents P2P metadata/notification replay after an idempotent ledger posting. The dispute feature MUST use only committed, owner-visible ledger transaction IDs, not notification text, browser-supplied amounts, or unverified P2P metadata. A submitted dispute must NEVER debit, credit, freeze, reverse, refund or change WLD; any eventual correction requires an independently authorized finance workflow with a new balanced transaction and exact-SHA gates. Existing F675-104 case history, F675-105 private evidence, and wallet A/B/C/QA candidates are dependencies, not code owned by this planning cycle.

- **GitHub planning tracking:** Draft PR #1048 (parent Draft #1043), engineering issue #1047. One independently approved correction transaction may resolve multiple affected-member cases; do not impose a global UNIQUE constraint on its reference here. Financial posting uniqueness belongs to the independently authorized ledger boundary.

### Purpose, personas, authorization and navigation
- **Member:** report an unexplained/mismatched WLD transaction, track human review, attach optional private evidence only after F675-105 is safe, and receive a verifiable resolution receipt. Entry from a real `/wallet/activity` row via **Review this transaction** → `/wallet/disputes/new?transactionId=<uuid>` (the ID is only a hint, never proof of ownership). History `/wallet/disputes`; detail `/wallet/disputes/:disputeId`; existing `/support` shows linked case without exposing private counterparty metadata. No guest, deleted account or unrelated member access.
- **Support reviewer:** `/admin/support/transaction-disputes` with scoped `support.dispute.read` and `support.dispute.triage`; a separate elevated `support.dispute.resolve` permission, fresh admin step-up, CSRF and mandatory reason for final decisions. Admins cannot modify ledger balances via dispute APIs; sensitive transaction identity and evidence fields are minimized and audited. A finance correction reference can be attached only after separate finance approval/ledger verification, never minted from the support screen.
- **UX state machine:** client-only DRAFT → SUBMITTED (only after committed server receipt) → TRIAGED / NEEDS_MEMBER_INFO / UNDER_REVIEW → RESOLVED_NO_CHANGE or RESOLVED_CORRECTION_REFERENCED or REJECTED → CLOSED. Invalid transitions return 409; missing or inaccessible IDs return the same non-enumerating 404. Member-facing status distinguishes pending, unavailable, rejected and final; do not promise reimbursement, SLA or a credit before an independently verified ledger receipt. CTA: **Report issue**, **Attach private evidence**, **Send additional details**, **View review history**, **Request re-review** (reuse F675-104, do not create a second escalation engine). Use server-confirmed receipt ID, timestamp and state for success; never URL query-string-only success.

### Proposed API contract (not implemented)
- `GET /api/v1/wallet/transactions/:transactionId/dispute-eligibility`: authenticated, owner-verified transaction from `ledger_postings JOIN accounts`; response `{transactionId, eligible, allowedCategories, existingOpenDisputeId?, observedAt}`, no counterparty identities, no caller-provided amount trust; 404 for non-owner/unknown. Eligibility is advisory and rechecked atomically at create.
- `POST /api/v1/wallet/disputes`: session+consent+CSRF, body `{transactionId, category: missing|incorrect_amount|duplicate|unrecognized|other, summary: 10..2000 chars, idempotencyKey: UUID}`. 201 `{disputeId, supportThreadId, state:'SUBMITTED', version:1, receiptId, createdAt}`; exact same actor+key+canonical request returns 200 same receipt; same key/different request or already-open case for the same actor+transaction returns 409 without leaking any other user's case. No implicit ledger write.
- `GET /api/v1/wallet/disputes?cursor&limit=1..50` and `GET /api/v1/wallet/disputes/:disputeId`: owner-scoped keyset `(created_at DESC,id DESC)`, signed/validated cursor, bounded projection, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, stable `nextCursor`. `GET /api/v1/admin/wallet/disputes?state&cursor`: elevated role, reason-coded audit and bounded queue; `POST /api/v1/admin/wallet/disputes/:id/decisions` requires `{expectedVersion, action, reasonCode, explanation, idempotencyKey, verifiedCorrectionTransactionId?}`, re-verifies actor, state and correction reference. App API `/app-api/v1/... ` must mirror contract, errors, CSRF/session and minimum-version gate; no header-only Android identity assumption.
- Error taxonomy: 400 invalid input; 401 unauthenticated; 403 insufficient reviewer privilege; 404 missing/not owned; 409 stale version or idempotency conflict; 422 invalid state/correction proof; 429 rate-limited; 503 source ledger/DB unavailable. No raw SQL, owner UUID, counterparty, storage key, internal metadata or finance secrets in responses/logs.

### Proposed database, constraints, transaction and privacy
- Additive forward-only migration after current migration tip, **never rewrite applied migration 266**. `wallet_disputes(id uuid PK, owner_user_id FK users, ledger_transaction_id FK ledger_transactions, support_thread_id FK support_threads UNIQUE, category CHECK, state CHECK, version bigint CHECK(version>0), request_key uuid, request_digest bytea, created_at timestamptz, updated_at timestamptz)`; `UNIQUE(owner_user_id,request_key)`; partial unique `(owner_user_id,ledger_transaction_id) WHERE state IN ('SUBMITTED','TRIAGED','NEEDS_MEMBER_INFO','UNDER_REVIEW')`; index `(owner_user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` and `(state,updated_at,id)`. The uniqueness scope is per owner; a counterparty may submit a separate case only for their own postings.
- `wallet_dispute_events(id uuid PK, dispute_id FK, version bigint, actor_user_id FK, action, from_state, to_state, reason_code, occurred_at, request_key uuid, request_digest bytea, UNIQUE(dispute_id,version), UNIQUE(dispute_id,request_key))`; append-only with denied app UPDATE/DELETE. Optional `wallet_dispute_resolution(dispute_id PK FK, correction_transaction_id NULL FK ledger_transactions, verified_by, verified_at, decision_reason)`; if linked, require explicit independently authorized finance decision, balanced ledger proof, and no cross-owner detail disclosure. `support_thread_id` and any F675-105 evidence reference must be checked against the same owner/case.
- Actor-bound `SECURITY DEFINER` functions (restricted EXECUTE, fixed search_path, authenticated actor bound by backend session) check posting ownership using `accounts.owner_user_id = actor` inside the transaction; never authorize solely by `ledger_transactions.actor_user_id` or by knowing a UUID. Create dispute, linked support case, first immutable event and deduplicated outbox receipt in **one database transaction**. On concurrent same-key requests use unique constraint + conflict readback after ownership AND canonical request-digest equality; on same-transaction open-case collision return 409. Admin state transitions `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`, compare `expectedVersion`, insert one event and increment version in same transaction. Any notification is an outbox intent, not a delivered-message claim. Existing support thread idempotency/atomicity gaps in v1190 must be addressed before wiring the new path.
- No raw posting payload, private notes, support screenshots, identity-provider IDs or recipient names in analytics. Private evidence is F675-105 gated; if not available, text-only disputes remain possible. Set retention and legal-hold policy before release, encrypted backups, audit every privileged view/export, and ensure exports are access-controlled. Nonpublic member/admin pages `noindex,nofollow` and private/no-store on web, BFF, app-api, CDN and download. Abuse controls: per-account/IP burst limits, category/length validation, duplicate-open-case gate, reviewer separation of duties, no auto-refund, no case-ID enumeration.

### Acceptance, analytics, release and rollback
- **P0 WAL1196-01** owner-verified ledger truth, non-enumerating 404, no fabricated submission success; source snapshot must be fresh enough to prove committed transaction. **P0 WAL1196-02** P2P replay/metadata side-effect fix #1025 before relying on P2P notification evidence. **P0 SEC1196-03** revoke DEV/Production app-role direct finance/support DML via separately reviewed forward-only migration, preserve SECURITY DEFINER/actor-bound boundary and admin step-up. **P0 REL1196-04** restore CI/lint/security (2 Critical/1 High), migration266 checksum, branch-preservation evidence, verified backup+isolated restore, exact-SHA authenticated Test and finance promotion HOLD.
- **P1 WAL1196-05** schema, atomic create and immutable receipt/event/outbox; **WAL1196-06** owner/admin APIs, App API parity and version/error contract; **WAL1196-07** member wallet entry/history and admin triage/review UX; **WAL1196-08** separate finance correction reference verification, private evidence and F675-104 re-review integration; **WAL1196-09** privacy, mobile WCAG 2.2 AA, noindex/no-store and KPIs; **WAL1196-10** concurrency, regression, release and rollback drills.
- Required QA: 100 concurrent same-key submissions → 1 case+1 thread+1 event+1 outbox intent; 100 different-payload same-key requests → deterministic 409; 100 non-owner transaction/dispute probes → zero disclosure; 100 concurrent admin version conflicts → no lost update or duplicate resolution; 10,001 same-timestamp cases → keyset no skips/duplicates; exact bigint cash/bank delta parity; replay after response timeout returns same receipt; failure between case/thread/event/outbox rolls back all; correction reference rejects unbalanced, unrelated or unapproved ledger; Android/web/accessibility/security/SEO regression and signed-cursor tampering tests.
- KPI only from real committed receipts: eligible→submitted conversion, p95 create latency, p95 reviewer first-response/resolve time, reopen rate, unauthorized access denials, duplicate suppression count, false success 0, unsupported credit promises 0. Set baseline/targets only after measured data; do not claim current production values.
- Rollout: feature flag `wallet_transaction_disputes` **OFF**, additive migration with audited grants/constraints and dry-run on restored Test, Test exact SHA and authenticated member/admin/Android 5-pass E2E, staged allowlist, zero-downtime deploy and reversible flag disable. Rollback hides entry/halts new writes but preserves dispute records, linked support cases and audit; never roll back applied migration, rewrite historical ledger, delete evidence, or reverse a financial transaction. Production promotion remains BLOCKED until independent QA signs exact SHA and restore/finance gates.
- External independent sources reviewed: OWASP API1:2023 BOLA, PostgreSQL 17 concurrency control, W3C WCAG 2.2 status messages (3 independent publishers; **0 newly approved reference records**; cumulative deduplicated approved **UNKNOWN**; required **10,000** evidence gate **BLOCKED / IN PROGRESS**, never claimed satisfied). References: https://github.com/OWASP/API-Security/blob/master/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization.md ; https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/mvcc.html ; https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/status-messages.html .

## v2026.10.24.1194 — F675-105 Private Support Evidence Vault: authorization, legacy containment and durable receipts (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)

- **Cycle and authority:** Previous Feature Addition v1192; Current Feature Improvement v1194; Next Feature Addition v1196 (provisional). Canonical origin/main 54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede, AGENTS.md/README.md/PROJECT_PLAN.md and Korean counterpart rechecked at start and mid-run; predecessor unmerged Draft #1038 and support security issue #1037. QA v1195 (2026-10-24 00:25 KST) FAILED/HOLD. This is a docs-only stacked proposal; no claim of implementation, Test acceptance, public exploitation, or Production leakage.
- **Verified implementation and DEV evidence:** backend/src/content/chat-upload.controller.ts: upload is guarded by Session/Authenticated/Consent/CSRF but returns BOTH url and raw storageKey; GET content/chat/media/:key has no explicit owner/session guard and sets public, max-age=86400, immutable. backend/src/content/chat-image-storage.ts writes the original scanned buffer with mode 0600; the scanner check is not the same as image re-encoding or EXIF removal. frontend/src/components/floating-support-chat-widget.tsx appends the media URL to inquiry/support reply/direct-message text and shows an image-attached toast after upload, before a support message is committed. frontend/src/app/app-api/v1/[...path]/route.ts buffers request.arrayBuffer and has a 15-second upstream timeout; frontend/src/lib/app-gateway.ts forwards Cache-Control. DEV read-only aggregates: 3 support threads, 6 messages, **1 support message containing a legacy /api/v1/content/chat/media/ URL**, support_evidence table absent; moneyverse_app can DELETE support_messages and UPDATE support_threads. These are DEV/source findings, not proof of any Production incident; no raw user message or URL was exported.
- **Design choice and trade-offs:** Preserve the current generic DM/lobby media contract until its consumers and authorization are audited; do not globally disable chat images or blindly revoke every random URL. Private support evidence must use opaque evidenceId + authenticated case-bound download and never expose storageKey or original-content SHA to browsers, Android, URLs, analytics, or error messages. The existing generic upload response returning storageKey is a separate P0 compatibility/security review: eliminate it from the support path immediately when implemented, and remove it from generic responses only after downstream clients are inventoried. Unguessable keys are not authorization.
- **Personas, rights, entry points and UX:** Case owner uses /support or /support/cases/:threadId: Select screenshot → Inspect privacy notice → Upload → Scanning → Ready to send → Send with message → Confirmed attachment receipt → View/revoke eligible unlinked evidence. Support operator at /admin/support uses dedicated support.evidence.read, support.evidence.quarantine, support.evidence.audit permissions with elevated admin session and mandatory reason; generic admin role alone is insufficient. No guest, other member, DM participant or automated bot gets support bytes. UI must distinguish selected/uploaded/scan-pending/READY-but-unlinked/ATTACHED/rejected/quarantined/revoked/expired; never say “attached” until the message+link commit receipt exists. Existing text-only support works when flag OFF; upload alone never changes WLD/ledger.
- **Member API and response contract (proposed, not implemented):** POST /api/v1/support/threads/:threadId/evidence: multipart one PNG/JPEG/WebP, <=8 MiB, CSRF, actor/session, UUID Idempotency-Key, content digest bound to actor+thread+bytes+declared operation; returns 202 with opaque evidenceId/receiptId and state=PENDING when scanning, or 201 with state=READY after durable verified object+DB commit. Never return raw storageKey, storage path, EXIF, full content hash or public media URL. GET .../evidence/:evidenceId/receipt: only case owner/operator, returns state/receipt/version without bytes; POST .../messages accepts 0..3 evidenceIds and an idempotency key, returns immutable messageId/link receipt after a single transaction. GET .../evidence/:evidenceId: per-request owner/operator check, READY+ATTACHED or authorized own READY preview, then stream sanitized bytes; DELETE only own unlinked READY with CAS, not silently delete attached evidence. Operator GET and POST quarantine endpoints require elevated support permission, reason and audit. Error contracts: 400 malformed, 401 unauthenticated, 404 unknown/foreign indistinguishable, 409 replay-fingerprint mismatch or stale version, 413 size, 415 type, 429 quota, 503 scanner/object-store/commit unknown; clients resolve ambiguous retries via receipt GET, not new keys.
- **Download/cache and privacy boundary:** For every private evidence GET/HEAD and error (including 304/206 if ever supported), enforce no shared or browser cache: Cache-Control: no-store, private; Pragma: no-cache for legacy; X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff; Referrer-Policy: no-referrer; X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow, noarchive; Content-Disposition: attachment unless a sanitized raster preview is explicitly authorized; strict CSP for any inline preview. Disable conditional 304 and byte Range/206 initially rather than leak metadata or bypass fresh ownership; HEAD returns no bytes and no unauthorized size, ETag or Last-Modified. The Next BFF must override any upstream public cache header on private support paths, not merely forward it. No query-string bearer tokens, raw object keys or unprotected redirects; download link itself is never proof of permission.
- **DB constraints and access (reviewed forward-only migration candidate):** Retain v1192 support_evidence, support_message_evidence, support_evidence_events concept, add scan_version, normalized_sha256 (internal only), object_generation, scanned_at and monotonically increasing version; DB READY requires nonnull scan_version/object_generation/scanned_at, and no client-supplied owner/object_key. UNIQUE (actor_id,thread_id,idempotency_key,operation_kind) in a private receipt table with stored canonical request digest; same key+same digest returns same receipt, different digest 409. UNIQUE(evidence_id) in message link, composite UNIQUE(thread_id,id) and composite FKs on message/evidence to reject cross-case linking; owner, READY, expiry and <=3 links checked by a SECURITY DEFINER function under sorted row locks. Index (thread_id,created_at DESC,id DESC) and (state,expires_at,id) for owner history and orphan cleanup; use keyset pagination for 10,001+ records. Audit table append-only. Revoke moneyverse_app direct SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE on support evidence and affected support tables; expose only owner-checked EXECUTE with fixed search_path, no PUBLIC execute. Coordinate with DB developer B; never rewrite applied migration 266.
- **Durability, transactions and scanner:** Filesystem/object storage and PostgreSQL cannot be one ACID transaction: define explicit PENDING → READY or QUARANTINED/REVOKED/DELETED state with an outbox/reconciler, not a fictitious cross-system atomic commit. Persist idempotency reservation and PENDING receipt first, store a quarantined object, scan with bounded CPU/memory/pixels, normalize/re-encode image and strip EXIF/GPS, persist immutable private object generation, then CAS-commit READY with scan attestation. Serve only after BOTH DB READY and matching object generation/normalized digest are verified. If DB commit fails, object remains quarantined and sweeper deletes it after a documented retention window; if object write fails, receipt stays non-READY with retryable failure. Do not hold DB locks during scanner/filesystem/network calls. Attach uses thread lock → sorted evidence locks → ownership/state/expiry/count check → message+links+receipt atomic commit; revoke vs attach resolves via version CAS. Fail closed on scanner timeout, corruption or mismatched object generation; never report false READY.
- **Legacy transition / incident discipline:** Authorized aggregate inventory shows 1 DEV support message with a legacy media URL; Production count, CDN cache population and third-party copies are UNKNOWN. Security/QA must use synthetic Test accounts to verify anonymous/foreign-owner GET/HEAD and edge/CDN behavior without retrieving real member media. Categorize support vs DM/lobby references, map eligible support records to case-bound evidence after owner and retention review, preserve old text and chain of custody. New support UI must stop emitting public media URLs only after private path works; retain generic DM functionality. Purge reachable shared caches and revoke/rotate supported legacy links where feasible, but do not claim previously downloaded copies are recalled. If confirmed Production exposure, follow incident-response and notification procedures, not a docs-only declaration.
- **Gateway, mobile, abuse and accessibility:** Current BFF buffers multipart and has 15s timeout: design bounded streaming or a controlled async upload route with explicit max body 8 MiB per file / 24 MiB per message, concurrent worker cap, backpressure and 202 receipt; do not increase global proxy limits blindly. Android App API must have equivalent actor/thread/CSRF or documented mobile session protection, retry identity, offline resume and content-URI expiry handling; app version compatibility gate. Per-user/IP rate and bytes/day quotas, decompression/pixel limits, secure temp cleanup, no client-provided remote URL. 320px mobile, keyboard file selection/removal, focus on errors, progress/status live regions, alt-text instructions, WCAG 2.2 AA; support/admin pages noindex and absent from sitemap.
- **Admin, observability, KPI:** Operator may review/quarantine only under support-specific RBAC, elevated session and immutable redacted reason; never display raw object_key, EXIF, file content or customer text in general logs/metrics. Retention and legal-hold decisions are explicit, with time-bounded unlinked-object garbage collection and auditable erasure. Metrics: verified upload-to-message attachment rate, scanner failure/fail-closed count, forbidden read attempts (aggregated), legacy support URL migration coverage, orphan recovery p95, same-key dedup rate, stale-client rate and 0 false READY receipts; analytics event fields exclude member IDs and hashes.
- **P0 backlog:** SUP1194-01 inventory/contain legacy support URLs and prevent storageKey/public-URL return on private support path; SEC1194-02 enforce per-request owner/operator authorization plus no-store at backend/BFF/edge for GET/HEAD/error; SEC1194-03 forward-only DB least-privilege and SECURITY DEFINER tests; REL1194-04 unblock QA v1195 lint, critical/high audit, migration266, backup/restore, branch preservation, exact-SHA Test/Production provenance. Owners: security/support developer + DB B + QA; keep A/B/C/Gemini code branches separate.
- **P1 backlog:** SUP1194-05 durable scan/object/receipt state machine and orphan reconciler; SUP1194-06 atomic case attachment and replay fingerprint/CAS; SUP1194-07 bounded streaming/async BFF upload and 15s timeout contract; SUP1194-08 member web/Android/SEO/a11y and truthful CTAs; SUP1194-09 support operator audit, retention, metrics; SUP1194-10 negative, concurrency, cache, scanner, mobile and rollback regression suite. Each ticket must name exact source/migration paths and TDD RED/GREEN before implementation.
- **Acceptance / release and rollback:** 100 anonymous/foreign-member GET+HEAD+Range attempts reveal 0 bytes/metadata; 100 same-key concurrent uploads/attaches yield exactly one verified receipt/link; 100 key reuse with changed bytes yield 409; 100 scanner failures yield 0 READY; 100 revoke/attach races yield no detached-but-readable object; 10,001 owner history rows paginate without gaps/duplicates; 8 MiB/3-file/24 MiB caps, 15s async receipt, Android parity, zero raw storageKey in private response and zero public cache at edge all verified on controlled Test. Keep support_private_evidence OFF by default, stage Test exact-SHA full lint/typecheck/build/audit/real-PG/owner E2E, verify restore drill and migration parity, then controlled canary with monitored error budget; Production remains BLOCKED until independent QA approval. Rollback disables new uploads, preserves authorized read/retention for committed evidence and immutable audit, never drops tables or deletes files blindly; a new code path requires separate developer branch and release promotion.
- **External evidence gate:** Independently inspected OWASP File Upload Cheat Sheet, Mozilla MDN Cache-Control, PostgreSQL constraints docs (3 publishers); 0 newly accepted/validated independent evidence records, cumulative deduplicated accepted total UNKNOWN, required 10,000 BLOCKED. These sources are design references, not 10,000 verified individual records.


## v2026.10.24.1192 — F675-105 Private Support Evidence Vault (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **Cycle / authority:** Previous Feature Improvement v1190; Current Feature Addition v1192; Next Feature Improvement v1194 (provisional). At start/mid main was `54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede`; canonical main AGENTS/README/PROJECT_PLAN EN+KO read, and prior F675-104 proposals in unmerged Draft #1028/#1033 inspected. Latest QA v1193 FAILED/HOLD. This docs-only stacked design does not imply implemented endpoints, actual Production leakage, passing tests or permission to deploy.
- **Problem / verified code:** `frontend/src/components/floating-support-chat-widget.tsx` uploads support images through `/app-api/v1/content/chat/upload`, then concatenates `capturedImg.url` into the plain-text support reply body. `backend/src/content/chat-upload.controller.ts` uses authenticated+consent+CSRF upload and a scanner-backed storage class, but its `GET content/chat/media/:key` has no explicit auth/owner guard and sends `Cache-Control: public, max-age=86400, immutable`. `ChatImageStorage` uses random UUID keys and private filesystem permissions; guessing a key is not the same as obtaining a URL. The Next app-api gateway forwards backend Cache-Control and allows the content group, while next.config rewrites /api/v1/* to /app-api/v1/*; whether the deployed edge actually exposes a given media URL and whether a specific support URL was disclosed still require controlled routing and access-log evidence. No Production exposure is asserted. Existing `/support` page supports only text and `support_messages.body` is a 2,000-character text field; no `support_evidence` table exists in DEV (3 threads, 6 messages). This new feature must reuse the existing scanner and chat, not introduce a parallel arbitrary public upload path.
- **Purpose / personas / boundaries:** Give signed-in case owners and specifically authorized support operators a private, verifiable way to exchange image evidence (screenshots of errors/receipts) without public media URLs, while preserving plain-text support, admin oversight and optional F675-104 case tracking. Scope is support-only image evidence, NOT direct-message/lobby public media, financial proof of WLD movements, generic document sharing, arbitrary URL fetching or a new public image host. User-visible evidence is not an automatic adjudication of a claim. Default `support_private_evidence` OFF; no new WLD/ledger action.
- **UX / entry / CTA / states:** On `/support` and proposed `/support/cases/:id`, show `Attach screenshot`, `Review`, `Remove before send`, `Send with message`, `View evidence`, `Report incorrect evidence`; operator `/admin/support` gets `View authorized evidence`, `Quarantine`, `Record decision`. Accepted formats proposal PNG/JPEG/WebP, max 8 MiB per file, 3 files per message (24 MiB aggregate), no GIF/SVG/PDF initially. State machine `selected -> uploading -> scanning -> ready -> attached`; failures `rejected_type|rejected_size|scan_unavailable|quarantined|upload_failed|link_conflict|expired|revoked|unavailable`. Only a committed server receipt shows `Attached`; an uploaded-but-unlinked file is `ready_pending_send`, not a sent message. Preview never loads a public object URL. Guest and wrong-owner see no file bytes or metadata.
- **Proposed API (not implemented):** Authenticated member `POST /api/v1/support/threads/:threadId/evidence` multipart single file with CSRF, UUID `Idempotency-Key`, request digest and owner-bound thread; response `202 SCANNING` with durable `evidenceId,receiptId` for asynchronous scans or `201 READY` with `evidenceId,receiptId,sha256,size,mimeType,expiresAt` only after safe scan and persisted metadata. `GET .../evidence/:evidenceId/receipt` is pollable; the existing app-api gateway buffers the request and has a 15-second upstream timeout, so bounded body/memory and scan latency require an explicit streaming/async gateway review before implementation. Extend existing `POST /api/v1/support/threads/:threadId/messages` to accept optional `evidenceIds:uuid[]` (0..3), stable UUID key and same thread/owner binding; persist message+attachments+receipt in one DB transaction, and retain the existing text-only contract when flag OFF. `GET /api/v1/support/threads/:threadId/evidence/:evidenceId` streams bytes after fresh owner check, `DELETE .../evidence/:evidenceId` revokes only an unlinked owner upload, and `GET .../evidence/:evidenceId/receipt` resolves uncertain retries without bytes. Admin equivalents `GET /api/v1/admin/support/threads/:threadId/evidence/:evidenceId` and `POST .../quarantine` require support-specific RBAC, elevated session, reason and immutable audit. Next BFF uses server-only internal API credential; Android `/app-api/v1/support/threads/:threadId/evidence/*` must map to identical owner, CSRF/session, MIME and receipt semantics (actual route-map to be reviewed). Errors: 400 invalid, 401 unauthenticated, 403 operator denied, 404 foreign/unknown, 409 key-fingerprint or wrong-thread, 413 size, 415 type, 429 quota, 503 scanner/storage/commit unknown. Never accept a client-supplied owner, filesystem key or remote URL.
- **DB and storage (proposed reviewed forward migration):** `support_evidence(id uuid PK,thread_id uuid NOT NULL FK support_threads(id),uploader_id uuid NOT NULL FK users(id),object_key text UNIQUE NOT NULL,content_sha256 bytea NOT NULL CHECK octet_length=32,mime_type text NOT NULL CHECK IN (image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp),size_bytes bigint CHECK BETWEEN 1 AND 8388608,state text CHECK IN (PENDING,READY,QUARANTINED,REVOKED,DELETED),scan_version text,created_at timestamptz,expires_at timestamptz,version bigint CHECK >0)`. `support_message_evidence(thread_id uuid,message_id uuid,evidence_id uuid,PRIMARY KEY(message_id,evidence_id),UNIQUE(evidence_id))`; add reviewed composite UNIQUE `(thread_id,id)` on both source tables and composite FKs `(thread_id,message_id)` and `(thread_id,evidence_id)` to enforce same-thread binding. Index `support_evidence(thread_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)`, `support_evidence(state,expires_at,id)`; limit 3 attachments/message in locked SECURITY DEFINER function, not an unenforceable CHECK. Evidence audit `support_evidence_events(id PK,evidence_id FK,actor_id,event_type,redacted_reason,created_at)` append-only. Private non-webroot object directory/bucket with encryption and backups; random storage key never in response, DB audit, URLs, analytics or log. No app-role direct table DML/SELECT; only owner-checked function EXECUTE with fixed search_path and support RBAC. Coordinate with DB owner B on existing P0 privilege remediation and migration 266; never edit applied migrations.
- **Atomicity / malware / abuse:** Reuse `AntivirusScannerService` and `ChatImageStorage` scanning primitives, but create a distinct private support storage namespace and authenticated streaming handler. Server validates decoded filename, actual magic bytes, dimensions/pixel cap, decompression ratio, content type and strict allowlist; re-encode image to strip EXIF/GPS and active content, with sandboxed resource limits. Scanner unavailable/timeout => fail closed, never mark READY. Upload bytes to isolated temporary object, scan/re-encode, durably store the private object first, then atomically commit READY metadata and immutable receipt; serve only when BOTH the object exists and the owner-checked DB row is READY. A failure leaves a nonservable orphan cleaned by bounded sweeper, never a false READY receipt. Message attach locks thread then evidence rows sorted by ID, checks owner/thread/READY/expiry/remaining quota, inserts message + mapping + operation receipt atomically; retry same key/fingerprint returns same receipt, different payload 409. Never hold a DB lock across scanner, disk, CDN or network calls. A delete/revoke races safely with attach through row lock/CAS; attached evidence cannot be silently deleted by uploader and requires audited operator retention decision. Enforce per-member/IP upload rate, daily byte quota, content hash abuse detection, bounded concurrency and antivirus alert redaction. No raw support text, image bytes, EXIF, user IDs or object keys in analytics.
- **Legacy URL transition / privacy gate:** Inventory legacy support messages containing `/api/v1/content/chat/media/` URLs using authorized read-only aggregate queries, separate from direct-message/lobby references. Before exposing a private feature, verify external route reachability, existing caches/CDN rules, retention obligations and whether legacy support URLs can be mapped to an owner-scoped proxy; do not blindly break direct-chat media or delete historical evidence. Stop NEW support replies from embedding public media URLs only after compatible private path is verified. If a legacy URL was shared or cached, changing headers alone cannot revoke copies already obtained; coordinate safe invalidation/notice/remediation with privacy owner. Until verified, `legacy_unverified` is displayed, not `private`. Do not claim historical data was secured retroactively.
- **SEO / mobile / accessibility / KPI:** Member/admin evidence and receipts `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `Content-Disposition: attachment` unless safe raster preview, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, strict CSP, noindex/nofollow, no sitemap, no referrer leak. 320px responsive, keyboard-operable picker/remove, clear 8MiB/3-file limits, progress + `role=status`, focus on errors, alternative text for screenshots, WCAG 2.2 AA; Android content URI/permission loss, background resume and retry preserve idempotency. KPIs: authorized upload-to-attach rate, scanner-fail-closed count, owner denial, orphan cleanup age p95, revoked-link access attempts, legacy URL inventory coverage, duplicate receipt rate, upload bytes and false-success count; aggregate/redacted only. Initial safety gates: 0 wrong-owner bytes, 0 public cache for protected evidence, 0 unscanned READY and 0 false attached receipts; engagement baselines must be measured, not invented.
- **Tests / deploy / rollback:** RED/GREEN real-PG+HTTP tests: 100 wrong-owner/guest/admin-role requests -> 0 bytes/metadata, 100 same-key concurrent uploads/attaches -> one receipt/object link, 100 key/different-content -> 409, 100 revoke-vs-attach -> no linked deleted bytes, 10,001 same-time evidence events -> cursor no duplicates, 8MiB boundary and 8MiB+1 rejection, polyglot/SVG/GIF, EXIF stripping, decompression bombs, scanner down, orphan sweeper, XSS in filename, range/HEAD/cache bypass, Android/web parity, mobile/a11y, flag-OFF legacy text flow. Test private media through actual edge with anonymous browser, authorized member, foreign member, and shared-cache simulation. Exact-SHA CI/lint/typecheck/build, real-PG negative app-role privilege checks, migration266 provenance, Next security audit, encrypted backup+isolated restore, authenticated Test E2E, deployment artifact SHA and approved GitOps release/rollback required. Flag OFF blocks new uploads and preserves historical support messages/immutable receipts; private objects stay access-controlled and quarantined bytes never become public. Production RELEASE HOLD; planning cannot deploy.
- **Concrete backlog:** P0 `SUP1192-01` inventory and close support public-media path before enabling private evidence; P0 `SEC1192-02` owner/RBAC private read, no-store and app-role least privilege; P0 `REL1192-03` QA v1193 lint/security/migration266/branch archive/exact-SHA Test/restore gates. P1 `SUP1192-04` schema, object namespace, scanner/re-encode and orphan lifecycle; `SUP1192-05` idempotent upload/attach API + receipt; `SUP1192-06` member/admin/Android UI and legacy compatibility; `SUP1192-07` abuse/privacy/SEO/a11y/KPI; `SUP1192-08` real-PG/edge/security/rollback regression. No runtime/DB work belongs to planning branch; implementation by separate A/B/C with QA ownership of gates.
- **External evidence gate:** Reviewed OWASP File Upload Cheat Sheet (https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/File_Upload_Cheat_Sheet.html) and API1 object authorization (https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/) (one publisher: OWASP), Mozilla MDN Cache-Control (https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Reference/Headers/Cache-Control) (one publisher: Mozilla); 2 independent publisher organizations reviewed, 0 newly accepted/deduplicated evidence records; cumulative accepted UNKNOWN; target 10,000 => BLOCKED. These are design references, not 10,000 verified independent citations.

## v2026.10.23.1190 — F675-104 Support Case State/Replay Integrity Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **Cycle and baseline:** Previous Feature Addition v1188; Current Feature Improvement v1190; Next Feature Addition v1192 (provisional). Inspected latest `origin/main` `54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede`, predecessor planning Draft PR #1028, open GitHub development issue #1029 and QA v1191 FAILED/HOLD. The v1188 case-tracking proposal remains unmerged. This is a docs-only stacked proposal, not evidence of implementation, Production impact or a reproduced race.
- **New source/DEV evidence (read-only):** `packages/database/migrations/196-user-admin-support-chat.sql` defines `support_threads.status` as `open|waiting_user|resolved`, `support_messages.idempotency_key UNIQUE`, and `support_threads.create_key UNIQUE`. Live DEV has 3 threads/6 messages; app role has direct UPDATE on `support_threads` and DELETE on `support_messages`. `support_create_thread` checks replay owner but not replay subject/initial body; same create key and different payload could return the original thread receipt. `support_add_message` / `admin_support_reply` SELECT dedupe key before INSERT without explicit row lock/`ON CONFLICT`; concurrent retries may surface UNIQUE violations (not proof of duplicate rows). `admin_support_set_status` accepts only target status, has no expected version/idempotency key, and updates even when a member reply changes the same thread. `frontend/src/app/support/actions.ts` and admin actions generate a new UUID on each form submission, not a stable persisted retry token. No verified case timeline API exists.
- **P0 invariant — serialized status and message timeline:** All legacy chat replies, case escalations, triage and status changes must lock the same `support_threads` row (`SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`) before checking actor/owner, current chat status, `case_version` and allowed transition. Lock order: thread -> case metadata -> escalation/receipt -> event -> outbox; no network calls inside transaction. Every successful mutation atomically updates chat status, version, immutable case event and durable receipt. Member reply after resolved reopens to `open` and appends `member_reopened`; admin resolution based on stale version returns HTTP 409 with fresh state and cannot silently overwrite a later reply. Admin reply vs member reply also uses versioned transition; a retry of an already committed mutation returns its original receipt, not a second state transition. Legacy routes remain operational through compatible server wrappers during flag-OFF.
- **P0 invariant — idempotency and replay fingerprint:** Keep existing unique keys; do not create duplicate indexes. For thread creation bind `create_key` to actor + normalized subject + initial body digest, and for messages bind `idempotency_key` to actor + thread + sender kind + normalized body digest. Acquire thread lock for existing thread mutations; use unique-key conflict-safe insert/reselect for new threads, where no thread row exists to lock. Same key/same fingerprint -> exactly one persisted thread/message/event/receipt; same key/different fingerprint -> 409 without revealing another member's receipt; concurrent same-key requests -> deterministic replay result rather than raw SQL 23505/500. Normalize Unicode consistently (do not silently mutate original message text); preserve exact body for audit and hash canonical request representation. Browser/Android must retain a UUID across timeout/retry, not generate a new key for each resend. Do not expose request digests or support bodies in logs.
- **P0 privilege and RBAC:** Forward-only reviewed migration by DB/Backend owner B revokes app-role direct UPDATE/DELETE on support tables and ensures restricted EXECUTE on owner/role-checked `SECURITY DEFINER` wrappers with fixed search_path. Admin queue/triage/decision actions require dedicated support permission plus admin session/step-up for sensitive escalation; generic `admin_current_roles` membership alone is not sufficient for new powers. Member case read, event cursor, escalation receipt and Android mirror must enforce thread owner in DB and Nest BFF. Unknown or wrong-owner case -> indistinguishable 404; unauthorized admin -> 403; no cross-tenant events, metadata, or timing-derived details.
- **P1 API and user contract:** Retain v1188 proposed `GET /api/v1/support/cases/:id`, `GET .../events` and `POST .../escalations`, plus admin queue/triage/decision. All new writes require `Idempotency-Key: UUID` and `If-Match: caseVersion`; return `receiptId,caseVersion,chatStatus,triageState,committedAt` from persisted transaction, with `notificationEvidence: pending|accepted|unknown|failed` (provider accepted does not mean delivered). `409` returns current version without private details to unauthorized users; `503` uncertain outcome instructs lookup by same key, never blind replay with new UUID. `GET .../receipts/:key` is owner/admin scoped and never changes state. Old `PUT /admin/support/threads/:id/status` gets a compatibility CAS gate before adding case flag, not an unversioned bypass. Member UI `/support/cases/:id` and admin `/admin/support/cases` show pending/conflict/unavailable distinctly; no success based on URL params or local optimistic state.
- **P1 schema and operations:** Reuse v1188 `support_case_meta` / `support_case_events` / `support_case_escalations` and existing chat tables; `case_version bigint CHECK >0` and `UNIQUE(thread_id,sequence)` enforce ordered immutable timeline. Add `support_case_operation_receipts(request_key uuid PRIMARY KEY, thread_id uuid FK, actor_id uuid, operation text, request_fingerprint bytea, resulting_case_version bigint, receipt_id uuid UNIQUE, committed_at timestamptz)` with immutable row and indexed owner/thread lookup. For status-only operations use same receipt contract. Index `support_case_events(thread_id,sequence DESC)` and `support_case_meta(triage_state,priority,target_at,thread_id)`; enforce no orphan events, no cross-owner escalations, and audit-verified policy version. Outbox notification intent is part of the transaction, external handoff after commit only. No automatic refunds, WLD balance changes, financial metadata leakage, arbitrary attachments, or new third-party messaging providers.
- **Member/admin, privacy and quality:** CTA `Request review` appears only for eligible owned case; disabled reason and server-derived target time are visible, target is not a guarantee. Admin `Assign / Change priority / Resolve / Review escalation` require reason, current version, and second confirmation for sensitive decisions. Case pages and API responses private/no-store/noindex/nofollow, excluded from sitemap/SEO and third-party analytics; 320px responsive and Android app-api parity, keyboard/focus and `aria-live` receipt/error, WCAG 2.2 AA, localized status names. Rate-limit proposed review requests (initial 3/case/7d, subject to real abuse evidence), throttle admin bulk changes, retain immutable redacted audit. KPI: stale-write conflicts, replay mismatches, p50/p95 first verified human reply, reopening, review response, wrong-owner denial, false success and case queue age; no support message bodies in metrics.
- **Acceptance / TDD / rollout:** RED/GREEN real PostgreSQL tests: 100 concurrent same-key thread creates/messages/escalations -> one durable object/receipt each; 100 same-key different-payload -> 409; 100 resolve-vs-member-reply and admin-vs-member-reply races -> monotonic version, ordered events and no lost reply; 100 wrong-owner member/admin attempts -> zero leaks; 10,001 equal-time events -> gap-free sequence cursor; timeout-after-commit returns original receipt; injected 23505 is normalized without duplicate writes; Unicode normalization, CSRF, HTML injection, mobile retry and feature flag rollback. Run full exact-SHA CI, real-PG negative ACL, migration266 provenance, security audit, authenticated Test E2E, Android parity, backup+isolated restore and approval before Production. Feature `support_case_tracking` default OFF; rollout read-only shadow -> limited Test -> canary -> gated Production; rollback OFF preserves existing chat, audit, receipts and case history, no destructive down-migration.
- **Prioritized backlog / ownership:** P0 `SUP1190-01` thread-create replay fingerprint + conflict-safe receipt; P0 `SUP1190-02` serialized chat/status/case version transitions; P0 `SEC1190-03` revoke direct support DML and enforce owner-scoped support RBAC; P0 `REL1190-04` QA v1191, CI/Next audit/migration266/repository 129 missing tips/exact-SHA Test/restore HOLD. P1 `SUP1190-05` operation receipt schema + cursor + notification intent; P1 `SUP1190-06` member/admin/web/Android state/CTA contracts; P1 `SUP1190-07` redacted admin/a11y/SEO/KPI and abuse control; P1 `SUP1190-08` concurrency/negative access/idempotency/rollback regression. Coordinate #1029 with Development B; Dev A wallet/P2P #1025, Dev C blog, Gemini, QA retain separate code ownership; no direct runtime deployment by planning.
- **Evidence gate:** Consulted PostgreSQL row locking <https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/explicit-locking.html>, PostgreSQL unique constraints <https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/indexes-unique.html> and OWASP API1 authorization <https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/>. Two independent publisher organizations (PostgreSQL Global Development Group, OWASP); newly **accepted** independent evidence records 0, cumulative deduplicated accepted UNKNOWN, target 10,000 **BLOCKED**. References reviewed are not counted as 10,000 verified evidence records.


## v2026.10.23.1188 — F675-104 Support Case Timeline, Triage and Escalation Receipts (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / RELEASE HOLD)
- **Cycle / authority:** Previous Feature Improvement v1186; Current Feature Addition v1188; Next Feature Improvement v1190 (provisional). Investigated `origin/main` `54d5140ddb49dd3e2193a2f82b71a6a0a57cfede` on Debian 13.6. QA v1189 FAILED/HOLD predates this main. Repository integrity incident #1016/#1022: 129 historical unmerged branch tips are not in main; no mass restore, PR reopening, branch pruning or Production promotion is authorized. Docs-only isolated planning branch, no runtime or DB mutation.
- **Source-grounded gap:** Existing `frontend/src/app/support/page.tsx` shows member threads and replies, `frontend/src/app/admin/support/admin-support-view.tsx` manages three chat statuses, `backend/src/support/support.controller.ts` provides session/CSRF-protected chat APIs, and migration `196-user-admin-support-chat.sql` creates `support_threads`, `support_messages` and owner-checking SECURITY DEFINER functions. No case escalation, triage priority, versioned SLA target or immutable member-facing status timeline was found in those interfaces. DEV read-only check: 3 threads, 6 messages, 8 thread columns, 7 message columns, app role currently has UPDATE on support_threads and DELETE on support_messages (global P0 privilege regression). These are DEV observations, not Production incident claims.
- **Purpose / personas / scope:** Let a signed-in member understand *what happened* to a support request, whether a human has acknowledged it, what action is pending, and how to request a review when stalled. Support operator triages, responds and records a reason; authorized supervisor reviews escalations; security auditor sees redacted events. Existing chat remains intact and is the canonical message source. No automatic refund, WLD grant, account sanction, guaranteed response time, autonomous AI resolution, arbitrary file upload or new third-party messaging provider.
- **UX and CTA:** Existing `/support?thread=<uuid>` adds a clearly labeled “Case progress” tab and links to `/support/cases/:id`; user sees case number, creation, last *verified* human reply, current chat status, priority label (informational), published *target* response window (never a promise), event timeline and “Request review” CTA. The button requires a reason, shows eligibility and cooldown, and returns a persisted receipt. States `new|triaged|awaiting_operator|awaiting_member|escalation_requested|under_review|resolved|unavailable`; loading, empty, error, conflict and permission-denied states are distinct. Admin `/admin/support/cases` has priority/age filters, cursor queue, assign/acknowledge/reclassify/resolve controls with mandatory reason and second confirmation for sensitive changes. No fake “resolved” badge based only on query parameters.
- **API proposal (not implemented):** Member `GET /api/v1/support/cases/:id`, `GET /api/v1/support/cases/:id/events?cursor=&limit=20`, `POST /api/v1/support/cases/:id/escalations` with `{reason:1..1000 chars}`, `Idempotency-Key: UUID` and `If-Match: caseVersion`. Admin `GET /api/v1/admin/support/cases?state=&priority=&cursor=&limit=`, `PATCH /api/v1/admin/support/cases/:id/triage` with version, assignee, category, priority, reason, `POST /api/v1/admin/support/cases/:id/escalations/:requestId/decision`, and audited `POST .../:id/resolve`. Existing chat APIs remain unchanged. Response includes `caseId, threadId, caseVersion, chatStatus, triageState, priority, nextActor, targetAt?, targetPolicyVersion?, eventsCursor, receiptId?`. Errors: 400 validation, 401 guest, 403 admin-role refusal, 404 other-member case, 409 version/idempotency mismatch, 429 rate limit, 503 unknown write outcome with safe receipt lookup; no PII in errors. Next server BFF derives actor from session, CSRF required on writes. Android `/app-api/v1/support/cases/*` mirrors the owner-bound semantics after route-map/contract review, without trusting user_id in payload.
- **DB / indexes / constraints:** Reuse `support_threads(id,user_id,status,last_message_at)` and `support_messages(thread_id,...)`, never duplicate their messages or replace the three existing chat states. Proposed `support_case_meta(thread_id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES support_threads(id), category CHECK, priority CHECK('normal','high','urgent'), triage_state CHECK, assigned_admin_id uuid NULL, policy_version integer NOT NULL, version bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, target_at timestamptz NULL, updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`; `support_case_events(id uuid PK, thread_id FK, sequence bigint, actor_id uuid NULL, actor_kind CHECK, kind CHECK, safe_metadata jsonb, created_at timestamptz, UNIQUE(thread_id,sequence))`; `support_case_escalations(id uuid PK, thread_id FK, requester_id FK, request_key uuid UNIQUE, payload_digest bytea, state CHECK, created_at, decided_at NULL)`; `support_case_policies(version PK, target_minutes integer CHECK >0, business_calendar text, effective_at, approved_by)`. Indexes: `(triage_state,priority,target_at,thread_id)`, `(thread_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` for events, `(thread_id,state,created_at DESC)` for escalation queue, and `(user_id,last_message_at DESC,id DESC)` reused from migration 196. Prefer policy target 48 business hours as a configurable **proposal**, not a measured SLA.
- **Atomicity / state machine:** Lock the existing thread row, validate actor/role, version and allowed transition, then insert case event, escalation or triage change, increment version and durable notification intent within ONE PostgreSQL transaction. Same idempotency key+same request fingerprint returns the same persisted receipt; same key+different fingerprint returns 409. A concurrent resolve/reply/escalation cannot yield contradictory chat and case states; closing triage requires chat status resolved and a final reason, while a member reply reopens chat and records a new case event. Never hold DB locks across external delivery. If notification evidence is unavailable, record case transition but show notification `unknown` rather than `sent`; no money or ledger mutation.
- **Security / abuse / privacy:** Use reviewed `SECURITY DEFINER` functions with fixed `search_path`, actor identity derived from server auth, owner check inside DB and explicit admin role/step-up checks; no direct app-role INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE grants. Case IDs are unguessable UUIDs but not authorization. Member escalation proposal: one active escalation per case, 48-hour cooldown after a decision, and rate limit 5 requests/member/day, subject to QA tuning. Reject HTML/script rendering, external URL fetches and unsafe attachments; plain text only. Keep staff notes/other-member identities out of member timeline and analytics. Immutable redacted audit, retention/legal-hold policy to be approved before implementation, deletion requests handled according to existing account lifecycle policy; no irreversible schema drop.
- **SEO / mobile / accessibility:** All support, case, event and admin pages `noindex,nofollow`, no sitemap, `Cache-Control: private,no-store`, no private OpenGraph/JSON-LD payload. At 320px and Android small screens show readable case progress; keyboard reachable timeline and CTA, visible focus, `aria-live` for receipt/error, WCAG 2.2 AA and localization parity; avoid leaking case identifiers through third-party analytics/referrers. Browser and Android cannot change support case ownership.
- **KPI / operations:** Aggregate median/p95 time-to-first-human-response, open age, escalations per 100 cases, overdue *target* count, reopened cases, member satisfaction opt-in, wrong-owner denials, idempotency conflicts, false-success count and queue age. Admin sees aggregates and minimum necessary case details under RBAC; no raw message bodies, personal IDs or WLD balances in metrics. Security events go to audited channels, never to public SEO pages.
- **Tests / regression / rollout:** 100 concurrent same-key escalations => exactly one escalation/event/receipt; 100 same-key different-payload requests => 409; 100 wrong-owner reads/writes => no data; 100 admin/non-admin role negatives; 100 resolve-vs-member-reply races => coherent version and chat state; 10,001 equal-timestamp events => gap-free stable cursor; verify DST/business calendar, XSS payload, HTML escaping, CSRF, malformed UUID, missing operator, empty queue, delayed reply, timeouts, rollback, and Android/web contract parity. Feature flag `support_case_tracking` default OFF, additive forward-only migrations owned by DB developer B, no backfill until audited; shadow-read existing support chat, then limited Test, canary, and gradual opt-in after exact-SHA CI, real-PG negative grants, migration 266 provenance, backup+isolated restore, authenticated Test E2E, PR review and GitOps approval. Flag OFF disables new triage/escalations without hiding existing chat or deleting case events; rollback is non-destructive.
- **P0/P1 backlog:** P0 `SUP1188-01` owner-bound case read and truthful receipt; P0 `SEC1188-02` direct app-role DML revocation + negative tests (coordinate B and #828/#993); P0 `REL1188-03` preserve repository branch evidence #1016/#1022 and Next audit/CI/migration266/backup/exact-SHA HOLD; P1 `SUP1188-04` versioned case metadata/event/triage migration; P1 `SUP1188-05` atomic escalation + idempotency + policy targets; P1 `SUP1188-06` member/admin UI and mobile app-api; P1 `SUP1188-07` privacy/a11y/SEO/admin analytics; P1 `SUP1188-08` concurrency/negative ACL/cursor/DST/rollback QA. Development A wallet/P2P (#1025), B DB/CI, C blog, Gemini and QA own their code; this branch is docs only.
- **Independent references gate:** Reviewed OWASP API1 object authorization (<https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0xa1-broken-object-level-authorization/>), PostgreSQL transaction locking (<https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/explicit-locking.html>), and W3C WCAG 2.2 (<https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/>): 3 independent publisher organizations, **0 newly admitted verified records**, cumulative deduplicated accepted count UNKNOWN, target 10,000 BLOCKED. No claim of passing the gate.

## v2026.10.23.1186 — F675-103 Verified Digest Dispatch Hardening (FEATURE IMPROVEMENT / DRAFT / HOLD)
- **Cycle / authority:** Previous Feature Addition v1184; Current Feature Improvement v1186; Next Feature Addition v1188 (provisional). This supplements, does not replace, F675-103 v1184. Latest main at investigation: `1a31a6f2df2b75a0accceef12662dc42d9ab2d45`; predecessor Draft PR #1014 `aed8d058`; QA v1187 FAILED/HOLD predates latest main. Planning-only stacked branch; no code, DB, balance, switch, Test or Production mutation.
- **Confirmed contract gaps:** Development `in_app_notifications.id` is UUID; `dedupe_key` has a partial UNIQUE index, but `category` is free text with no proven category→consent-purpose mapping. `notification_preferences` has `state DEFAULT 'OPTED_IN'`, nullable `consented_at`, and default `source_surface='WEB_SETTINGS'`: these defaults are NOT evidence of voluntary consent. `outbox_events` has UUID PK and retry metadata but NO UNIQUE digest idempotency key. Existing Nest notification list sorts `created_at DESC` with OFFSET and no ID tie-breaker; no digest API is implemented. Do not assert a Production incident or duplicate send from these structural findings.
- **P0 fail-closed consent & eligibility:** No optional digest external handoff unless current authenticated owner has an explicit `OPTED_IN` record for each eligible purpose AND channel, non-null verifiable `consented_at`, trusted immutable consent audit/source and policy version, no revocation, and active digest setting; DB defaults and client-supplied `source_surface` never establish consent. A reviewed, versioned `category→purpose_code` mapping and source-event verification are prerequisites; unknown category, missing event provenance, private/financially unverified amount, or ambiguous mandatory-vs-optional classification => EXCLUDE and audit redacted reason. Mandatory account/security/transaction alerts remain immediate, never deferred or silenced. Reuse existing notification preference PK; change dangerous default only through reviewed forward migration owned by F675-102, not a conflicting planning migration.
- **P0 unique dispatch invariant:** Existing `outbox_events` has no event-deduplication constraint; `notification_digest_windows.UNIQUE(user_id,channel,frequency,window_start_utc,window_end_utc)` prevents duplicate windows but does not alone prevent duplicate outbox intents. Propose `notification_digest_windows.outbox_event_id uuid UNIQUE REFERENCES outbox_events(id)` plus `notification_digest_dispatch_keys(digest_id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES notification_digest_windows(id), request_fingerprint bytea NOT NULL, outbox_event_id uuid UNIQUE NOT NULL REFERENCES outbox_events(id), created_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`, written atomically under one window row lock. Deterministic key covers user, channel, UTC closed-open window, purpose-policy version, settings version; same key+same fingerprint returns same receipt, same key+different fingerprint 409; existing notification `dedupe_key` is not a substitute. Do not add a global UNIQUE constraint to generic outbox without auditing other publishers.
- **P0 revocation vs in-flight provider handoff:** Do not promise cancellation of a request already accepted by a third-party provider. Persist per-user/channel dispatch generation and durable `CLAIMED→HANDOFF_STARTED→PROVIDER_ACCEPTED|UNKNOWN|FAILED` attempt records. Revoke immediately blocks new claims and new handoffs, marks an in-flight attempt `REVOCATION_PENDING` and shows honest pending status until acknowledged/reconciled; `revocation_effective_at` is committed only after active handoffs are drained/fenced. No DB lock is held across network provider calls. Recheck generation/consent/quiet hours just before handoff, reject stale leases, and do not blindly retry `UNKNOWN` acknowledgements. Provider acceptance ≠ delivery/read. A flag-OFF or rollback cancels new optional claims but preserves opt-outs, pending outcomes and immutable audit.
- **P1 API & owner/role boundaries:** Extend proposed owner-only `GET/PUT /api/v1/notifications/digest/preferences`, `POST .../preview`, `GET .../history` and admin retry contracts with `settingsVersion`, `consentPolicyVersion`, `dispatchGeneration`, `revocationStatus: NONE|PENDING|EFFECTIVE`, `receiptId` and `deliveryEvidence: NONE|ACCEPTED|UNKNOWN`. `If-Match` + UUID `Idempotency-Key` are mandatory for writes; stale versions and key/payload conflicts return 409, uncertain writes return 503 plus queryable receipt, wrong owner returns 404, unauthenticated 401. Server derives actor from session/app token, never from request body; admin retry requires RBAC, step-up, reason, immutable audit, and must not override consent. Web BFF and Android App API share the exact semantics; no false success from `?saved=1`.
- **P1 DB & cursor correctness:** Use owner-checked SECURITY DEFINER read/write functions with fixed `search_path`, least-privilege EXECUTE, no direct app-role DML, and tested wrong-owner denial. Link each digest item to `in_app_notifications(user_id,id)` using a reviewed composite UNIQUE/foreign-key strategy or an atomic function-enforced same-owner check, not UUID existence alone. Use `(user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` index and opaque signed keyset cursor for 10,001 same-timestamp history rows; no OFFSET drift. Keep scheduler `(status,scheduled_at,id)` index and bounded `SKIP LOCKED` claims. Do not copy private notification body into provider metadata, analytics or admin logs.
- **P1 deterministic schedule & UI:** Compute local DAILY/WEEKLY intervals using validated IANA timezone and closed-open UTC windows. DST missing hour shifts to first valid local instant; repeated hour selects first occurrence once; setting/timezone version change cancels stale queued windows without retroactive reclassification. Quiet-hours deferral moves optional send to first permitted instant, never mandatory alerts. Member screens `/account/notifications/digest` and `.../history` show `disabled|enabled|paused|revocation_pending|queued|accepted|unknown|failed`, Preview never sends, Save only confirms persisted receipt, Retry only when safe. Mobile >=320px, WCAG 2.2 AA keyboard/focus/status/error, Android 13+ OS permission independent of server consent, private/no-store/noindex/nofollow and sitemap exclusion.
- **P1 observability & abuse:** Redacted admin aggregate by purpose/channel and dispatch status, queue-age p95, verified consent suppression, unknown acknowledgements, dedupe conflicts, opt-out pending age, false-success count, complaints per 1,000 delivered; never publish recipient lists, message bodies or raw device tokens. Rate-limit previews (initial proposal 10/member/hour), retry budget and bulk approval; all limits require QA baseline. Data retention proposal 30 days for diagnostic events pending privacy approval, never silently delete mandatory financial audit evidence.
- **Acceptance and rollout:** 100 simultaneous window claims => exactly one dispatch key/outbox event; 100 identical PUT retries => one receipt, 100 conflicting payloads => 409; 100 wrong-owner reads/writes => no data; 100 opt-out vs handoff races => zero NEW handoffs after effective revoke and honest pending for in-flight; 100 unknown provider acknowledgements => no false delivered status or blind duplicate retry; 10,001 equal-timestamp records => zero gaps/duplicates; DST forward/back, timezone change, disabled permission, empty digest, malicious HTML, admin retry denial and flag rollback tests. Exact-SHA CI/full lint/typecheck/build, real-PG privilege negatives and migration 266 provenance, Android parity, authenticated Test E2E, backup+isolated restore and GitOps approval are required before any rollout. Keep `notification_digest` OFF; Production HOLD.
- **Reprioritized backlog:** P0 `NOTIF1186-01` fail-closed purpose/category provenance and explicit consent; P0 `NOTIF1186-02` unique dispatch/outbox key and honest provider acknowledgement; P0 `SEC1186-03` app-role grants/SECURITY DEFINER owner isolation; P0 `REL1186-04` CI/security/Migration266/backup/restore/exact-SHA gates. P1 `NOTIF1186-05` revocation/lease fence; P1 `NOTIF1186-06` keyset history and member/Android contract; P1 `NOTIF1186-07` admin privacy/SEO/a11y/KPI; P1 `NOTIF1186-08` concurrency/DST/rollback regression. Dependency PRs #994/#998/#1005/#1010/#1014 and issue #1015 stay unmerged; Dev A/B/C, Gemini and QA work remains independent.
- **Independent evidence gate:** Reviewed PostgreSQL SELECT/SKIP LOCKED, OWASP API1 object-level authorization and Android permission documentation; 3 publisher organizations reviewed this cycle, 0 newly accepted deduplicated evidence records; cumulative accepted UNKNOWN; 10,000 target BLOCKED. No claim of achieving the gate.


## v2026.10.23.1184 — F675-103 Opt-in Notification Digest & Quiet Delivery (FEATURE ADDITION / DRAFT / HOLD)
- **Cycle and authority:** Previous Feature Improvement v1182; Current Feature Addition v1184; Next Feature Improvement v1186 (provisional). Read-only baseline main `8dc8d7d34fb110db7c3b4702fe310d390165a502`, miniPC Debian 13.6; 27 open PRs; QA v1185 FAILED/HOLD is older than this main and does not certify it. EN canonical, KO semantically equivalent. Docs-only: no runtime, schema, balances, Test or Production changes.
- **Problem and dependencies:** The live source `backend/src/notification/notification.controller.ts` offers member list/unread/read operations; `notification.service.ts` lists `in_app_notifications` with offset pagination and no digest API. Development DB has 0 `in_app_notifications` and 0 `notification_preferences` rows, an existing partial UNIQUE `dedupe_key`, existing `notification_preferences` PK `(user_id,purpose_code,channel)`, and an `outbox_events` delivery-attempt model. This does not establish Production data counts. F675-101 verified inbox (#994/#998) and F675-102 consent/quiet hours (#1005/#1010) are UNMERGED drafts; this feature depends on their reviewed interfaces and must not assert them as implemented.
- **Purpose/personas and scope:** Let consenting members replace bursts of optional activity notices with one factual daily or weekly digest, while preserving immediate security, account, and essential transaction notices. Target: signed-in member, mobile app user, notification operator (aggregated diagnostics), authorized security auditor. Digest is an information presentation only: no WLD movement, no automatic claim, no advertising enrollment, no new email/push provider contract by implication. Default OFF; existing notifications continue their current behavior until opt-in is explicitly saved.
- **UX/entry/CTA/states:** Member `/account/notifications/digest` from notification settings; controls: Enable digest (explicit consent), Daily/Weekly, weekday (weekly only), local delivery hour (default proposal 09:00), IANA timezone, delivery channel, Preview, Save, Pause, Resume, View history. `/account/notifications/digest/history` shows own real receipts with cursor. `/admin/notifications/digests` shows redacted aggregates, failed dispatches and controlled retry. Guest redirects to login without leaking member data. States `disabled|editing|saving|enabled|paused|previewing|preview_ready|empty|queued|sent|failed|unknown`; display success only on server-committed receipt, never from a URL flag or local-only optimistic state. Preview is explicitly non-delivery and never asserts an actual notification or WLD reward. Unknown delivery remains unknown, not success.
- **Content and consent rules:** Digest includes only committed, owner-scoped eligible optional notifications from an exact UTC window; use event IDs, source verification and canonical detail links; omit unverified monetary amounts, fabricated event counts and unreadable source events. Critical security/transaction notices are NEVER held back or made optional by digest settings; their existing mandatory delivery path is unchanged. A member's purpose/channel consent must be `OPTED_IN` with verifiable consent evidence; missing preference, missing evidence, revoked, or unknown means NO optional external delivery. An in-app digest preview may be read without push/email consent but cannot generate a push/email send. Recheck opt-in, channel permission and quiet hours at actual dispatch; withdrawal after provider acceptance cannot be retroactively recalled and must be explained.
- **Proposed API (not implemented):** `GET /api/v1/notifications/digest/preferences` owner-only; `PUT /api/v1/notifications/digest/preferences` authenticated+consent+CSRF, `Idempotency-Key` UUID, `If-Match` version, strict `{enabled,frequency:DAILY|WEEKLY,weekday:1..7,localHour:0..23,timeZone:IANA,channel:IN_APP|PUSH|EMAIL}`; `POST /api/v1/notifications/digest/preview` owner-only, CSRF, non-persistent, rate-limited, max 20 redacted items; `GET /api/v1/notifications/digest/history?cursor=&limit=20` stable owner cursor (max 100); `GET /api/v1/admin/notifications/digests` operator RBAC; `POST /api/v1/admin/notifications/digests/:id/retry` audited operator+step-up+CSRF, only retryable and consent-valid. Next BFF routes forward session and CSRF without exposing `INTERNAL_API_TOKEN`. Android `/app-api/v1/notifications/digest/*` must map to equivalent owner-bound contract with app auth; exact paths are proposals pending route-map/contract review. Errors 400 validation, 401/403 auth, 404 not owned, 409 stale version or reused key/different payload, 429 limits, 503 uncertain; never reveal foreign membership.
- **DB design (proposed forward-only reviewed migration):** `notification_digest_settings(user_id uuid PK FK users, enabled boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false, frequency text CHECK IN (DAILY,WEEKLY), weekday smallint CHECK 1..7, local_hour smallint CHECK 0..23, time_zone text NOT NULL validated against IANA catalog, channel text CHECK IN (IN_APP,PUSH,EMAIL), version bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK >0, updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`; never duplicate the authoritative purpose/channel consent stored in `notification_preferences`. `notification_digest_windows(id uuid PK,user_id uuid FK,channel text,frequency text,window_start_utc timestamptz,window_end_utc timestamptz,scheduled_at timestamptz,status text CHECK IN (QUEUED,SKIPPED,SENT,FAILED,UNKNOWN,CANCELLED),request_fingerprint bytea,attempts integer CHECK >=0,version bigint,created_at,updated_at, UNIQUE(user_id,channel,frequency,window_start_utc,window_end_utc), CHECK(window_start_utc<window_end_utc))`. `notification_digest_items(digest_id uuid FK,notification_id uuid FK,PRIMARY KEY(digest_id,notification_id))` with function-enforced same-owner invariant; `notification_digest_delivery_events(id uuid PK,digest_id uuid FK,event_kind,redacted_reason,created_at)` append-only. Indices `(user_id,created_at DESC,id DESC)` for history, `(status,scheduled_at,id)` for workers, `(digest_id,created_at,id)` for audit. Do not recreate existing notification `dedupe_key` unique index. No app-role direct DML: reviewed SECURITY DEFINER functions with fixed search_path, actor binding, function EXECUTE allowlist, negative grants/RLS tests.
- **Atomic workflow / schedule:** A scheduled worker claims due windows with `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, verifies owner/purpose/channel and current settings version, computes the closed-open UTC event window, persists immutable eligible notification IDs plus one deduplicated outbox intent in ONE transaction. Use deterministic idempotency key `digest:user:channel:window_start:window_end:settings_version` and request fingerprint; same key/different payload returns 409. Never hold DB locks across provider calls. Dispatch worker rechecks consent/quiet hours immediately before provider handoff; revocation locks/versions the relevant settings row and cancels queued optional sends; already accepted provider sends are recorded as `UNKNOWN/SENT` only with actual provider evidence. Provider retries use bounded exponential backoff and provider idempotency where available; unknown acknowledgement must not be reported as delivered or blindly resent. Recompute next eligible local slot from IANA timezone; DST nonexistent hour -> next valid instant, repeated hour -> first occurrence only, unique UTC window prevents duplicate sends; quiet-hour collision defers to first permitted instant without silently dropping mandatory alerts.
- **Admin/security/abuse:** Admin sees aggregate scheduled/suppressed/failed counts, queue age and hashed correlation IDs, not private bodies or recipient lists by default. Retry needs RBAC, recent step-up, CSRF, reason, immutable audit and 2-person approval for bulk sends; never allow manual bypass of revoked consent. Owner-only DB reads; BOLA/CSRF/replay tests, per-user/IP preview rate limits (initial proposal 10/hour, tunable), scheduler global budget, 20-item preview cap, no arbitrary URLs/HTML, safe internal link allowlist, no PII in logs/analytics/provider metadata, retention proposal 30 days for delivery diagnostics pending privacy review. Zero external send without consent proof. Security and financial notifications remain separate.
- **SEO/mobile/a11y:** All member, history, preview, admin and digest links `noindex,nofollow`, `Cache-Control: private,no-store`, excluded from sitemap; only a non-personal public help page may be indexed. Responsive >=320px, WCAG 2.2 AA, semantic switches, keyboard-only, accessible time-zone selector, error summary, `role=status` for confirmed saves/preview results, `role=alert` for errors, focus preserved; Android 13+ POST_NOTIFICATIONS permission is distinct from server consent and never inferred from it. Device permission denied => no push, in-app state remains available.
- **Analytics/KPIs:** Consent opt-in/out (aggregate), scheduled vs actually provider-accepted digests, duplicate-window count, suppression-after-revoke count, send-to-open rate, preview-to-opt-in, error/unknown rate, queue lag p95, per-member complaint rate; no raw event bodies or member IDs in analytics. Initial release goals: unauthorized digest access 0/100, sends after committed revocation 0/100 simulated races, duplicate dispatch 0/100 concurrent claims, false success receipts 0, missing consent sends 0, >99% correct window mapping in deterministic fixture; business engagement targets require baseline data and are not invented.
- **Test/QA and release/rollback:** RED/GREEN DB function tests for wrong-owner 100/100, 100 identical PUT retries and 100 conflicting concurrent edits, 100 parallel window claims produce one outbox intent, consent revoke vs dispatch 100 races, 10,001 same-timestamp notifications with cursor no duplicates/gaps, DST spring/fall and timezone changes, empty windows, provider timeout/unknown ack, Android permission denial, HTML injection, admin bulk denial, a11y and noindex. Exact-SHA CI/typecheck/lint, real PostgreSQL ACL negative tests, frontend/backend/app-api contract and authenticated Test E2E, migration 266 reconciliation, encrypted backup + isolated restore, runtime identity/image digest and rollback proof are mandatory. Feature flag `notification_digest` defaults OFF; staged canary only after gates; disable flag halts new optional dispatch without erasing settings, event receipts or mandatory security/transaction notices; rollback must preserve revoked consent and prevent old workers from sending. No direct Production deploy from planning.
- **Backlog:** P0 `NOTIF1184-01` owner-scoped verified source/consent boundary; P0 `SEC1184-02` app-role least privilege, DB function authorization; P0 `REL1184-03` latest-main CI/security/migration266/backup/restore/exact-SHA Test release blockers; P1 `NOTIF1184-04` versioned settings + idempotent API; P1 `NOTIF1184-05` deterministic windows, outbox and DST; P1 `NOTIF1184-06` member web + Android parity; P1 `NOTIF1184-07` admin RBAC, privacy, SEO, a11y, KPIs; P1 `NOTIF1184-08` concurrency, regression, canary and rollback. Dev A/B/C/QA/Gemini own separate implementation branches; planning owns docs only.
- **Evidence gate:** Independently reviewed publishers: OWASP API Top 10 (https://api-security.owasp.org/editions/2023/en/0x00-header/), W3C WCAG status (https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/status-messages.html), Android notification permission (https://developer.android.com/develop/ui/compose/notifications/notification-permission), IETF RFC 5545 (https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc5545), PostgreSQL INSERT/ON CONFLICT (https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-insert.html): 5 distinct publisher organizations, NOT 5 accepted independent evidence records. Newly accepted/deduplicated evidence **0**; cumulative accepted **UNKNOWN**; target **10,000**; evidence gate **BLOCKED**. No false attainment claim.


## v2026.10.22.1140 — F675-92 Calculator Accuracy Reports & Verified Correction Notices (PLANNING / DRAFT)
- **Cycle:** Previous Feature Improvement v1138; Current Feature Addition v1140; Next Feature Improvement v1142 (provisional). Scope is documentation only. Baseline GitHub main `c3e62cd49da66c0b7c48223fcc81be888166f183`; local miniPC main lagged by one commit; open PRs #975/#977/#978/#981/#985; QA v1135 FAILED/HOLD (7 frontend CI failures, finance ACL/migration unresolved; 40/40 sampled ledger balances reconciled in development only). Never infer a Test/Production pass from this.
- **Purpose/personas:** Public calculator users need to flag questionable formula, tax-law year, assumptions or output; signed-in members can submit a reproducible, privacy-minimized issue; qualified editor triages, independent reviewer approves a correction, administrator publishes a verified notice. The system does NOT auto-correct historical financial results or award WLD. New feature F675-92 is independent of unmerged F675-91 saved-scenario drafts.
- **Source-observed risk:** `frontend/src/lib/capital-gains-tax-calculator.ts` subtracts `spouseGiftDeductionAmount` (default 600,000,000 KRW) directly from capital gain when a checkbox is selected, although the official NTS capital-gains computation flow does not establish such an unconditional gain deduction; legal validation is required before using the option.
